The image route, QGVI-I, filters LoGS-based geometric estimates using distinct inlier query keypoints. It omits photometric refinement and preserves accepted pose coordinates. On nine development scenes, QGVI-T achieves 58.3% Top-1 and 78.3% Top-5 joint relevance-and-validity success, compared with 26.1% and 34.6% for our CityLoC reimplementation with refinement. QGVI-I achieves 97.3% scene-mean pose success at 1 m/2 degrees, with pooled false-acceptance and false-rejection rates of 0.707% and 1.142%. A paired cached evaluation retains all 1,775 initializer poses correct at this threshold. Recorded median text query latency is 0.020 s. Matched controls show competitive BM25 retrieval and slightly lower false acceptance for thresholded full LoGS; grid augmentation suggests modest gains on novel-view-derived descriptions. We report model-judged semantic success separately from source-camera recovery, with source-verified or estimated scales for geometric evaluation.

</details>


### [37] [Parse Before You Execute: Efficient Spatial Reasoning with Executable Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41389)
*Xiwei Liu,Yulong Li,Xinling Zhuang,Xuhui Li,Yichen Li,Haolin Yang,Zhixiang Lu,Yutong Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Spatial reasoning in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) has increasingly benefited from tool-integrated pipelines that ground computation in explicit geometry. However, existing systems often compile queries into relatively open-ended artifacts, such as code, task constraints, tool plans, or gathered evidence, leaving what must be resolved before execution under-specified. This can lead to unnecessary tool acquisition and iterative refinement, while still producing plausible reasoning traces from incomplete spatial commitments. We identify this failure mode as under-committed query compilation. To address it, we propose PEPS, a training-free agentic paradigm Parsing Executable Primitives for Spatial reasoning under a typed FESM schema over Frame, Entity, State, and Metric. PEPS follows a parse-before-execute principle: the Parser commits each query to a compact set of executable requirements; the Executor selectively grounds the required primitives through fixed geometric APIs and deterministic computation; and the Verifier checks execution sufficiency, enabling early termination or targeted repair. Extensive experiments across challenging spatial reasoning benchmarks show that PEPS consistently improves reasoning accuracy while substantially reducing refinement rounds, API cost, and end-to-end latency over strong tool-integrated baselines. These results demonstrate that explicit requirement commitment can make tool-integrated spatial reasoning not only more reliable, but also more execution-efficient.

</details>


### [38] [DUGO: Dual-Scale Gaussian Memory for Online Temporal Action Localization in Egocentric Procedural Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41402)
*Mohammed Aman Bhuiyan,Md. Sazzad Hossain Adib,Samiul Basir Bhuiyan,Mir Faiyaz Hossain,Syed Mohammed Shamsul Islam,Md Zakir Hossain,Shafin Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DUGO框架，通过双尺度时序编码器与高斯记忆库联合建模局部运动动态与长程时序依赖，在流式在线时序动作定位任务上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线时序动作定位（OnTAL）无法访问未来帧且不能回溯修正预测，实时性与窄时间窗约束使任务困难；现有方法多依赖短时锚点上下文或单尺度历史帧记忆，难以捕捉跨多时间尺度展开、需长程推理的程序性视频动作。

Method: DUGO包含两部分：(1) 双尺度时序编码器，将深度可分离局部表征与全局自注意力结合，兼顾细粒度时序模式与更宽的上下文线索；(2) 高斯记忆库，将动作记忆建模为随机、以输入为条件的高斯原型，并在编码器阶段与锚点查询阶段通过门控交叉注意力进行读取。

Result: 在程序性第一视角数据集EGTEA、EPIC-Kitchens-100上达到SOTA，并在非第一视角基准Muses上也有显著提升。

Conclusion: 双尺度时序建模与高斯原型记忆的组合可有效缓解在线场景下多尺度动作与长程依赖的建模难题，且具备跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Online Temporal Action Localization (OnTAL) aims to detect and localize action instances from untrimmed streaming videos in real time. Unlike offline approaches, OnTAL models have no access to future frames and cannot go back and refine earlier predictions, which makes the task considerably harder given the strict real-time constraints and narrow temporal window available. Most existing OnTAL methods lean on either brief anchor-based context or a single-scale memory of past frames, which limits how well they can capture actions unfolding across multiple time scales or requiring long-range reasoning in Procedural Videos. To address these gaps, we present DUGO (Dual-Scale Encoding and Gaussian Memory Online), a new OnTAL framework that jointly models local motion dynamics and long-range temporal dependencies. At its core, DUGO uses a Dual-Scale Temporal Encoder, pairing depth-wise local representations with global self-attention so it can pick up both fine-grained temporal patterns and broader contextual cues. We also introduce a Gaussian Memory Bank, which represents action memory as stochastic, input-conditioned Gaussian prototypes, allowing for richer temporal memory through gated cross-attention applied at both the encoder and anchor-query stages. Extensive experiments on procedural egocentric datasets (EGTEA, EPIC-Kitchens-100) demonstrate that DUGO achieves state-of-the-art performance while also providing substantial improvements on non-egocentric benchmarks such as Muses. The project page is available at: https://aman554-eq.github.io/DUGO

</details>


### [39] [SACQ: Structure-Aware Codebook Quantization for Ultra-Low-Bit Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41408)
*Haesung Lee,Insu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SACQ：面向VLA模型的超低比特权重向量码本量化框架，结合结构感知码本拟合、动作感知混合比特分配与两阶段量化后精调，在1.5比特下保持93.85%成功率并将内存降低4.83倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型内存占用大，难以部署在资源受限机器人平台；沿用自LLM的PTQ方法在超低比特下显著退化，且现有低比特PTQ未显式考虑下游算子结构与量化对策略动作的影响。

Method: 提出SACQ，仅权重量化的向量码本框架，含三部分：(1) 结构感知码本拟合，采用反映输入统计与下游敏感度的角色特定二次度量；(2) 动作感知混合比特分配，依据逐层压缩导致的最终动作误差增量分配精度；(3) 两阶段量化后精调，先用二阶误差补偿重分配码字索引，再针对解码动作校准码本与行组尺度。

Result: 在GR00T N1.7上，语言/动作权重1.5比特时四个LIBERO套件平均成功率93.85%，内存降低4.83倍；叠加INT8激活量化与低精度注意力后保持93.55%成功率，较BF16加速1.51倍。在SimplER上，2比特下Visual Matching与Variant Aggregation成功率分别为63.76%和52.27%，BF16为58.90%和53.45%。

Conclusion: 显式引入算子结构与最终动作误差作为量化目标，可使VLA在超低比特下几乎无损，实现显著内存与推理效率收益，部分任务甚至超过BF16基线。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable capable robot behavior, but their large memory footprint limits deployment on resource-constrained robotic platforms. Weight quantization is a natural remedy, yet post-training quantization (PTQ) recipes inherited from large language models degrade when applied to VLAs at ultra-low precision. Existing low-bit PTQ methods often fail to explicitly account for the structure of downstream operations and allocate precision without considering its effect on the policy actions. We introduce SACQ, an ultra- low-bit weight-only vector-codebook quantization framework for VLAs. SACQ combines (1) structure-aware codebook fitting with role-specific quadratic metrics that reflect input statistics and downstream sensitivity; (2) action-aware mixed-bit allocation based on the increase in final-action error as individual layers are compressed; and (3) two-stage post-quantization refinement that reassigns codeword indices with second-order error compensation and calibrates codebooks and row-group scales against the decoded action. Using GR00T N1.7, SACQ achieves 93.85% average success across four LIBERO suites at 1.5 bits per language/action weight while reducing model memory by 4.83x. With INT8 activation quantization and low-precision attention, it retains 93.55% success while running 1.51x faster than BF16. On SimplER, SACQ further achieves 63.76% and 52.27% success under Visual Matching and Variant Aggregation at 2 bits per language/action weight, compared to 58.90% and 53.45% for BF16, respectively.

</details>


### [40] [FlexReader: Towards Adaptive Decoding Paths for Efficient Arbitrary-Length Scene Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41426)
*Zhibin Ma,Pengwen Dai,Zhigang Yao,Xugong Qin,Wenqi Ren,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FlexReader，一个支持多种解码步长的场景文本识别模型，单模型即可在不同数据集上实现精度-效率最优权衡，并配合自适应前缀解码按置信度动态选择步长，最高较自回归解码提速6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STR模型的训练范式将其与特定解码策略强绑定，部署时缺乏灵活性，难以在不同识别场景下同时兼顾精度与效率。

Method: 1) 提出Sub-string Perception模块，从视觉特征中感知目标子串；2) 接多个并行解码块，独立解码子串内各字符；3) 推理时支持在感知子串范围内以任意步长解码；4) 提出Adaptive Prefix Decoding，依据已解码字符的置信度逐步自适应选择解码步长，为每个输入寻找最优解码路径。

Result: 在长文本、弯曲文本、遮挡等多类基准上均表现出稳健识别能力，识别精度一致优于现有解码策略，相对自回归解码最高实现6倍加速。

Conclusion: FlexReader通过解耦模型与固定解码策略，实现单一模型的多步长灵活解码与自适应路径选择，在精度与效率间取得更优权衡，具备良好的部署适应性。

Abstract: Existing Scene Text Recognition (STR) models are inherently coupled with a specific decoding strategy by their training paradigms, limiting their flexibility in deployment across diverse recognition scenarios. In this paper, we propose FlexReader, an STR model that supports multiple decoding strides, enabling a single model to achieve an optimal accuracy-efficiency trade-off across different datasets. Specifically, we first introduce a Sub-string Perception module to perceive the target sub-string from visual features, followed by multiple parallel decoding blocks that independently decode each character in the sub-string. During inference, FlexReader supports decoding with arbitrary strides within the perceived sub-string range. Furthermore, we propose Adaptive Prefix Decoding, which adaptively selects the decoding stride at each step based on the model's confidence in the decoded characters, thereby identifying the optimal decoding path for each input. Experimental results demonstrate that FlexReader exhibits robust recognition capability across a diverse range of benchmarks, including those featuring long text, curved text, and occlusions. It consistently outperforms existing decoding strategies in recognition accuracy while achieving up to 6-flod decoding speedup over autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [41] [Beyond Trial-and-Error: A Geospatial-Guided Active Reasoning Framework for Robust Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41446)
*Jiawei Ge,Wenkang Gong,Jiuxin Cao,Bo Liu,Lei Cui,Haoran Wang,Xuelin Zhu,Biwei Cao,Chang Liu,Chen Feng,Ioannis Patras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GeoReasoner 框架，将空中视觉语言导航（Aerial VLN）从"反应式试错"转变为"地理空间引导的主动推理"，通过预搜索地理空间推理、假设驱动探索与一致性感知目标验证三个环节，在 CityNav 基准上取得零样本 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中 VLN 需在大型城市场景中定位语言描述的目标，高空视角下目标通常很小且视觉歧义大。现有方法沿用室内 VLN 的反应式范式，仅从局部观测反复更新导航状态，未能利用导航过程中稳定的地理空间结构；视觉相似的干扰物会使反应式搜索基于被动积累的证据不断强化局部看似合理的错误假设，导致定位不可靠。

Method: GeoReasoner 包含三个模块：(1) 预搜索地理空间推理，在搜索前推导区域级先验，约束目标假设空间；(2) 假设驱动探索，主动获取判别性证据以区分并精化相互竞争的目标假设；(3) 一致性感知目标验证，在停止前以对抗式方式评估互补证据源，降低过早或错误确认目标的概率。

Result: 在真实世界 CityNav 基准上，GeoReasoner 在零样本 Aerial VLN 方法中达到新的 SOTA，并与监督方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 利用地理空间结构先验并主动获取证据、辅以一致性验证，能有效缓解高空视角下的视觉歧义与反应式搜索的假设强化问题，为 Aerial VLN 提供了更可靠的新范式。

Abstract: Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires UAVs to locate language-specified goals in large-scale urban environments, where objects often appear small and visually ambiguous from high-altitude viewpoints. Existing methods largely follow a reactive paradigm common in indoor VLN, repeatedly updating navigation states from local observations while underutilizing the geospatial structure that remains stable during navigation. As a result, visually similar distractors can cause a reactive goal search to repeatedly reinforce locally plausible goal hypotheses based on passively accumulated evidence, leading to unreliable localization. To address this, we propose GeoReasoner, a robust framework that shifts the paradigm of Aerial VLN from reactive trial-and-error to geospatial-guided active reasoning. Specifically, GeoReasoner first performs pre-search geospatial reasoning to derive region-level priors and constrain the goal hypothesis space. Building on these, it further conducts hypothesis-driven exploration to actively acquire discriminative evidence and refine competing goal hypotheses. Finally, consistency-aware goal verification adversarially assesses complementary evidence sources before stopping, hence reducing premature or erroneous goal confirmations. Experiments on the real-world CityNav benchmark demonstrate that GeoReasoner sets a new SOTA among zero-shot Aerial VLN methods while competing favorably with supervised approaches.

</details>


### [42] [BDNet: Fast-slow thinking is all you need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41485)
*Chenzhi Zheng,Zhuoran Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BDNet 提出一种以“快速图像特征 + 慢速统计参考”为核心的分层分类骨干，用基线形变算子直接在参考相对残差上建模，实现线性复杂度，并以 20.70M 参数在 ImageNet-1K 上评估其干净精度与扰动鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制依赖成对 token 相似度，复杂度随 token 数呈二次增长，且实现复杂。作者希望在不引入输入依赖注意力的前提下，用更简单、线性复杂度的张量运算提升紧凑表示的鲁棒性。

Method: 核心为基线形变算子（BDO）：对特征相对阶段特定参考的偏差做低秩投影与可学习通道缩放，直接作用于基线相对残差而非成对 token 相似度，复杂度随 token 数线性增长。四个参考缓冲区在训练中累积脱梯度（detached）的阶段统计量，推理时冻结。骨干为四阶段金字塔，深度 (2,2,6,2)、宽度 (96,192,384,768)，224×224 输入下共 20.70M 参数。

Result: 研究围绕 ImageNet-1K 展开，包含干净分类、八组受控消融，以及高斯噪声、对比度与亮度三类扰动实验，用于检验慢速参考机制对紧凑表示鲁棒性的增益。

Conclusion: 慢速统计参考可在保持简单张量实现的同时改善紧凑表示的鲁棒性；该机制属于训练期统计建模，与输入依赖注意力有本质区别，且推理阶段计算量为线性。

Abstract: We present BDNet, a hierarchical image-classification backbone built around fast image features and slow statistical references. Its baseline deformation operator (BDO) transforms the deviation of a feature from a stage-specific reference through a low-rank projection and a learned channel scale. Rather than computing pairwise token similarity, the branch operates directly on baseline-relative residuals with complexity linear in the token count. Each of four reference buffers accumulates detached stage statistics during training and remains frozen at inference. A four-stage pyramid with depths $(2,2,6,2)$ and widths $(96,192,384,768)$ yields a 20.70M-parameter classifier at $224\times224$ resolution. We organize an ImageNet-1K study around clean classification, eight controlled ablations, and Gaussian-noise, contrast, and brightness perturbations. The central hypothesis is that a slow reference can improve the robustness of a compact representation while retaining a simple tensor implementation. We distinguish this training-time statistical mechanism from input-dependent attention and make its linear inference behavior explicit.

</details>


### [43] [VariaFlow: Uncertainty-Aware Flow Matching for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41494)
*Arnela Hadzic,Martin Urschler,Cosmin I. Bercea*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VariaFlow 通过流匹配集成估计每个位置的预测方差，并用其归一化残差以区分真实病灶与正常解剖变异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重建的异常检测中，残差同时来自真实病灶与健康个体间的解剖变异，二者难以区分；用单一重建打分会把两者都判为异常，导致解剖变异大的区域假阳性升高，而细小微小病灶被淹没在重建误差中。作者将缺失的关键量识别为"预测方差"，即健康重建在每个位置可允许的波动幅度。

Method: 提出 VariaFlow：在无条件 flow matching 先验下，以掩码引导的修复（mask-guided restoration）构建集成，每个样本从不同的噪声初始化出发，由集成的离散程度估计逐位置的预测方差，再以标准差归一化残差；采用少步 flow matching 保证集成计算可负担；在两个空间尺度上做修复以覆盖不同大小的病灶；仅在健康解剖上训练一次。

Result: 在涵盖卒中与肿瘤的三个人脑 MRI 数据集上，VariaFlow 单次训练即可跨全部三个数据集泛化，AUROC 分别达 0.94、0.80、0.78，均为最高；在两个跨域数据集上取得最高 AUPRC，较最强基线分别提升 37% 与 50%。在域偏移下，该基线 AUPRC 最多下降 52%，而 VariaFlow 更稳定，并在全部三个数据集上取得最佳小病灶 Dice。

Conclusion: 显式建模重建的预测方差并对残差做方差归一化，是解决重建式异常检测中"病灶 vs. 解剖变异"混淆问题的有效途径，且在域偏移与小病灶检测上表现更稳健。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based anomaly detection no longer fails at reconstruction. Generative models produce high-quality pseudo-healthy reconstructions, and the remaining difficulty is the step of scoring residuals. The residual between a scan and its reconstruction has two indistinguishable sources, genuine pathology and the anatomical variation that separates one healthy brain from another. Scoring against a single reconstruction treats both as anomalous, inflating false positives where healthy anatomy varies most while small or subtle lesions are lost in the surrounding reconstruction error. We identify the missing quantity as the predictive variance, how much a healthy reconstruction can vary at each location. We introduce VariaFlow, which estimates this variance from an ensemble of mask-guided restorations under an unconditional flow matching prior, each started from a different noise initialization, and which normalizes the residual by the resulting standard deviation. Few-step flow matching keeps the ensemble computationally affordable, and restoring at two spatial scales captures lesions of different size. Across three brain MRI datasets spanning stroke and tumor, VariaFlow is trained once on healthy anatomy and generalizes across all three, attaining the highest AUROC (0.94, 0.80, 0.78) on each and the highest AUPRC on both cross-domain datasets, exceeding the strongest baseline by 37% and 50%. Under domain shift, the same baseline drops by up to 52% in AUPRC, whereas VariaFlow stays more stable and achieves the best small-lesion Dice on all three datasets.

</details>


### [44] [VoxLution: Evolving Voxels for High-Fidelity 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41501)
*Daoyi Gao,Xinyi Zhang,Alexey Bokhovkin,Lei Li,Angela Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VoxLution：基于Transformer的由粗到细高分辨率3D室内场景生成，将3D超分建模为多分辨率稀疏体素序列建模问题，实现更细体素分辨率下的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率3D室内场景对VR、空间计算与机器人仿真至关重要，但现有生成模型几何过于平滑、细节缺失，难以生成高保真细节。

Method: 采用由粗到细的Transformer框架：先生成低分辨率空间布局，再以其为结构引导，按块（chunk）局部渐进细化几何。核心是将3D超分辨率形式化为多分辨率稀疏体素网格上的序列建模问题；提出免训练、分辨率不变的分词方案，将八个八分体的几何信息紧凑编码为单个十进制数；Transformer序列包含多个分辨率层级，使粗布局引导细部生成；并将相邻块边界信息直接纳入序列以保证局部块间连贯性。

Result: 在比现有基线更细的体素分辨率下完成场景生成；在标准基准上取得SOTA：3D-FRONT上FPD降低59.6%、FID降低51.1%；Scannet++上FPD降低70.5%、FID降低73.8%。

Conclusion: 将3D超分辨率转化为多分辨率稀疏体素序列建模，配合分辨率不变编码与边界感知序列设计，可有效突破生成几何过度平滑的限制，实现高保真、局部细节丰富的室内场景生成。

Abstract: High-resolution 3D indoor scene generation is crucial for immersive applications such as virtual reality, spatial computing, and robotics simulation. We introduce VoxLution, a coarse-to-fine transformer-based approach that generates high-fidelity indoor scenes with rich details, overcoming the overly smooth geometry typical of existing generative models. First, we generate a low-resolution spatial layout; then, based on this structural information, we progressively refine the scene geometry locally in chunks. Key to our approach is formulating 3D super-resolution as a sequence modeling problem over multi-resolution sparse voxel grids. To achieve this, we introduce a training-free, resolution-invariant tokenization scheme that compactly encodes the geometric information of eight octants into a single decimal number. We formulate the transformer sequence to include multiple resolutions, allowing the coarse layout to guide the generation of fine local details. To ensure coherence across local chunks, we incorporate adjacent chunk boundary information directly into the transformer sequencing. VoxLution achieves scene generation at a finer voxel resolution than existing baselines, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance on standard benchmarks with 59.6% lower FPD and 51.1% lower FID on 3D-FRONT, and 70.5% lower FPD and 73.8% lower FID on Scannet++.

</details>


### [45] [Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Transformer Encoders for Audio-Image-Symbolic Music Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41512)
*Eliseo Fuentes-Martinez,Antonio Hidalgo-Centeno,Jorge Calvo-Zaragoza,Antonio Javier Gallego*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统考察参数高效微调（PEFT）在多模态音乐检索中的效果：在9个预训练音频、图像、符号乐谱编码器上比较全量微调、LoRA与QLoRA，发现PEFT在18个模态-任务组合中的14个被选中，但单模态判别力的提升并不能一致地转化为更好的多模态表征组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于分数的多模态音乐检索需要各模态内具判别性、跨音频/乐谱图像/符号谱之间又相互兼容的表征。已有工作多关注单模态性能，尚不清楚PEFT对预训练音乐编码器的适配能否带来多模态层面的收益。

Method: 在TriScore与Musical Theme Dataset上，对9个预训练音频、图像、符号乐谱编码器比较全量微调、LoRA与QLoRA；先以冻结编码器线性探针为参照进行监督式单模态分类以评估并选择编码器；再采用2×2受控设计，交叉"编码器适配"与"任务特定共享投影"，比较投影前后冻结与适配表征；并辅以跨模态排除与邻域分析考察投影空间结构。

Result: LoRA或QLoRA在18个模态-任务组合中的14个被选中，常可匹配或超越全量微调且优化参数显著更少。但单模态增益向多模态分类的迁移并不一致：在TriScore作曲家分类上适配与投影互补，在TriScore乐器及Musical Theme Dataset乐器与合奏任务上大体冗余，在Musical Theme Dataset作曲家分类上部分相互干扰。最终"适配+投影"的标题表征在TriScore上支持曲目级检索，平均Top-5与Top-10准确率分别为55.32与64.68；跨模态排除与邻域分析显示投影空间具有强的模态特异性局部结构。

Conclusion: PEFT是全量微调的有效且计算高效的替代方案，但单模态判别力的提升本身并不保证多模态组织的改善，适配与投影的效果依任务而异。

Abstract: Score-based multimodal music retrieval requires representations that are both discriminative within each modality and compatible across audio recordings, sheet music images, and symbolic scores. This paper investigates the effectiveness of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) for adapting pretrained music encoders and examines whether modality-specific improvements translate into better multimodal representations. We compare full fine-tuning, LoRA, and QLoRA across nine pretrained audio, image, and symbolic score encoders on TriScore and the Musical Theme Dataset. Encoders are first evaluated and selected through supervised unimodal classification, using frozen-encoder linear probing as a reference. We then introduce a controlled 2x2 design crossing encoder adaptation with task-specific shared projection, comparing frozen and adapted representations before and after projection. LoRA or QLoRA is selected in 14 of 18 modality-target combinations, frequently matching or outperforming full fine-tuning while optimizing substantially fewer parameters. However, these unimodal gains do not transfer uniformly to multimodal classification. Adaptation and projection are complementary for composer classification on TriScore, largely redundant for TriScore instrument and the Musical Theme Dataset instrument and ensemble tasks, and partially interfering for composer classification on the Musical Theme Dataset. The final adapted-and-projected title representation on TriScore supports piece-level retrieval, reaching average Top-5 and Top-10 accuracies of 55.32 and 64.68, respectively. Cross-modal exclusion and neighborhood analyses further reveal strong modality-specific local structure in the projected spaces. Overall, PEFT provides an effective and computationally efficient alternative to full fine-tuning, but improved unimodal discrimination alone does not guarantee better multimodal organization.

</details>


### [46] [TACO: Trajectory-Aware Curvature Observation for Memorization Localization and Mitigation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41520)
*Ruxi Gu,Zilei Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TACO统一框架，沿去噪轨迹聚合曲率响应实现扩散模型局部记忆的精准定位与定向缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图扩散模型会在局部图像区域复现训练记忆中内容，精准定位这些区域是定向缓解的前提；但单一步骤的曲率观测含瞬态响应（噪声干扰），且空间均匀的缓解策略会破坏与提示相关的内容。

Method: 实证发现记忆相关曲率在去噪后期显著、且在相邻状态间位置一致。据此TACO沿后期去噪轨迹聚合提示相关的曲率响应，并用潜变量引导的空间细化提升空间一致性，生成记忆图。该图既作定位信号，又作缓解的空间控制信号：通过空间重加权与局部引导校正，将现有推理时干预导向记忆相关区域。

Result: 大量实验表明TACO在定位上持续优于对比方法，并改善记忆抑制与生成质量之间的Pareto权衡。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的空间分析是连接记忆定位与定向缓解的有效桥梁。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can reproduce memorized training content within localized image regions, making precise localization essential for targeted mitigation. However, curvature observations at a single denoising state can contain transient responses, while spatially uniform mitigation may disrupt prompt-relevant content. We present Trajectory-Aware Curvature Observation (TACO), a unified framework for local memorization detection and mitigation in diffusion models. Our empirical analysis reveals that memorization-related curvature becomes pronounced during late-stage denoising and persists at consistent spatial locations across neighboring states. Motivated by these observations, TACO aggregates prompt-dependent curvature responses along the late denoising trajectory and applies latent-guided spatial refinement to improve spatial coherence. The resulting memorization map serves both as a localization signal and as a spatial control signal for mitigation. Specifically, TACO uses this map to steer existing inference-time interventions toward memorization-related regions through spatial reweighting and localized guidance correction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TACO consistently outperforms competing localization methods and improves the Pareto trade-off between memorization suppression and generation quality. These results establish trajectory-aware spatial analysis as an effective link between memorization localization and targeted mitigation.

</details>


### [47] [Scout-Refine-Correct: Teaching Flow Models to Refine Their Own Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41598)
*Sergey Karpukhin,Aibek Alanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Scout-Refine-Correct (SRC) 训练配方，使流匹配生成器获得真正的推理时自我精炼能力：借助 scout rollout 的自条件信号与两种校正损失，在无外部验证器条件下显著降低 FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流匹配模型的生成质量在训练预算固定后基本被锁死，推理阶段唯一可调的是采样步数，且增益有上限。作者希望赋予模型真正的推理时精炼能力，而非仅依赖增加 NFE。

Method: SRC 在每步采样时先做 scout rollout：沿自身轨迹向前推演 K 步，外推得到全样本估计，并将其作为自条件信号回灌给模型后再执行真实步，可重复 R 轮精炼。训练时施加两种校正损失，且施加于模型自身 rollout 实际到达的状态（而非仅真值路径），以教会模型利用该信号探索新样本、避免塌缩到次优解。方法不使用外部验证器或奖励信号，经消融后几乎不增加参数。

Result: ImageNet-256 上，SRC 将 LightningDiT-B 在 NFE≈550 时从 FID≈14.5 提升至 7.26，追平使用 oracle FID 验证器择优的 best-of-N 特权基线；与 best-of-N 结合后在 NFE≈4400 达到 FID≈3.81，显示两条轴线互补。

Conclusion: SRC 证明自条件式的 scout 精炼可作为独立于采样步数的质量提升维度，与 best-of-N 正交可叠加。消融验证了 scout 信号架构通路、scout 视野、各校正损失及训练调度均为关键设计。

Abstract: The quality of image generation models trained with diffusion or flow matching is largely fixed by the training budget: at inference, the only available lever is the number of sampling steps, and additional steps improve quality only up to a point. We present a training recipe, Scout-Refine-Correct (SRC), that instead gives a flow-matching generator a genuine inference-time refinement capability. At each sampling step the model takes a scout rollout -- $K$ steps ahead along its own trajectory -- extrapolates a full-sample estimate, and feeds that estimate back to itself as a self-conditioning signal before taking the real step. This can be repeated for $R$ refinement rounds. Two correction losses, applied during training at states the model's own rollouts actually reach rather than only on the ground-truth path, teach the model to use this signal to reach new samples instead of collapsing to a suboptimal one. On ImageNet-256, SRC improves LightningDiT-B from FID~14.5 to FID~7.26 at NFE~550, matching a privileged best-of-$N$ baseline that uses an oracle FID verifier to choose among samples. SRC uses no external verifier, no reward signal, and, after ablation, almost no extra parameters over the base architecture. Combining SRC with best-of-$N$ improves the result further, to FID~3.81 at NFE~4400, showing that the two axes are complementary. We ablate the main design choices -- the architectural pathway for the scout signal, the scout horizon, each correction loss, and the training schedule to demonstrate key working components of training.

</details>


### [48] [DrawSplat: Atlanta World-Guided Implicit Gaussian SLAM for Indoor Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41602)
*Fan Zhu,Zhisong Xu,Ziyu Chen,Zhenjun Zhao,Takayuki Okatani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DrawSplat：一种以亚特兰大世界先验引导的隐式高斯SLAM框架，用“点-线几何先勾结构、神经高斯后填外观”的类绘画流程实现室内RGB-D重建，通过结构平面转可微正则提升跟踪、渲染与几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯SLAM主要依赖光度、深度或局部帧到模型约束优化高斯基元，在弱纹理、大平面结构、深度噪声大、长轨迹的室内场景中不足，导致位姿漂移、表面厚度伪影、法向噪声与结构边界模糊。

Method: 1) 点-线联合跟踪同时估计相机位姿、稀疏几何与重力方向；2) 利用Atlanta World先验的共享重力分量从跟踪线推断结构平面；3) 将结构平面转为对高斯中心与法向的可微正则项；4) 采用锚点式隐式神经高斯表示与自适应结构采样，把高斯容量分配给结构信息丰富区域。

Result: 在多个数据集上，DrawSplat在高斯类SLAM系统中实现鲁棒跟踪、高保真渲染与较强几何重建，并在质量、效率与模型规模间取得实用平衡。

Conclusion: 将显式点-线结构先验（Atlanta World）与隐式神经高斯表示耦合，可有效缓解室内弱纹理与大平面场景中的漂移和表面伪影，为高质量、紧凑的室内RGB-D高斯SLAM提供可行范式。

Abstract: RGB-D SLAM requires accurate online tracking, high-quality rendering, and geometrically consistent reconstruction. Existing Gaussian SLAM methods mainly optimize Gaussian primitives with photometric, depth, or local frame-to-model constraints, which are often insufficient in indoor scenes with weak textures, large planar structures, noisy depth, and long trajectories. The resulting maps may suffer from pose drift, surface thickness artifacts, noisy normals, and blurred structural boundaries. We propose DrawSplat, an Atlanta World-guided implicit Gaussian SLAM framework for indoor reconstruction. DrawSplat follows a drawing-like process: explicit point-line geometry first sketches the structural layout, and implicit neural Gaussians then fill in high-fidelity appearance and continuous geometric details. It estimates camera poses, sparse geometry, and gravity direction through point-line joint tracking, infers structural planes from tracked lines using the shared-gravity component of the Atlanta World prior, and converts them into differentiable regularization terms on Gaussian centers and normals. To improve compactness and efficiency, DrawSplat further combines an anchor-based implicit neural Gaussian representation with adaptive structural sampling, which allocates Gaussian capacity to structurally informative regions. Experiments on multiple datasets show that DrawSplat achieves robust tracking, high-fidelity rendering, and strong geometric reconstruction among Gaussian-based SLAM systems, while maintaining a practical balance among quality, efficiency, and model size.

</details>


### [49] [Attention Maps Know When to Think: Training-Free Difficulty-Aware Chain-of-Thought for Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41618)
*Chanseul Cho,Jaesung Jun,Dongheon Lee,Youngmin Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的自适应框架DA-CoT：以注意力图熵作为难度信号选择性触发CoT，并引入双向重思机制纠正过度/欠推理，在推理分割基准上取得SOTA且推理效率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT已成为MLLM的标准推理策略并在推理分割等任务上表现良好，但作者发现不加区分地使用CoT会因引入不必要的推理而降低性能，即"过度思考"（overthinking），在推理分割中表现为查询意图与视觉定位的错位；同时也存在欠思考问题。

Method: 1) 分析发现模型注意力图的熵可可靠反映任务难度：低熵表示置信且局部化的定位，高熵表示不确定、可能受益于CoT。2) DA-CoT为免训练自适应框架，依据该熵信号决定是否施加CoT提示。3) 引入双向重思（Bidirectional Rethinking）机制：当初始输出失败时自动翻转推理决策，从过度思考与欠思考导致的失败中恢复。

Result: 在推理分割（Reasoning Segmentation）基准上达到SOTA性能，同时显著提升推理效率。

Conclusion: CoT应按输入难度自适应触发而非普遍使用；本文的实证结论是注意力熵可作为有效的难度代理指标，结合双向重思机制可在免训练条件下兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting has become a standard reasoning strategy in Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs), showing strong performance in complex vision-language tasks such as reasoning segmentation. However, our analysis reveals that indiscriminate CoT usage can lead to degraded performance by inducing unnecessary reasoning, a phenomenon we term overthinking. In reasoning segmentation, overthinking often leads to misalignment between the query's intent and visual grounding. To address this, we propose Difficulty-Aware Chain-of-Thought (DA-CoT), a training-free adaptive framework that selectively triggers CoT based on the input's intrinsic difficulty. We find that the entropy of the model's attention maps reliably reflects task difficulty: low entropy indicates confident, localized grounding, while high entropy suggests uncertainty and potential benefit from CoT. DA-CoT uses this entropy signal to determine whether to apply CoT prompting. Additionally, we introduce a Bidirectional Rethinking mechanism that enables the model to automatically flip its reasoning decision when the initial output fails, enhancing robustness by recovering from failures induced by overthinking and underthinking. DA-CoT demonstrates its effectiveness by achieving state-of-the-art performance on the Reasoning Segmentation benchmarks while significantly improving inference efficiency.

</details>


### [50] [GRIT: Gradient Masking for Visually Misgrounded Tokens in Text-Rich Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41629)
*Chanseul Cho,Noori Bae,Dongheon Lee,Youngmin Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出RLVR在富文本视觉语言模型推理中存在"正确但视觉错接"(CVMR)现象,提出GRIT框架将视觉 grounding 融入RLVR,并构建GRIT-Bench基准,实验证明其显著提升推理准确率与grounding保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR已扩展至VLM但在富文本推理任务上效果有限。作者识别出关键原因:模型常在中间步骤误读视觉信息却仍得出正确最终答案,即CVMR;而标准RLVR仅以最终答案正确性为奖励,无法区分正确grounding与错误grounding的推理,对二者同等强化。

Method: 通过token级分析发现CVMR token具有与良好grounded token系统性不同的注意力模式;据此提出GRIT训练框架,将视觉grounding信号引入RLVR以缓解CVMR影响;同时构建GRIT-Bench,以支持跨文本长度与推理深度的系统评估。

Result: 实验显示GRIT在推理准确率与grounding保真度两方面均显著优于现有RLVR方法。

Conclusion: 最终答案正确不等于视觉认知正确;将视觉grounding纳入奖励/训练目标可有效抑制CVMR,提升富文本VLM推理的可靠性与可解释性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has recently been extended to vision-language models (VLMs), yet its effectiveness remains limited for text-rich reasoning tasks. We highlight a key factor contributing to this limitation: models frequently arrive at correct final answers despite misperceiving visual information during intermediate steps. We define this phenomenon as Correct-but-Visually-Misgrounded Reasoning (CVMR). Because standard RLVR rewards only final-answer correctness, it fails to differentiate between properly grounded and misgrounded reasoning, reinforcing both equally. Through token-level analysis, we reveal that CVMR tokens exhibit distinct attention patterns that differ systematically from well-grounded ones. Based on this observation, we propose GRIT, a training framework that incorporates visual grounding into RLVR to mitigate the impact of CVMR. Furthermore, to address the lack of text-rich reasoning benchmarks that allow for systematic evaluation across text length and reasoning depth, we introduce GRIT-Bench. Experiments demonstrate that GRIT significantly improves both reasoning accuracy and grounding fidelity over existing RLVR methods.

</details>


### [51] [TADP: Dual-Branch Temporally Adaptive Differential Privacy for Expression-Aware Video Anonymization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41642)
*Feng Xu,Xun Li,Lars Petersson,Yulei Sui,David Ahmedt-Aristizabal,Dadong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TADP——面向推理阶段隐私保护视频生成的时间自适应差分隐私框架，在固定长度片段替换邻接关系下形式化满足片段级DP，并在面部表情数据集上取得较好的隐私—效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私保护视频生成方法缺乏在片段级邻接关系下的形式化DP保证，且统一噪声分配未考虑时序动态差异，难以同时控制身份泄漏与保持表情识别效用。

Method: 双分支结构分别提取身份与表情表征；对该两分支分别施加时间自适应高斯机制，噪声尺度仅依赖于同一片段内此前的已私密化发布结果，并以历史均匀标定的下限约束；通过自适应RDP组合计入联合发布；将片段级RDP保证转换为(ε,δ)-DP；软量化与后续时序处理仅在私密化之后进行，视为DP后处理。

Result: 在面部表情数据集上，所评估攻击下身份泄漏降低，感知质量与时序稳定性可用，面部表情识别效用相比隐私保护基线更高。

Conclusion: TADP形式化满足片段级差分隐私，并在所评估的威胁模型与数据集上呈现有利的隐私—效用权衡。

Abstract: We present TADP, a temporally adaptive differential privacy (DP) framework for inference-time privacy-preserving video generation under fixed-length clip-replacement adjacency. TADP uses a dual-branch design to extract identity and expression representations, clips and privatizes both branches with temporally adaptive Gaussian mechanisms whose noise scales depend only on preceding privatized releases within the same clip and are bounded below by history-uniform calibrated minima, and accounts for their joint release via adaptive Rényi Differential Privacy (RDP) composition. The clip-level RDP guarantee is converted to $(\varepsilon,δ)$-differential privacy, while soft quantization and subsequent temporal processing are applied only after privatization as DP post-processing. Experiments on facial expression datasets show reduced identity leakage under evaluated attacks, usable perceptual quality and temporal stability, and high facial expression recognition utility relative to the evaluated privacy-preserving baselines. TADP formally satisfies clip-level DP and empirically provides a favorable privacy--utility trade-off under the evaluated threat models and datasets.

</details>


### [52] [When Depth Lies: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models on Mirror-Induced RGB-D Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41651)
*Hao Yin,Tianchen Guo,Heming Du,Yan Ke,Yanbin Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MIRA-Bench镜像场景RGB-D基准，揭示VLM在反射场景空间推理中的系统性失败，且深度信息反而有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM空间推理研究普遍假设深度图忠实反映物理距离，但镜面会扭曲深度读数并复制物体外观，使反射难以与真实物体区分；已有基准极少覆盖反射场景，VLM在镜面场景中的表现基本未知。

Method: 构建面向反射场景的RGB-D数据集：采集525个真实场景的1,011张RGB-D图像，涵盖室内外、6类反射表面、昼夜条件；标注反射表面区域掩码及直接观测/镜像反射物体的掩码。据此整理MIRA-Bench，含14,140个QA对、8个子任务，分为三个认知层次：反射感知、空间推理、场景级决策。

Result: 在MIRA-Bench上评测主流VLM，发现跨任务一致失败：无法判断物体是直接被观测还是反射成像，无法将反射实例与其真实对应物匹配，产生错误空间推断；且提供深度数据反而降低推理性能，说明模型无法识别镜面场景中的错误深度。

Conclusion: 镜面反射是RGB-D空间推理的关键盲区，深度线索在反射场景下不可盲目信赖；MIRA-Bench为评估与改进VLM的反射感知空间推理提供基准，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have made significant progress in spatial reasoning, particularly by integrating RGB images with depth data, which is often assumed to faithfully reflect physical distances. However, mirrors violate this assumption: they distort depth readings while reproducing object appearances, making reflections difficult to distinguish from real objects. As existing benchmarks rarely consider such reflective scenarios, VLM performance in mirror scenes remains largely unexplored. In this work, we introduce a mirror-centric RGB-D dataset from reflective scenes to evaluate the spatial reasoning capabilities of state-of-the-art VLMs in these scenarios. Specifically, we collect 1,011 RGB-D images from 525 real-world scenes, spanning diverse indoor and outdoor environments, six reflective surface types, and both daytime and nighttime conditions. All images are annotated with reflective surface region masks and mask annotations for both directly observed and mirror-reflected objects. Then, we curate a benchmark named MIRA-Bench (Mirror-Induced Reasoning Ambiguity Benchmark), comprising 14,140 QA pairs across eight sub-tasks organized into three cognitive levels: Reflection-aware Perception, Spatial Reasoning, and Scene-level Decision-making. We evaluate state-of-the-art VLMs on MIRA-Bench and find consistent failures across tasks: models misclassify whether objects are directly observed or reflected, fail to match reflected instances to their real-world counterparts, and produce erroneous spatial inferences. Remarkably, providing depth data actually degrades reasoning performance, implying that VLMs cannot identify incorrect depth in mirror scenes. The code and data are available at https://github.com/EvaYin-cloud/MIRA-Bench

</details>


### [53] [A Self-Supervised Pose Estimation Approach for Construction Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41673)
*Alaa Alshubbak,Daniel Görges*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于对比学习的自监督三阶段模型，用于挖掘机等重型施工机械的骨架姿态估计，第一阶段无需标注图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 姿态估计主流方法依赖监督学习，需大量标注数据；而重型施工机械（如挖掘机）的标注图像稀缺、获取成本高，因此希望借助无标注数据实现有效的骨架姿态估计。

Method: 构建三阶段自监督框架：第一阶段用SimCLR对比学习在大型无标注数据集ACID上预训练；第二阶段进行迁移学习的微调；第三阶段针对下游姿态估计任务微调。模型可利用ACID特征学习，并迁移至NVIDIA Isaac与MATLAB Simscape仿真器生成的两个小数据集，以及仅占原ACID数据3.5%比例的小数据集。

Result: 在真实图像上，重型施工机械姿态估计精度相比普通自监督方法分别提升11%（ResNet-50骨干）和13%（HRNet-W32骨干）。

Conclusion: 对比学习驱动的三阶段自监督迁移方案可在缺乏标注的情况下有效提升重型施工机械姿态估计精度，验证了从大规模无标注数据向小规模仿真/真实数据迁移的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Pose estimation is a computer vision task used to estimate a skeleton of dynamic systems to predict future movements. Most of the research in this direction is based on a supervised learning approach which requires a massive amount of labeled datasets. In this paper, a self-supervised three-stage model based on a contrastive learning approach is introduced for estimating a skeleton of dynamic construction machines; such as excavators without using any labeled images for the first stage. The whole model structure is divided into three stages: the pre-train stage using the SimCLR contrastive approach, and two fine-tuning stages for the transfer learning and the downstream task. The model can leverage the features and learn from a huge unlabeled dataset called ACID to two small datasets generated from NVIDIA Isaac and MATLAB Simscape simulators as well as transfer the knowledge to a smaller dataset with a ratio of 3.5% from the original ACID dataset. The results show that the proposed approach can improve the accuracy of pose estimation for heavy construction machines in real images by 11% and 13% in comparison to the normal self-supervised approach with two backbones ResNet-50 and HRNet-W32, respectively.

</details>


### [54] [OT-CAL: Optimal-Transport-Guided Adaptive Sample Selection for Efficient Visual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41677)
*Suryansh Talukdar,Jin Soung Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OT-CAL：用五维信息性特征＋课程向量＋最优传输匹配实现随训练自适应调整的样本选择，半数样本即可超越AdaSelection 1.3–2.1点并加速训练1.18–1.24倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并非所有训练样本对学习的贡献相同，可仅在信息性样本上做反向传播以降低计算量；但样本信息性随训练阶段变化（早期难样本、后期可学习/易改善样本、末段类别多样性），现有方法依赖固定选择规则或昂贵的逐样本梯度计算，缺乏自适应机制。

Method: OT-CAL为每个样本构造五维信息性特征（含难度、可学习性、不确定性等）；用一个课程向量刻画当前偏好的样本特性；基于最优传输的匹配按样本与课程向量的接近程度分配选择权重；再用被选样本更新课程向量，形成无需人工设定调度的闭环反馈。

Result: 在三个标准视觉学习基准上，每mini-batch仅选50%样本时，OT-CAL较AdaSelection提升1.3–2.1个点，且训练速度比全量数据训练快1.18–1.24倍。

Conclusion: 以课程向量＋最优传输匹配替代固定规则与逐样本梯度计算，可低成本地实现训练过程自适应的样本选择，在保持甚至提升精度的同时显著节省反向传播开销。

Abstract: Deep neural networks for image recognition are typically trained using mini-batches, with a forward pass to compute predictions and losses followed by a more computationally expensive backward pass to update model parameters. However, not all training samples contribute equally to learning, suggesting that computation can be reduced by performing backward passes only on informative samples. The challenge is that sample informativeness changes throughout training: difficult samples may be valuable early on, improving samples become important later, and class diversity becomes increasingly relevant toward the end. Existing methods often rely on fixed selection rules or computationally expensive per-sample gradient calculations. We propose OT-CAL, an adaptive data selection method that automatically adjusts its sampling preferences during training. OT-CAL represents each sample using five informativeness features, including difficulty, learnability, and uncertainty. A curriculum vector describes the currently preferred sample characteristics, and optimal-transport-based matching assigns selection weights according to how closely samples match this target. The curriculum vector is then updated using the selected samples, forming a feedback loop that adapts without a manually defined schedule. Experiments on three standard visual learning benchmarks show that, when selecting 50% of each mini-batch, OT-CAL improves performance by 1.3--2.1 points over AdaSelection while achieving 1.18--1.24 times faster training than full-data training.

</details>


### [55] [Retrieval-Augmented Initialization with Dual-Guided Inversion for Flow-Based Inverse Problem Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41695)
*Hansam Cho,Seoung Bum Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出检索增强初始化方法RA-DGI，通过检索代理干净图像和双引导反演构造更优的初始噪声，显著提升基于流模型的逆问题求解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流模型的逆问题求解器多以随机高斯噪声作为初始化，而图像生成研究表明初始噪声对生成结果影响显著；初始化这一设计选择在流模型逆问题求解中尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在固定下游流模型逆问题求解器的前提下，系统评估多种噪声初始化策略，并提出RA-DGI：利用检索到的代理干净图像结合双引导反演（dual-guided inversion）构造改进的初始噪声。

Result: 多类逆问题实验表明初始噪声质量显著影响重建保真度与感知性能；RA-DGI优于标准随机初始化，并在计算开销更低的情况下超过推理时扩展（inference-time scaling）策略。

Conclusion: 初始化是基于流模型逆问题求解中被忽视但关键的因素，合理设计初始噪声可在低开销下带来显著增益。

Abstract: Flow models have recently emerged as powerful generative priors for solving inverse problems. Despite their success, most existing flow-based inverse problem solvers initialize the reconstruction process with random Gaussian noise. However, recent studies in image generation have shown that the choice of initial noise can significantly influence generation outcomes. The role of initialization as a distinct design choice in flow-based inverse problem solving remains comparatively underexplored. In this work, we systematically evaluate noise initialization strategies while holding the downstream flow-based inverse solver fixed. We propose Retrieval-Augmented Initialization with Dual-Guided Inversion (RA-DGI), a method that constructs improved initial noise by leveraging retrieved proxy clean images and dual-guided inversion. Extensive experiments across multiple inverse problems demonstrate that the quality of the initial noise substantially affects reconstruction fidelity and perceptual performance. RA-DGI outperforms standard random initialization and achieves superior results compared to inference-time scaling strategies, while requiring less computational overhead. These findings highlight initialization as a critical yet previously overlooked factor in flow-based inverse problem solvers.

</details>


### [56] [Nereus: A Large-Scale Underwater Dataset for Fine-Grained Attribute Understanding and Grounded Counting Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41705)
*Jingtao Deng,Zihao Jin,Yuehe Chen,Feixiang He,Shuiwang Li,Dan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出Nereus，一个面向水下细粒度感知的大规模数据集，同时支持细粒度目标属性理解与带定位的计数感知，含88K图像、2.68M问答对，覆盖136K生物目标、2,690物种与1,515属，并提供65K点标注与67K定位计数问答对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下影像蕴含丰富生态信息，支撑科学传播、渔业监测、生态保护与海洋研究等应用，这些任务要求模型识别细粒度生物性状并基于空间证据推理。但现有水下数据集多仅提供粗类别标签、泛化描述或总量计数监督，难以支撑个体级分析与生态推理。

Method: 构建Nereus大规模水下细粒度感知数据集，在图像上联合标注两类任务：细粒度对象属性理解（物种/属级标注与属性问答）和带定位的计数感知（点标注与grounded counting问答对）；并以代表性多模态模型进行实验评估。

Result: 数据集规模为88K图像、2.68M问答对，涵盖136K海洋生物目标、2,690个物种、1,515个属，另有65K点标注和67K定位计数问答对。实验表明，相较现有水下领域数据，Nereus在下游适配中带来提升。

Conclusion: Nereus为细粒度海洋感知研究提供了数据基础，可支持个体级属性理解与空间定位计数，并为后续水下多模态研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Underwater imagery records rich ecological information and supports applications such as science communication, fishery monitoring, ecological conservation, and oceanographic research. Beyond general scene understanding, these applications require models to recognize fine-grained biological traits and reason over spatially grounded evidence. However, existing underwater datasets often provide coarse category labels, generic captions, or total-count supervision, which limits their use for organism-level analysis and ecological reasoning. We introduce Nereus, a large-scale dataset for Underwater Fine-grained Perception that jointly supports Fine-Grained Object Attribute Understanding and Grounded Counting Perception. Nereus contains 88K images and 2.68M question-answer pairs, including annotations for 136K marine organism objects across 2,690 species and 1,515 genera, as well as 65K point annotations and 67K grounded counting QA pairs. Experiments with representative multimodal models show that Nereus improves downstream adaptation over existing underwater-domain data and provides a foundation for future research on fine-grained marine perception.

</details>


### [57] [SAR-TS-BERT: Physics-Guided Self-Supervised Pre-Training With Polarimetric Features for SAR Time-Series Crop Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41728)
*Jieqiu Wang,Yuhan Zhang,Bo Zheng,Shaoming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物理引导的 BERT 式 SAR 时序自监督预训练框架 SAR-TS-BERT，将后向散射强度与极化分解特征融合，显著提升少样本作物分类精度与地块一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多时相 SAR 可提供云雨区作物物候的无云连续观测，但高精度分类依赖大量标注样本；现有自监督预训练多针对光学影像或通用 SAR 空间场景，未充分利用时序后向散射动态与极化散射机理。

Method: 构建六波段输入（VV、VH、VV/VH 及双极化 Cloude-Pottier 分解参数 H/α/A）；设计两项核心机制：(1) 物理引导跨度掩码 SpanPhysics，依据时序后向散射变化率自适应选取连续掩码区间以聚焦关键物候转换；(2) 双分支跨模态融合编码器，分别用独立 Transformer 分支建模后向散射与极化分解特征，并通过双向交叉注意力融合。

Result: 爱沙尼亚 EuroCropsML OA 96.78%，美国 California CDL OA 87.29%；上海崇明岛月均 OA 达 99.37%。爱沙尼亚基准上，全监督下预训练较从头训练 OA/Macro F1 提升 1.17/1.59 个百分点；仅用 1% 标注数据时提升 2.58/5.19 个百分点。

Conclusion: 将 SAR 散射物理先验引入时序自监督预训练，可有效提升作物分类精度、地块级制图一致性与少样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Multitemporal synthetic aperture radar (SAR) imagery provides continuous, cloud-free observations of crop phenology for agricultural monitoring in cloudy and rainy regions, yet high-accuracy classification typically requires extensive labeled samples. Although self-supervised pre-training mitigates label dependency in remote sensing, existing frameworks primarily target optical imagery or generic spatial SAR scenes, underutilizing temporal backscatter dynamics and polarimetric scattering mechanisms. We propose SAR-TS-BERT, a physics-guided BERT-style self-supervised pre-training framework for SAR time-series crop classification. SAR-TS-BERT integrates backscatter intensities and polarimetric ratio features (VV, VH, and VV/VH) with dual-polarimetric Cloude-Pottier decomposition parameters ($H/α/A$) into a six-band input representation through two core designs: 1) a physics-guided span masking strategy (SpanPhysics) that adaptively selects contiguous masked spans based on temporal backscatter change rates to prioritize key phenological transitions; and 2) a dual-branch cross-modal fusion encoder that models backscatter and polarimetric decomposition features through separate Transformer branches followed by bidirectional cross-attention fusion. On independent test sets in Estonia (EuroCropsML) and the USA (California CDL), SAR-TS-BERT achieves overall accuracies (OAs) of 96.78% and 87.29%, respectively, and attains a mean monthly OA of 99.37% on Chongming Island, Shanghai. On the Estonia benchmark, pre-training improves OA and Macro F1 by 1.17 and 1.59 percentage points over training from scratch under full supervision, and by 2.58 and 5.19 percentage points using only 1% labeled data. Incorporating SAR scattering physics into temporal self-supervised pre-training effectively enhances crop classification accuracy, parcel-level mapping consistency, and few-shot transferability.

</details>


### [58] [Measuring What Matters: A Causal Reliance Score for Histopathology Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41763)
*Samuel Thomas,Daniel Tweneboah Anyimadu,Ahmed Karam Eldaly,Somaia Elsheikh,Mohammed M. Abdelsamea*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于因果干预的医学图像分割评测框架与Causal Reliance Score（CRS），量化模型对前景组织与背景/域变化的相对敏感度，并设计反事实式增强策略；实验表明该干预方法能揭示Dice/IoU无法反映的模型行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的病理组学分割要求模型依赖病理相关结构而非背景伪相关；作者认为仅改进网络架构不足以解决伪相关依赖问题，且现有Dice/IoU等指标无法刻画模型的行为依据。

Method: 构建因果启发的评测框架，通过受控干预（前景组织扰动 vs. 背景/域变化）探测模型敏感度；提出CRS指标度量两类扰动下预测的相对敏感性；设计作为隐式反事实干预的数据增强策略，在保留形态学的前提下促使模型对冗余/干扰因素不变；以自监督Transformer（CAUSE-ViT）作为实例化载体。

Result: 受控干预揭示了不同模型间的行为差异，而这些差异在Dice、IoU等标准指标下不可见。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献不是新架构，而是原则化的干预式方法论与审计模型行为的评测指标；通过反事实干预评估模型行为对构建可靠、临床鲁棒的医学图像分割系统至关重要。

Abstract: Reliable histopathology segmentation requires models that rely on pathology-relevant structures rather than spurious background correlations. In this work, we argue that improving architectures alone is insufficient if models continue to rely on spurious correlations rather than pathology-relevant features. We introduce a causality-inspired evaluation framework based on controlled interventions that explicitly probes model sensitivity to foreground tissue structure and background/domain variation. Building on this, we propose the Causal Reliance Score (CRS), a metric that quantifies the relative sensitivity of model predictions to foreground perturbation versus background/domain variation under controlled interventions. To support this analysis, we design augmentation strategies that act as implicit counterfactual interventions during training, encouraging invariance to nuisance factors while preserving morphology. We demonstrate how these interventions reveal behavioural differences between models that are not captured by standard metrics such as Dice or IoU. While we instantiate our framework using a self-supervised transformer (CAUSE-ViT), the primary contribution of this work is not a new architecture, but a principled intervention-based methodology and evaluation metric for auditing model behaviour in medical image segmentation. Our results suggest that evaluating model behaviour through counterfactual interventions is essential for developing reliable and clinically robust medical image segmentation systems.

</details>


### [59] [WorkIng: A Real-World and Synthetic Highway Work-Zone Benchmark Dataset for Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41772)
*Xujun Xie,Dominik Rößle,Adithya Mohan,Daniel Cremers,Torsten Schön*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双域基准WorkIng：WorkIng-Highway提供360°多模态高速公路作业区真实数据集，WorkIng-CARLA提供法规感知的程序化仿真；实验表明真实数据仍是主要性能驱动，场景分层合成增强可提升2D识别、3D检测与轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速公路作业区是自动驾驶的关键边缘场景，兼具非典型几何结构与地区特定法规，而现有基准无法覆盖：真实数据集缺乏360°多模态、全场景3D作业区标注；单一采集地点难以涵盖模型泛化所需的多样布局与法规变体。

Method: 构建双域基准与训练资源。真实域WorkIng-Highway：同步128线LiDAR、6路RGB相机与RTK-GPS，覆盖32 km，产出1.2万余标注帧与227,399个跟踪3D包围框。仿真域WorkIng-CARLA：程序化生成24个法规感知场景，覆盖6种典型拓扑与6种国际交通标准，由100余个定制3D资产填充。并在此基准上评测代表性3D检测与端到端规划架构。

Result: 基准评测显示真实世界数据是性能的首要驱动因素；场景分层（scenario-stratified）合成增强在2D识别、3D检测和轨迹规划三类任务上均带来跨架构性能提升。

Conclusion: 真实多模态作业区数据与法规感知仿真的双域组合可有效支撑作业区场景下的感知与规划研究，合成数据的价值在于按场景分层针对性补充而非替代真实数据。

Abstract: Highway work zones are critical edge cases for autonomous vehicles (AVs), combining atypical geometries and jurisdiction-specific regulations that existing benchmarks fail to capture: real-world datasets lack 360° multi-modal, full-scene 3D work zone annotation, and no single recorded site can span the layouts and regulatory variants needed for generalizable models. We present WorkIng, a dual-domain benchmark and training resource. WorkIng-Highway provides a 360° multi-modal work zone benchmark with synchronized 128-channel LiDAR, six RGB cameras, and RTK-GPS across 32 km, yielding 12,000+ annotated frames and 227,399 tracked 3D bounding boxes. WorkIng-CARLA complements this with procedural simulation spanning 24 regulation-aware scenarios across six canonical topologies and six international traffic standards, populated by 100+ custom 3D assets. Benchmarking representative 3D detection and end-to-end planning architectures shows that real-world data remains the primary performance driver, and that scenario-stratified synthetic augmentation improves architectures across 2D recognition, 3D detection, and trajectory planning. Resources: https://github.com/cvims/WorkIng

</details>


### [60] [Hard Sample-Conditioned Decorrelated Attention for Robust Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41789)
*Tahar Chettaoui,Guray Ozgur,Eduarda Caldeira,Naser Damer,Fadi Boutros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ViT-HarDis，按样本难度条件化注意力头解耦：对与竞争身份余弦相似度高的难样本施加更强头部去相关正则，提升人脸识别性能且无额外推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT多头注意力缺乏显式正则时易学到冗余而非互补的注意力模式；现有基于相关性的惩罚对所有样本施加统一正则，忽略了人脸识别中难样本（嵌入接近竞争身份）对优化与泛化的关键作用。

Method: 以样本与负类中心的余弦相似度衡量难度，对高相似度难样本施加更强的头间去相关惩罚，实现难度条件化的注意力头解耦；并分析发现仅在最后若干Transformer层施加解耦优于全网络正则。无架构改动、无额外推理成本。

Result: 多数基准上优于基线及均匀解耦；难样本与竞争类中心相似度下降、头间注意力相似度降低；WebFace4M训练下在IJB-B、IJB-C、TinyFace Rank-1上取得ViT类方法SOTA，MS1MV3训练下IJB-B与TinyFace最佳。

Conclusion: 将注意力头解耦与样本难度相耦合是提升ViT人脸识别的有效、低成本正则策略，且解耦应集中于末端层。

Abstract: Multi-head attention in Vision Transformers (ViTs) allows independent attention patterns across heads, but without explicit regularization, heads may learn redundant patterns rather than complementary roles. Correlation-based penalties encourage head disentanglement, yet apply uniform regularization across all training samples. In face recognition (FR), hard samples whose embeddings lie close to competing identities are critical for optimization and generalization. Motivated by this, we propose ViT-HarDis, which conditions attention head disentanglement on sample difficulty: samples with high cosine similarity to negative class centers receive stronger head decorrelation, enforcing specialization where identity ambiguity is highest. Experiments show improvements over both the baseline and uniform disentanglement on most benchmarks. Our analysis shows that ViT-HarDis reduces the similarity of hard samples to competing class centers while decreasing inter-head attention similarity, and that applying disentanglement only to the final transformer layers outperforms regularizing the entire network. Trained on WebFace4M, ViT-HarDis achieves SOTA results among ViT-based methods on IJB-B, IJB-C, and TinyFace Rank-1, while achieving the best IJB-B and TinyFace results when trained on MS1MV3, improving FR without architectural modifications or additional inference cost. Code: https://github.com/TaharChettaoui/Hard-Sample-Conditioned-Attention-for-FR.

</details>


### [61] [Different Models, Shared Failures: Auditing Frozen-Encoder Pools for Medical Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41790)
*Eunseob Choi,Kyeonghun Kim,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 CoFailMed，对医学分割中预训练编码器池的相关性失效进行审计；指出平均精度与模型数量无法反映模型是否在同一病例上失败，并主张以"绝对依赖度"和"阈值特定有效池规模"作为池评估的补充指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均分割精度与模型池规模（model count）掩盖了模型间失败模式的冗余性：若多个模型在同一病例上同时失败，名义上的集成规模无法兑现为实际鲁棒性。论文因此对医学分割预训练编码器池中的"相关失效"（correlated failures）进行系统性审计，检验同源检查点、同族编码器与跨族编码器在逐病例失败相关性上的差异。

Method: 在五个 2D 医学分割任务上，采用冻结特征 + 单独训练的 1×1 readout，每个编码器四个随机种子，评估 11 个公开预训练编码器（受控样本，非全量普查）；另扩展至 17 个编码器的相关性分析。以逐病例 Dice 相关系数度量编码器对之间的依赖程度，按同检查点对、同族不同检查点对、跨族对分组比较；在池化的 33 百分位 Dice 阈值下，用池中同时具备足够失败与通过样本的成员对估计四成员池的有效规模（effective size）。进一步考察更丰富的 readout 与微调对相关性与有效规模的影响，并对每种训练配方重新计算 33 百分位阈值。

Result: 共享检查点的编码器对具有最高的平均逐病例 Dice 相关性；扩展的 17 编码器分析显示同族不同检查点之间仍比跨族对更相关。在 33 百分位阈值下，由不同编码器组成的四成员池平均有效规模仅为 1.56–2.92（名义规模为 4）。更强的 readout 与微调缩小了"共享检查点（或 DINOv2-B/S 发布）对"与其余对之间的 Dice 相关差距，但不同编码器之间的依赖度可能反而上升，且微调并未在按各自配方重算的 33 百分位阈值上稳定提升有效池规模——即组间差距缩小与整体依赖度上升可以并存。

Conclusion: 模型池评估不应只报告平均精度与名义池规模，而应同时报告绝对依赖度（如逐病例失败相关性）与阈值特定的有效池规模；同源或同族模型带来的精度增益不代表多样性。CoFailMed 发布对齐的逐病例轨迹、分析脚本与研究代码，以复现核心审计结果。

Abstract: Average segmentation accuracy and model count do not reveal whether models fail on the same cases. We present CoFailMed, an audit of correlated failures in pretrained-encoder pools for medical segmentation. Across five 2D tasks, we evaluate 11 public pretrained encoders (a controlled sample, not a census) using frozen features and separately trained $1\times 1$ readouts with four seeds per encoder. Shared-checkpoint pairs have the highest mean per-case Dice correlation. An expanded 17-encoder analysis shows that distinct checkpoints within the same encoder family remain more correlated than cross-family pairs. At the pooled 33rd-percentile Dice threshold, four-member pools drawn from distinct encoders have mean effective sizes of 1.56-2.92, estimated from failure correlations in pools with enough failures and passes per member. In the primary pool, richer readouts and fine-tuning narrow the Dice-correlation gap between pairs sharing a checkpoint (or the DINOv2-B/S release) and all other pairs. Yet dependence between different encoders can increase, and fine-tuning does not consistently increase effective pool size at 33rd-percentile thresholds recomputed for each training recipe. A smaller between-group gap can coexist with greater dependence. Pool evaluation should therefore report absolute dependence and threshold-specific effective size alongside accuracy and nominal pool size. The CoFailMed release provides aligned per-case traces, analysis scripts and study code to reproduce the central audit results.

</details>


### [62] [A Patch is More Than a Token](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41814)
*Zixiong Wang,Haiping Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PMTT框架，将ViT的单一patch token扩展为"以canonical为中心的表示状态族"，并构建Patch级局部特征空间；在冻结DINOv3上验证表示可组织成解析Manifold，其隐含信息解释力与下游能力优于原始token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT以单一观测条件下产生的canonical token表示每个图像块，该token虽是有力接口，但仅是patch众多可能状态中的一种实现，可能掩盖了预训练骨干中更丰富的patch信息。

Method: PMTT含四部分：Patch信息获取与Dense Orbital Views揭示同patch的合法状态；Visible-to-Hidden预测检验观测状态能否揭示超出自身的信息；内在分析刻画低维、能量集中、patch特异、可预测的几何结构；表示组织将"获取信息"与"组织信息"分离，以解析Manifold为中枢结构。在冻结的DINOv3 S/B/L/H+上实现，不做神经后训练。

Result: 两组4,096锚点队列中，Manifold解释Hidden变异的70.8–79.6%（Observed为55.0–72.3%），各尺度下在>95%锚点上胜出；有效秩由约2.5降至2.0，主能量集中度随骨干规模上升。6种视觉能力×4尺度共24条件下，Observed在21项超Center，Manifold在23项超Center，Manifold在16项超Observed；MegaDepth上各尺度均满足Center<Observed<Manifold。

Conclusion: 预训练视觉骨干蕴含比标准单token接口所直接暴露的更丰富的patch信息，且该信息可被解析地组织成低维结构表示；表示组织本身（而非仅获取更多信息）带来稳定增益。

Abstract: Vision Transformers represent each image patch through a canonical token produced under one observation condition. That token is a powerful interface, but it is still one realized state of the patch. We introduce PMTT, which establishes two foundations: a patch representation object can extend from one canonical state to a canonical-centered Representation State Family, and identity-preserving variation opens Patch-Level Local Feature Space. We develop four parts of this representation program. Patch Information Acquisition and Dense Orbital Views expose lawful same-patch states. Visible-to-Hidden prediction tests whether observed states reveal structure beyond themselves. Intrinsic analysis identifies low-dimensional, energy-concentrated, patch-specific, predictive geometry. Representation Organization separates acquiring more information from organizing that information, with an analytic Manifold as the central structured representation. We realize PMTT on frozen DINOv3 S/B/L/H+ backbones without neural post-training of the representation. Across two independent 4,096-anchor cohorts, Manifold explains 70.8-79.6% of Hidden variation, compared with 55.0-72.3% for directly Observed states, and wins on more than 95% of anchors at every scale. Effective rank falls from about 2.5 to 2.0 while dominant energy concentration rises with backbone scale. Across six visual capabilities and four scales, Observed exceeds the canonical Center in 21 of 24 conditions, Manifold exceeds Center in 23, and Manifold exceeds Observed in 16. The strongest current consequence appears in physical correspondence, where MegaDepth satisfies Center < Observed < Manifold at every scale. These results show that pretrained visual backbones contain richer patch information than the standard single-token interface directly exposes. Code and experimental assets are available at https://github.com/zixiongwang-ai/PMTT.

</details>


### [63] [PGGT-Loc: Primitive-Grounded Geometry Transformer for Feed-Forward Camera Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41833)
*Hanqiao Ye,Yuzhou Liu,Mengqi Rong,Jian Liu,Yangdong Liu,Shuhan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PGGT（Primitive-Grounded Geometry Transformer），以“将观测 grounding 到地图基元”的新范式实现直接 6-DoF 相机定位，在稠密表面地图与极简布局模型上均刷新 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机定位多为多阶段流水线，依赖专用复杂地图，构建、存储与长期维护成本高；而结构化、以紧凑且具表达力基元组织的场景地图已易得，蕴含丰富几何语义线索。但因地图基元与视觉观测间存在显著模态鸿沟，其用于直接定位的潜力尚未被探索。

Method: 提出“将查询观测 grounding 到地图基元以估计相机位姿”的新概念，并在平面基元场景下实例化为 PGGT：输入单张查询图像与一组平面地图基元，网络被训练预测多个定位目标，实现前馈式 6-DoF 定位；架构简洁、可扩展，并可泛化至未见环境。

Result: 在稠密表面地图与极简布局模型两类设定上，均超越现有最优定位性能。

Conclusion: 以基元为中介的直接相机定位是可行且有效的，简洁可扩展的前馈架构可兼得高精度与跨环境泛化能力，为降低对复杂专用地图的依赖提供了新路径。

Abstract: Camera localization methods are mostly multi-stage pipelines relying on dedicated and complex maps that are costly to construct, store, and maintain over time. Alternatively, well-structured maps that organize scenes with compact yet expressive primitives are often readily available, providing rich structural and semantic cues that humans can interpret. However, their potential for direct camera localization remains largely unexplored due to the significant modality gap between map primitives and visual observations. This motivates a new concept for localization, which estimates camera poses by grounding query observations to map primitives. In this paper, we instantiate the concept in the context of planar primitives with PGGT, the Primitive-Grounded Geometry Transformer network. Given a single query image and a set of planar map primitives, the network is trained to predict multiple localization targets for feed-forward 6-DoF camera localization that generalizes to unseen environments. With this straightforward and scalable architecture, our method pushes the state-of-the-art localization performance on both dense surface maps and minimal layout models. The code and models are available at https://github.com/3dv-casia/PGGT-Loc

</details>


### [64] [ObjectGuidedMap: An Object-Aware Model for Robust Bird's Eye View Map Prediction in High-Occlusion Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41834)
*Christian Bohn,Harsh Yadav,Klaus Friedrichs,Hasan Tercan,Tobias Meisen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多任务架构联合预测BEV地图与3D目标，通过BEV特征级与实例查询级跨任务融合，在两个数据集上稳定提升BEV地图预测精度，遮挡严重场景mAP提升21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BEV地图预测模型（如Mask2Map）与目标级场景理解相互独立，而交通参与者常遮挡道路或隐含道路几何信息，因此需要显式建模两任务间的信息交换以提升遮挡鲁棒性。

Method: 在Mask2Map基础上增加3D目标检测分支，构建多任务架构；在BEV特征层采用deformable attention、在实例查询层采用cross-attention实现跨任务特征融合，使地图实例可利用目标上下文。

Result: 在nuScenes与Argoverse 2数据集上，BEV地图预测精度获得一致提升；在目标遮挡严重的场景中mAP提升达21%。

Conclusion: 联合BEV地图预测与3D目标检测并引入跨任务特征融合，可有效利用目标上下文缓解遮挡问题，显著提升地图预测精度。

Abstract: Accurate BEV map prediction is a critical component of autonomous driving systems, enabling downstream planning and control. Recent approaches such as Mask2Map achieve strong performance by predicting vectorized, instance-level map elements directly from multi-view images. However, existing BEV map prediction models operate largely independently of object-level scene understanding, despite the fact that traffic participants frequently occlude or implicitly encode information about road geometry. In this work, we propose a multitask architecture that jointly predicts BEV maps and 3D objects, explicitly enabling information exchange between the two tasks. Building upon Mask2Map, we introduce an object detection branch and incorporate cross-task feature fusion at both the BEV feature level and the instance-query level via deformable attention and cross-attention mechanisms. This design allows map instances to leverage object context while remaining robust to occlusions caused by traffic participants. Experimental results on the nuScenes and Argoverse 2 datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently improves BEV map prediction accuracy, with particularly significant gains of 21% mAP in scenes containing heavy object occlusion.

</details>


### [65] [Open-Weight MLLMs Cannot Compare What They Cannot Name](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41841)
*Wenxin Su,Anna Kreshuk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 当前开源权重多模态大模型在合成蚂蚁图像的体部尺寸比较任务上部分失败，作者将其归因于"识别驱动的定位失败"：模型无法识别的结构会被错误关联到可识别的语义概念，从而同时损害定位与比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显微成像数据规模与多样性快速增长，已远超人工目视分析可承受的范围，因此希望借助MLLM实现复杂表型的自动化视觉比较。为此作者从最简基线任务出发，检验MLLM视觉比较能力的基本可靠性。

Method: 构建极简基线任务：对合成蚂蚁图像中身体各部位的尺寸进行两两比较。评测多个最先进的开源权重MLLM，比较不同部位的表现差异；尝试多种上下文学习（in-context learning）策略；并对相关的目标识别任务进行对照分析；最后对视觉编码器进行进一步表征分析。

Result: MLLM在某些身体部位上失败、在另一些部位上成功，尽管该比较对所有模型而言在视觉上都是平凡的。识别任务的性能与比较任务的表现相关联，提示失败源于识别驱动的定位（grounding）失败。视觉编码器分析结果与"细粒度视觉结构在缺乏语义支撑时保存较差"的假设一致。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM存在一个出人意料的局限：语义识别会"门控"那些本质上并不需要语义理解的简单视觉比较。即模型无法命名的结构，也就无法可靠比较。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly capable of understanding images and performing complex visual reasoning, suggesting the possibility of using them to automate biological image analysis. This need is becoming increasingly urgent as advances in technology continue to generate ever larger and more diverse microscopy datasets, already exceeding the scale at which manual visual analysis is feasible. With the final aim to use MLLMs for visual comparison of complex phenotypes, we investigate a very simple baseline: comparison of the size of body parts in synthetic images of ants. We find that state-of-the-art open-weight MLLMs fail for some body parts, but not for others, even though the comparison itself is visually trivial for all of them. We evaluate different in-context learning strategies and analyze performance on the related object recognition task. Our results suggest a recognition-driven grounding failure: structures that the models cannot recognize are instead associated with a recognizable semantic concept, impairing the grounding and comparison of both. Further analysis of the vision encoder is consistent with the hypothesis that fine-grained visual structure may be less well preserved without semantic support. Together, our results reveal a surprising limitation of current MLLMs: semantic recognition can gate simple visual comparisons that do not intrinsically require semantic understanding. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/kreshuklab/Open-Weight-MLLMs-Cannot-Compare-What-They-Cannot-Name

</details>


### [66] [TwinPast: Benchmarking Historical Evidence Use in Streaming Video Understanding with Evidence Contrastive Video Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41983)
*Haotian Yang,Yong Chen,Cheng Peng,Haobo Wang,Zhongle Xie,Xuanru Li,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TwinPast 基准：固定近期视频上下文、问题与选项顺序，仅改变关键历史证据及正确答案，用视频对与 PairAcc 检验模型是否真正依据历史变化作答；34 组配置中推理型通用模型达 66.57–91.43% PairAcc，最强流式系统仅 49.14%，揭示流式视频模型"历史记忆—检索—时序推理"的脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频基准的高准确率不能证明模型使用了视觉历史。作者复核 OVO-Bench 与 StreamingBench 发现：32% 的历史类抽样问题仅用近期帧即可回答，且模型仅凭问题和选项也能答对不少题目，说明既有评测存在捷径与信息泄漏，无法衡量真正的历史依赖。

Method: 构建 TwinPast 双视频对照基准，含 350 组人工审核视频对，覆盖五类历史任务。每对样本保持近期视频上下文、问题及有序选项不变，仅改动关键历史证据与对应正确答案；每条视频独立评测，PairAcc 要求两种历史下均答对。共评测 34 种模型与输入配置。

Result: 在 TwinPast 上，Qwen3-VL-8B 与 DeepSeek-Flash 使用历史输入后性能提升，逆转了其在既有基准上的"近期帧优势"；最强流式系统 PairAcc 仅 49.14%，而通用模型为 66.57–91.43%，且该差距在五类任务上一致存在。案例研究显示，模型即便保留相关证据，仍会选取错误时间点的状态或违背已记录的事件顺序。

Conclusion: 现有流式视频模型的瓶颈不在记忆容量，而在历史记忆与检索同"依据问题的时间指涉与事件关系使用证据"的推理之间的衔接。TwinPast 提供了更严格的因果性检验范式；未来需将时序推理与证据定位显式结合，而非依赖近期帧或选项先验。

Abstract: High accuracy on streaming video benchmarks does not establish whether a model uses visual history. Our review of OVO-Bench and StreamingBench finds that recent frames suffice for 32% of sampled historical questions, while models also answer many questions correctly from the question and options alone. We introduce TwinPast to test whether answers correctly follow changes in history, using 350 human-reviewed video pairs spanning five historical tasks. Each pair holds the recent video context, question, and ordered options fixed while changing critical historical evidence and the correct answer. Each video is evaluated independently, and PairAcc requires correct answers for both histories. We evaluate 34 model and input configurations. For Qwen3-VL-8B and DeepSeek-Flash, historical inputs improve performance on TwinPast, reversing the recent-frame advantage on existing benchmarks. The strongest evaluated streaming system reaches 49.14% PairAcc, compared with 66.57-91.43% for the evaluated general-purpose models. This gap persists across all five tasks. Case studies further show that models select states from the wrong time or contradict recorded event order despite retaining the relevant evidence. These findings highlight the need to connect historical memory and retrieval with reasoning that uses evidence according to the question's temporal references and event relations.

</details>


### [67] [FoAViS: Analyzing Learned Fixation Strategies in a Foveated Active Vision System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41987)
*Valentin Hassler,Jonathan Henrich,Yohaï-Eliel Berreby,Haoze Zhu,Alexander Ecker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FoAViS系统：采用log-polar（对数极坐标）中心凹采样，在2D空间记忆中累积信息，注视点由与任务联合训练的2D策略图产生，同一记忆可全局读取做分类或稠密读取做语义分割，无需改动结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉具有中心凹化与主动性（注视点主动指向感兴趣区域）。构建按同样方式处理信息的机器系统，可在组件与目标已知的可控环境中研究注视行为，系统考察注视特性的涌现，并反向启发对生物视觉的理解。这需要一个能像中心凹一样采样、覆盖从分类到稠密预测的多任务、且能与注视策略联合训练的模型。

Method: 提出FoAViS：以既有的log-polar中心凹化方式采样视觉输入，并将信息累积于2D空间记忆；由于记忆为2D结构，同一记忆可全局读取用于分类、也可稠密读取用于语义分割，架构无需改动；注视点由2D注视图采样，该策略与任务目标同时训练。

Result: 学习得到的注视策略所选位置比非学习的启发式更具信息量、更任务相关；这些策略以有益方式塑造任务表征：在分类与分割上，与策略联合训练的模型均优于使用随机位置的模型，即便后者被提供了学习到的扫描路径。此外，这些扫描路径在若干实证扫描路径特性上与人类注视行为存在相似性。

Conclusion: 该工作迈出了从标准被动、单遍架构走向有生物依据的视觉模型的重要一步，为研究目标导向的注视行为与视觉系统中的信息表征提供了路径。

Abstract: Human vision is foveated and active: resolution is highest at the fovea and falls off towards the periphery, and fixations are actively directed to locations of interest. Building a machine system that processes information in the same way lets us study gaze behavior in a controlled setting where the system's components and objectives are known. The emergence of gaze characteristics can therefore be examined systematically, which may in turn inform our understanding of biological vision. This requires a model that can sample like the fovea, handle various tasks from classification to dense prediction, and be trained concurrently with a fixation policy. In our study, we present such a system and study its capabilities and the fixation policies that emerge from it. Our system, FoAViS, samples visual input by adopting the established log-polar foveation and accumulates information in a 2D spatial memory. Due to its 2D structure, the same memory can be read out globally for classification or densely for semantic segmentation, without any architectural change. Fixations are drawn from a 2D map by a policy trained concurrently with the task. We find that learned fixation policies select more informative, task-specific locations than non-learned heuristics. These policies also shape the task representations in a beneficial way: for both classification and segmentation, models trained concurrently with a policy outperform models trained with random locations, even when the latter are provided with learned scanpaths. Moreover, these scanpaths exhibit similarities to human gaze behavior by capturing aspects of empirical scanpath characteristics. Our work marks an important step away from standard passive, single-pass architectures, towards biologically grounded models of vision. It opens a path for studying goal-directed gaze behavior and information representation in the visual system.

</details>


### [68] [MoETok: Sparse Expert Routing for 1D Visual Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41992)
*Jiahao Guo,Bao Tang,Haoxiang Cao,Jingfeng Yao,Bo Li,Wenyu Liu,Kun Gai,Changqian Yu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Modern visual tokenizers aim to preserve fine-grained visual details while learning high-level semantic representations for downstream generation and understanding. Query-based 1D visual tokenizers encode images into flexible latent sequences, yet how individual learnable queries gather and encode image information remains poorly understood. We reveal that these queries spontaneously develop complementary roles under semantic supervision: a small set of Content queries captures high-level semantic information, while a larger set of Detail queries preserves fine-grained visual details for reconstruction. To adapt token processing to these heterogeneous roles, we introduce MoETok, a 1D visual tokenizer that routes tokens through sparsely activated experts. Jointly optimized with reconstruction and semantic objectives, the router learns role-specialized processing paths adapted to each token's representation. Across model scales and token budgets, MoETok consistently improves reconstruction, semantic representation, and autoregressive generation over dense tokenizers under matched supervision and active computation. On ImageNet, it combines strong reconstruction and semantic representation with state-of-the-art class-conditional generation within the 1D tokenizer family. We further validate MoETok at scale in a unified autoregressive model jointly trained for understanding and generation with a next-token cross-entropy objective. The resulting model combines competitive visual understanding with state-of-the-art generation among unified discrete models, matching or surpassing diffusion-based models such as BAGEL and Qwen-Image on benchmarks including DPG-Bench (87.61), GenEval2 (40.04 GM), and WISE (0.49).

</details>


### [69] [GeoFlip: Benchmarking Geometry-Sensitive Physics in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42002)
*Yuqi Li,Junming Liu,Bowen Hu,Ruize Niu,Huiran Duan,Yingli Tian,Hao Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Video generation models synthesize realistic motion, but can they respond correctly to changes in initial geometry? We introduce GeoFlip, a video generation benchmark centered on physical outcome flips induced by geometry. With shared physical settings and textual prompts held fixed, GeoFlip applies controlled interventions to support extent, object placement, and mechanism configuration, constructing paired scenes with contrasting reference events, such as remaining stable versus falling or tipping. These pairs test whether models translate geometric differences in the first frame into correct physical evolution. The benchmark comprises 482 scene pairs and 964 reference videos across six physical categories, with reference outcomes established through procedural scene construction, physics simulation, and numerical validation and verification of physical events. We evaluate 16 models for image to video generation along three dimensions: event correctness, structural integrity, and similarity to reference videos. We examine evaluation consistency with independent vision language judges. On the 442 conditions shared by all models, the highest event correctness averaged across tasks is 27.98%, revealing substantial limitations in physical generation conditioned on geometry. Model strengths vary across physical mechanisms, and the three evaluation dimensions capture distinct capabilities. Typical failures include ignoring geometric changes, generating incorrect object motion, bending rigid objects, and introducing additional components. Rank correlations between judges reach 0.906 for event correctness and 0.929 for structural integrity. By combining controlled geometric interventions, verifiable physical references, and multidimensional evaluation, GeoFlip assesses progress from visual realism toward physical consistency conditioned on geometry.

</details>


### [70] [Any Forcing: Any-Step Generality and One-Step Stability for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42031)
*Jiahua Xu,Yijie Fang,Nannan Wang,Xunhao Lin,Li Cao,Lei Hao,Mingrui Zhu,Fei Gao,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Autoregressive video generation enables efficient inference through streaming prediction. However, existing methods are typically restricted to fixed sampling steps, limiting the flexibility to balance generation quality and efficiency, while one-step generation suffers from degraded visual quality and error accumulation over long sequences, leading to content drift and generation collapse. To address these issues, we propose Any Forcing, a unified framework for achieving Any-Step Generality and One-Step Stability. Specifically, we introduce a unified adversarial discriminator that jointly performs real-distribution alignment, inter-step consistency alignment, and first-frame quality enhancement, enabling flexible sampling while preserving one-step generation quality. Furthermore, we propose a training-free adaptive long-term memory selection mechanism that selects historical states based on global semantic relevance and local value contribution, while remapping their temporal positions to preserve temporal relationships within the memory cache, thereby enabling stable one-step long-video generation. Experiments demonstrate that Any Forcing achieves high-quality and stable one-step autoregressive video generation, achieving 20.9 FPS and 24.7 FPS for short and long video generation on a single H100 GPU, respectively.

</details>


### [71] [Learning Clean Generative Processes from Noisy Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42036)
*Fabian Kruse,Lorenzo Baldassari,Ivan Dokmanić*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出仅用独立噪声观测学习干净图像生成模型的方法：Noise2Noise回归只能学到不低于训练噪声水平的后验预测，通过引入"一致性"关系可将其延拓至干净图像分布，并在多种噪声模型与任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散过程及其非高斯推广在每个噪声水平都需后验预测，但实际中往往只能获得噪声图像、缺少干净图像配对，因此需要研究如何从纯噪声观测中学习这些后验预测并生成干净图像。
