No clinical efficacy is claimed; the system is positioned as a relaxation and attentional-support tool whose benefit and possible reliance must be evaluated empirically.

</details>


### [140] [The psychophysics of affect in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40985)
*Joseph Marks,Marius C. Vollberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM对赌博或股票交易结果的预期情绪评分呈现“硅基获益放大”现象（获益评分强于等额损失），孤立评分时对幅度呈递减敏感性，联合评分时线性化；在决策中获益-损失不对称基本消失，且模型自身评分并不比货币效用更好地解释其选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益进入决策流程，但其如何将结果映射为情绪体验、以及是否在选择中运用这种映射尚不清楚；本文借鉴行为决策中“预期效用/主观价值函数”的框架来检验LLM的情绪预测机制。

Method: 让LLM预期赌博或股票交易结果的情绪感受（预期情绪评分），并测试其是否将这些评分纳入选择。实验设置了两种评分条件：孤立评分（单独评估单一结果，无法直接比较）与联合评分（同时呈现多个结果以支持直接比较），随后在决策任务中考察获益-损失不对称性，并将模型自身评分与货币效用作竞争性解释变量进行对比。

Result: 孤立评分下，评分对幅度呈递减敏感性，类似人类价值函数；但模型对获益的评分强度高于等额损失，构成对经典损失厌恶的反转，称为“硅基获益放大”（silicon gain amplification）。联合评分下，评分线性化。在决策中，获益-损失不对称性基本消失，模型自身评分对选择的解释力并不显著优于货币效用。

Conclusion: LLM从结果到情感的映射是可塑的、依赖评估情境的；所测情绪评分并未可靠地驱动其选择，这引出智能体LLM是否以及应如何将预期情绪纳入决策的问题。

Abstract: Good decisions often require considering how outcomes will feel. Yet large language models (LLMs) increasingly enter decision workflows, despite little being known about how they map outcomes to feelings or use those mappings in choice. We tested how LLMs anticipated gambling or stock-trading outcomes would feel and whether they incorporated these ratings into choice. When outcomes were rated in isolation, ratings exhibited diminishing sensitivity to magnitude, resembling human value functions; but models rated gains more intensely than equivalent losses, an inversion of canonical loss aversion we call silicon gain amplification. When outcomes were rated together, enabling direct comparison, ratings linearised. In choice, the gain-loss asymmetry largely disappeared, and models' own ratings did not reliably explain their choices better than monetary utility did. These findings reveal malleable mappings from outcomes to affect, raising questions about whether and how agentic LLMs should incorporate anticipated feelings into decisions.

</details>


### [141] [Can Personal Requirements Become Supervision for Planning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40992)
*Junwei Wu,Runze Yan,Yuzhang Xie,Hanqi Luo,Ziwei Dong,Jiaying Lu,Xiao Hu,Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Noura：将大模型规划与"可执行个人规范"（由公开临床指南与个体健康/饮食档案实例化）结合，规范在规划中完成食物落地、份量优化、整周计划校验与修订引导，并把执行后的计划与修订转化为紧凑模型的后训练监督，使 Qwen3-14B 完整计划通过率从 24.0% 提升至 72.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多病共存者的饮食需同时满足疾病与药物要求，且要跨餐次、跨天联合满足，同时保留既有饮食习惯。这些要求虽在临床指南和药品说明中公开，但语言模型智能体难以将其转化为针对单个人的可行计划：在基于 NHANES 构建、经营养师认可的周计划评测中，13 个模型（从医学微调的 8B 到 GPT-6.1 Sol）直接生成的完整计划通过率不足 3%，显式规则、更高推理强度或模型自选工具也仅把 GPT-6.1 Sol 提升到不超过 8%。

Method: 核心思路：能否决一份计划的规范，同时也能决定从中学习什么。Noura 把 LLM 规划与可执行个人规范配对，规范在规划过程中锚定食物选择、优化份量、校验完整多日计划并指导修订；随后将已执行的计划与"上下文归因"的修订转化为紧凑模型的监督信号，即使轨迹从未完全成功也能回收目标；同一规范还同时提供后训练所需的监督目标、偏好与奖励。

Result: 在 4,993 名留出参与者上，Noura-SFT 在同一执行回路内把 Qwen3-14B 的完整计划通过率从 24.0% 提升到 72.5%，规则违规减少 97.5%，未观察到关键性失败，膳食质量亦提升。本地部署的 14B 规划器规则违规少于其教师模型 GPT-6 Luna。匹配对照显示，基于执行结果的目标相比模型自写提案可减少 41% 的规则违规。在 Qwen、Phi、Llama 四种骨干上，仅自训练即在 1,249 人子集上降低规则违规 41–61%。

Conclusion: 将公开临床指南实例化为可执行、可校验的个人规范，并以其作为监督与奖励来源，是让小型模型在多病共存膳食规划上超越直接生成乃至更大教师模型的有效路径；规范驱动的执行反馈可显著提升可行性、合规性与膳食质量。

Abstract: Managing multiple chronic conditions through diet requires satisfying disease- and medication-specific requirements jointly across meals and days while preserving established eating habits. These requirements are public in clinical guidelines and drug labels, yet turning them into a feasible plan for one person remains difficult for language-model agents. In our dietitian-approved weekly planning evaluation built from NHANES, thirteen models, from medically fine-tuned 8B models to GPT-6.1 Sol, pass fewer than 3% of complete plans through direct generation. Explicit rules, higher reasoning effort, or model-directed tools leave GPT-6.1 Sol at no more than 8%. We present Noura, which pairs LLM planning with executable personal specifications instantiated from public clinical guidance and individual health and dietary profiles. During planning, the specification grounds foods, optimizes portions, checks the complete multi-day plan, and guides revision. Our key insight is that a specification that rejects a plan can also determine what to learn from it. Noura turns executed plans and contextually credited revisions into supervision for compact models, recovering targets even from trajectories that never achieve complete success. The same specification supplies supervised targets, preferences, and rewards for post-training. On 4,993 held-out participants, Noura-SFT raises Qwen3-14B's complete-plan pass rate from 24.0% to 72.5% within the same execution loop, cuts rule violations by 97.5% with no observed critical failures, and improves dietary quality. The locally hosted 14B planner incurs fewer rule violations than its GPT-6 Luna teacher. A matched comparison shows that executed targets reduce rule violations by 41% over model-written proposals. Across four Qwen, Phi, and Llama backbones, self-training alone reduces rule violations by 41-61% on a 1,249-person population subset.

</details>


### [142] [Violet Teaming in Practice: Lessons from a Pilot Expert Workshop on Governing Complex AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41018)
*Steph Batalis,Kait Connors,Ben C. Snyder,Vikram Venkatram,Josh Wentzel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文记录了一次"紫色团队"（violet teaming）专家研讨试点，将其作为生物防御相关AI能力的治理方法学，并通过三阶段假设情景演练说明其设计、产出与经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴技术（尤其是具有两用性质的生物防御相关AI能力）的评估需要同时纳入技术、政策与社会层面考量，而传统评估往往将技术安全、滥用风险与落地实施问题割裂处理。

Method: 采用专家研讨工作坊形式的"紫色团队"方法：设计一个假设性技术情景，按能力开发、滥用潜力、实施落地三个连续阶段推进，并在全过程中融合技术、安全、运营与政策四类视角。

Result: 演练揭示了非显而易见的滥用路径；将潜在干预措施从纯技术防护扩展到更广范围；并识别出可能制约实施效果的运营与政策约束。

Conclusion: 顺序化、多视角整合的紫色团队方法能对复杂两用技术进行整体性评估，优于孤立审视各类风险；本文总结的工作坊设计、产出与经验可为推广该治理方法学提供参考。

Abstract: Violet teaming has been proposed as a method to integrate technical, policy, and societal considerations into the evaluation of emerging technologies. Here, we document a pilot expert workshop used to explore violet teaming as a governance methodology for biodefense-relevant AI capabilities and describe observations from the exercise. The workshop used a hypothetical technology scenario across three sequential stages - capability development, misuse potential, and implementation - while incorporating technical, security, operational, and policy perspectives. The sequential format provided an integrated way to examine potential benefits, misuse risks, and real-world implementation challenges, rather than considering these issues in isolation. The exercise surfaced non-obvious misuse pathways, expanded potential interventions beyond purely technical safeguards, and highlighted operational and policy constraints that could affect implementation. This report summarizes the workshop design, outputs, and lessons learned for applying violet teaming to the evaluation of complex, dual-use technologies.

</details>


### [143] [ReplayRCA: Measuring Post-hoc Diagnosis and Live Oversight of Agent Failures by Counterfactual Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41025)
*Jeremy Canale,Fabien Soulis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReplayRCA 提出一种以“干预”而非“标注一致性”来评测语言模型“根因诊断/纠错”能力的基准：把环境回放到所诊断步骤之前，用诊断模型给出的动作替换原动作，再由同一 agent 继续执行；得分（causal repair）等于续跑成功率减去配对安慰剂（重放原动作）的成功率。实验显示最优模型仅达 +51.3 点，且事后复盘能力与实时监督能力排名严重不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有失败归因/根因分析（RCA）基准多以“与人工标注的关键步骤是否一致”作为评分标准，容易受到“最后一步捷径”等偏差影响，且与真实纠错效果脱节。作者希望用可干预、可验证的因果指标来评测诊断模型是否真正找到决定性错误并能一步修复。

Method: 1) 评测协议：将环境恢复到所选步骤之前的状态，用诊断模型提出的动作替换 agent 原动作，然后让同一 agent 继续执行；评分为 causal repair = 续跑成功率 − 配对安慰剂（在同一机制下重放 agent 原动作）成功率。2) 两条赛道：post-hoc autopsy（看到完整失败轨迹并被告知失败）与 live oversight（在线监督，事先不知是否会失败，可在不可逆动作前干预一次）。3) 任务环境 OpsWorld：确定性、程序化生成的后台办公环境，六个难度等级，最高级含多模态元素（二维码、手写）。4) 在 62 个经过校准的系统性失败样本上评测 13 个前沿模型，并在 87 条轨迹上评测 live supervisor。

Result: 62 个系统性失败上，13 个前沿模型中最佳（Grok 4.7）causal repair 为 +51.3 点；前八名统计上不可区分；两家最大厂商的旗舰模型分别排第 8 和第 12；有 5 个样本无任何模型能修复。强制强模型在最后一步干预会损失 17.1 点，说明基于标注的归因基准中的“最后一步捷径”不可迁移。87 条运行中，最佳两名 live supervisor 统计打平，其中一名为小规模开放权重模型（DeepSeek V4.1 Flash）；而事后诊断最强的模型之一（GPT-6.1 Sol）在实时监督中表现最弱（同一批运行上 −14.5 点，p = 0.002）。

Conclusion: 应以干预式因果指标（causal repair）替代标注一致性来评测失败诊断；事后复盘能力不能作为实时监督能力的可靠代理，二者需分别评测；基准、环境与评测代码已开源。

Abstract: When a language-model agent fails a multi-step task, can another model find the decisive mistake and fix it with a single action? We introduce ReplayRCA, a benchmark that scores such diagnoses by intervention rather than by agreement with annotations: the environment is restored to the state preceding the chosen step, the proposed action replaces the agent's own, and the same agent continues. The score, causal repair, is the continuation success rate minus that of a paired placebo that replays the agent's original action through the same mechanism. Two tracks share this protocol: a post-hoc autopsy, where the diagnoser sees the whole failed run and is told that it failed, and live oversight, where a supervisor watches the run unfold and may intervene once, before an irreversible action, without knowing whether the run will fail. Tasks come from OpsWorld, a deterministic, procedurally generated back-office environment with six difficulty levels, the last of which is multimodal (QR codes, handwriting). On 62 calibrated systematic failures, the best of 13 frontier models (Grok 4.7) reaches a causal repair of +51.3 points; the top eight models are statistically indistinguishable, the flagship models of the two largest vendors rank 8th and 12th, and 5 items are repaired by no model. Forcing a strong model to intervene at the last step costs it 17.1 points, so the last-step shortcut of annotation-based attribution benchmarks does not transfer. On 87 runs, the two best live supervisors are statistically tied and one of them is a small open-weight model (DeepSeek V4.1 Flash); the supervisor that is among the best post-hoc diagnosers (GPT-6.1 Sol) is the weakest live one (-14.5 points on the same runs, p = 0.002), so post-hoc diagnostic performance can be a poor proxy for live oversight. The environment and the evaluation code are released at https://github.com/jeremy1392/RCAreplay.

</details>


### [144] [Capability-Grounded Agentic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41040)
*Shikha Shrestha,William Yeoh,Ning Zhang,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CGAP 将工具目录确定性地编译为抽象 PDDL 模型，由 LLM 把请求转成目标后用经典规划器求解，执行阶段由 LLM 补充实体级调用并用确定性校验与 LLM 验证器把关，在四个基准上比标准工具调用 agent 最高提升 28.9 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 处理自然语言请求时通常靠试错选择相互依赖的多次调用，缺乏可执行计划的保证，即使是简单请求也容易失败；作者希望让 agent 先推理"请求需要什么"与"工具有什么"，从而生成有保证的可执行计划。

Method: 1) 将工具目录确定性编译为 PDDL 模型；2) 用 LLM 把请求转化为目标；3) 用经典规划器求解；4) 由于工具输出预先未知，模型保持抽象，只跟踪哪些输入可用而非其具体取值；5) 执行阶段由 LLM 补充抽象计划省略的实体特定调用，确定性检查拒绝破坏计划的调用；6) 每个参数均来自可引用的来源（对话片段或先前工具响应），结果须经 LLM 验证器对照请求检查后才算交付。

Result: 在 ToolSandbox、ComplexFuncBench、BFCL multi-turn 与 τ²-bench retail 上，使用三种 LLM，CGAP 相比标准工具调用 agent 提升最高达 28.9 个百分点。

Conclusion: 以能力/需求为核心、结合经典规划与 LLM 执行细化的混合架构，能显著提升多步工具调用任务的可靠性与成功率，且对多种 LLM 与基准具有一致有效性。

Abstract: LLM agents serve natural-language requests through tools, data sources, and other agents, and even simple requests often need several interdependent calls. Typically, the LLM picks these calls by trial and error, with no guarantee of an executable plan. We present Capability-Grounded Agentic Planning (CGAP), which instead reasons about what a request requires and what tools provide. CGAP compiles the tool catalog deterministically into a PDDL model, uses an LLM to turn the request into goals, and solves the model with a classical planner. Since tool outputs are unknown in advance, the model is abstract, tracking which inputs are available, not their values. During execution, an LLM adds entity-specific calls that abstract plans omit, and deterministic checks reject any that break the plan. Every argument is drawn from a cited source, such as a conversation span or an earlier tool response, and each requested result counts as delivered only after an LLM verifier checks it against the request. With three LLMs on ToolSandbox, ComplexFuncBench, BFCL multi-turn, and $τ^2$-bench retail, CGAP outperforms a standard tool-calling agent by up to 28.9 points.

</details>


### [145] [Consensus Within, Competition Between: Decentralized LLM Teams for Perimeter Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41044)
*Khawaja Abaid Ullah,Ananya Acharya,Mohammad Javad Khojasteh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在简化周界防御仿真中比较了集中式指挥、拍卖式共识团队与独立LLM智能体三种架构在不可靠通信下的表现，发现带外部冲突解决协议的共识团队突破率最低（15%），并揭示了视觉输入与伪装对LLM识别入侵者的显著影响及提示词预警的缓解作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM协调多智能体团队时存在根本性架构问题：任务分配应集中于单一LLM控制器，还是分散给自主LLM智能体，尤其在通信不可靠时缺乏实证依据；同时LLM在感知与决策中对伪装目标、非威胁目标的分辨能力也值得考察。

Method: 构建刻意简化的周界防御仿真，对手为LLM控制的入侵者；对比三种架构——集中式指挥官、基于拍卖的共识团队、独立智能体，覆盖五种网络条件；通过给低成本LLM更多竞价计算权限进行消融；向文本状态中加入鸟类与民用无人机的照片，测试近前沿与低成本模型的区分能力；引入伪装成标本鸟无人机的入侵者并测试一句预警提示的效果。

Result: 突破率：共识团队15%、指挥官32%、独立智能体29%；五种网络条件中仅有限无线电通信范围会改变突破率（使指挥官更差）。共识团队中赋予低成本LLM更多竞价权限使突破率上升38个百分点。加入照片后，仅近前沿模型能将鸟类与民用无人机同入侵者区分。伪装成标本鸟无人机的入侵者被两类模型误判为鸟类，近前沿模型未追击比例达59-61%（原本1-16%）；一句提示词预警可将其降至0-25%。

Conclusion: 配备专门外部冲突解决协议的LLM团队突破率最低，分布式共识加外部协议优于集中控制；感知层面的多模态输入与提示词预警对目标辨识和决策质量有显著影响，低成本模型在关键决策环节的权限需谨慎分配。

Abstract: The use of large language models (LLMs) to coordinate multi-agent teams raises a fundamental architectural question: whether task allocation should be centralized in a single LLM controller or distributed across autonomous LLM agents, particularly when communication is unreliable. In a deliberately simplified perimeter-defense simulation against LLM-controlled intruders, we compared a central commander, an auction-based consensus team and independent agents under five network conditions. Breach rates were 15% for the consensus team, 32% for the commander and 29% for independent agents, and of the different network conditions, only limited radio range changed a team's breach rate, raising the commander's. In consensus team, giving a low-cost LLM more authority over the bid computation raised breaches by 38 percentage points. With birds and civilian drones in airspace, we added a photograph for each to the input text state. Only the near-frontier model treated them apart from intruders. Intruders disguised as existing taxidermy bird drones were mistook as birds by both low-cost and near-frontier LLMs, going unpursued in 59-61% of near-frontier decisions (1-16% before). However, a one-sentence forewarning in the prompt lowered it to 0-25%. We find that LLM team with a dedicated external conflict resolution protocol results in fewest breaches. Code: https://github.com/the-kas-lab/consensus-within-competition-between.

</details>


### [146] [LanG 2.0: Energy-Efficient Agentic AI for Semi-Automated Security Operations at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41072)
*Anes Abdennebi,Nadjia Kara,Laaziz Lahlou,Hakima Ould-Slimane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LanG 2.0 是一个面向 SOC 的智能体系统，通过实体锚定关联、规则变更人工审批与防篡改账本等结构性保障，使小型本地模型能以极低能耗替代前沿模型完成威胁检测、证据关联与规则编写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOC 需将海量告警、日志与流量转化为可信、低成本且可揭示隐蔽威胁的决策；核心疑问是小模型能否在"结构性保障而非模型精度"提供保证的前提下，经济且安全地取代前沿模型。

Method: 系统包含实体锚定关联（entity-anchored correlation）、规则变更的人工审批机制、防篡改账本（tamper-evident ledger）；评测覆盖来自 5 个网络的 8 个流量数据集、真实 XDR 告警与多阶段攻击活动。

Result: 相较前代，活动关联精确率由 18.75% 提升至 51.81%，规则接受率由≤60.00% 提升至 98.33%。相较 Claude Opus 5.5，在未见网络上以各网络正常流量为基准检测攻击达 73.42% F1 且零误报，对比 58.10% F1 伴随 49.00% 误报，能耗低至少 650 倍；微调模型在告警分类与规则编写上与前者统计等价，能耗低 5.6–59 倍。处理 333 万条记录耗时 6.4 分钟，每小时逾 3000 万条；保障机制可抵御提示注入、标签投毒与账本篡改。

Conclusion: 结构性保障使小型本地模型能够经济且安全地承担 SOC 的大部分检测任务，具备实际可扩展性。

Abstract: Security operations centers (SOCs) need to turn terabytes of alerts, logs and flows into trusted, affordable decisions to unveil hidden threats. We present LanG 2.0, an agentic system that detects threats, correlates evidence and writes rules, investigating whether small local models can replace frontier models affordably and safely when structural safeguards, not model accuracy, provide the guarantees. It incorporates entity-anchored correlation, human approval of rule changes and a tamper-evident ledger. Tests span eight flow datasets from five networks, real XDR alerts and multistage campaigns. Against its predecessor, campaign correlation precision rises from 18.75% to 51.81%, and rule acceptance from at most 60.00% to 98.33%. Against Claude Opus 5.5, scoring flows against each network's normal traffic detects attacks on unseen networks at 73.42% F1 without false positives, versus 58.10% with 49.00% false positives, for at least 650 times less energy. Finetuned models statistically match it on alert classification and rule writing for 5.6 to 59 times less energy. The work demonstrates LanG's practical scalability, processing 3.33 million records in 6.4 minutes, over 30 million hourly, and its safeguards resisted prompt injection, label poisoning and ledger tampering, so small local models can carry most SOC detection affordably and safely.

</details>


### [147] [BABA-Bench: Evaluating Long-Horizon Patient-Advocacy Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41076)
*Shriram Vasudevan,Videet Mehta,Connor Sweeney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 BABA-Bench：47 个基于真实患者维权案例构建的交互式环境，同时考核 LLM 智能体在医疗事务中"结果正确"与"程序合规"，发现智能体普遍"结果对、程序错"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗场景的 LLM 智能体必须既达成正确结果，又遵守必要程序（如共享病历前须获患者授权）。现有基准多只评最终结果，忽视过程合规性，无法揭示"以违规路径达成正确结果"的失效模式。

Method: 构建 47 个交互世界，每个源自去标识化的真实患者维权案例并经专家维权人员审核；智能体需挖掘隐藏信息并协调保险公司、诊所、供应商、药房与社区组织。引擎在执行动作时即时校验三项：是否有授权、是否有证据支撑、动作是否被禁止。对 14 个前沿模型、每世界 3 次运行，共 1,974 次评测；并开展"给定已验证路线"的对照实验。

Result: 921 次达成全部必需结果的运行中，254 次（28%）未遵守必要程序。Claude Opus 5 结果达成率 81.6%，但全项检查通过率仅 52.5%。提供已验证路线后，两个模型通过率分别由 29.8% 升至 91.5%、由 44.0% 升至 97.2%。

Conclusion: 当前前沿模型在医疗智能体任务中的主要瓶颈在于"路径发现"而非"路径执行"；仅以结果为导向的评测会高估能力，程序合规须作为独立评估维度。

Abstract: An LLM agent that helps a patient get care must do two things. It must reach the correct outcome, and it must follow the necessary procedure. For example, the agent must get the patient's permission before it shares the patient's records. We introduce BABA-Bench, a benchmark of 47 interactive worlds. We built each world from a de-identified, real patient advocacy case that expert advocates reviewed. In each world, the agent must find hidden information and coordinate insurers, clinics, suppliers, pharmacies, and community organizations. The engine checks each action at the time the agent does it. It checks if the agent has permission, if the agent has evidence for the action, and if the action is prohibited. We evaluated 14 frontier models with three runs on each world, for a total of 1,974 runs. Agents often reached the correct outcome by an invalid route. Of the 921 runs that reached all required outcomes, 254 (28%) did not follow the necessary procedure. Claude Opus 5 reached all required outcomes in 81.6% of its runs, but only 52.5% of its runs passed all checks. When we gave two models a verified route, their pass rates increased from 29.8% to 91.5% and from 44.0% to 97.2%. For these models, the main problem is to find a route, not to execute it.

</details>


### [148] [Optimization Techniques for Solving the Minimum Red--Blue Separating Set Problem with an Application to Human--AI Text Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41081)
*Marko Djukanović,Staša Vujičić Stanković,Žiga Zebec,Christian Blum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究源自计算几何的最小红蓝分离集问题（MRBSSP），提出紧凑集覆盖ILP模型、预处理规则、GRASP启发式，以及基于逻辑的Benders分解（LBBD）与分支-Benders-割（BBC）两种精确算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRBSSP源自计算几何，缺乏统一的高效求解框架；同时现有方法在可行解质量、下界强度与最优性证明三方面各有不足，需要系统的建模、预处理与多策略算法比较。

Method: 提出紧凑的集覆盖ILP公式化；设计安全的预处理规则；构造GRASP元启发式；实现两种精确方法——LBBD与新颖的Branch-and-Benders-Cut（BBC）；并与ILP、文献中的VNS进行对比实验；还将模型推广到基于词汇图特征区分人类与AI生成文本的NLP应用。

Result: 在250个顶点数最多500的随机图上：ILP产出最佳可行解，LBBD下界最强、平均最优间隙最小，BBC最频繁地证明最优性。在42个NLP实例上，BBC平均最优间隙最小并证明16个最优解，GRASP优于VNS；对272个测试中最优词汇分离集的结构分析识别出113个必需特征与151个可替换特征。

Conclusion: 实验表明三种优化策略具有互补优势，组合使用效果最佳；最优化词汇分离集存在显著多解性与可解释性，为词汇判别特征的可解释性提供了依据。

Abstract: We study the so-called Minimum Red''Blue Separating Set Problem (MRBSSP) which arises from computational geometry. In particular, we propose a compact Set-Cover ILP formulation, safe preprocessing rules, a Greedy Randomized Adaptive Search Procedure (GRASP), and two exact approaches: one is based on Logic-Based Benders Decomposition (LBBD) approach, and the second represents a novel Branch-and-Benders-Cut (BBC) approach. These two exact approaches are compared to ILP--alongside GRASP and the Variable Neighborhood Search (VNS) from literature. Experiments on 250 random graphs with up to 500 vertices reveal complementary strengths: ILP yields the best feasible solutions, LBBD provides the strongest lower bounds and smallest mean optimality gap, and BBC certifies optimality most frequently. We further introduce an NLP application distinguishing human- and AI-generated texts through vocabulary-based graph features. On 42 instances, BBC achieves the smallest mean optimality gap, certifying 16 optima, while GRASP outperforms VNS. Structural analysis of optimal vocabulary separators identifies 113 mandatory and 151 replaceable features across 272 tests, highlighting substantial variability among optimal solutions. Overall, our results demonstrate the effectiveness of complementary optimization strategies and the interpretability of optimal vocabulary-based discrimination.

</details>


### [149] [Payroll Fraud Detection in Synthetic HR/ERP Graphs: An Inductive Comparison of Rules, Path Features, and Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41085)
*Xiaobo Chen,Genliang Zhu,Ziyuan Wang,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在合成的HR/ERP图上将薪酬欺诈筛查建模为归纳式链接预测与实体排序，比较规则、显式路径特征与GNN；调优的链式连接提升模型取得最高链接AP（0.823），4跳场景优势更明显，说明多跳增益并非GNN专属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薪酬欺诈筛查需要在员工—收款人之间发现隐藏关联，并将可疑关系与合法关系区分开。作者希望在一个可控制、可复现的合成评估框架中，厘清多跳候选发现、基于记录的判别与复核排序三者的贡献，并检验GNN相对传统特征模型是否具有特异性优势。

Method: 将问题形式化为归纳式链接预测与实体排序，构建合成HR/ERP图，涉及未见组织，并为每个植入案例配备匹配的良性孪生样本。实验包括24个留出组织的原始研究与前瞻性冻结扩展，对比分析员规则、显式路径（链式连接）特征与GraIL风格GNN、关系实体GNN；并做结构-only审计以检测生成器捷径。

Result: 调优的链式连接梯度提升模型链接AP最高（0.823，高于GraIL式GNN的0.663）；4跳AP为0.646对0.272。新合成队列（正实体比例2.0%）中链式连接提升仍领先（AP 0.798），关系实体GNN亦优于记录感知规则（0.707对0.504）；2%复核预算下精度0.695对0.602。在40%标识符连接失败时，实体GNN描述性优于链式连接提升（AP 0.712对0.683）。结构-only审计发现初始生成器存在捷径，匹配孪生使审计孪生池AUROC降至0.493–0.514。

Conclusion: 多跳收益并非GNN独有，链式连接特征在常规条件下更优；GNN在标识符连接缺失时更具鲁棒性。合成评估设计需引入匹配孪生与结构审计以排除捷径。研究明确区分了多跳候选发现、基于记录的判别与复核优先级排序三个环节；代码与数据需向作者申请。

Abstract: Payroll-fraud screening requires finding hidden employee--payee links while distinguishing suspicious relationships from legitimate ones. We formulate this problem as inductive link prediction and entity ranking on synthetic human-resources and enterprise resource planning (HR/ERP) graphs, with unseen organizations and a matched benign twin for every planted case. An original study on 24 held-out organizations and a prospectively frozen extension compare analyst rules, explicit path features and graph neural networks (GNNs). A tuned gradient-boosted model on chained-join features achieved the highest overall link average precision (AP; 0.823), above the GraIL-style GNN (0.663); its 4-hop AP was 0.646 versus 0.272. Thus, the observed multi-hop gain is not specific to GNNs. On a new synthetic cohort with 2.0% positive entities, chained-join boosting again led (AP 0.798); the relational entity GNN also exceeded record-aware rules (0.707 versus 0.504). At a 2% review budget, their precision was 0.695 versus 0.602. Under 40% failed identifier joins, the entity GNN ranked above chained-join boosting (AP 0.712 versus 0.683, descriptively). Structure-only auditing exposed a shortcut in the initial generator; matched twins reduced the audited twin-pool AUROC to 0.493--0.514. These results separate multi-hop candidate discovery, record-based discrimination and review prioritization within an explicit synthetic evaluation design. Code and data are available from the authors on request.

</details>


### [150] [CIRCE Divines LLM Task Complexity from Constraint Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41089)
*Chenhao Hong,Jiakai Xu,Kaushiki Singh,Yusen Zhang,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CIRCE，用廉价LLM将问题转化为约束中间表示并编译为约束图，再用GNN编码预测LLM能否正确作答，在7个推理基准、4个LLM上均优于现有难度估计基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM问题难度估计依赖问题文本、LLM标注的难度标签或模型尝试后的信号（如隐状态），均未显式利用问题内在的约束结构（待赋值变量及其约束间交互），而该结构对判断问题是否过于复杂具有指示意义。

Method: CIRCE：先用一个低成本LLM从问题文本中抽取结构，构建"约束中间表示"（Constraint Intermediate Representation），该表示可确定性地编译为约束图；随后用图神经网络（GNN）编码该约束图，预测LLM给出正确回答的概率。实验中甚至采用随机初始化的GNN编码器。

Result: 在七个推理基准（含SAT、逻辑、数学）与四个LLM上，相对各指标最强基线，负对数似然从0.465降至0.423，AUROC从0.691提升至0.727。

Conclusion: 显式建模问题的约束结构可作为有效的LLM难度/正确性预估计信号，支撑模型路由、测试时算力分配及是否需要新工具的决策，且无需依赖模型内部状态或昂贵的尝试求解。

Abstract: Estimating whether a problem is too complex for a large language model (LLM) before the model attempts it can guide model routing, how to allocate test-time compute, and suggest the need for new tools to aid the agent. While existing estimators rely on the problem text, LLM-annotated task difficulty labels, or signals obtained after a model attempts to solve the problem, such as its hidden states, none explicitly leverage the constraint structure inherent in the problem: the variables to assign and how the constraints over them interact.
  We introduce CIRCE, which extracts a problem's structure by using a cheap LLM to construct a Constraint Intermediate Representation that deterministically compiles into the constraint graph. Across seven reasoning benchmarks (including SAT, logic, and math) and four LLMs, even a randomly initialized GNN encoder improves predictions of LLM response correctness, reducing negative log-likelihood from 0.465 to 0.423 and increasing AUROC from 0.691 to 0.727 relative to the strongest baseline for each metric.

</details>


### [151] [Verifying Text-to-SQL Queries under Changing Business Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41090)
*Genliang Zhu,Xiaobo Chen,Ziyuan Wang,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Successful SQL execution does not guarantee semantic correctness under changing business definitions; the paper proposes an executable verification profile for revalidating fixed text-to-SQL queries after definition revisions, showing that obligation-guided checking improves discrepancy detection.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: In text-to-SQL and AI-agent analytics, a query that runs successfully can still be semantically wrong once business definitions change (refund attribution, tax treatment, eligible populations, exchange-rate dates). Existing evaluation assesses execution success rather than conformance to the requested revision, leaving a revalidation gap.

Method: An executable verification profile binds the requested revision and knowledge cutoff to legal counterexample data and exact monetary comparison. A preregistered confirmation uses 32 newly authored revision families plus two local producer bundles, yielding 192 query-definition cells (144 nonconforming, 36 finite-suite agreeing, 12 unresolved). Checking methods are compared at an eight-ledger budget with family-bootstrap and sign-flip statistics; current-obligation and equal-weight scoring ablate the source of the benefit; controlled assays, a natural-query pilot and repeated samples provide mechanism and robustness evidence.

Result: 63 of 144 nonconforming cells agree on the current snapshot. At eight ledgers, obligation-guided checking detects 137 discrepancies vs 127 for generic ordering (paired difference 6.9 pp, 95% family bootstrap interval [2.1, 13.6], exact sign-flip p=0.0156). Every ledger arm rejects zero of the 36 agreeing cells (no false rejections). Current-obligation and equal-weight scoring match the guided result, indicating coverage rather than revision-specific priority drives the gain.

Conclusion: A legal discrepancy refutes conformance, whereas agreement remains limited to the recorded suite. The work establishes an auditable business-semantic revalidation profile, scoped to its authored schema and local-model setting.

Abstract: In text-to-SQL and AI-agent analytics, successful SQL execution does not establish semantic correctness under changing business definitions. We study revalidation of fixed queries after changes to refund attribution, tax treatment, eligible populations and exchange-rate dates. An executable verification profile binds the requested revision and knowledge cutoff to legal counterexample data and exact monetary comparison. A preregistered confirmation on 32 newly authored revision families and two local producer bundles retains 192 query-definition cells: 144 nonconforming, 36 finite-suite agreeing and 12 unresolved. Of the nonconforming cells, 63 agree on the current snapshot. At an eight-ledger budget, obligation-guided checking detects 137 discrepancies versus 127 for generic ordering, a paired difference of 6.9 percentage points (family bootstrap 95% interval [2.1, 13.6]; exact family sign-flip p=0.0156). Every ledger arm rejects zero of the 36 agreeing cells. Current-obligation and equal-weight scoring match the guided result, identifying coverage rather than revision-specific priority as the observed source of benefit. Controlled assays, a natural-query pilot and repeated samples provide separate mechanism and robustness evidence. A legal discrepancy refutes conformance; agreement remains restricted to the recorded suite. The study establishes an auditable business-semantic revalidation profile within its authored schema and local-model scope.

</details>


### [152] [QUAL: Quantile Alignment Improves Zero-Shot Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41100)
*Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Jun Seo,Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Dongwan Kang,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Tae Yoon Lim,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免标签框架QUAL，通过“池相对分位数对齐”改进并组合多个时间序列基础模型（TSFM）的零样本预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSFM零样本预测精度跨数据集波动大；多模型组合可降低波动，但零样本部署下无目标标签，无法选择或加权模型。

Method: 发现模型池不仅可作为最终集成，还可作为概率参考来变换各成员预测；据此提出QUAL：先将各成员预测通过池相对参考对齐分位数，再用Huber聚合与重排（rearrangement）得到稳健组合预测，全程无训练、不改动模型。

Result: 在GIFT-Eval上，分位数对齐使成员平均MASE与CRPS分别下降7.4%和9.8%；当弱预测器进入无法预先验证质量的候选池时，完整框架仍保持稳健。

Conclusion: 模型池的相对分位数对齐即可在无标签条件下普遍提升单个TSFM预测，并支撑简单、稳健、免训练的集成方案。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) provide zero-shot forecasts for unseen series, but their accuracy varies across datasets. Combining several models can reduce this variability, yet zero-shot deployment provides no target labels for selecting or weighting them. We find that a model pool is useful not only as a final ensemble, but also as a probability reference for transforming each member forecast. Aligning quantiles through this pool-relative reference improves every member forecast in aggregate. Motivated by this finding, we propose QUAL (Quantile alignment), a simple label-free framework for improving and combining diverse TSFMs. QUAL first produces aligned member forecasts, then applies Huber aggregation and rearrangement to obtain a robust combined forecast without training or model modification. On GIFT-Eval, quantile alignment reduces average member MASE and CRPS by 7.4% and 9.8%. The full framework remains robust when weak forecasters enter a candidate pool whose quality cannot be verified in advance. Code is available at https://github.com/junhyeokkang/QUAL.

</details>


### [153] [Differential rank preservation manifold learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41107)
*Henry Han,Luyu Niu,Diane Li,Jiacun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出平滑秩t-SNE（SR-tSNE），在标准t-SNE损失中加入可微秩项与sigmoid秩近似，显式保留邻域顺序；并提出序数DLP（O-DLP）指标。在8个基准、12个基线上提升秩保持与邻域重叠，并在标签迁移、细胞类型发现与日内交易等下游任务中取得最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t-SNE、UMAP等流形学习方法只保持局部邻域，却不保持邻域内部邻居的排序：两个嵌入可有相同邻居集合但最近点不同。这种顺序信息丢失会损害重视顺序的下游应用，而现有t-SNE族方法缺乏显式顺序保持机制。

Method: 在标准t-SNE损失上增加可微的秩项（rank term）；为高效训练采用sigmoid秩近似，强制某点的高维真实邻居排名高于无关“入侵者”并保持原始顺序；同时提出O-DLP指标，仅当邻居以原秩返回时才计入保真度。

Result: 在涵盖图像、文本、语音、遥感与单细胞数据的8个基准上，SR-tSNE在秩保持与邻域重叠上优于12个基线；消融显示仅秩项即可将O-DLP提升5%–28%，标准成对损失无法达到；7个视觉数据集标签迁移最优；6个单细胞数据集的18个数据集-指标组合中17项第一（选簇分辨率含/不含标签均如此）；日内交易中收益更高、风险与换手更低。

Conclusion: SR-tSNE是首个显式保持顺序的t-SNE族方法，秩项带来标准成对损失无法获得的增益，且优势可迁移至多类下游任务。

Abstract: Manifold learning methods like t-SNE and UMAP preserve local neighborhoods, but they do not preserve the ranking of the neighbors inside them. Two embeddings can return the same neighbor set while disagreeing on which point is closest. This loss of order degrades downstream applications that value the order in the embedding. We propose Smooth Rank t-SNE (SR-tSNE), which adds a differentiable rank term to the standard t-SNE loss. To enable efficient training, this term uses a sigmoid rank approximation to explicitly force a point's true high-dimensional neighbors to outrank unrelated intruders and retain their original order. We also introduce Ordinal DLP (O-DLP), an extension of the Degree of Locality Preservation (DLP) metric that credits a neighbor only when it returns at its original rank. Across eight benchmarks spanning image, text, speech, remote-sensing and single-cell data, SR-tSNE outperforms 12 baselines in rank preservation and neighborhood overlap. Ablation studies demonstrate our rank term alone boosts O-DLP by 5% to 28%, a gain not achievable using standard pairwise loss. This advantage transfers to downstream applications. SR-tSNE achieves the best label-transfer performance across seven vision datasets. For cell-type discovery across six single-cell datasets, it ranks first in 17 of 18 dataset-metric combinations, whether clustering resolution is selected with or without cell-type labels. In intraday trading, SR-tSNE achieves higher returns, lower risk, and lower turnover than the baselines. To our knowledge, SR-tSNE is the first t-SNE-family method with explicit order preservation.

</details>


### [154] [Calibration of Large Model Quantization based on HiFloat4 Format](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41112)
*Jing Huang,Yu Cheng,Ziwei Yu,Lan Zhao,Sitan Li,Guangxiu Yao,Xin Wang,Yuanyong Luo,Hengyi Liang,Ting Li,Bo Liu,Hu Liu,Michael Bi Mi,Heng Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对HiFloat4（HiF4）这一层级块浮点量化格式，提出HiGPTQ、HiAutoRound与HiFlatQuant三种校准方法，显著缩小低精度量化与BF16的精度差距并超越NVFP4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM量化从8比特走向4比特，催生MXFP4、NVFP4等定制格式；HiF4采用三级缩放层级的块浮点结构，动态范围更大，但量化仍带来精度损失，而现有校准方法（如针对INT4）无法适配其层级BFP结构，因此需要专门的校准方案。

Method: 将三种校准思路迁移并适配到HiF4格式：基于GPTQ的HiGPTQ、基于AutoRound的HiAutoRound、基于FlatQuant的HiFlatQuant，使其兼容HiF4的层级块浮点缩放结构。

Result: 大量实验表明，三种方法相较直接HiF4转换均提升推理精度，将HiF4与BF16的差距缩小至1%以内，并在多种模型与下游任务上优于SOTA的NVFP4格式。

Conclusion: 校准对HiF4量化至关重要，所提出的三种校准方法有效支撑HiF4的实际部署，使其成为优于NVFP4的高精度4比特量化格式。

Abstract: Quantization of Large Language Models (LLMs) has be- coming increasingly important for reducing the computa- tional costs and resource requirements for practical deploy- ment.Growinginterestinthisareahasmotivatedthecommu- nity to move beyond 8-bit quantization towards 4-bit preci- sion, leading to the development of customized 4-bit formats such as MXFP4 and NVFP4. Recently, HiFloat4 (HiF4) has emerged as a competitive alternative which adopts a Block Floating-Point(BFP)formatwithathree-levelscalinghierar- chy, thereby achieving a significantly larger dynamic range. While quantization can substantially reduce memory usage and accelerate inference, it frequently introduces accuracy degradation. Calibration is therefore essential for narrowing theperformancegapbetweenlow-precisionquantizedmodels and their full-precision counterparts. Nevertheless, existing calibration methods are mainly designed and validated us- ing integer-precision formats, such as INT4, lacking support for HiF4 due to its hierarchical BFP structure. In this paper, weinvestigatethreecalibrationapproaches,namelyHiGPTQ, HiAutoRound, and HiFlatQuant, to further enhance model quantizationbasedontheHiF4format.Extensiveexperiments demonstratethatallthreeproposedapproachesimproveinfer- ence accuracy compared with direct HiF4 conversion, reduc- ing the gap between HiF4 and BF16 to less than 1% and out- performing the state-of-the-art NVFP4 format across various models and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [155] [Predicting Human-Gain Curves under Local Proxy Optimization from Repeated Ratings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41114)
*Moonwon Choi,Kisung Nam,Seunggeun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PACE——一种优化前诊断方法，用重复人工评分预测固定代理指标随优化强度变化所诱导的人类感知质量增益曲线，并在多数据集上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理指标与人类感知的平均相关性无法揭示“优化强度增加时人类感知质量如何变化”；仅靠平均相关性无法预判优化收益的走向与拐点。

Method: 将代理优化建模为KL正则化的指数倾斜（exponential tilt）；在KL半径d处将局部增益近似为 A√d + Bd，其中A刻画一阶代理-人类对齐，B刻画沿代理优化路径的二阶修正；用校准样本上的重复人工评分估计A、B，并在留出评估项上检验增益曲线预测。对比A-only、零增益、仅可靠性基线及柔性代理条件校准，并辅以LoRA-DPO实验。

Result: 在SummEval、合规的WMT MQM聚类、OpenMEVA-MAGS ROC/WP重复评分切片及GPT-4o判官代理实验中，PACE一致降低留出增益预测误差；同等预池化信息下与柔性代理条件校准相当；LoRA-DPO提供与真实优化的适度衔接；跨数据集/切片迁移中源系数可作有用先验，目标校准可进一步提升。

Conclusion: PACE是一种局部、单侧的诊断工具，刻画固定题池正倾斜分支上的温和优化，能事前预测人类感知增益曲线，但不适用于大幅优化或负向分支。

Abstract: Optimizing a proxy objective can improve human-perceived quality, but the resulting gain can vary with optimization strength. Average proxy-human correlation alone does not reveal how human-perceived quality changes as optimization strength increases. We propose PACE (Proxy Alignment and Curvature Expansion), a pre-optimization diagnostic that uses repeated human ratings to predict the human-gain curve induced by a fixed proxy. The method models proxy optimization as a KL-regularized exponential tilt. At KL radius $d$, the local gain is approximated by $A\sqrt{d}+Bd$, where $A$ measures first-order proxy-human alignment and $B$ captures the leading second-order correction along the proxy-optimization path. We estimate $A$ and $B$ from repeated human ratings on calibration items and test the resulting gain-curve prediction on held-out evaluation items. Across SummEval, analysis-eligible WMT MQM clusters, an OpenMEVA-MAGS ROC/WP repeated-rating slice, and a GPT-4o judge proxy experiment, PACE consistently reduces held-out gain-prediction error relative to $A$-only, zero-gain, and reliability-only baselines. With the same pre-pool information, it is competitive with flexible proxy-conditional calibration, and a LoRA-DPO experiment provides a modest bridge to actual optimization. Transfer experiments across datasets and evaluation slices show that source coefficients are useful priors, but target calibration further improves prediction. The resulting diagnostic is local and one-sided: it characterizes gentle optimization along the positive tilt branch of a fixed item pool.

</details>


### [156] [Confident Fools or Careful Searchers? A Behavioural Study of When LLMs Search Beyond Their Static Internal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41124)
*Nipun D. Pathirage,Kwan Srijomkwan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出400题基准（IK+OK多选题），用NAS/KIS两个动作级分数审计五个商用LLM是否在应搜时搜索、不应搜时克制，发现产品间差异极大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多测量语言化置信度或训练新的自适应检索方法，很少审计已部署产品实际的"搜索/不搜索"决策；而不必要搜索浪费延迟与成本，漏搜则产生自信却无依据的答案。

Method: 构建400道多选题基准：混合应直接作答的常识（IK）题与应触发搜索（无工具时应弃答）的训练截止后新闻事实（OK）题；每题设弃答选项；定义NAS与KIS两项动作级评分，分别奖励对OK题搜索、对IK题克制；在五个商用LLM及其厂商web search开关下评测。

Result: GPT-5 mini与Grok 4 Fast在>90%的OK题上搜索，仅约1%的IK题搜索；Mistral Small仅在20%的OK题上搜索，常弃答而非调用工具；一个产品的搜索指示器不可用；无显式时间线索的OK题上，两个选择性模型NAS从约0.72降至0.57–0.58。

Conclusion: 该评分衡量的是具体产品的搜索触发行为，而非严格意义上的元认知；搜索部分由显式时间线索驱动。

Abstract: Agentic LLM assistants must repeatedly decide whether to answer from memory or to call a tool such as web search. Unnecessary searches cost latency and money; missing ones yield confident but unsupported answers. Prior work mostly measures verbalised confidence or trains new adaptive-retrieval methods, and rarely audits the search decisions that deployed products actually make. We introduce a benchmark of 400 multiple-choice questions that combines widely known inside-knowledge (IK) questions, which should be answered directly, with outside-knowledge (OK) questions about facts from news published after the models' training cut-offs (to our knowledge), which should trigger search or, without tools, abstention. Each item offers an abstain option, and two action-level scores, NAS and KIS, reward searching on OK questions and restraint on IK questions. Across five commercial LLMs with and without their vendors' web search, GPT-5 mini and Grok 4 Fast search on over 90% of OK but only 1% of IK questions, whereas Mistral Small searches on just 20% of OK questions, often abstaining instead of using its tool; one product's search indicator proved unusable. Searching is partly triggered by explicit temporal cues: on uncued OK questions, the NAS of the two selective models falls from about 0.72 to 0.57--0.58. The scores thus measure the search triggering of specific products, not metacognition in a strict sense.

</details>


### [157] [When Should AI Agents Ask? Clarification Requests Under Limited Human Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41129)
*Hanzhao Wang,Shengjie Liu,Zhengchao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究单个有限注意力人类监督多个并行AI智能体时的信息获取决策协调问题，提出成对协调策略（PCP），在理论上给出最优性保证，并在合成基准与LLM案例中验证其显著降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体越来越多地并行执行委派任务，且由一人同时监督多个智能体。实际中智能体常缺少任务所需信息（未明说的偏好、约束或规格），可选择向人类澄清或自行检索（耗时费力且可能失败）。当多个智能体共享同一注意力有限的人类时，各自独立的"何时提问"决策会相互干扰，需要协调机制。

Method: 构建风格化模型：每个智能体可选择搜索、询问人类、或继续执行不依赖缺失信息的任务部分，并将问题形式化为动态规划。分析表明是否提问不仅取决于自身不确定性（有其它工作的智能体更宜早问），且问题回答顺序依赖所有待答问题，故逐智能体的排序不可能最优。据此提出Pairwise Coordination Policy（PCP），利用精确求解的一任务与两任务子问题来估计任务对在共享人类时的相互延迟。

Result: 理论上，当至多剩两个未解决需求时PCP精确最优；当所有未解决需求必须由人类解决时，PCP成本不超过最优的2倍。实验中，在864个合成案例上PCP平均与最优成本差距仅0.07%，相对三种标准提问规则平均降低成本18–31%；在LLM智能体案例研究中，智能体全部正确完成任务，且PCP在所有测试设置下平均成本最低。

Conclusion: 共享有限注意力人类的多智能体信息获取需要一个整体协调的提问排序策略；PCP以简单的成对近似实现了接近最优的性能并具备可证明的近似保证，是协调多智能体"搜索/询问/继续"决策的有效方案。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly carry out delegated tasks in parallel, with one person overseeing several of them at once. In practice, an agent often lacks information its task requires, such as an unstated preference, constraint, or specification. It can ask the human to clarify, or it can search for the information itself, for example in past records, which takes time and effort and may fail. In this paper, we study how to coordinate these decisions across several agents that share one human with limited attention. We build a stylized model in which each agent can search, ask, or continue the parts of its task that do not depend on the missing information, and we formulate the problem as a dynamic program. We first show that whether an agent should ask depends on more than its own uncertainty: an agent with other work to do can be better off asking earlier, and which question the human should answer first depends on all waiting questions, so no ranking computed agent by agent is optimal. We then propose the Pairwise Coordination Policy (PCP), which uses exact one- and two-task problems to estimate how pairs of tasks delay each other when they share the human. Theoretically, PCP is exactly optimal when at most two requirements remain unresolved and costs at most twice the optimum when every unresolved requirement must be resolved by the human. We evaluate PCP on 864 synthetic cases and in a case study with LLM agents. On the synthetic benchmark, PCP's mean gap to optimal cost is 0.07%, and it reduces cost by an average of 18-31% relative to three standard rules for when to ask. In the LLM case study, agents completed every task correctly, and PCP achieved the lowest mean cost in all tested settings.

</details>


### [158] [Counterfactual Pairwise Augmentation Improves Small Pointwise LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41149)
*Mohammad Zangooei,Peyvand Teymoori,Mohammad Robati Shirzad,Soroosh Shahtalebi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CPA（反事实成对增强），为逐点评判的每个样本合成并审核反事实回答，构造同源最小差异的成对监督，联合训练后仅在逐点模式部署；在Qwen3-8B LoRA上六项二值准则平均提升2.5个macro-F1点，并在所有合并权重下优于模型合并，领先最强零样本前沿评判模型11.8点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型适合作为智能体流程中逐步把关的逐点评判器，但逐点标签在微调中欠定决策边界：不同查询的回答在多维特征上同时变化，无法揭示是哪些特征决定标签。

Method: CPA为每个逐点样本合成并审核一个同查询、意图携带相反标签的反事实回答，将接受的样本对表述为"哪个回答满足准则"的成对任务；评判器在逐点与成对数据上联合微调，但部署时只使用逐点模式。作者给出二阶分析，说明联合训练相较"分别训练后合并专家"的优势：合并在所有方向上按同一比例移向成对专家，而联合训练可在成对损失平坦处保持逐点最优、在其陡峭处吸收其曲率。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B LoRA适配器、六项二值准则上，CPA相对纯逐点训练将准则平均逐点macro-F1提升2.5点；在所有测试的合并权重下，于全部六项准则上优于模型合并；并比所评估的最强零样本前沿评判模型Gemini 3.6 Flash高出11.8点。

Conclusion: 显式化"决定标签的差异"是训练小型逐点评判器的有效途径；同源、反事实最小的成对视图所诱导的损失几何，正是联合训练优于专家合并的条件，且该收益可完全通过逐点部署获得。

Abstract: Agentic systems increasingly gate individual steps with a pointwise judge that scores a response against a criterion. Small language models are attractive for this role because they can be served and specialized efficiently. Such a judge must locate the criterion's decision boundary, but pointwise labels under-determine that boundary in fine-tuning: labeled responses to different queries differ along many dimensions at once, so they do not reveal which features decide the label. We introduce CPA (Counterfactual Pairwise Augmentation), which makes the deciding difference explicit in training. For each pointwise item, CPA synthesizes and audits a counterfactual response to the same query intended to carry the opposite label. It frames the accepted pair as a pairwise task asking which of the two responses satisfies the criterion. The judge is fine-tuned jointly on both pointwise and pairwise datasets and deployed in pointwise mode only. A second-order analysis shows when this should outperform the prevailing strategy of merging separately trained pointwise and pairwise experts: merging moves every parameter direction toward the pairwise expert by the same fraction, whereas joint training can hold the pointwise optimum wherever the pairwise loss is flat and absorb its curvature where it is sharp. This is the regime that CPA's same-source, counterfactually minimal pairwise view is designed to produce. On separate Qwen3-8B LoRA adapters for six binary criteria, CPA improves criterion-averaged pointwise macro-F1 by 2.5 points over pointwise-only training and outperforms model merging on all six criteria at every tested merge weight. It also surpasses the strongest zero-shot frontier judge we evaluate, Gemini 3.6 Flash, by 11.8 points.

</details>


### [159] [Lifelong Learning of Scheduling Rules with Surrogate-assisted Genetic Programming for Dynamic Flexible Job Shop Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41158)
*Luyao Zhu,Fangfang Zhang,Yi Mei,Ke Chen,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种代理辅助的遗传规划（GP）终身学习方法，用于处理作业或批次到达的序列异构动态柔性作业车间调度（DFJSS）任务，在缓解灾难性遗忘的同时保持对新任务的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GP训练的调度规则仅在相似规模与分布的任务上泛化良好；任务特征改变时性能显著下降，而微调预训练规则又会导致灾难性遗忘。DFJSS中的GP终身学习研究尚不充分，故需能兼顾新任务适应与历史知识保留的方法。

Method: 提出代理辅助的GP终身学习框架，含三个关键组件：(1) 自适应两阶段训练策略，平衡可塑性与稳定性；(2) 基于任务相似性的加权机制，调控知识复用；(3) 带距离约束的改进代理模型，提升估计精度。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法有效缓解灾难性遗忘并保持较强适应性，优于多个对比方法，展现了终身GP在动态调度环境中的潜力。

Conclusion: 终身学习型GP可有效应对序列异构DFJSS任务，实现稳定性与可塑性的平衡，为动态调度中的知识迁移与持续学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Genetic programming (GP) has been widely used to learn scheduling rules for dynamic flexible job shop scheduling (DFJSS). However, trained rules tend to only generalise to tasks of similar scales and distributions, and when task characteristics change, learned rules often suffer significant performance degradation. Moreover, fine-tuning pre-trained rules on new tasks often leads to catastrophic forgetting, where prior knowledge is lost. This motivates the need for GP methods that can adapt to new tasks while preserving past knowledge, leading to a lifelong learning paradigm. However, lifelong learning in GP for DFJSS remains largely under-explored. To address this gap, this paper proposes a surrogate-assisted GP method for lifelong learning to handle sequential heterogeneous DFJSS tasks with job or batch arrivals. The framework includes three key components: an adaptive two-stage training strategy to balance plasticity and stability, a task-similarity-based weighting mechanism to regulate knowledge reuse, and an improved surrogate model with distance constraints to enhance estimation accuracy. Experimental results show that the proposed method effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting while maintaining strong adaptability, outperforming several compared methods and demonstrating the potential of lifelong GP in dynamic scheduling environments.

</details>


### [160] [FrontierMat: Benchmarking Language Models at the Frontier of Materials R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41162)
*Feng Chen,Jianguo Huang,Yushan Xiao,Yue Li,Bijun Tang,Xuyu Dong,Xingguo Wang,Feiyang Xie,Jianhui Gu,Dongfeng Cao,Hongda Chen,Yongji Gong,Ruiqi Li,Shuxin Li,Xingji Li,Ruitao Lv,Li Song,Lei Wang,Ye Zhang,Xiaoxu Zhao,Xitao Zheng,Bo An,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: To accelerate materials discovery, language models must be able to address problems at the research frontier. Yet textbook- and examination-derived benchmarks primarily assess established knowledge, leaving performance on frontier research problems largely untested. We introduce FrontierMat, a benchmark of 330 problems drawn from frontier academic and industrial research in fields including aerospace, semiconductors, embodied intelligence and nuclear fusion. Every problem is validated by leading experimentalists and industry experts in its field. Each problem presents a real study and asks the model to infer a withheld outcome or draw a conclusion from selected experimental evidence. Problems are annotated by functional domain, material system and the scale of the governing mechanism; 130 require cross-scale reasoning. The strongest general-purpose model scores only 53.7%, and on matched problems, cross-scale problems score 8.7 points below single-scale ones. Half of the problems are solved by at most one model, and models fail chiefly by endorsing what is plausible rather than what was observed. A domain-adaptation study with meta-weighted continued pretraining and scale-modular self-distillation raises an open-weight model from 36.7% to 55.4%, matching the strongest proprietary model, with improvements on public materials benchmarks and no decline in aggregate performance on general benchmarks. FrontierMat provides a testbed for measuring progress toward language models that support frontier materials R&D.

</details>


### [161] [LLM Detoxification via Transition-Aware Reward-Guided Representation Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41165)
*Aishan Liu,Yisong Xiao,Xianglong Liu,Yongxin Huang,Siyuan Liang,Zonghao Ying,Yang Qu,Jian Yang,Yaodong Yang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Transition-Aware Reward-Guided Representation Sampling（TARGRS）测试时去毒框架，在隐表示空间学习毒性转移的奖励引导，通过由粗到细的采样实现去毒，显著降低多种LLM与LVLM的毒性输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示引导（representation steering）去毒方法多采用静态或粗粒度干预，未能充分建模有毒与无毒生成之间的状态转移，导致引导不精确。

Method: 探索阶段沿毒性方向渐进引导，构造由无毒到有毒的表示轨迹，利用轨迹上的毒性分数将稀疏标注转为稠密的listwise监督，训练转移感知的token级奖励模型；推理阶段用该模型沿相反（无毒）方向进行由粗到细采样：粗采样确定到达无毒区域所需干预，细化阶段以最小扰动选出净化表示。

Result: 在RealToxicityPrompts、BOLD、AttaQ上对10个LLM实验，一致优于SOTA基线，RealToxicityPrompts毒性最高降低69.38%；速度接近原LLM且基本保持核心能力；在语言解码器空间干预使其可扩展至LVLM，3个代表性LVLM毒性最高降低64.23%。

Conclusion: 以转移感知的奖励引导替代静态粗粒度干预，可精确控制表示、实现高效去毒，并能迁移至视觉语言模型。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate toxic content, motivating effective test-time detoxification. Existing representation steering methods often rely on static or coarse interventions and provide imprecise guidance because they inadequately model transitions between toxic and non-toxic generations. We propose Transition-Aware Reward-Guided Representation Sampling (TARGRS), a test-time framework that learns reward guidance over toxicity transitions in latent representation space. During exploration, progressive steering along the toxicity direction constructs trajectories from non-toxic to toxic states. Toxicity scores along these trajectories turn sparse annotations into dense, listwise supervision for a transition-aware token-level reward model. During inference, TARGRS uses this model to guide coarse-to-fine sampling along the opposite, non-toxic direction: coarse sampling identifies the intervention needed to reach non-toxic regions, and refinement selects a detoxified representation with minimal perturbation. Experiments on 10 LLMs across RealToxicityPrompts, BOLD, and AttaQ show that TARGRS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing toxicity by up to 69.38% on RealToxicityPrompts. TARGRS runs nearly as fast as the original LLM while preserving its core capabilities with minimal impact. By intervening in the language decoder space, TARGRS also extends to Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), reducing toxicity by up to 64.23% across 3 representative LVLMs.

</details>


### [162] [ImplicitHop: Evaluating knowledge integration across context and memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41169)
*Alexander Detkov,Dashiel Karish,Matthew Oleynikov,Matt Thomson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出两个自然语言基准（形式图基准与真实知识图的知识组合基准），系统比较上下文信息与预训练权重中信息在多步演绎推理中的整合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM需要不仅能存储局部事实，还要把信息的远程蕴含全局传播开。但目前缺乏对"信息整合/推理深度"的可控大规模评估，也不清楚上下文信息与权重编码信息在整合能力上是否存在差异。

Method: 构建两个互补基准：一是形式图基准，可控制图拓扑与整合任务类型；二是基于真实世界知识图的知识组合基准。在此基础上，对一组开放模型进行隐式评估（不使用思维链），以推理深度为自变量，比较上下文编码知识与预训练权重编码知识在演绎推理中的表现。

Result: 随组合长度（推理深度）增加，两类知识来源的准确率均急剧下降；但上下文呈现的信息显著优于权重中编码信息的演绎表现。

Conclusion: 量化了当前模型的根本性局限，对基于提示的智能体行为控制和持续学习策略有直接影响，并为支持更深推理的训练、提示与整合方法奠定基础。

Abstract: The ability to integrate new information and deduce its consequences is central to learning, understanding, and reasoning in large language models (LLMs). Whether information arrives during training or in-context, models must not only store local facts, but propagate their long-range implications globally. To explore information integration in LLMs, we introduce two complementary natural language benchmarks for large-scale evaluation of deductive reasoning as a function of inference depth. The first is a formal graph benchmark enabling controlled evaluation over a wide range of graph topologies and integration tasks; the second is a knowledge composition benchmark grounded in real-world knowledge graphs. We use these benchmarks to compare knowledge integration from information encoded in context versus information encoded in model weights during pretraining, evaluating a set of open models implicitly, without chain of thought. We find that accuracy decays sharply with increased composition length for both pretrained and in-context knowledge. Information presented in-context, however, substantially outperforms deduction over information encoded in weights. These results quantify a fundamental limitation of current models with direct implications for prompt-based control of agent behavior and for continual learning strategies, and they provide a foundation for developing training, prompting, and integration methods that support deeper reasoning.

</details>


### [163] [Researcher-in-the-Loop Evolutionary Discovery of Entanglement-Assisted Quantum Code Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41183)
*Jae-Hyun Baek,Byung-Sun Won,Jon-Lark Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出研究者参与（researcher-in-the-loop）的LLM引导程序演化发现流程，融合程序演化、精确求值、数学推广、可认证非存在性检验与形式化证明检查，用于发现EAQECC码族并产出可独立核验的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM引导的程序演化虽能产出数学构造，但其有效性、普适性与可复现性需要彼此独立的证据支撑；同时在纠缠辅助量子纠错码（EAQECC）领域，缺乏将有限构造推广为参数族并给出形式化、可验证证据的系统方法。

Method: 构建研究者参与闭环的发现流水线：程序演化生成有限量子比特构造 → 精确求值 → 研究者将构造结构推广为家族公式 → Lean 检验代数证书 → DRAT 证明检验非存在性结果 → 结合纠缠提升（entanglement lifting）补全归档表格空缺；并保留搜索程序以支持精确检查与固定种子重放。采用八组配对生成块的对照实验评估泛化能力。

Result: 演化程序从未给定家族公式的任务出发，得到可推广为族 $[[n,1,n-1;n-q-1]]_q$（素数幂 $q\ge3$、$n\ge2q+1$，及 $q=2$、奇数 $n\ge5$）的构造，Lean 验证其代数证书；结合纠缠提升，该族填补归档EAQECC表54个空缺。发布证据含115条已核验码记录、10项带DRAT证明的非存在性结果、族实现及可重放的搜索程序。对照实验中，验证集筛选的迭代程序在未见长度 $n=13,15$ 的留出执行成功率分别为46.1%与31.6%，独立生成程序为19.5%与3.1%。

Conclusion: 在该设定下，程序演化与研究者分析相结合，可将有限计算发现转化为具有可独立核验证据的已证明码族，并显著提升对未见规模的泛化成功率。

Abstract: LLM-guided program evolution can produce mathematical constructions, but validity, generality, and reproducibility require distinct evidence. We develop a researcher-in-the-loop discovery pipeline that combines program evolution, exact evaluation, mathematical generalization, certified nonexistence checks, and formal proof checking. We apply it to entanglement-assisted quantum error-correcting codes (EAQECCs). Starting from tasks that did not specify a family formula, evolved programs produced finite qubit constructions whose structure researchers generalized to the family $[[n,1,n-1;n-q-1]]_q$ for prime powers $q\ge3$ at $n\ge2q+1$ and for $q=2$ at odd $n\ge5$. Lean checks the algebraic certificate underlying this family. Together with entanglement lifting, the family closes 54 gaps in archived EAQECC tables. The released evidence includes 115 verified code records, ten nonexistence results with checked DRAT proofs, family implementations, and preserved search programs for exact checks and seeded replay. In a controlled cohort of eight paired generation blocks, validation-selected iterative programs achieve success in 46.1% and 31.6% of held-out executions at unseen lengths $n=13,15$, compared with 19.5% and 3.1% for independently generated programs. In this setting, program evolution and researcher analysis turn finite computational discoveries into a proved family with independently checkable evidence.

</details>


### [164] [RTL-Interact: Training EDA-Interactive Models for Multi-Task RTL Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41225)
*Wenxuan Shi,Yaoyu Zhu,Di Huang,Chongxiao Li,Xiaoyun Zhang,Muxin Song,Yutong Wu,Ziyuan Nan,Pengwei Jin,Tian Zhi,Xishan Zhang,Rui Zhang,Xing Hu,Zidong Du,Qi Guo,Yunji Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RTL-Interact多任务后训练框架，训练单一RTL专用模型完成RTL生成、EDA检查构建与基于工具反馈的修正，并引入Pattern-Aware Policy Optimization (PAPO)多任务强化学习；32B模型在CVDP上达48.7%加权Pass@1、68.9% Pass@5，超过教师模型DeepSeek-V3.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用于RTL设计虽具前景，但可靠实现需依赖EDA工具反馈进行检验与修正。现有RTL专用模型普遍缺乏与EDA工具的学习式交互，或依赖分离的生成与修复模块，导致流程割裂、效率与可靠性不足。

Method: 1) 构建自动化多任务演示数据；2) 采用从思维链到工具集成推理的分阶段监督，训练单一模型同时具备RTL生成、任务适配EDA检查构建与工具反馈驱动修正能力；3) 提出PAPO多任务强化学习，在GRPO基础上加入正确性门控奖励（偏好简洁且成功的检查、奖励有效修正），并用任务分组保持各目标覆盖度。

Result: CVDP上RTL-Interact-32B取得48.7%加权Pass@1与68.9% Pass@5，超过教师模型DeepSeek-V3.2；RTL-Interact-8B取得44.9%加权Pass@1。PAPO在提升Pass@1的同时将平均工具调用次数减少63.8%。两模型在VerilogEval v2与RTLLM v2上均优于全部RTL专用基线。

Conclusion: 将生成、检查构建与修正统一到单一RTL专用模型中，并结合分阶段监督与多任务强化学习，可有效提升RTL设计的正确性与工具使用效率，验证了该框架与PAPO方法的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models offer a promising route to register-transfer-level (RTL) design, but reliable implementations require checking and revision based on electronic design automation (EDA) tools' feedback. Existing RTL-specialized models often lack learned interaction with such EDA tools or rely on separate generation and repair components. We introduce RTL-Interact, a multi-task post-training framework that trains a single RTL-specialized model to generate RTL, construct task-appropriate EDA checks, and revise implementations using tool feedback. The framework combines automatically constructed multi-task demonstrations with staged supervision from chain-of-thought to tool-integrated reasoning. We further introduce Pattern-Aware Policy Optimization (PAPO) for multi-task reinforcement learning. PAPO augments Group Relative Policy Optimization with correctness-gated rewards that favor concise successful checking while rewarding effective revision, and uses task grouping to preserve coverage across objectives. On CVDP, RTL-Interact-32B achieves 48.7% weighted Pass@1 and 68.9% Pass@5, surpassing its teacher model, DeepSeek-V3.2, while RTL-Interact-8B achieves 44.9% weighted Pass@1. Additionally, we show that PAPO can reduce average tool calls by 63.8% while improving Pass@1. Both models also outperform all RTL-specialized baselines on VerilogEval v2 and RTLLM v2, demonstrating the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [165] [TRIDENT and SHAKTI: A Framework and Benchmark for Hindi AI-Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41232)
*Shifali Agrahari,Sujit Kumar,Sanasam Ranbir Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出印地语AI生成文本检测基准SHAKTI，并基于回译语义一致性提出TRIDENT检测框架（S/D/C三变体），TRIDENT-S整体性能最佳，且在多语言、对抗与未见模型设置下具备鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多语言化使区分人类写作与AI生成文本对信息完整性至关重要，但相关研究以英语为中心，缺乏印地语等低资源语言的基准，因此需要构建印地语检测基准并提出有效方法。

Method: 构建SHAKTI基准：覆盖4种文体、8个LLM家族、多种提示策略及对抗变换，并通过人工标注评估质量、分析人机文本在语言/语法/情感层面的差异。基于发现提出TRIDENT——基于往返翻译（RTT）的检测框架，利用原文与回译文本的语义一致性，包含静态阈值（S）、动态阈值（D）与基于多距离RTT特征的逻辑回归（C）三种变体。

Result: TRIDENT优于传统与监督基线，其中TRIDENT-S综合表现最佳；在7种语言、对抗设置及未见LLM上验证了多语言适用性与鲁棒性，同时揭示其对翻译模型的敏感性。

Conclusion: SHAKTI填补了印地语AI文本检测基准空白，TRIDENT以回译语义一致性为信号实现强检测性能，具有跨语言与对抗鲁棒性，但性能受翻译模型质量制约。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) become increasingly multilingual, distinguishing human-written from AI-generated text is essential for maintaining information integrity. However, research remains English-centric, with limited benchmarks for low-resource languages such as Hindi. To address this gap, we introduce SHAKTI, a large-scale Hindi benchmark covering four genres, eight LLM families, multiple prompting strategies, and adversarial transformations. We evaluate its quality through human annotation and analyze linguistic, grammatical, and sentiment-based differences between human- and AI-generated texts. Building on these findings, we propose TRIDENT, a round-trip translation-based detection framework with three variants: TRIDENT-S (static threshold), TRIDENT-D (dynamic threshold), and TRIDENT-C (logistic regression using multi-distance RTT features). TRIDENT leverages semantic consistency between original and round-trip translated texts to identify AI-generated content. Extensive experiments show that TRIDENT outperforms conventional and supervised baselines, with TRIDENT-S achieving the best overall performance. Further evaluations across seven languages, adversarial settings, and unseen LLMs demonstrate its multilingual applicability and robustness, while highlighting sensitivity to the translation model.

</details>


### [166] [Experts Exhibit Structured Redundancy: Structure-Preserving Expert Pruning for Mixture-of-Experts Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41236)
*Tian Wang,Yongding Tao,Yihong Dong,Xiaoming Wu,Ying Guo,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文发现MoE层专家输出存在结构化冗余，将专家剪枝重构为结构保持的专家子集选择问题，并提出PEEP与DCEP两种贪心剪枝方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE虽改善容量与推理效率的权衡，但部署时仍需存储全部专家，带来显著内存开销；现有剪枝方法仅独立评分排序单个专家或穷举专家组合，未利用专家间的结构关系。

Method: 观察到跨模型、跨任务下专家输出的稳定秩与熵有效秩远低于名义专家数、有效模态更少，说明存在结构化冗余。据此将专家剪枝形式化为结构保持的专家子集选择问题，用贪心策略高效求解。提出PEEP（最大化加权投影能量以保持功能子空间）与DCEP（最小化加权覆盖误差以保持分布几何），两者均以路由器权重作为先验，兼顾全局结构关系与局部专家重要性。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V2-Lite与Qwen3-30B-A3B上，于75%和50%专家保留率下，方法在多数基准上优于SOTA基线，并在通用任务与数学推理任务上均取得最佳平均性能。

Conclusion: MoE专家输出具有可利用的结构化冗余，基于结构保持的子集选择可高效实现专家剪枝，在显著压缩专家数量的同时保持甚至优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Sparse MoE mechanism significantly improves the trade-off between model capacity and inference efficiency. However, practical deployment still requires storing all experts, resulting in substantial memory overhead. We show that expert outputs in MoE layers exhibit structured redundancy. Across different models and task categories, the stable rank and entropy effective rank of expert outputs are consistently far below the nominal number of experts, while expert output distributions also exhibit fewer effective modes. Motivated by these observations, we reformulate expert pruning as a structure-preserving expert subset selection problem and efficiently optimize it via a greedy strategy, rather than only independently scoring and ranking individual experts or exhaustively searching over expert combinations. We propose two expert pruning methods under the unified structure-preserving framework: Projection Energy Expert Pruning (PEEP), which preserves the functional subspace by maximizing weighted projection energy, and Distributional Coverage Expert Pruning (DCEP), which preserves the distributional geometry by minimizing weighted coverage error. Both methods incorporate router-derived weights as priors, enabling them to jointly preserve the global structural relationships among experts while accounting for the local importance of individual experts. Experiments on DeepSeek-V2-Lite and Qwen3-30B-A3B demonstrate that, at the expert retention ratios of 75% and 50%, our methods outperform the state-of-the-art baselines on the majority of benchmarks and consistently achieve the best average performance across both general tasks and mathematical reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [167] [MEMIG: Self-evolving Memory Construction for Antibody Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41245)
*Yunhui Jang,Zach Wang,Jai Doshi,Kangway Chuang,Sidney Lisanza,Taylor Joren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向生物分子优化的智能体记忆框架 MEMIG，将经验表示为可索引、定量、结构化的记录，以局部更新与精确检索替代自然语言重写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自演化 LLM 智能体将经验表示为自由文本并通过重写维护，适用于可叙述型任务；但在科学发现与优化中，经验是特定上下文下由 oracle 测得的量，文本重写缺乏索引、以描述替代测量、且无法表达干预间的交互效应。

Method: MEMIG 为每条 insight 分配领域接地的标识符，并累积属性 oracle 测得的定量效应，使更新局部化、检索变为精确查找；同时对共同施加的干预进行连接，通过分解每个被评估集合来量化其上位性（epistasis）。

Result: 在多项 in silico 抗体优化任务中，MEMIG 在大多数设置下提升了性能。

Conclusion: 生物分子优化的智能体记忆应具备可索引性、定量性与结构化三项性质，MEMIG 通过标识符锚定、定量效应累积与上位性建模实现了这三点，并在抗体优化任务中验证有效。

Abstract: Memory has become central to self-evolving LLM agents, which store and retrieve experience from past rollouts to guide future decisions. Existing systems represent this experience as free-form natural language and maintain it by rewriting, which fits tasks whose experience is narratable. However, in scientific discovery and optimization, experience is often a quantity measured by an oracle under a specific context. Rewriting it as text leaves no index for where a record should be, replaces a measurement with a description, and cannot express how interventions interact. To address this, we argue that agent memory for biomolecule optimization should instead be indexable, quantitative, and structured, and we introduce MEMIG. This memory construction framework realizes these three properties. In detail, MEMIG addresses each insight with a domain-grounded identifier and accumulates the quantitative effect measured by a property oracle, so that updates remain local and retrieval becomes an exact lookup. Next, MEMIG connects insights whose interventions are applied together and measures their epistasis by decomposing each evaluated set. Our results show that MEMIG improves performance in most settings across in silico antibody optimization tasks.

</details>


### [168] [Rome Wasn't Built in One Turn: Replay-Grounded Reward Signals for Long-Horizon Freeciv Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41264)
*Yanchen Liu,Xinyu Che,Brihi Joshi,Renzo Fukuda,Junhua Liu,Ruifeng Li,Yunfei Ge,Hang Yan,Xiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出策略游戏中"记分板陷阱"现象（高即时分数未必导向更优结局），构建可分支回放的CivHarness系统，训练LLM裁判CivTelescope预测局面优劣，并以其作为密集奖励训练Freeciv智能体（CivMarshmallow），在中期阶段与全程混合奖励下均取得最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程策略游戏训练LLM智能体困难：确定性反馈稀疏且往往只在终局出现；游戏内即时分数虽密集但不可靠——在8款策略游戏中，8–68%的局面配对里高分局面反而导致更差结局，即"记分板陷阱"。因此需要更可靠的奖励信号。

Method: 1) 构建CivHarness开源系统：保存游戏状态、对不同决策分支、回放至未来回合，从存档局面提供可度量的长时程反馈；2) 用其训练CivTelescope——一个预测两局面中哪个结局更优的LLM裁判（基于CivHarness生成的标签）；3) 以CivTelescope为密集奖励训练Freeciv游戏智能体，形成CivMarshmallow配方，并设计从记分板/终局奖励逐步过渡到CivTelescope的混合奖励。

Result: 在记分板陷阱配对上，CivTelescope准确率为现成语言模型的1.3–2.0倍，并可泛化到新开局、更晚回合与未见规则；在陷阱频发的中期阶段，CivMarshmallow平均训练阶段增益8.94分，比最佳基线（终局奖励）高77%；在完整120回合中，混合奖励在对比奖励中得分最高。

Conclusion: 即时分数与终局稀疏奖励均非理想信号，通过可回放的环境分支获取长时程真值来蒸馏LLM裁判，可作为可靠的密集奖励；随游戏进程从低成本信号向裁判模型过渡的混合奖励策略最优。

Abstract: Training language-model agents to play long-horizon strategy games is hard because deterministic feedback is sparse and often arrives only at the end of a game. In-game scores can offer dense signals, but they do not reliably predict future outcomes: in 8-68% of position pairs across eight strategy games, the higher-scoring position leads to the worse outcome. We term this phenomenon scoreboard traps. In Freeciv, an open-source turn-based empire-building game where several players manage units, cities, and diplomacy over hundreds of turns, we look into better reward signals for gameplay agents. To facilitate this, we build CivHarness, an open system that saves current game states, branches on different decisions, and replays the game to future turns to provide measurable long-horizon feedback from saved positions. We use CivHarness to train CivTelescope, an LLM Judge that predicts which one of two game positions will yield the better outcome. On scoreboard-trap pairs, CivTelescope is 1.3-2.0 times more accurate than off-the-shelf language models, and it generalizes to new game starts, later turns, and unseen rules. We then train a Freeciv gameplay agent with CivTelescope as a dense reward, and call this recipe CivMarshmallow. When used in the middle stage of the game where scoreboard traps are common, CivMarshmallow reaches an average training phase gain of 8.94 points, 77% more than the best baseline, the end-of-game reward. Over the full 120-turn game, CivMarshmallow uses a hybrid reward that starts from the scoreboard or the end-of-game reward and shifts to CivTelescope as the game advances; this hybrid reward scores highest among the rewards we compare.

</details>


### [169] [From 1D Benchmarks to Complex 3D Geometries: Can Agents Reliably Specify Problems for Automated PDE Simulation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41267)
*Akshat Jani,Sakhinana Sagar Srinivas,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"模型抽取+确定性科学检查+显式澄清+局部补丁修复"的工作流，用于将工程请求可靠转化为完整可执行的仿真JSON规范，并在规格正确性、执行完成度和数值验证三个层面分开评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agent 辅助仿真系统虽能自动搭建仿真，但"执行成功"不等于"规格保真"：难以判断生成规范是否保持了原问题意图、是否正确处理了缺失信息。因此需要研究 agent 能否可靠构建完整可执行的 JSON 规格，并对其正确性做独立评估。

Method: 工作流包含四部分：模型抽取（model extraction）、确定性的科学性与能力性检查、显式澄清（clarification）、以及保留无关已接受字段的校验式局部补丁（validated local patches）。评估上区分三项指标：规格正确性、执行完成度、数值验证。另做组件消融与下游多物理/多维度验证。

Result: 在120条模板相关请求上，完整工作流首轮规格与决策正确率98.3%，对比 schema 辅助单次生成92.5%、纯模型82.5%，分别提升5.8与15.8个百分点；在42条需澄清或拒绝的请求中无"误判为就绪"（false-ready）决策。组件消融确认科学检查与条件修复均有贡献。下游覆盖1D/2D/3D、定常/非定常、线性/非线性问题，含复杂三维机翼几何的流动与弹性问题；30例画廊展示基于保留JSON的自动执行；12例真实用户端到端测试中9例实现规格-执行-导出-数值参考的联合成功（75.0%）。

Conclusion: 在测试范围内，该方法支持可追溯、工程师复核的仿真辅助；但新响应回归（fresh-response regressions）暴露的一致性问题是后续改进重点。

Abstract: Turning an engineering request into a simulation requires consistent geometry, physics, boundary conditions, and numerical settings. Recent agent-assisted systems automate simulation setup, but successful execution alone does not establish whether the specification preserves the intended problem or correctly handles missing information. We study whether agents can reliably construct complete executable JSON specifications for automated geometry creation, meshing, PDE solving, and result export. Our workflow combines model extraction with deterministic scientific and capability checks, explicit clarification, and validated local patches that preserve unrelated accepted fields. We evaluate specification correctness, execution completion, and numerical verification separately. On 120 template-related requests, the full workflow achieves 98.3% correct first-turn specification and decision outcomes, compared with 92.5% for schema-assisted single shot and 82.5% for model-only: gains of 5.8 and 15.8 percentage points, respectively. It makes no false-ready decisions among the 42 requests requiring clarification or rejection. Component ablations identify contributions from scientific checks and conditional repair. Separate downstream studies cover 1D, 2D, and 3D steady and unsteady, linear and nonlinear problems, including flow and elasticity with complex three-dimensional wing geometries. A 30-case gallery demonstrates automated execution from retained JSON specifications, while 9 of 12 selected live user-to-results tests achieve joint specification, execution, export, and numerical-reference success (75.0%). These results support traceable, engineer-reviewed simulation assistance within the tested scope, while fresh-response regressions identify consistency as a priority for further improvement.

</details>


### [170] [SciGraphSelect: Auditable Scientific Data Selection through Knowledge--Reasoning Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41280)
*MiHyeon Kim,Ji-Eun Han,Eunkyeong Lee,Jeehyun Lim,Hwijung Ryu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SciGraphSelect，一个以参考文献为锚定、可审计的科学数据选择框架：将领域知识、可复用推理操作与文献引证关系组织为类型化知识-推理图，并用概念覆盖、关系/路径共支持与方向对齐三类信号对候选文档打分，同时给出可追溯的选择证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学数据选择主要依赖相关性或效用分数，只能回答"是否保留"，无法解释"为何保留"，缺乏可解释性与可追溯性，难以支撑科学训练语料的上游构建质量控制。

Method: 1) 构建类型化知识-推理图，节点包含领域特定知识、可复用推理操作及经参考文献佐证的关系；2) 对候选文档从三方面评分：概念覆盖、关系与路径共支持、方向性对齐；3) 证据非对称处理——在参考侧保留丰富关系结构，候选侧采取保守假设，不假定关系蕴含，以避免对文档嵌入过度解读；4) 输出每个选择背后的参考锚定证据。

Result: 框架可为每次数据选择暴露参考锚定的证据链，使科学训练语料的上游构建具备可追溯性，并与下游科学验证形成互补。

Conclusion: SciGraphSelect将数据选择从不可解释的效用打分转变为证据驱动、可审计的过程，为科学语料构建提供可追溯性，并可与下游科学事实核验环节协同。

Abstract: Scientific data selection is typically driven by relevance or utility scores, providing limited insight into why a document is retained. We introduce SciGraphSelect, a reference-grounded framework for auditable scientific data selection. SciGraphSelect organizes domain-specific knowledge, reusable reasoning operations, and reference-attested relations into a typed knowledge--reasoning graph, then scores candidate documents using concept coverage, relation and path co-support, and directional alignment. To avoid over-interpreting document embeddings, rich relational structure is preserved on the reference side while candidate-side evidence is treated conservatively without assuming relation entailment. By exposing the reference-grounded evidence behind each selection, SciGraphSelect brings traceability to the upstream construction of scientific training corpora and complements downstream scientific verification.

</details>


### [171] [SAT-ACT: Learning CDCL Branching from Outcome-Derived Action Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41290)
*Zhenchao Sun,Chongyang Tao,Ping Lu,Tongkai Lu,Jianling Gao,Shuai Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SAT-ACT，一种基于结果的学习框架，用于CDCL分支决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习方法依赖公式静态结构或求解器反馈动态信号，对状态相关的分支效应证据有限，且单一标量指标会偏向特定分支选择。

Method: 设计三种互补的结果比较准则，从同一求解器状态出发的独立分支动作rollout中提取动作偏好，证据不足或冲突时不强制排序；用状态条件网络学习分支优先级，直接引导搜索而无需在线rollout；并引入非支配启发式动作监督。

Result: 在三个SAT族上持续降低CDCL搜索成本，并在SAT Competition基准上改善PAR-2。

Conclusion: 基于结果的偏好学习可有效提升CDCL分支质量。

Abstract: Learning-based approaches have increasingly been explored for SAT solving, with particular attention to improving branching decisions in conflict-driven clause learning (CDCL) solvers. These approaches derive branching guidance either from static structural properties of SAT formulas or from dynamic signals obtained through solver feedback and execution traces. However, these forms of supervision provide limited evidence about state-dependent branching effects, while different outcome measures may favor different branching choices. To address these limitations, we propose SAT-ACT, an outcome-derived learning framework for CDCL branching. We design three complementary outcome comparison criteria to extract action preferences from independent branching action rollouts initiated at the same solver state. The criteria identify preferences supported by the outcomes and leave comparisons unresolved when the evidence is insufficient or conflicting, instead of forcing an ordering using a predefined scalar metric. A state-conditioned network learns branching priorities from the extracted preferences and uses them to guide CDCL search directly without online action rollouts. We further introduce non-dominated heuristic action supervision, which retains the solver-selected action as a training target only when it remains non-dominated under these preferences. Solver-level experiments show that SAT-ACT consistently reduces CDCL search costs on three SAT families and improves PAR-2 on the SAT Competition benchmarks.

</details>


### [172] [Self-Improvement with Auxiliary Rewards: Learning from Verified Traces and Evaluating Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41296)
*Minju Gwak,Alan Ritter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究在有限监督下,利用辅助轨迹偏好(置信度SC与熵类UID-Conj.)改进小模型自我提升,UID-Conj.在线较TTRL提升2.08个百分点,离线加权SFT较均匀加权提升1.93点(95%CI [0.44,3.44])。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 答案级奖励无法区分结果同样正确的多条推理轨迹,需要引入轨迹层面的辅助偏好信号来利用同等正确轨迹中的学习价值。

Method: 对比基于置信度的SC与基于熵的UID-Conj.(融合全局熵变化与局部平滑性);在线侧在30道无参考答案的AIME题上对Qwen3-1.7B做奖励增强适配,离线侧在历史AIME题上对正确回答进行加权训练。

Result: 在线:UID-Conj.在适配基准及三个迁移基准上的宏平均较未修改的TTRL提升2.08个百分点;离线:同一回答池上UID-Conj.加权较均匀加权提升1.93点,配对prompt-bootstrap 95%CI [0.44,3.44]。收益具有任务依赖性:SC偏向评判轨迹质量与指令遵循,UID-Conj.在多个设置下训练效果更强。

Conclusion: 辅助偏好能够揭示推理时无法体现的、同等正确轨迹间的学习价值,因此应通过下游增益而非推理期指标来评估此类信号。

Abstract: Answer-level rewards cannot distinguish reasoning traces with equally correct outcomes. We study whether auxiliary trajectory preferences improve small-language-model self-improvement and transfer under limited supervision. We compare confidence-based (SC) and entropy-based metrics (UID-Conj.), which combines global entropy variation and local smoothness, through online reward augmentation and offline training on correct responses. Online experiments adapt Qwen3-1.7B on 30 AIME problems without reference answers; offline experiments use historical AIME problems. UID-Conj. improves the online macro-average across the adaptation benchmark and three transfer benchmarks by 2.08 percentage points over unmodified TTRL. The strongest evidence comes from offline weighted SFT: UID-Conj. exceeds uniform weighting on the same response pool by 1.93 points, with a paired prompt-bootstrap 95\% CI of [0.44, 3.44]. Benefits are task-dependent: SC rewards judged trace quality and instruction following, while UID-Conj. rewards yield stronger training outcomes in several settings. We find that auxiliary preferences can expose learning value among equally correct traces not captured during inference time, motivating their evaluation through downstream gains.

</details>


### [173] [Subliminal Reinforcement Learning: traits travel through a judge's scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41301)
*Keuntae Kim,Yong Suk Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR:  subliminal learning可通过仅含无意义字符串与单一LLM评审（judge）评分的GRPO后训练实现：评审对某动物偏好的评分差足以把该偏好迁移到学生模型，且迁移由"评审分数显影—偏好可移动字符串—累积—抵达答案"四阶段链决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明学生模型在教师文本上训练时会发生"潜意识学习"（数据中无该特质的语义痕迹却迁移了特质）。本文将问题推进到后训练（post-training）场景，追问：仅靠学生自身输出加单一语言模型评审的GRPO，是否也足以传递特质，其机制与边界是什么。

Method: 学生模型生成虚构音节或数字串，由被设定偏好某一动物的LLM评审打分，用GRPO后训练；以评审偏好另一动物的平行实验为对照。设计48个cell并设有效性门控，采用5% FDR判定特质是否确立。进一步做消融（仅移除两位评审之间的奖励差异）与跨评审一致性检验（同偏好但由其他数据构建的评审、换用其他模型），并提出四阶段因果链解释。

Result: 48个cell中2个未通过有效性门控，17个在5% FDR下确立特质，含仅数字的cell；学生偏好相对对照显著向评审偏好动物移动。仅去除评审间奖励差即消除迁移。同一偏好但由不同数据构建或置于另一模型上的评审会把偏好落在其他字符串上，此时检测不到迁移。

Conclusion: 仅凭自身输出与单一评审评分的GRPO足以产生潜意识学习，机制为四阶段链：特质须体现在评审分数中→偏好使特定字符串移动学生→累积→抵达答案；该链可外推到AI安全相关倾向，训练前测得的各阶段可预测风险落点，有助于部分预防该风险。

Abstract: Subliminal learning, the transfer of a model's trait through data with no semantic trace of it, has been shown for students trained on a teacher's text. Post-training relies on group relative policy optimization with a language-model judge, where the student learns only from its own outputs and one judge's scores. We show this suffices: a student writes invented syllables or numbers, a judge made to prefer one animal scores them, and the student's preference moves toward that animal relative to a run whose judge prefers another.
