Of 48 design cells, two fail the validity gate and 17 establish the trait at 5% false-discovery rate, numbers-only cells included; elsewhere, our account gives the reason. Removing only the reward difference between judges removes transfer. We establish the principle as a chain of four stages: the trait must show in the judge's scores, favour strings that move the student, accumulate and reach the answers. Judges with the same preference built from other data, or on another model, place it on other strings, and transfer is not detected. Carried unchanged to AI-safety dispositions, the chain holds: undesirable dispositions reach the student's answers, and stages measured before training anticipate where, helping to prevent part of this risk.

</details>


### [174] [SERIAL-7: Deriving, Realizing, and Preserving Machine-Verifiable Outputs Across Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41308)
*Youngkyung Seo,MiHyeon Kim,Jisoo Lee,Hwijung Ryu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SERIAL-7，一个基于可执行验证框架的韩英双语基准，覆盖七种表示形式，用于评测机器可验证的结构化生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型越来越多地生成被程序与下游系统消费的结构化输出，此时语义正确并不足以保证可可靠执行，因此需要可机器验证的结构化生成评测。

Method: 基于177个经验证的规范化工件，将任务拆分为语义推导（Derive）、约束性工件生成（Realize）与跨表示保持（Preserve）；配套43条原子表示约束，并对格式、结构与任务相关语义执行可执行检查，实现从表示合规到工件完全有效性的渐进式严格验证。

Result: 在十个模型上，REALIZE-L3的HSR介于42.0%至93.9%；Full Compliance指标进一步暴露出在最终答案正确、表示有效之外的工件级失败。

Conclusion: 可靠的结构化生成需要对工件如何被实现以及如何跨表示保持进行显式、可执行的验证。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate structured outputs consumed by programs and downstream systems, where semantic correctness alone does not guarantee reliable execution. We introduce SERIAL-7, a Korean-English benchmark built around an executable verification framework for machine-verifiable generation across seven representations. Using 177 validated canonical artifacts, SERIAL-7 separates semantic derivation (Derive), constrained artifact generation (Realize), and cross-representation preservation (Preserve). Its 43 atomic representation constraints are paired with executable checks over format, structure, and task-specific semantics, enabling progressively stricter verification from representation compliance to full artifact validity. Across ten models, REALIZE-L3 HSR ranges from 42.0% to 93.9%, while Full Compliance exposes additional artifact-level failures beyond correct final answers and valid representations. These results show that reliable structured generation requires explicit, executable verification of how artifacts are realized and preserved across representations.

</details>


### [175] [Operon-S1: a fast decision controller for long-horizon biomedical agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41350)
*Genoria AI Team,Yuejie Hou,Yueyuxiao Yang,Weiting Tang,Meng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Operon-S1 将智能体在科研工作中的"判断"与"执行"解耦：用13个决策点上的封闭式问题表征反复出现的判断，由本地小模型在CPU上约0.6秒内作答（零请求成本），在6个基准中的5个上不引起可测分数变化，并将每次判断连同其结果记录、可重训与门控上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体已能在生物学中承担长程科研工作，但其对惯例、技能、错误、证据与停止的判断都内嵌于执行推理之中，无法被单独测量、替换或改进；同时让驱动智能体的大模型承担这些判断会显著占用任务成本。

Method: 提出"决策框架"（decision harness）：在13个决策点把智能体的反复判断表述为封闭式问题，仅通过不含答案的固定消息与智能体交互，因此任何能回答这些问题的模型均可充当裁判。采用本地决策模型作为"系统1"接管判断；决策均记录其后果；利用来自任务构造、验证器或执行的标签进行重训，并通过晋升门（promotion gate）控制新版本上线；任务级结果使用公开检查点。方法亦迁移至湿实验方案排错与目标富集panel设计。

Result: 在7个生命科学基准上，本地决策模型在环路请求的中位耗时0.6秒、CPU运行、零请求成本；在6个基准中的5个上智能体分数无可测变化；而驱动智能体的大模型回答同样问题时占任务成本的8.0%–35.2%。三个重训版本在所有问题上均提升了决策准确率。

Conclusion: Operon-S1 如同操纵子的调控子——开关基因而不制造其产物——在不代替智能体执行工作的前提下引导其行为，使智能体的判断成为一个可测量、可替换、可训练的独立层次。

Abstract: Language-model agents now carry out long scientific work in biology, but their judgements about conventions, skills, errors, evidence and stopping are made inside the reasoning that does the work, where they cannot be measured, replaced or improved on their own. Here we present Operon-S1, a fast decision system, a System 1 for the agent, that separates judging from doing. A decision harness poses the agent's recurring judgements as closed questions at 13 decision points and acts only through fixed messages that never contain an answer, so any model that answers the questions can serve as the judge. Across seven life-science benchmarks, a local decision model took over these judgements in 0.6 s at the median for an in-loop request on CPU at no request cost, with no measurable change in the agent's score on five of six benchmarks, whereas the general language model that drives the agent, asked the same questions, accounted for 8.0% to 35.2% of task cost. Every decision is recorded with its consequence. Retraining on labels from task construction, verifiers or execution raised decision accuracy on every question in each of three trained versions, and a new version enters service only through a promotion gate. Task-level results in this study use the public checkpoint. The same closed decisions serve wet-laboratory protocol troubleshooting and the design of a germline target enrichment panel. Like the regulator of an operon, which switches genes on and off without making their products, Operon-S1 steers the agent without doing its work, and makes the agent's judgements a layer that can be measured, replaced and trained.

</details>


### [176] [Do Transaction Prices Reflect Visual Preference? A Diagnostic Study with Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41358)
*Fengxian Ji,Jingpu Yang,Fan Zhang,Junhong Liang,Qian Jiang,Yuke Li,Jinri Guo,Rongxin Zhu,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MarketImage诊断框架，用于识别在线市场交易价格何时能反映视觉偏好、何时失效，并检验多模态模型能否恢复人支持的视觉偏好信号。结果显示仅24.2%的图像对有非并列价格方向，其中34.7%与人类多数一致，说明价格只能作为不确定的代理信号而非真值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉偏好评估依赖昂贵的人工标注或偏好奖励模型；在线市场交易价格易获取且反映真实购买行为，可提供低成本监督，但价格混杂了来源、需求、竞争等非视觉因素，聚合准确率无法区分模型错误、不可靠价格信号与协议混淆，构成可识别性挑战。

Method: MarketImage含三部分：(i) MarketImageArt——266组六图比较集，附交易价格与价格盲测人类排序；(ii) MarketImageLens——保留并列的协议，将3,990个组内图像对划分为价格-人类一致、冲突、价格并列三类区域，并单独审计呈现顺序效应；(iii) MarketValuator——六种诱导方法（直接比较、独立打分、预算约束、多智能体聚合等），评估静态视觉评分器与三个多模态语言模型的信号恢复能力。

Result: 仅24.2%的图像对具有非并列价格方向，其中34.7%与人类多数一致。在该区域内，HPSv2以66.4%的Recoverable Accuracy领先；Qwen的预算约束与聚合变体落后于基线，GPT接口的增益在统计上尚不显著。

Conclusion: 交易价格仅在有限条件下编码可恢复的视觉偏好，应视为不确定的代理信号而非真值。

Abstract: Existing visual preference evaluation relies on costly human annotations or preference reward models, whereas online marketplace transaction prices are readily available and reflect real purchasing behavior, offering potential low-cost supervision. However, transaction prices conflate visual preferences with non-visual factors such as provenance, demand, and competition, making aggregate accuracy insufficient to distinguish model errors, unreliable price signals, and protocol confounds. To address this identifiability challenge, we introduce MarketImage, a diagnostic framework that identifies when transaction prices reflect visual preferences, where they fail, and whether multimodal language models can recover human-supported visual preference signals. MarketImage comprises three components: (i) MarketImageArt, 266 six-image comparison sets with transaction prices and price-blind human rankings; (ii) MarketImageLens, a tie-preserving protocol that partitions 3,990 within-set image pairs into price-human agreement, conflict, and price-tie regions while separately auditing presentation-order effects; and (iii) MarketValuator, six elicitation methods spanning direct comparison, independent scoring, budget constraints, and multi-agent aggregation to evaluate signal recovery by static visual scorers and three multimodal language models. Experiments show that only 24.2% of image pairs have a non-tied price direction, of which 34.7% agree with the human majority. Within this region, HPSv2 leads at 66.4% Recoverable Accuracy; Qwen's budgeted and aggregated variants trail baselines, while GPT interface gains remain statistically unresolved. Thus, transaction prices encode recoverable visual preferences only under limited conditions and should be treated as uncertain proxies rather than ground truth.

</details>


### [177] [LEAP: Efficient LLM Inference Scheduling with Generalizable Length Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41359)
*Kwan Yan,Jialun Zhong,Yanzeng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LEAP：复用预填充隐状态+线性读出+在线递归最小二乘自适应的LLM请求长度预测系统，CPU异步执行，跨域排序稳健且开销低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长度感知调度通过优先处理短请求降低延迟，但现有长度预测器在域/任务漂移下排序性能下降、需昂贵重训，且预测开销可能抵消调度收益，因此需提升泛化性与效率。

Method: 复用服务模型预填充隐藏状态（含输出长度信息），用线性读出提取信号；测试时以已完成请求为监督、用递归最小二乘更新读出权重，无需梯度计算与历史数据回放；预测与更新在CPU异步执行，并与GPU剩余预填充重叠。

Result: 跨域迁移中保持强排序性能；大域偏移下在线自适应使Spearman相关系数提升64.7%；平均每token延迟降低15.9%–40.8%，吞吐提升1.9%，预测器参数比最强基线少30,569倍。

Conclusion: 隐状态线性读出+在线递归最小二乘自适应+CPU异步执行，可在低开销下实现通用、高效的长度预测调度。

Abstract: Length-aware scheduling reduces request latency in LLM serving by prioritizing shorter requests. However, despite their large model sizes, length predictors can suffer degraded ranking performance under domain and task shifts, and adapting them can require costly retraining. Their prediction overhead can further offset the benefits of scheduling. To improve the generalization and efficiency of length-aware scheduling, we propose \textbf{LEAP}, a length prediction system with fast online adaptation and asynchronous CPU execution. LEAP reuses the serving model's prefill hidden states, which contain information about the output length, and extracts this signal through a linear readout. To adapt to substantial domain and task shifts, it updates the readout at test time through recursive least squares, using completed requests as supervision without gradient computation or historical-data replay. Prediction and updates run asynchronously on CPU, while prediction and initial batch ordering overlap the remaining GPU prefill. Across the evaluated cross-domain transfers, LEAP retains strong ranking performance, and online adaptation improves Spearman correlation by 64.7\% under a larger domain shift. Serving experiments show that LEAP reduces average per-token latency by 15.9--40.8\% compared with the evaluated length predictors, while achieving 1.9\% higher throughput with $30{,}569\times$ fewer predictor parameters than the strongest evaluated baseline.

</details>


### [178] [RECITE: Revising Eligibility Criteria Incorporating Textual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41368)
*Russell Ro,Reza Abbasi-Asl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RECITE框架，基于ClinicalTrials.gov方案修订文档构建11,913条入排标准修订实例数据集，基准测试15种LLM配置，并用智能体文献发现系统实现证据到试验设计的落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过于严苛的入排标准（eligibility criteria）导致临床试验招募失败普遍发生；而已发表的"应如何放宽具体标准"的建议与大规模试验设计实践相脱节，缺乏自动化、可规模化的证据到设计的转化路径。

Method: 1) 利用ClinicalTrials.gov的方案修订（protocol amendment）文档构建数据集：5,735项试验中11,913条经整理的实例，每条将原始标准与文件证据及目标修订配对；2) 在截断至上下文窗口与检索增强生成（RAG）两种条件下对15种LLM配置进行基准测试，评测样本3,116条；3) 采用LLM-as-judge协议，并与10位领域专家验证（judge与人类二次加权Cohen's κ_qw=0.694±0.096）；4) 使用智能体式文献发现系统进行论文—试验匹配。

Result: 最佳开源权重模型Gemma 2 9B在0–4量表上取得κ_qw对齐序数得分3.0±0.9（归一化0.742）；最佳闭源模型GPT-4o为3.0±0.8（归一化0.745）。智能体文献发现系统在5篇出版物中识别出44个论文—试验匹配，提供试验设计与EC修改证据。

Conclusion: 现有临床知识可被操作化为可执行的试验设计建议；RECITE将入排标准修订的证据发现、抽取与实施打通为自动化流程，为规模化优化试验设计提供可行路径。

Abstract: Overly restrictive eligibility criteria contribute to widespread enrollment failure in clinical trials, yet published recommendations for how specific criteria should be broadened remain disconnected from trial design at scale. We introduce RECITE (Revising Eligibility Criteria Incorporating Textual Evidence), a framework for automated discovery, extraction, and implementation of evidence-based eligibility criteria revisions. RECITE uses protocol amendment documents from ClinicalTrials.gov to construct a dataset of 11,913 curated instances across 5,735 trials, each pairing original criteria with documentary evidence and target revisions. We benchmark fifteen LLM configurations under truncated-to-context-window and retrieval-augmented generation conditions, on a 3,116-instance sample under an LLM-as-judge protocol validated against ten domain experts (judge vs. human quadratic-weighted Cohen's $κ_{qw} = 0.694 \pm 0.096$). The best open-weight model, Gemma 2 9B, achieves $κ_{qw}$-aligned ordinal score $3.0 \pm 0.9$ on a 0--4 scale (normalized 0.742) and the best closed-weight model, GPT-4o, reaches $3.0 \pm 0.8$ (normalized 0.745). Using an agentic literature discovery system, we then identified 44 paper--trial matches across five publications providing trial-design and EC-modification evidence. Our findings suggest that existing clinical knowledge can be operationalized into actionable trial design recommendations.

</details>


### [179] [From the Zoo to the BULLPEN: Sample-Efficient LLM Profiling via Multi-Channel Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41375)
*Russell Ro,Qiming Cui,Reza Abbasi-Asl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BULLPEN 通过联合建模模型作答文本、正确性模式与问题文本，将模型压缩为定长嵌入向量，用少量问题即可低成本预测模型在行为、风格、安全等属性上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从海量 LLM 中选型需了解候选模型的多种属性，而每类属性通常需独立基准评测，成本高昂。既有模型嵌入方法（如 EmbedLLM）主要依赖正确性模式与问题特征，且需大量探针；但选型中关键的冗长度、安全性与风格等行为更多体现在"如何作答"而非"是否正确"。

Method: 提出 BULLPEN 框架，对 EmbedLLM 做三点扩展：(1) 系统梳理现有下游任务，并新增 20 个基于行为、风格与来源（provenance）的任务基准；(2) 首次将模型自身作答文本与正确性信号、问题文本联合编码；(3) 仅用数百个问题（而非完整的 24,118 题题库）构建模型嵌入。

Result: 在 260 个开源权重模型上，作答文本编码器在新任务上得分 0.48±0.10，优于最佳现有编码器的 0.35±0.10；读取作答文本的编码器仅用 400 题（约为完整题库推理算力的 1.3%）即可保留至少 91% 的全题库性能，而仅用正确性的基线在同样 400 题下仅保留 83%。

Conclusion: BULLPEN 的嵌入仅依赖模型输出，无需访问权重或内部信息，因而支持行为审计与低成本画像，并可推广至仅提供 API 的模型。

Abstract: Choosing among the hundreds of thousands of available large language models (LLMs) requires a thorough understanding of each candidate model's properties. In practice, each property is measured by its own benchmark suite, so profiling a model on many properties means paying for many separate evaluations. To address this, model embeddings compress each model into a fixed-length vector from which many properties can be predicted. Existing methods, such as EmbedLLM, build these vectors from correctness patterns and question features, and often need large probe batteries to do so. However, many behaviors that are important when choosing a model, such as its verbosity, safety and style, show up more in how it answers than in whether it is correct. We introduce BULLPEN (Behavioral Understanding of Large Language models via Profile ENcodings), a framework that extends EmbedLLM in three ways. First, BULLPEN organizes the downstream tasks that prior work uses and adds 20 task benchmarks we built on behavior, style and provenance. Second, it is the first framework to read a model's own answer text jointly with its correctness and the question text. Third, it encodes models from a few hundred questions instead of the full 24,118-question pool. Across 260 open-weight models, our answer-text encoder scores 0.48 +/- 0.10 on the new tasks against 0.35 +/- 0.10 for the best prior encoder. Our encoders that read answer text keep at least 91% of their full-pool skill from only 400 questions (1.3% of the inference compute of the full question pool), while a correctness-only baseline keeps 83% with the same 400 questions. Because these embeddings need only model outputs, they support behavioral auditing and low-cost profiling, and could extend to API-only models. Code and data: https://github.com/wcquesera/bullpen

</details>


### [180] [Ask on Demand: Targeted Peer Communication for Parallel Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41378)
*Haofu Yang,Hongyang Yin,Ran Bi,Faguo Wu,Peng Xue,Feng Zhou,Han Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出按需询问（AOD）通信框架，让并行LLM智能体在出现具体信息需求时向同伴定向求助，通过轻量门控判断求助时机，在AppWorld与WebShop上提升任务表现且推理成本低于现有共享基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行LLM智能体通过探索多条轨迹处理复杂任务，但有用发现往往孤立在各自执行历史中；现有信息共享方法在他人需求明确之前就进行选择或摘要，可能遗漏后期才变得重要的细节。

Method: AOD在请求获批后从同伴执行历史中检索相关证据、选择合适的同伴并生成针对请求的定制响应；为判断求助是否值得，使用配对团队续跑（paired team continuations）训练轻量门控，监督信号来自最终团队结果与推理成本。

Result: 在AppWorld与WebShop上的实验表明，AOD相对独立并行执行一致提升任务性能，同时推理成本低于现有信息共享基线。

Conclusion: 将同伴通信与智能体不断演进的信息需求对齐，可实现更有效且更高效的协作。

Abstract: Parallel LLM agents tackle complex tasks by exploring multiple trajectories, yet useful discoveries remain isolated in individual execution histories. Existing sharing methods often select or summarize information before other agents' needs become clear, potentially overlooking details that become important later. We propose Ask on Demand (AOD), a communication framework that enables agents to seek targeted peer assistance when specific information needs arise. Upon an approved request, AOD retrieves relevant evidence from peer execution histories, selects a suitable peer, and generates a response tailored to the request. To determine when such assistance is worthwhile, we train a lightweight gate using paired team continuations, with supervision derived from final team outcomes and inference cost. Experiments on AppWorld and WebShop demonstrate that AOD consistently improves task performance over independent parallel execution while achieving lower inference cost than existing information-sharing baselines. These findings highlight the benefits of aligning peer communication with agents' evolving information needs to achieve more effective and efficient collaboration.

</details>


### [181] [Online Evidence-Containment Prediction over Text Spans under Distinct Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41384)
*Xiang Liu,Haochen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文把嵌套文本区域的顺序验证形式化为在线证据包含预测（OECP），将其归约为三角二维乘积序上的在线等渗回归，给出近似线性最坏遗憾界与单目标情形的最优对数遗憾可学习器，并在 NQ、SciFact、ERASER 及 DPR 层级上验证了演绎增益、学习增益与检索空间缩减带来的验证调用减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证据落地型 NLP 系统常需依次验证嵌套文本区域（如 span、层级结构），验证一个区域会对其他区域产生约束，但现有方法把各候选的验证视为彼此独立，既未形式化这种“先预测、后精确验证、每个候选至多查询一次”的在线约束结构，也未刻画由此带来的遗憾下界与可学习性。

Method: 形式化 Online Evidence-Containment Prediction（OECP）：每个候选在精确验证前被预测且至多查询一次。指出 span 包含关系恰为三角二维乘积序，从而 OECP 等价于 distinct-query 在线等渗回归。多可容许目标情形通过端点秩压缩（endpoint-rank compression）定义有效规模 m_eff，分析最坏遗憾；单固定目标情形（标准 QA）设计显式多项式时间学习器。再用 Expected Search-space Reduction（ESR）把预测概率转化为验证决策与调度。

Result: 多目标情形最坏遗憾为 m_eff 的近似线性 \widetildeΘ(m_eff)；单目标情形学习器达到最优 Θ(log m_eff) 遗憾，并在真实 NQ 片段上运行。在 Natural Questions 上，包含关系带来较大演绎增益，并对未解决候选有较小学习增益，且在两种骨干与 DPR 层级上可复现。ESR 在 SciFact、ERASER 与 DPR 层级上以更少验证器调用完成证据定位；在调用预算约束下的 DPR 层级上优于四个近期外部选择器，并相对学习式与生成器贪心调度提升固定阅读器 F1。

Conclusion: 嵌套证据验证的序列约束可被严格地建模为在线等渗回归问题，兼具理论保证（近似线性/最优对数遗憾）与实践收益（演绎+学习增益、更少验证调用、更高 F1），说明利用包含结构的在线预测是提升证据落地系统效率与精度的有效范式。

Abstract: Evidence-grounded NLP systems often verify nested text regions sequentially, so verifying one region constrains others. We formalize this as Online Evidence-Containment Prediction (OECP): each candidate is predicted before exact verification and queried at most once. Span containment is exactly a triangular 2D product order, making OECP distinct-query online isotonic regression. With multiple admissible targets, worst-case regret is near-linear, $\widetildeΘ(m_{\mathrm{eff}})$, in the effective scale given by endpoint-rank compression; a single fixed target, as in standard QA, admits an explicit polynomial-time learner with optimal $Θ(\log m_{\mathrm{eff}})$ regret, run on real NQ episodes. On Natural Questions, containment yields a large deductive gain plus a smaller learned gain on unresolved candidates, reproduced across two backbones and DPR hierarchies. Expected Search-space Reduction (ESR) turns these probabilities into decisions, localizing evidence in fewer verifier calls on SciFact, ERASER, and DPR hierarchies; on DPR hierarchies under call budgets it outperforms four recent external selectors and raises fixed-reader F1 over learned and generator-greedy scheduling.

</details>


### [182] [Cross-Interface Authorization for AI Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41397)
*Genliang Zhu,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向固定版本计算机使用部署的"画像有界"授权协议，通过路由—汇聚点强制切面、规范化效应、路由绑定准备票据与提交时复核，保证多接口下策略决策与可执行授权的对应关系，并在双运行时上通过大规模轨迹与变异测试验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用代理可经由图形界面、命令行、API、脚本与 MCP 服务器等多种通道产生作用。策略决策可能接口无关，但可执行授权并非如此：其他路由可绕过中介、篡改安全相关默认值，或在授权状态改变后提交，从而出现"决策一致而授权不一致"的漏洞。

Method: 在给定假设下形式化并证明：画像有界精化、匹配效应决策不变性、对替代路由洗白与声明式跨面重组合的抵抗、提交时状态安全、以及基于回执的结果确定性。工程侧构建参考画像（12 个效应族、5 个接口、60 条路由），用两种结构不同的运行时映射分别生成 1,732 个场景、共 3,464 条轨迹；辅以独立检查器、覆盖与规范化变异测试、提交探针、冻结源码审计与源码形态适配器测试。

Result: 轨迹含 960 次提交、2,312 次拒绝、192 次重准备后提交；独立检查器接受全部保留轨迹与配对，拒绝 26/26 语义回归；覆盖与规范化套件分别拒绝 24/24 与 720/720 变异体；168/168 提交探针结果与声明一致；冻结源码审计在 12 个文件上通过 52 项检查；适配器 48/48 仪器调用提交、15/15 未绑定场景被拒。

Conclusion: 所提供保证仅约束声明的画像及其效应语义，属画像有界而非全局保证；画像外路由与效应需通过扩展画像与重新审计来覆盖。

Abstract: Computer-use agents act through graphical interfaces, command lines, APIs, scripts, and Model Context Protocol servers. A policy decision can be interface-invariant while its executable authorization is not: another route can bypass mediation, alter a security-relevant default, or commit after the authorized state has changed. We present a profile-bounded protocol for a fixed, versioned computer-use deployment. It enumerates effect-bearing routes and durable sinks, checks a route-to-sink enforcement cut, normalizes each concrete request to a canonical effect, issues a route-bound prepare ticket, and verifies the route, effect, grant, trace, profile, inventory, and authoritative state again at commit. A committed result requires an agreeing sink receipt. Under explicit assumptions, we prove profile-bounded refinement, matched-effect decision invariance, resistance to alternate-route laundering and declared cross-surface recomposition, commit-time state safety, and receipt-grounded outcomes.
  The reference profile registers 12 effect families, five interfaces, and 60 routes. Two structurally distinct runtime mappings produce 3,464 traces over 1,732 scenarios each: 960 commits, 2,312 denials, and 192 commits after re-preparation. An independent checker accepts every retained trace and pair and rejects 26/26 semantic regressions; coverage and normalizer suites reject 24/24 and 720/720 mutants; and 168/168 commit probes match their declared outcomes. A frozen-source audit passes 52 checks over 12 files from two independent hybrid runtimes and one public authorization substrate. Source-shaped adapter refinements commit 48/48 instrumented calls and reject 15/15 unbound cases. The resulting guarantees bind the declared profile and its effect semantics.

</details>


### [183] [scAnyOmics: Foundation-Model Distillation for Any-to-Any Completion of Multi-omics Virtual Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41399)
*Zhaoqi Song,Ye Du,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Qian Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出生成式框架scAnyOmics，将冻结的单细胞基础模型知识蒸馏进多模态学生模型，统一整合scRNA-seq、蛋白标志物与scATAC-seq，实现跨模态预测与缺失模态重建，并在69万余细胞数据及留出供体上超越基线，扩展至细胞类型注释、扰动响应预测与生物学年龄估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞多组学测量（转录组、蛋白组、染色质可及性）虽能更全面刻画细胞状态，但异质模态整合与缺失分子信息预测仍困难，尤其在配对测量有限的情况下。

Method: 构建生成式框架scAnyOmics：以冻结的单细胞基础模型为教师，将知识蒸馏至多模态学生模型，把scRNA-seq、蛋白标志物测量与scATAC-seq映射到共享表征空间，从而支持跨模态预测与缺失模态重建；在来自六项公开研究的690,880个细胞（含配对与部分配对多组学数据）上系统评测。

Result: 在留出供体数据上，scAnyOmics在多项评价指标上优于基线方法；除跨模态重建外，在细胞类型注释、扰动响应预测和生物学年龄估计等下游任务中亦展现应用潜力。

Conclusion: scAnyOmics为整合不完整的单细胞多组学数据与迁移预训练生物学知识提供了统一框架，可支撑跨细胞情境与实验平台的虚拟细胞模型构建。

Abstract: Recent advances in single-cell technologies have enabled the profiling of multiple molecular modalities, including transcriptomics, proteomics, and chromatin accessibility, providing a more comprehensive understanding of cellular states. However, integrating heterogeneous modalities and predicting missing molecular information remain challenging, particularly when paired measurements are limited. Here, we introduce scAnyOmics, a generative framework that distills knowledge from a frozen single-cell foundation model into a multimodal student model. scAnyOmics integrates scRNA-seq, protein-marker measurements, and scATAC-seq into a shared representation, enabling cross-modal prediction and reconstruction of missing modalities. We assembled 690,880 cells from six publicly available studies, covering both paired and partially paired multi-omics measurements, to systematically evaluate its predictive performance. We further evaluated scAnyOmics on held-out donor data, where it outperformed baseline methods across multiple evaluation metrics. Beyond cross-modal reconstruction, we explored its applications in cell-type annotation, perturbation-response prediction, and biological age estimation. Overall, scAnyOmics provides a unified framework for integrating incomplete single-cell multi-omics data and transferring pretrained biological knowledge, supporting the development of virtual cell models across diverse cellular contexts and experimental platforms.

</details>


### [184] [CryoAtlas: A Large Curated Dataset and Unified Benchmark for Cryo-EM Atomic Model Building](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41411)
*Mingrui Li,Minzhang Li,Weichen Qin,Yufan Xie,Zhangzhi Xiong,Sixian Shen,Jiakai Zhang,Yuan Pei,Jingyi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出CryoAtlas——一个大规模、严格筛选的冷冻电镜密度图引导蛋白链原子建模数据集与统一评测基准，含17,809个图-模型-序列条目（训练集16,657）与618目标评测集，并在其上系统评测学习类方法与Phenix、AlphaFold3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已加速从冷冻电镜密度图自动构建原子模型，但缺乏大规模高质量数据集与标准化评测基准，制约了方法间的可比性与进展。

Method: 整合并筛选截至2026年3月的EMDB与PDB条目构建CryoAtlas；提供17,809条图-模型-序列条目（含16,657条训练划分）；从中构建618个目标的基准，并设计共享评测协议，联合评估结构精度、模型-密度图拟合度与立体化学合理性；基于该框架对现有学习类方法与Phenix、AlphaFold3进行对比，分析分辨率、序列长度、蛋白类别与序列一致性对精度的影响；并以在CryoAtlas上重训练ModelAngelo的CNN组件作为案例研究。

Result: 提供了统一的评测协议与多维（结构精度/拟合度/立体化学）评估结果；系统揭示了分辨率、序列长度、蛋白类别与序列一致性对建模精度的影响规律；案例表明在CryoAtlas上重训练ModelAngelo的CNN组件可提升其在基准上的性能。

Conclusion: CryoAtlas为冷冻电镜原子模型构建研究提供了开放、可复现的基础数据集与基准平台，并验证了数据规模与质量对模型性能的促进作用。

Abstract: Deep learning has accelerated automated atomic model building from cryo-EM maps, yet progress remains limited by the lack of large, high-quality datasets and standardized evaluation benchmarks. We address this gap by introducing CryoAtlas, a rigorously curated dataset and unified benchmark for density-guided atomic model building of protein chains. By integrating and filtering EMDB and PDB entries (up to March 2026), CryoAtlas provides 17,809 map-model-sequence entries, including a curated 16,657-entry training split. From this resource, we construct a 618-target benchmark with a shared evaluation protocol that jointly assesses structural accuracy, model-to-map fit, and stereochemical validity. Using this framework, we benchmark state-of-the-art learning-based methods against Phenix and AlphaFold3, comprehensively analyzing the impact of map resolution, sequence length, protein category and sequence identity on modeling accuracy. As a case study, retraining the ModelAngelo CNN component on CryoAtlas improves performance on the benchmark. CryoAtlas provides an open, reproducible foundation for future cryo-EM atomic model building research. Project details and resource release updates are available at https://oulling.github.io/cryoatlas/.

</details>


### [185] [Evidence-Guided Recovery for AI Agents with Ambiguous Tool Outcomes and Partial Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41453)
*Genliang Zhu,Xiaobo Chen,Ziyuan Wang,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究工具超时导致的AI智能体"歧义结果"与"部分执行"恢复问题，提出基于证据指导的任务恢复框架，通过共享可采纳性检查与终端证书区分真实目标达成与公开可支持的安全完成，并在有限研究与预注册的留出对比中验证规划建议的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具超时会使AI智能体面临模糊的工具结果与部分执行：一个动作可能已失败、已提交或仍在执行中，而其他工作流步骤已完成。现有方法缺少对"已批准义务、提供方能力与公开证据相容状态"的统一处理，难以给出可验证的恢复与完成判定。

Method: 提出证据指导的任务恢复问题形式化：以已批准义务、提供方能力和与公开证据相容的状态为约束。设计共享可采纳性检查（处理密钥过期、授权时序、依赖关系与残余效应）与终端证书（区分真实目标满足与公开支持的安全完成）。已知识模型实现将标准观测相依规划与独立实现的效果历史裁定器相结合。

Result: 37项任务的有限研究中，规划与三个主动控制各认证28次完成，节省4个合成费用单位但增加计算量。预注册的任务留出对比用两个本地模型包评估规划建议与证据提示：在19个任务族的58个配对单元中，建议认证30次完成对9次，差异36.2个百分点（精确族符号翻转p=0.0078），提案数272对399。全部116条轨迹通过独立重放，两模型均呈正向完成差异。

Conclusion: 该对比在共同防护盾下衡量所评估的建议接口；搜索式与规则式建议需另行匹配比较。未知与移交结果保持显式，保证绑定于声明的提供方能力，而非任意的第三方恰好一次执行。

Abstract: Tool timeouts can leave AI agents facing ambiguous tool outcomes and partial execution: an action may have failed, committed or remained in flight while other workflow steps have already completed. We formulate evidence-guided task recovery over approved obligations, provider capabilities and states compatible with public evidence. A shared admissibility check handles key expiry, authorization timing, dependencies and residual effects; a terminal certificate separates actual goal satisfaction from publicly supported safe completion. The known-model implementation combines standard observation-contingent planning with a separately implemented effect-history adjudicator. In a finite study of 37 assignments, planning and three active controls each certify 28 completions; four saved synthetic fee units incur additional computation. A preregistered assignment-held-out comparison then evaluates planning advice against evidence prompting with two local model bundles. Across 58 paired units in 19 previously developed task families, advice certifies 30 completions versus 9, a 36.2-percentage-point difference (exact family sign-flip p=0.0078), using 272 proposals versus 399. All 116 trajectories pass independent replay. Both models exhibit a positive completion difference. The comparison measures the evaluated advice interface under a common shield; search-versus-rule advice requires a separate matched comparison. Unknown and handoff outcomes remain explicit, with guarantees tied to the declared provider capabilities rather than arbitrary third-party exactly-once execution.

</details>


### [186] [EvoMCP: Co-evolving MCP agents and training data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41479)
*Xuanang Gao,Kepeng Lin,Xinyan Shi,Yong Deng,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng,Xiaofeng Wu,Jie Yang,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 agent–data 互演化框架 EvoMCP，通过任务演化、轨迹演化与监督微调的闭环，使 MCP 智能体在自适应数据分布上持续提升，在 Toolathlon、MCPMark、MCP-Atlas 上分别取得 16.5%、10.2%、12.6% 的绝对增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对 MCP 智能体的监督微调通常基于静态数据分布，无法适应学生模型不断变化的能力，也难以充分针对新暴露的弱点进行训练。

Method: 1) 依据 rollout 反馈评估学生的已有能力与能力缺口，确定学习目标；2) 任务演化：在保持 verifier 对齐的前提下，修改已验证种子任务的指令与可观测环境状态，将目标转化为可执行任务；3) 轨迹演化：利用可复用技能修复失败的教师 rollout 并精炼成功轨迹，提供可靠高效示范；4) 用任务–轨迹对微调学生，被接受的任务作为后续演化的种子；5) 重新评估更新后的学生，用其反馈引导下一轮数据演化，形成闭环。

Result: 在 Toolathlon、MCPMark、MCP-Atlas 三个基准上相较初始学生模型均取得一致提升，绝对增益分别为 16.5%、10.2%、12.6%。

Conclusion: agent 与数据的协同演化能够有效缓解静态数据分布带来的失配问题，形成可持续自我改进的 MCP 智能体训练范式。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning of Model Context Protocol (MCP) agents is typically conducted on a static data distribution, which fails to adapt to the student's evolving capabilities and leaves newly exposed weaknesses insufficiently addressed. To address these challenges, we propose \method, an agent--data mutual evolution framework that enables MCP agents to improve through a closed loop of task evolution, trajectory evolution, and supervised fine-tuning. Specifically, \method\ assesses the student's established capabilities and capability gaps from rollout feedback to identify learning targets. Task evolution then translates these targets into executable tasks by adapting the instructions and observable environment states of compatible verified seed tasks while keeping their verifiers aligned. To provide reliable and efficient demonstrations for these tasks, trajectory evolution uses reusable skills to recover failed teacher rollouts and refine successful ones. The resulting task--trajectory pairs are used to fine-tune the student, while accepted tasks seed subsequent evolution. To close the loop, we re-evaluate the updated student and use its feedback to guide the next round of data evolution. Experiments on Toolathlon, MCPMark, and MCP-Atlas demonstrate consistent improvements over the initial student, yielding absolute gains of 16.5\%, 10.2\%, and 12.6\%, respectively. Project page: https://github.com/xagao/EvoMCP.

</details>


### [187] [Multi-Test Nation Bias Benchmarking of LLMs with Explicit Unbiased Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41486)
*Jonghyeon Choi,Yeonjun Choi,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文用722份联合国安理会官方记录与五种互补测试，对4个LLM在安理会五常上的国家级偏向进行定量、可基准化的评测，发现存在与测试无关的宏观模式：模型输出对中俄偏"反对"一侧，对英法美偏"支持"一侧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究指出LLM存在系统性的国家级偏向，会给国际关系（IR）应用带来操作风险；但现有评测大多缺乏明确的无偏参照（ground truth），导致难以进行完全定量化和跨情境可比的测量。作者因而希望构建有明确参照基准的国家级偏向评测框架。

Method: 扩展语料至722份联合国安理会官方记录，采用五种互补测试覆盖更广的偏向表达渠道：意见排序（WOW）、关联排序（AK+-）、投票模拟（PVS）、反对归因（F-Oppose）与框架（Headliner）；为每个测试显式定义无偏状态，并在WOW与AK+-这类ground truth难以指定的测试中，提出基于可观测投票行为的"行动式伪无偏状态"以构建可量化基线；在此基础上对GPT-4o-mini、Llama 3.3-70B、Mistral-22B-Small与Qwen3-30B四个模型在安理会五常上进行基准测试。

Result: 在五常范围内，四个模型呈现一致的、与测试无关的宏观模式：相对于各测试所定义的参照基线，模型输出对中国和俄罗斯偏向"反对"（red）一侧，对英国、美国、法国偏向"支持"（blue）一侧。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了以明确参照点、多测试基准对面向国际关系的LLM部署进行审计的必要性，即需要"有据可依（grounded）"的偏向评测方法。

Abstract: While prior work has highlighted that systematic nation-level bias in large language models (LLMs) can pose operational risks for international relations (IR) applications, many existing evaluations still lack a clearly specified unbiased reference (ground truth), limiting fully quantitative and cross-setting measurement. Using an expanded corpus of 722 official UN Security Council (UNSC) records, we measure nation-level bias with five complementary tests, thereby extending prior approaches to cover a broader set of bias expression channels: opinion ranking (WOW), association sorting (AK+-), vote simulation (PVS), opposition attribution (F-Oppose), and framing (Headliner). To enable grounded bias evaluation, we explicitly define an unbiased status for each test; in particular, for WOW and AK+- where ground truth is difficult to specify, we propose an action-based pseudo unbiased status derived from observable voting behavior to construct a quantifiable baseline. Benchmarking 4 LLMs (GPT-4o-mini, Llama 3.3-70B, Mistral-22B-Small, and Qwen3-30B) across the five permanent members of the UNSC, we find a test-agnostic macro-pattern: relative to the reference baselines defined for each test, model outputs lean toward the opposition (red) side for China and Russia and toward the supportive (blue) side for the U.K., the U.S., and France. These results underscore the need to audit IR-facing LLM deployments with grounded, multi-test benchmarks tied to explicit reference points.

</details>


### [188] [Specification-Guided Generation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41511)
*Alessandro Previti,Konstantinos Vandikas,Mario Alviano,Mats Carlsson,Albin Larsson Forsberg,Alexandros Nikou,Athanasios Karapantelakis,Aneta Vulgarakis Feljan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种神经-符号、规范引导的解码框架，在语法约束解码的基础上用符号推理过滤无法满足形式规范的值，含前向变体与回溯变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束解码方法主要只能保证语法合法性，无法直接保证输出满足形式规范（formal specification），因此需要将LLM生成与符号推理结合以在解码阶段强制规范满足。

Method: 在每一步解码时细化语法，过滤掉无法扩展为满足规范的输出值。框架模型无关、无需重训练，构建于现有语法约束解码机制之上。包含：(1) 前向变体，按构造保证规范满足；(2) 回溯变体，当下游后果仅剩LLM自身判定不合适的取值时，回溯修正先前可行选择。

Result: 在配置与约束求解基准上，前向变体可靠生成符合规范的输出，相比拒绝采样减少重复LLM推理，仅带来适度的符号推理开销；回溯变体进一步减少模型被迫选择其自身判定不合适取值的情形，显著提升对LLM所表达提示理解的遵循度。

Conclusion: 规范引导的神经-符号解码能够在保证形式规范满足的同时保持模型无关、免训练的特性，前向变体高效可靠，回溯变体进一步改善输出与LLM语义判断的一致性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate structured outputs, but existing constrained-decoding methods mainly enforce syntax and cannot directly enforce satisfaction of a formal specification. We introduce a neuro-symbolic, specification-guided decoding framework that combines LLM generation with symbolic reasoning. At each step, the framework refines the decoding grammar by filtering out values that cannot be extended to an output satisfying the specification. We present a forward-only variant that guarantees specification satisfaction by construction, and a backtracking variant that revises earlier feasible choices when their downstream consequences leave only values that the LLM itself judges unsuitable. The framework is model-agnostic, requires no retraining, and builds on existing grammar-constrained decoding mechanisms. Experiments on configuration and constraint-solving benchmarks show that the forward-only variant reliably produces specification-compliant outputs while reducing repeated LLM inference compared with rejection sampling and incurring modest symbolic-reasoning overhead. The backtracking variant further reduces cases in which the model is forced to select values that it has itself judged unsuitable, thereby substantially improving adherence to the LLM's stated interpretation of the prompt.

</details>


### [189] [Verify--Backtrack Loop: Structured Precondition Recovery for Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41564)
*Mingyang Liu,Qingcan Kang,Shixiong Kai,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan,Xin Li,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练的Verify–Backtrack Loop（VBL），用有界目标栈与双视野验证+类型化失败归因显式维护规划状态，实现结构化恢复，在三个基准、三个骨干上均优于ReAct与反思类方法，AppWorld上任务成功率最多提升22.07个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体在交互环境中处理长程序列任务时，动作常依赖潜在前置条件；核心难点在于失败恢复而非轨迹长度。ReAct及后续反思/自适应规划方法虽支持局部纠错，但把恢复表达为自由文本，缺乏将终极目标、当前活跃目标与失败源关联的显式控制状态。

Method: VBL作为ReAct的训练无关扩展，将任务分解维护为有界目标栈；在目标激活、执行失败或实质状态变化时，联合验证两个视野——当前活跃目标是否服务于终极目标、在当前状态下是否可行；结合类型化失败归因触发显式操作：Push、Pop、retry或BacktrackTo父目标。

Result: 在AppWorld、ALFWorld、ScienceWorld上，以Qwen3.5-9B、GPT-4o、DeepSeek-V3.2为骨干，VBL在每个“骨干—基准”组合上均优于ReAct与基于反思的控制方法；AppWorld上较ReAct任务成功率最多提升22.07个百分点。消融显示移除验证或归因任一环节均导致性能下降，部分设置低于ReAct，表明二者互补。

Conclusion: 可靠的长程交互得益于修正规划状态而非仅修正下一步动作；双视野验证与失败归因是互补且必要的机制。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly tackle long-horizon tasks in interactive environments, where actions often depend on latent preconditions. The core difficulty is not trajectory length but recovery: when a prerequisite is missing or an earlier decomposition fails, trying another plausible action rarely helps. ReAct and later reflection or adaptive-planning methods support local correction, but express recovery as free-form text, with no explicit control state linking the terminal goal, the active goal, and the failure source. We introduce the Verify--Backtrack Loop (VBL), a training-free extension of ReAct that turns precondition discovery and failure diagnosis into structured planning-state updates. VBL maintains the task decomposition as a bounded goal stack and, on goal activation, execution failure, or material state change, jointly verifies two horizons---whether the active goal contributes to the terminal goal and whether it is feasible in the current state. These dual-horizon judgments, combined with typed failure attribution, trigger explicit operations---Push, Pop, retry, or BacktrackTo the parent. We evaluate VBL with Qwen3.5-9B, GPT-4o, and DeepSeek-V3.2 on AppWorld, ALFWorld, and ScienceWorld. VBL improves over ReAct and reflection-based control on every backbone--benchmark pair. On the challenging AppWorld setting, it improves task success by up to 22.07 percentage points over ReAct. Ablations show that removing either verification or diagnosis degrades performance, in several settings below ReAct, confirming that the two mechanisms are complementary. These results show that reliable long-horizon interaction benefits from revising the planning state, not merely the next action.

</details>


### [190] [TSBPO: Tree-Structured Branching for Policy Optimization in Interactive Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41697)
*Rui Yu,Chong Mu,Jinchuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TSBPO：树结构采样的策略优化范式，统一解决交互式语言智能体从终局反馈训练中的采样、信用分配与多token动作损失聚合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从终局结果训练交互式语言智能体存在三个耦合难题：应采样哪些任务与状态延续；如何将延迟反馈分配给具体决策；如何为多token动作聚合token级损失。

Method: 提出TSBPO，在单一训练循环中统筹：1) 基于似然拟合的Root/Branch分配器，利用保留的任务与状态证据，在固定rollout预算下针对易产生对比性终局结果的分支采样；2) 在重放的物理树上，用轨迹优势比较相邻兄弟片段，用观测分组步优势比较同任务/观测状态下的结果；3) actor对完整响应的裁剪token替代目标求和，并按活跃轮次归一化，避免策略梯度项中的响应长度平均。

Result: Qwen2.5-1.5B actor在ALFWorld上固定检查点获得97.79%已见、96.64%未见随机成功；WebShop 500个测试目标各6个随机回合的每回合成功率为81.07%。消融：WebShop去掉步项在同为update-100评估下为75.93%；ALFWorld聚合对比显示完整轮次加权获得更高指令有效性。

Conclusion: 结果在ALFWorld与WebShop两个交互式基准上支持所评估的训练配置有效。

Abstract: Training interactive language agents from terminal outcomes poses three coupled problems: which task and state continuations to sample, how to assign delayed feedback to decisions, and how to aggregate token losses for multi-token actions. We present TSBPO, a tree-structured sampling-to-update paradigm that addresses these problems within one training loop. A likelihood-fitted Root/Branch allocator uses retained task and state evidence to target continuations likely to produce contrasting terminal outcomes under fixed rollout budgets. On the resulting replayed physical trees, trajectory advantages compare immediate sibling segments, while observation-group step advantages compare outcomes at matched task/observation states. The actor then sums clipped token surrogates over each complete response and normalizes across active turns, avoiding response-length averaging in the policy-gradient term. With a Qwen2.5-1.5B actor, fixed checkpoints obtain 97.79% seen and 96.64% unseen stochastic success on ALFWorld and an 81.07% per-episode success rate over six stochastic episodes for each of the 500 WebShop test goals. A WebShop step-term ablation yields 75.93% at the same update-100 evaluation; an ALFWorld aggregation comparison shows higher observed command validity with complete-turn weighting. These results support the evaluated training configuration on two interactive benchmarks.

</details>


### [191] [Connectivity-Aware Client Selection in Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41570)
*Usevalad Milasheuski,Monica Nicoli,Stefano Savazzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于谱图工具的轻量客户端选择方法，通过特征向量嵌入与热/扩散核相似度，让每个客户端在固定通信图上仅选择结构相关的邻居子集，从而在保证精度的同时将消息传输量减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习（DFL）虽消除了服务器单点故障与通信瓶颈，但现有方法多采用全邻域广播，每轮向所有邻居发送模型更新，导致显著的通信开销与高能耗。因此需要一种低开销且不损失收敛性的邻居选择机制。

Method: 在固定通信图上分析图拉普拉斯矩阵的谱性质，利用特征向量嵌入以及热核/扩散核相似度作为轻量级客户端选择依据，引导每个客户端仅选取对其收敛最有帮助的结构相关邻居，形成一种拓扑感知的随机化选择策略。该策略与均匀邻域子采样及全广播进行对比评估。

Result: 拓扑感知随机化的表现达到或超过均匀邻域子采样，并接近全广播的精度；在较大且结构丰富的图上、强数据异构场景下收益最大，约提升3个百分点；同时总消息传输数相比朴素广播减少50%。

Conclusion: 谱图工具可作为有效的轻量客户端选择手段，在去中心化联邦学习中实现通信效率与模型精度之间的良好折衷，尤其适用于强异构数据与结构丰富的图拓扑场景。

Abstract: Decentralized federated learning (DFL) has emerged as a scalable alternative to centralized federated learning, removing the single point of failure and communication bottleneck at the server. However, most existing decentralized approaches rely on full-neighborhood broadcasting, where each client transmits its model update to all of its neighbors in each communication round. While effective, this results in significant communication overhead and high energy consumption. This paper applies spectral graph tools as lightweight client selection methods that leverage the graph topology by analyzing the spectral properties of the graph Laplacian over a fixed communication graph. Specifically, we exploit eigenvector embeddings and heat/diffusion kernel similarities to guide each client in selecting only a subset of structurally relevant neighbors, i.e., those that are most beneficial for the convergence of the federation process. This topology aware randomization matches or outperforms uniform neighborhood subsampling and approaches the accuracy of full broadcasting. The largest gains, about $3$ percentage points, emerge under strong data heterogeneity on larger, structurally rich graphs, while the total number of transmitted messages is reduced by $50\%$ compared to naive broadcasting.

</details>


### [192] [Patch-Based Deep Kernel Learning for Data Assimilation and Bias Correction with Extremely Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41596)
*Emanuele Mele,Angelo Coluccia,Massimo Cafaro,Italo Epicoco,Paola Nassisi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CNN+可学习核高斯过程混合框架，从极稀疏观测联合完成数据同化与偏差校正，兼得MAP分析与不确定度，训练O(N)、推理O(m³)，性能优于3D-Var等方法且推理提速98%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变分同化(如3D-Var)依赖手工构造的背景误差协方差，假设误差无偏，无法刻画状态相关相关性与系统性模型偏差，且代价随域规模增长；深度学习方法虽能非线性同化与偏差校正，但可解释性差、缺乏校准的不确定度。

Method: 将卷积神经网络(CNN)与可学习核高斯过程(GP)耦合的混合框架：端到端最大化边缘对数似然训练，在全网格上学习数据驱动协方差，直接输出MAP分析与不确定度；采用分块(patch)策略把精确GP的O(N³)训练成本降为固定块大小下的O(N)；推理仍为精确GP，仅需解线性系统，成本O(m³)取决于观测数m≪N；学到的协方差可替换3D-Var中的手工协方差而不改变变分形式。

Result: 在海表温度与盐度、合成与真实极稀疏现场观测实验中，框架优于3D-Var、经典GP及竞争性深度学习模型，同时相对3D-Var将推理时间降低98%以上。

Conclusion: 以CNN+可学习核GP的混合设计可在保持GP式概率化不确定度与可解释性的同时实现高效的大尺度数据同化与偏差校正，且其学到的协方差可作为3D-Var的即插即用改进模块。

Abstract: Data Assimilation (DA) reconstructs the state of a spatio-temporal system by combining sensor observations with the output of a numerical model. Since model states are high-dimensional while observations cover only a small fraction of the domain, DA is a challenging large-scale estimation problem, central to weather forecasting and oceanography. Classical variational methods such as 3D-Var rely on a hand-crafted background error covariance that assumes unbiased errors, and therefore cannot capture state-dependent correlations or systematic model biases. Moreover, their cost grows with the size of the domain. Deep learning (DL) methods can perform DA and bias correction (BC) non-linearly, but typically offer limited interpretability and no calibrated uncertainty estimates. We propose a hybrid framework that couples a convolutional neural network (CNN) with a learnable-kernel Gaussian Process (GP) to jointly perform DA and BC from extremely sparse observations. The model is trained end-to-end by maximizing the marginal log-likelihood, learns a data-driven covariance over the whole grid, and provides both the Maximum A Posteriori analysis and its uncertainty by design. A patch-based strategy reduces the $O(N^3)$ training cost of exact GPs over $N$ grid points to $O(N)$ for a fixed patch size. Inference remains exact and only requires solving a linear system whose $O(m^3)$ cost depends on the number of observations $m \ll N$, not on the domain. The learned covariance can also replace the hand-crafted one in 3D-Var, improving its accuracy without changing the variational formulation. Experiments on sea surface temperature and salinity, with both synthetic and real, extremely sparse in-situ observations, show that the framework outperforms 3D-Var, a classical GP and a competing DL model, while reducing inference time by more than 98% with respect to 3D-Var.

</details>

Abstract: Agent systems built on local large language models are increasingly marketed through the vocabulary of cognitive architecture: modules named global workspace, theory of mind, causal reasoning, or active inference are listed as features, and the presence of the name is taken as the presence of the mechanism. We ask the prior question -- does the named computation actually happen -- and contribute a transferable verification method for the claim-implementation gap: a four-question static audit protocol with line-number evidence and call-chain tracing, demonstrated on two systems. We audit a system claiming 25 cognitive mechanisms, pre-register falsifiable predictions for 11 modules and execute the interventions, replicate the protocol on the IIT-phi core of a second, independent system with its own pre-committed falsification criteria, and run two repair experiments as positive controls. In 18 of 25 modules the computed value never reaches any production output; three idle-wiring shapes new to the dead-code and code-smell literatures emerge (connected-but-never-called, called-but-inert, called-but-content-blind). Of 61 frozen criteria across 12 interventions, 53 were confirmed, two partially adjudicated, five falsified, one adjudication-ambiguous. Repairs raised planner task success from 0.028 to 1.000 and cut state-controller error by 52%. Module names and module computations must be audited as independent claims; the protocol and the five-class failure taxonomy are reusable for the claim-implementation gap at two-system, case-level depth.

</details>


### [194] [What Should LLMs Forget: Reinforcement Unlearning of Knowledge Associations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41616)
*Wutong Xu,Yun Qu,Haiyan Feng,Qi Wang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) acquire extensive factual knowledge during pre-training, which may include sensitive, private, or copyrighted information, motivating the development of unlearning methods that selectively remove specific knowledge while preserving general capabilities. However, existing approaches typically optimize entire responses without explicitly identifying the knowledge to be forgotten, resulting in coarse and uncontrolled forgetting. To address this, we revisit the forgetting target of LLM unlearning and introduce Knowledge Association (KA), which characterizes the association between a query context and its target knowledge and is instantiated by the corresponding conditional predictive distribution, as the fundamental target of forgetting. Based on KA, we propose Knowledge Association Reinforcement Unlearning (KARU), a unified reinforcement learning framework that suppresses forgotten knowledge associations while preserving retained ones. Specifically, KARU derives complementary mechanisms from the KA-based formulation: token-level reward construction for finer-grained forgetting supervision, distribution-level regularization for precise knowledge suppression, and proximity-aware retain sampling to mitigate collateral degradation. Experiments across diverse LLMs on representative benchmarks demonstrate that KARU consistently improves forgetting effectiveness over existing baselines while maintaining comparable retention performance and general utility.

</details>


### [195] [DAMI: Dual-Axis Memory Initialization for Enhanced Long-Context Retrieval in Gated Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41994)
*Zhangchi Feng,Harry Jiang,Yangyifei Luo,Ray Zhang,Richong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Linear attention scales linearly with sequence length, but its finite-state memory can limit long-context retrieval. In gated linear recurrences, memory retention depends on state decay, which is shaped jointly by gate parameters A_log and dt_bias and by input activations. In controlled Gated DeltaNet (GDN) experiments, both parameters remain highly correlated with their initial values after training, highlighting the importance of initialization. Therefore, we introduce Dual-Axis Memory Initialization (DAMI), which jointly configures these parameters before training. DAMI assigns geometric timescales across heads for varied retrieval distances and a low-high-low input-sensitivity profile across layers. A small activation sample calibrates the GDN-family implementation to match both behavioral targets without adding trainable parameters. Across GDN, KDA, GDN2, Mamba-2, and RWKV-7, DAMI improves average RULER-style retrieval accuracy by 5.29 to 16.71 percentage points over the corresponding random-initialization baselines. Performance on language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and 2K real-world retrieval remains broadly comparable to those baselines. Our code is available at https://speedai.com/research/dami.

</details>


### [196] [Chance-Constrained Inspection Viewpoint Sequencing for On-Orbit Servicing via Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41639)
*Chloé Carer,Adam Abdin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将航天器近距离观测的视点选择建模为CMDP，用PPO+拉格朗日松弛求解，并以PID控制的自适应拉格朗日乘子实现机会约束，在保证覆盖与Δv预算的同时显著降低禁飞区违规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主在轨服务需先对目标航天器进行近距离观测，而观测视点序列规划是受覆盖、燃料与安全多重约束的组合优化问题；导航与机动误差使实际安全间距具有随机性，传统"固定安全裕度+确定性规划"需逐案调参且风险不可控。

Method: 视点选择建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程，采用带拉格朗日松弛的PPO求解；采用分层结构：高层规划器在目标周围观测球面上安排相机视点，低层模型基于Clohessy–Wiltshire方程评估每次转移；单脉冲可达性与Δv上限等确定性约束直接施加于动作集；针对随机安全间距，对实际轨迹施加机会约束，其拉格朗日乘子由PID控制调节以在训练中自适应惩罚；仿真以MEO伽利略级导航卫星为对象，点云建模，视点可见性考虑视场与视线遮挡。

Result: 在多种风险预算下，训练所得策略将禁飞区违规率控制在规定上限内，覆盖度接近最大值且满足任务Δv预算；相较于对禁飞半径施加固定裕度的确定性规划，在相近覆盖下违规率不到其一半；碰撞风险成为显式设计参数，无需逐案重调；策略单次前向即可选定视点，无需贪心机会约束规划器在每个决策点重复进行蒙特卡洛风险估计。

Conclusion: 带机会约束的强化学习框架为受约束的近距离观测视点规划提供了风险可控、计算高效的方案，将安全风险转化为可设计的显式参数。

Abstract: Autonomous inspection of a target spacecraft is typically the initial step in on-orbit servicing. Planning close-proximity observation viewpoints involves a complex combinatorial problem constrained by coverage, fuel, and safety considerations. This paper models the viewpoint selection as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP) and applies Proximal Policy Optimization with Lagrangian relaxation to solve it. The system combines a high-level planner, which sequences camera viewpoints on an observation sphere around the target, with a low-level model that assesses each transfer using the Clohessy--Wiltshire (CW) equations for relative motion. The deterministic constraints on single-impulse reachability and $Δv$ cap are enforced directly on the action set. Safety margins are affected by navigation and manoeuvre errors, making the actual clearance stochastic. To address this, a chance constraint on the realised trajectory is implemented, with a Lagrangian multiplier governed by PID control to adapt the penalty during training. The approach is tested on a Galileo-class navigation satellite in medium Earth orbit, modelled as a point cloud where visibility from each viewpoint considers field-of-view and line-of-sight occlusion. Across various risk budgets, the trained policy maintains the keep-out violation rate within the specified limit, achieving coverage close to its maximum and staying within the mission $Δv$ budget. Compared to the usual practice of applying a fixed margin to the keep-out radius and planning deterministically, the chance-constrained policy attains less than half the violation rate at comparable coverage. This makes the collision risk an explicit design parameter, eliminating the need for re-tuning per case. The learned policy selects a viewpoint without the repeated Monte Carlo risk estimates required by a greedy, chance-constrained planner at every decision point.

</details>


### [197] [Risk-Sensitive Mission Planning for Multi-Target On-Orbit Inspection via Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41647)
*Adam Abdin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于分布式强化学习（QR-DQN）的自适应多目标在轨服务任务规划方法，学习回报分布并以均值-方差准则实现风险敏感决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标在轨服务任务需适应动态运行条件并处理安全关键航天飞行中国有的结果不确定性，需支持显式风险敏感规划的工具。

Method: 构建圆轨道、倾角分散且采用Edelbaum联合机动低推力转移估计的任务环境；观测含轨道要素、访问历史、目标风险状态与剩余时间预算；风险状态随机演化。QR-DQN智能体依据预测回报的期望与方差（均值-方差准则）选择下一目标，并因时间预算限制需同时筛选访问子集。通过枚举可行访问次序精确计算高效前沿进行验证。

Result: 五目标仿真中，单一风险厌恶权重可在环境与奖励不变下使策略遍历不同均值回报与回报方差；风险厌恶智能体将回合回报方差约减半并降低任务损失概率，代价是均值回报下降；等方差下学习策略接近高效前沿，且风险厌恶策略的任务损失概率低于前沿策略。

Conclusion: 分布式强化学习可有效支持多目标任务的风险敏感自适应规划，单一风险参数即可灵活调节风险-收益权衡并优于精确前沿在安全性上的表现。

Abstract: Multi-target on-orbit servicing missions require planning tools that can adapt to evolving operational conditions while accounting for the outcome variability inherent to safety-critical spaceflight. This paper presents an adaptive planning approach for multi-target servicing campaigns in low Earth orbit and applies it to inspection missions flown by a low-thrust servicer. The approach is based on Distributional Reinforcement Learning, which learns the full distribution of mission returns and thereby supports explicit risk-sensitive decision-making at each planning step. Specifically, a Quantile-Regression Deep Q-Network (QR-DQN) agent selects which target to inspect next by jointly considering the expectation and the variance of the predicted return through a mean-variance criterion. The mission environment models circular orbits with a spread in inclination and uses Edelbaum's combined-manoeuvre low-thrust transfer estimates. The observation comprises the orbital elements, the visit history, the observed risk state of each target and the remaining mission-time budget. The risk state of each target changes stochastically during the campaign, making the outcome of an inspection uncertain and creating operationally meaningful distinctions between risk-neutral and risk-averse planning strategies. Because the mission-time budget generally admits only a subset of the candidate targets, the planner must also select which targets to inspect. Numerical experiments on scenarios with five target satellites show that one risk-aversion weight moves the policy across a range of mean return and return variance, over an unchanged environment and reward. Risk-averse agents reduce the variance of the episodic return by approximately one half relative to risk-neutral agents and lower the probability of a mission loss, at a cost in mean return. To validate the learned policies, the efficient frontier, which gives the highest mean return attainable at each level of return variance, is computed exactly by enumerating the admissible visit orders. At equal variance, the learned policies lie close to this frontier in mean return, and the risk-averse policies have a lower probability of a mission loss than the frontier policies.

</details>


### [198] [The Knowledge Layer Model (KLM): Evidence-Visible AI Output, Routing Diagnostics, and Negative Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42066)
*Omer Asim Ozturk,Cihan Aydiner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出知识层模型（KLM）——一种开放、与实现无关的推理记录规范，包含版本化的认知评估、证据关系与显式未知项；SEDIM 作为参考实现。作者报告若干内部实验结果，但强调其非端到端验证，证据可见性不等于正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 回答仅"表面上正确"不足够，审查者需要检查其记录的依据（基据）。为此需要一种标准化、可审计的推理记录格式来承载证据与认知状态。

Method: 提出 KLM（引用 v0.3 版本，仓库 tag v0.3.0）规范，以 SEDIM 为参考实现；通过若干独立内部实验进行评估：历史平台域路由基准、原生模型权重路径配置测试、容量增长对比、词法与范围诊断（AUC）、旧版 vs 修复版打包的文本保留率、Python/TypeScript 验证器一致性检查。

Result: 路由基准 137/150（91.3%，Wilson 95% 置信区间 85.7–94.9%，分子由取整摘要反推、样例归属未核实）；原生权重路径 修正双源测试 0/15 成功；容量增长对比因评分器缺陷与原始输出缺失无法确立效应；词法与范围诊断 AUC 均为 0.008，远低于注册下限 0.65；旧版打包在 118/221 例中丢失含金标文本，修复版 221/221 全部保留（仅测文本存在性）；Python/TypeScript 验证器在合成夹具上六项检查一致，生产披露未获接受。

Conclusion: 结果均为内部、作者自报，历史观测与后续复现被明确区分。证据可见性并不确立正确性、因果依赖、安全性或合规性；多项评估（还原、再推导、人工评分支持、边界隔离、有界响应）仍属提议；独立复现与完整公开审计包为未来工作。

Abstract: AI answers require more than apparent correctness: reviewers need to inspect their recorded basis. We introduce the Knowledge Layer Model (KLM), an open, implementation-neutral specification for inference records with versioned epistemic assessments, evidence relations, and explicit unknowns. SEDIM, the knowledge and evidence architecture of Nage AI OS, is a reference implementation. We reference KLM v0.3 (repository tag v0.3.0). Empirical material consists of separate internal experiments, not end-to-end validation. A historical platform-domain routing benchmark reports 137/150 classifications across 15 classes (91.3%; nominal 95% Wilson interval 85.7-94.9%); the numerator is recovered from a rounded summary, and historical example membership is unverified. A native-model weight-path configuration yielded 0/15 successful answers in a corrected two-source test. A capacity-growth comparison could not establish a reliable effect because of a defective legacy scorer and missing original outputs. Lexical and scoped diagnostics each yielded AUC 0.008 for silent-versus-contradicted examples, below the registered minimum of 0.65. Legacy packing lost gold-containing text in 118/221 selected cases; repaired packing retained it in 221/221. These measure text presence, not answer quality. Python and TypeScript verifiers reportedly agreed on six checks per synthetic fixture; a separate production disclosure was not accepted. Restoration, re-derivation, human-rubric support, boundary isolation, and bounded-response evaluations remain proposed. Findings are internal and author-reported; historical observations are distinguished from later reproductions. Evidence visibility does not establish correctness, causal reliance, security, or regulatory compliance. Independent replication and a complete public audit package remain future work.

</details>


### [199] [Breaking Information Islands in Sparse Tuning via Small-World Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41665)
*Xianchao Guan,Zijun Xiong,Yifeng Wang,Yubo Cui,Yaowei Wang,Guo-Sen Xie,Xin Li,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SWIFT稀疏微调框架，用块对角局部混合加固定散射置换实现"小世界"连接，消除信息孤岛，性能超越PEFT基线并可比肩全量微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏微调的效果不仅取决于可训练参数量，还取决于稀疏更新结构支撑上的特征交互拓扑。支撑连接有限或不均匀会导致部分特征维度被隔离，形成"信息孤岛"，限制通过可训练更新进行任务特定的直接交互。

Method: SWIFT由两部分组成：(1) 块对角稀疏更新，实现局部特征混合；(2) 固定的确定性散射置换，将混合输出跨特征组路由，且不引入额外可训练参数。理论上证明某些随机稀疏更新模型与块对角更新会出现信息孤岛，而当可训练块对角因子为等尺寸、结构稠密的方阵时，充分散射的置换可保证正的块级扩展性。

Result: 在常识推理、自然语言生成与图像分类任务上的大量实验表明，SWIFT平均优于所评估的PEFT基线，并可匹配或超越全量微调。

Conclusion: 通过在块对角局部混合基础上施加固定散射置换，可在参数量不变前提下获得小世界式连接、避免信息孤岛，是一种简单有效的稀疏微调方案；代码已开源。

Abstract: Sparse tuning is widely used to adapt large language models due to its parameter efficiency. However, its effectiveness depends not only on the number of trainable parameters, but also on the feature-interaction topology of the sparse update's structural support. Limited or uneven connectivity in this support may isolate subsets of feature dimensions, forming information islands that restrict direct task-specific interactions through the trainable update. To address this issue, we propose Small-World Induced Fine-Tuning (SWIFT), a sparse tuning framework inspired by small-world connectivity. SWIFT combines block-diagonal sparse updates for local feature mixing with a fixed deterministic scattering permutation that routes the mixed outputs across feature groups without additional trainable parameters. We further show theoretically that certain random sparse-update models and block-diagonal updates can exhibit information islands, whereas a sufficiently scattering permutation guarantees positive block-level expansion when the trainable block-diagonal factor consists of equal-sized, structurally dense square blocks. Extensive experiments on commonsense reasoning, natural language generation, and image classification demonstrate that SWIFT outperforms the evaluated parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) baselines on average and can match or surpass full fine-tuning. Implementation is available at https://github.com/xianchaoguan/SWIFT

</details>


### [200] [Radial Kernel Attention for Global Feature Exchange in Polymer Machine Learning Force Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41675)
*Wenbo Fu,Chenghui Mao,Chengwen Hu,Haifeng Lv,Zhou Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出径向核注意力（Radial Kernel Attention, RKA），用于聚合物机器学习力场中的长程建模：将原子到簇质心的距离映射为可学习径向特征并与内容相关的 query/key 做核化重叠，配合因子化聚合与读出，在固定特征维度下以关于原子数的线性复杂度实现全局特征交换；将其不变标量更新插入 EquiformerV3 局部模块之间，通过能量对（含径向坐标的）全微分得到保守力。在 OPoly26 上，5M 匹配对比使每原子能量与力 MAE 分别降低 67.4% 与 36.3%；20M 模型达到总能量 MAE 15.81 meV（0.1182 meV/atom）与力 MAE 2.619 meV/Å，三项指标均优于 UMA-S-1.2/1.2.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚合物力场多依赖局部邻居图，跨链及其周围环境的信息传递受限；而显式全对注意力带来 O(N²) 的计算开销，难以兼顾长程建模与可扩展性。

Method: RKA 将原子到簇质心的原子间距离映射为学习到的径向特征，并与内容相关的 query/key 计算核化重叠，通过因子化聚合与读出在固定特征维度下实现关于原子数的线性复杂度全局交换；为保证物理一致性，将不变标量更新插入 EquiformerV3 局部模块之间，并对总能量（含其对径向坐标的依赖）求导获得保守力。

Result: 在 OPoly26 官方训练集训练 12 个 epoch，并在完整官方验证集上直接评估：5M 规模匹配对比下每原子能量 MAE 降低 67.4%、力 MAE 降低 36.3%；20M RKA 模型总能量 MAE 为 15.81 meV（0.1182 meV/atom），力 MAE 为 2.619 meV/Å，三项指标在同一验证结构上均优于 UMA-S-1.2 与 UMA-S-1.2.1。

Conclusion: RKA 为聚合物机器学习力场提供了超越局部图连通性的紧凑信息交换途径，在保持线性复杂度与物理一致性的同时显著提升能量与力预测精度。

Abstract: We introduce Radial Kernel Attention (RKA) for long-range modeling in polymer machine learning force fields. Existing approaches often rely on local neighbor graphs, which limit communication across polymer chains and their surrounding environments, whereas explicit all-pair attention incurs quadratic computational cost. RKA maps interatomic distances from the cluster centroid to learned radial features and combines their kernelized overlap with content-dependent queries and keys. Factorized aggregation and readout enable global feature exchange at linear complexity in the number of atoms for fixed feature dimensions. To ensure physically consistent predictions, we insert the invariant scalar update between EquiformerV3 local blocks and obtain conservative forces by differentiating the total energy, including its dependence on radial coordinate . Our models are trained on official OPoly26 training set for 12 epochs and evaluated directly after training on the complete official validation set. A matched 5M comparison shows that RKA reduces per-atom energy and force MAEs by 67.4% and 36.3%, respectively. The 20M RKA model attains a total-energy MAE of 15.81 meV (per-atom MAE of 0.1182 meV/atom) and a force MAE of 2.619 meV/Angstrom,improving all three metrics over UMA-S-1.2 and UMA-S-1.2.1 on the same validation structures. RKA provides a compact pathway for information exchange beyond local graph connectivity in polymer machine learning force fields.

</details>


### [201] [Advancing Clinical Trial Outcome Prediction with Domain-Specific Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41710)
*Burak Bakkar,Hana Moalla,Mohamed Salem Jedidi,Haifa Ben Messaoud,Nicolas Lopez Carranza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 

Method: 

Result: 

Conclusion: 

Abstract: Clinical trial failures are a major barrier to efficient drug development, incurring substantial costs and delaying patient access to effective therapies. Structured approaches such as the Hierarchical Interaction Network (HINT) predict trial outcomes by explicitly modelling interactions between drugs, diseases and protocols, but make limited use of the free text in trial registrations. We evaluate two domain-specific language models, BioBERT (108M parameters) and BioMistral (7B parameters), for phase-level outcome prediction on the TOP benchmark (12,477 trials), representing each trial as text built from ten ClinicalTrials.gov fields. We compare fine-tuning (full fine-tuning for BioBERT, low-rank adaptation for BioMistral) with probing, in which the pretrained model is frozen and only a lightweight classifier is trained on its embeddings. On the held-out test set of each phase, all four language-model variants achieved higher ROC-AUC (0.613--0.772) and PR-AUC (0.649--0.901) than the published HINT results, corresponding to relative PR-AUC gains of 10--20\%. F1 differences were small and mixed, and no model, including HINT, clearly exceeded the F1 of a trivial classifier that predicts success for every trial. Under the configurations tested, probing performed comparably to fine-tuning: fine-tuned models scored highest on ROC-AUC and PR-AUC in Phases I and II, and frozen BioMistral embeddings in Phase III (ROC-AUC 0.772; PR-AUC 0.901), although most differences between variants were below 0.03 and were not tested for significance. These results suggest that pretrained biomedical representations already encode signal relevant to trial outcome, and that probing is a computationally cheaper alternative to fine-tuning for this task.

</details>


### [202] [Multimodal In-Context Learning with Explicit Rule Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41754)
*Yuquan Li,Xinyang Jiang,Yuhao Xue,Cairong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ERIM提出"显式规则归纳"的多模态上下文学习框架：借助MLLM推理能力构建以规则为中心的数据集、引入规则归纳的显式监督，并用"混合冷启动SFT+强化学习"两阶段训练微调MLLM，在多种MICL设置下取得稳定提升并增强可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态上下文学习（MICL）中的核心挑战在于：部分任务在缺乏上下文时定义不清，需从演示样本中挖掘潜在规则。现有方法多将MICL视为直接的输入-输出映射，忽视了对规则的显式归纳与推理，因而限制了性能与可解释性。

Method: 提出ERIM统一框架：1）利用MLLM推理能力构建以规则为中心的MICL数据集；2）为规则归纳引入显式监督；3）采用两阶段训练策略微调MLLM——混合冷启动监督微调（SFT）结合强化学习，使其执行显式规则归纳。

Result: 在多样化的MICL设置中实验均取得一致的性能提升；并验证正确归纳潜在规则可直接带来更强、更稳定的性能表现。

Conclusion: 显式规则归纳是提升MICL性能与可解释性的有效路径，规则归纳正确性与下游性能正相关，为MICL从"隐式映射"转向"规则驱动推理"提供范式参考。

Abstract: A central challenge in multimodal in-context learning (MICL) arises from tasks that are ill-defined without context and require uncovering the underlying rules from demonstrations. Existing methods predominantly treat MICL as direct input-output mapping, overlooking the importance of explicitly inducing and reasoning over such rules, limiting both performance and interpretability. We propose ERIM, Explicit Rule-Induction for Multimodal In-Context Learning, a unified framework that enhances MICL by explicitly inducing the latent rules governing demonstration samples. Leveraging the reasoning ability of multi-modal large language model (MLLM), ERIM constructs rule-centric MICL datasets, introduces explicit supervision for rule induction, and fine-tunes MLLM to perform explicit rule-induction via a two-stage training strategy that combines mixed cold-start supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments show consistent performance improvements across diverse MICL settings and demonstrate that correctly inducing underlying rules directly leads to stronger and more stable performance.

</details>


### [203] [Clinical-Protocol-Aware Cyber Resilience for Connected Clinical Technologies Under AI-Enabled Adversarial Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41784)
*Pranav Patel,Siva Kannathasan,Prerak Patel,Jigar Patel,Vansh Patel,Sheel Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于有界临床微网络与受控互联的临床协议感知网络韧性框架，并结合参数化蒙特卡洛仿真区分技术暴露与临床后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗日益依赖互联医疗设备、临床信息系统、云服务与分布式照护技术，临床工作流中的网络风险上升；AI能力加速漏洞发现与攻击路径分析，使限制失陷传播更为迫切，而现有安全设计缺乏对临床因素的整合。

Method: 设计将临床因素纳入网络安全设计、管理与评估的框架，覆盖预防、遏制与事件响应；采用参数化蒙特卡洛仿真，区分技术性网络暴露与建模的临床后果；实验考察架构连通性、对抗能力、安全控制、协议依赖性、风险对齐、通信方向性、生命周期集成与响应时序等维度。

Result: 在仿真假设下，互联架构较分段架构使平均网络暴露提高56.3%；预防与遏制组合相较无控制使暴露降低60.1%；提高协议依赖性使建模临床影响指数由1.021升至7.567而平均网络暴露不变，表明技术暴露与临床后果可背离；风险对齐实验显示技术风险与临床重要性同向时的建模临床影响更高。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持架构对比分析而非真实世界网络风险、控制有效性或患者伤害的校准估计；该框架为互联医疗环境中的安全边界设计、控制优先级排序与协同遏制提供临床情境感知方法。

Abstract: Healthcare increasingly relies on connected medical devices, clinical information systems, cloud services, and distributed-care technologies, creating cybersecurity risks across clinical workflows. AI-enabled capabilities can accelerate vulnerability discovery and attack-path analysis, increasing the need to limit compromise propagation. This paper proposes a clinical-protocol-aware cyber-resilience framework based on bounded clinical micro-networks and controlled interconnections. The framework integrates clinical factors into cybersecurity design, management, and evaluation, covering prevention, containment, and incident response. A parameterized Monte Carlo simulation distinguishes technical cyber exposure from modeled clinical consequence. Experiments investigate architectural connectivity, adversarial capability, security controls, protocol dependency, risk alignment, communication directionality, lifecycle integration, and response timing. Under the simulation assumptions, connected architecture increases mean cyber exposure by 56.3% relative to segmented architecture, while combined prevention and containment reduce exposure by 60.1% relative to no controls. Increasing protocol dependency raises the modeled clinical-impact index from 1.021 to 7.567 without changing mean cyber exposure, demonstrating that technical exposure and clinical consequence can diverge. Risk-alignment experiments further indicate higher modeled clinical impact when greater technical risk corresponds to greater clinical significance. The findings support comparative architectural analysis, not calibrated estimates of real-world cyber risk, control effectiveness, or patient harm. The framework provides a clinical-context-aware approach to security-boundary design, control prioritization, and coordinated containment in connected healthcare environments.

</details>


### [204] [Whose Values, Which Contexts, and What Signals? Grounding Cultural Value Alignment with Re3Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41793)
*Jing Yao,Hyunjin Hwang,Weihua Zheng,Bryan Chen Zhengyu Tan,Jia Wang Peh,Dylan Raharja,JinYeong Bak,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Rui Cao,Yuki Arase,Jacki O'Neill,Zhicheng Dou,Xing Xie,Xiaoyuan Yi,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Re3Culture是一个大规模人类证据数据集，围绕"代表谁、在哪观察、何种信号"三问构建（700+参与者四文化、社区挑选的场景、1,400+价值问卷与20,000+行为原则及6,000+情境问题回应），为文化价值对齐的目标定义提供首个以人类证据为基础的资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化价值对齐不仅依赖模型优化，还需一个基于真实人类证据、明确界定的对齐目标；然而该目标应如何在人类数据中落地尚缺乏探索，由此引出三个基本问题：应代表谁的价值观、应在何处观察文化价值、何种信号能揭示内在价值。

Method: 构建Re3Culture数据集，包含三个环节：(i) 代表性社区——通过文化归属筛选与人口学感知抽样招募四个文化的700余名参与者；(ii) 现实场景——由社区成员遴选的文化敏感场景（如家庭与亲属关系）；(iii) 外显价值信号——1,400余份价值问卷、20,000余条话题锚定的行为原则与显式价值优先级、以及6,000余道情境问题的回应；并进行对照分析（替代其他文化、限制子群覆盖、LLM模拟、抽象问卷画像与情境优先级比较等）。

Result: 分析表明：(1) 估计文化价值需同时具备社区归属与充分的内部覆盖，替换文化或限制子群覆盖会改变价值估计，LLM模拟亦不成比例地反映特定子群；(2) 价值须在具体的、由社区引导的情境中观察，抽象问卷画像难以还原情境优先级，且不同社区对"哪些场景能揭示文化价值"的判断存在差异；(3) 价值优先级应显式引出，而非仅从回答偏好或底线式答案中学习——偏好会把价值认同与其他回答层面属性相混淆，相似答案也可能源自不同的价值优先级。

Conclusion: 通过满足这些要求，Re3Culture提供了首个以人类证据为基础、可显式指导文化价值对齐目标定义的资源。

Abstract: Cultural value alignment requires not only model optimization but also a well-specified target grounded in real-human evidence. Yet how such targets should be grounded in human data remains largely unexplored, raising three fundamental questions: (1) whose values should be represented? (2) where should cultural values be observed? and (3) what signals reveal underlying values? We investigate these questions through Re3Culture, a large-scale dataset linking (i) Representative communities: 700+ human participants across four cultures recruited through cultural-affiliation screening and demographic-aware sampling; (ii) Realistic scenario: culturally sensitive scenarios selected by community members, e.g., family and kinship; and (iii) Revealed value signals: 1,400+ value surveys, 20,000+ topic-grounded behavioral principles and explicit value priorities with responses for 6,000+ situated questions. Our analysis shows why these choices matter for defining the target, with three implications. First, estimating cultural values requires both community affiliation and broad internal coverage. Substituting other cultures or restricting subgroup coverage shifts value estimates. LLM simulations also disproportionately reflect particular subgroups. Second, values must be observed in concrete, community-guided situations. Abstract questionnaire profiles weakly recover situated priorities, and communities differ in which scenarios they identify as culturally value-revealing. Third, value priorities should be elicited explicitly, not learned from response preferences or bottom-line answers alone. Preferences conflate value agreement with other response-level qualities, and similar answers can be derived from different value priorities. By addressing these requirements, Re3Culture provides the first human-grounded resource that explicitly informs target definition for cultural value alignment.

</details>


### [205] [RECAST: REconstruction of Chain-of-Thought via Auditable Structured Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41813)
*Qi Wang,Kang Xu,Chengcheng Wan,Jiangtao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RECAST 框架：在无隐藏推理通道的仅答案协议下，通过条件解码重建闭源前沿模型的思维链，并生成可用于 SFT 的数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭源推理模型仅暴露最终答案，现有 CoT 抽取方法依赖可见推理字段、工具调用草稿板或签名/加密推理块等侧信道；作者复现三种 SOTA 抽取方法后发现，在 Claude-Sonnet-5.5 标准接口上均返回零 CoT token，且失败是结构性的，因此需要一条不依赖隐藏推理通道的新路径。

Method: 先复现三种代表性抽取方法以验证侧信道缺失；再提出 RECAST：以问题与目标模型最终答案为条件，驱动解码器重建第一人称结构化推理依据（rationale），经清洗后构建 SFT-ready 数据；系统开源并封装为 agent skill，单条 prompt 即可部署并跑通完整流水线。

Result: 三种抽取方法在 Claude-Sonnet-5.5 的 chat-completions 接口上均输出 0 token CoT；RECAST 在 Claude-Sonnet-5.5 上完成实例化，并在 GPT-6.1、Gemini 3 上以相同仅答案协议获得成功的定性重建。论文自述为预印本，仅报告系统、威胁模型与受控的产物验证，不声称大规模基准提升。

Conclusion: 该工作主张是首个无需隐藏推理通道即从前沿闭源模型重建 CoT 的系统，强调工程可用性与数据产出（开源、agent skill 化），但缺乏定量基准证据与重建保真度评估，属系统描述性预印本。

Abstract: Large closed-source reasoning models expose only their final answers. The intermediate reasoning that produces those answers is hidden behind API surfaces, tool-call policies, and safety filters. Existing chain-of-thought (CoT) extraction methods assume a side channel: a visible reasoning field, a scratchpad carried through tool calls, or a signed/encrypted reasoning block. We reproduce three representative state-of-the-art extraction methods and find that, on Claude-Sonnet-5.5 accessed through the standard chat-completions interface, all three return zero CoT tokens. The failure is structural rather than a bug: the reasoning field is absent, named tool selection is rejected, free-text scratchpad arguments are filtered, and no replayable reasoning block is exposed. We therefore take a different route. We present RECAST, a reconstruction-based framework that conditions a decoder on the question and the target model's final answer, reconstructs a first-person structured rationale, cleans it, and builds SFT-ready data. To the best of our knowledge, RECAST is the first system to reconstruct CoT from frontier closed models such as Claude-Sonnet-5.5, GPT-6.1, and Gemini 3 without using hidden reasoning channels. We instantiate RECAST on Claude-Sonnet-5.5 and additionally obtain successful qualitative reconstructions on GPT-6.1 and Gemini 3 with the same answer-only protocol. The system is open-source and packaged as an agent skill: one prompt deploys it and runs the complete pipeline to produce SFT-ready CoT data. This preprint describes the system, its threat model, and a controlled artifact validation; it does not claim large-scale benchmark improvements.

</details>


### [206] [ReuScope: Auditing Data Reuse and Evaluation Sensitivity in Capsule Vision 2024](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41835)
*Eunseob Choi,Kyeonghun Kim,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReuScope协议审计数据复用与评测敏感性，以Capsule Vision 2024为例：公共训练-验证分区存在字节级相同文件与源录像交叉重叠，移除Kvasir-Capsule相关训练行使验证平衡准确率从82.5%降至61.2%，但该下降反映的是对来源移除的敏感性而非泄漏导致的虚高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据复用在公开基准中普遍存在，但"存在重叠"与"重叠导致评测分数虚高"常被混为一谈。论文旨在提供一套可复用的审计协议，把观测到的数据复用与条件化的性能敏感性区分开，并明确每个结论所对应的总体、类别支撑与不确定性单位。

Method: ReuScope协议结合数据关系测量与两类对比实验：(1)固定预测、改变评测总体；(2)固定评测、改变训练数据。具体包括：跨公共训练-验证边界的字节级相同文件检测、基于源录像的跨分区重叠识别、10组配对随机种子的来源移除实验、匹配对照组（控制样本量与类别计数），以及探索性实验——移除验证帧在训练集中近邻或等量远邻，采用相对匹配未标记帧的双重差分估计。

Result: 41个参与帧贡献的Kvasir-Capsule录像全部同时出现在训练与验证分区；因缺少跨录像患者标识，无法统计唯一共享患者数。10组配对种子下，移除归属Kvasir-Capsule的全部训练行及来自其他来源的5个近匹配行，公共验证平衡准确率由82.5%降至61.2%；而将同一拟合模型在其余来源的验证行上评测时比较方向反转，移除使平衡准确率提升1.38个百分点。匹配对照显示样本量与类别计数不足以解释分数差异。探索性近邻移除实验的双重差分估计为真类概率−0.355个百分点，准确率的配对t区间跨越零。

Conclusion: 公共验证集的分数下降度量的是对特定来源移除的敏感性，而非泄漏所致虚高的量值。ReuScope能够区分观测到的数据复用与条件化性能敏感性，并为每项结论明确指定评测总体、类别支撑和不确定性单位，可作为数据集审慎发布的规范性流程。

Abstract: We develop ReuScope, a protocol for auditing data reuse and evaluation sensitivity, using Capsule Vision 2024 as a case study. It combines measurements of data relationships with two comparisons: changing the evaluation population while holding predictions fixed, and changing training data while holding evaluation fixed. Byte-identical files cross the public training-validation boundary, and all 41 Kvasir-Capsule recordings that contribute frames occur in both partitions. Shared source recordings establish cross-split overlap; missing cross-recording patient identifiers prevent counting unique shared patients. Across ten paired seeds, removing all training rows attributed to Kvasir-Capsule, plus five rows from other sources flagged as near-matches to it, lowers public-validation balanced accuracy from 82.5% to 61.2%. Evaluating the same fitted models on the validation rows of the other sources reverses the comparison: removal improves balanced accuracy by 1.38 percentage points. The public-validation drop measures sensitivity to source removal, not an amount of leakage-induced inflation. Matched controls show that sample size and class counts alone do not explain the observed score differences. An exploratory experiment removes the training neighbors of validation frames that have close training matches, or equally many distant training rows, and yields a difference-in-differences estimate, relative to matched unflagged frames, of -0.355 percentage points in true-class probability; the paired-t interval for accuracy spans zero.
