The resulting procedure distinguishes observed reuse from conditional performance sensitivity and specifies the population, class support and uncertainty unit for each conclusion.

</details>


### [207] [Dynamical Memory Transformer: Selective Recurrent Memories with Constant Cost Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41850)
*Yannis Bendi-Ouis,Xavier Hinaut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DMT是一种混合架构，将序列编码（复数对角循环的动力系统记忆单元）与信息读取（注意力解码器）分离，兼得O(T)扫描复杂度、固定规模推理缓存与较强长度外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 的 KV-Cache 随上下文线性增长且每生成一个新 token 的代价随长度上升；状态空间模型（SSM）虽将历史压缩为固定大小循环状态，但牺牲了对历史信息的可寻址性。作者希望同时保留固定规模状态与可读性。

Method: 提出 Dynamical Memory Transformer（DMT）：编码器由多个记忆单元构成，每个单元为具有复数对角循环矩阵的动力系统，其状态更新由输入相关的 leak rate 控制；解码器用注意力读取并组合这些单元的输出，而不回看 token 历史。该递归形式支持关联扫描，总计算量 O(T)、并行深度 O(log T)，且维度固定；自回归推理时循环状态与卷积缓存大小保持不变。实验上与 Mamba 和 Transformer 在认知任务与语言建模任务上对比。

Result: DMT 在多项认知任务上取得最佳结果；语言建模性能接近 Transformer，但落后于 Mamba；在 selective copy 任务上，其长度外推能力优于 Mamba 与 Transformer。

Conclusion: 并行动力记忆单元是一种在序列上保持并利用信息的有前景机制，但其语言建模能力仍待提升。

Abstract: Transformers retain an explicit history of the sequence, allowing attention to directly retrieve past representations. However, the size of the KV-Cache and the cost of a new token increase with the context length. State Space Models compress the past into a fixed-size recurrent state, at the cost of reduced addressability. We present the Dynamical Memory Transformer (DMT), a hybrid architecture that separates sequence encoding from information reading. The encoder uses several memory units, each implemented by a dynamical system with a complex diagonal recurrent matrix. An input-dependent leak rate controls their updates. The decoder then uses attention to read and combine their outputs, without going through the token history. The formulation of the recurrence allows an associative scan with O(T ) total work and O(log T ) parallel depth, with fixed dimensions. During autoregressive inference, the recurrent states and the convolution cache remain fixed in size. We compare DMT with Mamba and Transformer on cognitive and language modeling tasks, and show its strengths and limitations: DMT achieves the best results on several cognitive tasks and language modeling performance close to that of Transformer, but remains behind Mamba in language modeling. On selective copy, DMT also shows stronger length extrapolation than Mamba and Transformer. These results demonstrate that parallel dynamical memory units are a promising mechanism for retaining and using information over sequences.

</details>


### [208] [Learning to Interact and Direct: Co-Evolving Video Director Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41865)
*Yingzhao Jian,Zhongan Wang,Yuxuan Hou,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LIT框架，通过导演智能体、模拟用户与元工程师构建进化闭环，协同提升视频导演智能体的制作与交互能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频导演智能体的自进化研究主要聚焦剧本规划，忽略了"导演能力"与"用户交互"之间的相互促进关系：更好的交互能澄清用户意图并优化工作流，更可靠的执行则使协作从排错转向创意决策。

Method: LIT由三部分组成：导演智能体、模拟用户和元工程师。模拟用户分配创作任务以探测导演的弱点，元工程师利用任务轨迹修订导演的交互与制作策略。针对元智能体被动归因的局限，作者引入协同进化范式：元智能体主动学习外部知识以增强问题发现与解决能力，三者形成进化闭环。

Result: 在Test与OOD Test划分上，LIT相对初始流水线的制作分与交互分分别提升19.1%与32.0%。

Conclusion: 受控消融实验揭示了制作能力提升与交互能力提升之间的相关性，协同进化机制进一步促进导演技能与交互模式的探索。

Abstract: Video director agents orchestrate multistage production to realize users' creative visions. Emerging research on their self-evolution advances screenplay planning effectively. However, the mutual reinforcement between directing and user interaction remains largely overlooked. Improved interaction clarifies user intent and informs workflow refinement, while more reliable execution shifts collaboration from troubleshooting to creative decisions. To this end, we introduce LIT (Learning to Interact and DirecT), a framework comprises a director agent, a simulated user, and a meta engineer. Simulated user assigns creative tasks to probe the director's weaknesses, while the meta engineer uses the task traces to revise the director's interaction and production strategy. Although this approach can address discovered interaction and production issues, it still relies on passive attribution by the meta agent. This limits its ability to adapt to broader user interaction patterns and production techniques. To solve this problem, we equip LIT with a co-evolution paradigm. The meta agent proactively learns external knowledge, thereby improving its ability to discover problems and propose solutions. The meta agent, simulated user, and director together form an evolutionary closed loop, collaboratively enhances the system's directing and interaction capabilities. Across the Test and OOD Test splits, LIT improves production and Interaction scores by 19.1% and 32.0%, respectively, relative to the initial pipeline. Controlled ablations reveal the correlation between improvements in production and interaction, where the co-evolution mechanism further encourage directing skill and interaction mode exploration.

</details>


### [209] [Fuzzy selective state model for forecasting the dynamics of consumer preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41879)
*P. A. Golovinski,A. A. Drobyshev,A. O. Shatalova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种隐状态为三角模糊数的Mamba型选择性状态空间模型，在Matveev双分量模糊数代数下预测零售客户偏好动态，证明状态保持为真三角模糊数且为历史观测的凸组合，模糊性不随时间累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售连锁客户的偏好具有内在模糊性（"大致偏向某品牌"），传统清晰值模型无法刻画预测的不确定性程度；而递推式模糊模型的一个关键风险是模糊宽度随迭代不断累积，导致预测失去可解释性。因此需要在保持模糊语义与Zadeh扩展原理一致的前提下，构建可控、稳定的模糊动态预测器。

Method: 以三角模糊数描述客户偏好；用Mamba型选择性状态空间模型刻画偏好演化，隐状态取三角模糊数，运算在Matveev双分量模糊数代数中进行；递推参数由清晰特征计算，模糊部分保持非负系数线性结构。理论上证明状态始终为真三角模糊数且为过去观测的凸组合，从而模糊度有界，代数计算与Zadeh扩展原理等价。在含竞品促销与客户流失的合成数据上，与恒定惯性演化算子及朴素预测对比，评估中心值MAE，并给出模糊度的语言化输出用于流失检测。

Result: 107参数轻量模型预测中心值MAE为0.154，优于恒定惯性算子（0.163）与朴素预测（0.179）；性能与同架构清晰模型相当（不牺牲精度），并额外提供可语言解释的预测模糊度估计；语言化读数可用于识别客户向竞品的永久性转移。

Conclusion: 模糊Mamba型状态空间模型在凸组合结构约束下可实现"模糊度不累积"的稳定长程预测，兼具清晰模型的精度与模糊模型的解释性，适合零售客户偏好的可解释动态预测与流失预警。

Abstract: We consider forecasting the dynamics of consumer preferences of retail chain customers. A customer's preference is described by a triangular fuzzy number. The evolution of the preference over time is governed by a Mamba-type selective state space model whose hidden state is a triangular number, with computations carried out in M.G.~Matveev's two-component algebra of fuzzy numbers. The recurrence parameters are computed from crisp features, while the fuzzy part of the model remains linear with non-negative coefficients. We show that the state then always remains a proper triangular number and is a convex combination of past observations, so that the fuzziness of the forecast does not accumulate over time and the algebraic computations coincide with Zadeh's extension principle. On a synthetic example with competitor promotions and customer switching, a lightweight 107-parameter model reaches a mean absolute error of the forecast centre of 0.154 versus 0.163 for the constant-inertia evolution operator and 0.179 for the naive forecast, matches a crisp model of the same architecture and additionally provides a linguistically interpretable estimate of forecast vagueness. We also show how the linguistic readout of the forecast can be used to detect a permanent switch of a customer to a competitor.

</details>


### [210] [Automated Keyword Generation Using LLMs in a High-Volume Data Virtualization Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41893)
*Saiful Khan,Yiwen Xing,Joyraj Chakraborty,Afrah Farea,Philip Beaucamp,Samuel Jackson,Min Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出用大语言模型为数据虚拟化服务(DVS)中的虚拟数据集自动生成关键词，设计五种文本数据选取策略，在五个应用案例、三种LLM生成器与两种嵌入技术下评测，并据此实现多策略关键词生成服务(KGS)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DVS 中用户会创建大量虚拟数据集（用于分类、检测、分割、预测等任务），用户需频繁发现已有虚拟数据集以避免重复劳动；要使 DVS 具备有效搜索引擎，需为每个虚拟数据集自动生成关键词。

Method: 基于 LLM 的关键词生成方案；提出五种针对 LLM 输入选取不同文本数据集合的策略；将五种策略应用于五个不同源数据集的应用案例所创建的虚拟数据集；实验采用三种 LLM 生成器与两种嵌入技术进行评测。

Result: 实验表明五种策略的性能取决于文本描述的质量。基于该结论，采用多策略方式实现新的关键词生成服务(KGS)，并作为扩展 DVS 的组成部分。

Conclusion: 自动化关键词生成能力对于数据量大、数据集丰富且被频繁访问的 DVS 不可或缺，可为构建高效虚拟数据集搜索引擎提供支撑。

Abstract: In modern data infrastructure, a data virtualization service (DVS) allows users to create many versions of source datasets for different processing tasks. For example, a multivariate or multi-modality dataset can be used for different machine learning tasks (e.g., classification, detection, segmentation, forecasting, and so on). Data selection, participation, integration, and preprocessing in different tasks result in many virtual datasets [Khan et al., SSC 2026]. Meanwhile, different users need to discover existing virtual datasets frequently and easily to avoid duplicate efforts. To enable an effective search engine in a DVS, it is desirable to automatically generate keywords for each virtual dataset. In this work, we present a solution for generating keywords for virtual datasets using large language models (LLMs). We propose five strategies for selecting different collections of text data for processing by the LLMs. We applied the five strategies to virtual datasets created in five application case studies with different source datasets. We conducted experiments with three LLM generators and two embedding techniques. The experiments show that the performances of the five strategies depend on the quality of the text descriptions. The results help us adopt a multi-strategy implementation of a new keyword generation service (KGS) as part of an extended DVS. The work demonstrates that the capability of automated keyword generation is indispensable for a high-volume DVS that is dataset-rich and frequently accessed.

</details>


### [211] [Using Machine Learning to Measure Self-Efficacy Belief Through Learners' Interaction Traces in Technology-Enhanced Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41900)
*Maeva Somny,Linda Chouati,Marie Lefevre*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究构建基于学习者在技术增强学习(TEL)系统中交互轨迹的低自我效能感检测系统，采用数据挖掘与机器学习方法，通过两所大学的实验收集数据并建立多分类器流水线，探索性验证了自我效能感可检测性及其动态性对预测的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我效能感影响学习者表现、学业选择与未来职业发展路径；TEL环境在支持表现与动机方面潜力显著，尽早实施补救性干预以支持学习者的自我效能感信念具有重要价值，而通过TEL系统使用数据检测自我效能感仍基本未被探索。

Method: 在两所大学开展实验，收集学习者在TEL系统中解题时的交互轨迹数据及自我效能感自评量表；构建基于数据挖掘与机器学习技术的检测方法，形成包含多个分类模型的机器学习流水线，以识别指示低自我效能感的特征。

Result: 给出探索性初步结果，表明可从交互轨迹中识别低自我效能感相关特征；并揭示因自我效能感在学习过程中具有高度动态性，对其进行密切监测对提升预测性能具有重要意义。

Conclusion: 本研究为TEL环境下基于交互轨迹的自我效能感自动检测提供初步证据与未来展望，强调动态监测自我效能感的必要性，为早期补救性教学实践提供支持方向。

Abstract: Self-efficacy beliefs play a critical role in learners' performance, academic choices, and, more broadly, in their future career trajectories. As Technology-Enhanced Learning (TEL) environments have demonstrated strong potential for supporting both performance and motivation, investigating self-efficacy within such environments is of particular interest, especially for implementing remedial practices as early as possible to support learners' self-efficacy beliefs. Our research aims to develop a system capable of detecting low self-efficacy based on learners' interaction traces while they solve exercises in a TEL system. In this paper, we present initial results and future perspectives on a detection approach based on data mining and machine learning techniques to identify features indicative of low self-efficacy beliefs. Because the detection of learners' self-efficacy beliefs through their use of TEL systems remains largely unexplored, this work is exploratory in nature. To this end, we conducted an experiment across two universities to collect interaction traces and self-reported measures of self-efficacy. We then developed a machine learning pipeline comprising multiple classification models, enabling us to highlight the importance of closely monitoring of learners' self-efficacy beliefs for improved prediction performance, given their highly dynamic nature during learning.

</details>


### [212] [ReX-GG: a LLM Ensemble Pipeline for Relation-extraction and Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41901)
*Giacomo Magnifico,Eduard Barbu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于JSON结构化输出的轻量级多LLM协同集成流程，用匿名同行评审式排名缓解幻觉与单点故障，并在英文科普文章关系抽取任务上验证，感知准确率达0.778。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM集成框架依赖多步流程与答案排序等附加模块，多采用token/span分析而非结构化输出，导致架构臃肿且流水线存在潜在失败点；而更轻快的方案又易受幻觉传播影响、缺乏输出可控性。

Method: 可定制的轻量级协同LLM集成工作流：以JSON结构化输出约束各模型，并通过匿名同行评审式排名进行答案聚合；流程用于英文科普文章的关系抽取，覆盖四类本体论关系（强因果、弱因果、对比、组合），包含语义节点规范化，最终生成交互式彩色编码HTML因果图；采用匿名用户研究评估。

Result: 相对人工标注金标准，用户研究得到平均感知准确率0.778；模块化架构支持API与本地部署两种LLM方案；框架以开源许可发布。

Conclusion: 结构化输出与匿名同行评审排名可在不引入重型排序模块的前提下降低幻觉与单模型失败风险，实现轻量可定制的集成式关系抽取与因果图生成，并具备可复现与灵活部署优势。

Abstract: Current LLM ensemble frameworks focus on multi-step setups with additional modules for answer ranking, often opting for token and span analysis rather than structured outputs, leading to heavyweight architectures with potential fail states along the pipeline. Faster, lighter solutions are more vulnerable to hallucination propagation and can lack output control in more complex pipelines. This paper proposes a customisable, lightweight ensemble workflow of coordinated Large Language Models that leverages JSON-structured outputs and anonymous peer-review ranking to mitigate hallucinatory outputs and single-model failure points. The pipeline is demonstrated on a relation extraction task applied to popular science articles in English, targeting four ontologically-grounded relation types (strong causation, weak causation, contrastive, and compositional), with semantic node canonicalisation and interactive, colour-coded HTML causal graphs as the final output. Performance is evaluated through an anonymous user study, achieving an average perceived accuracy of 0.778 against a human-annotated gold standard. The modular architecture supports flexible deployment across both API-based and in-house LLM setups, and the full framework is released under an open license to foster reproducibility and collaborative research.

</details>


### [213] [Structure-Aware Skill Retrieval and Arbitration for Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41909)
*Anran Liu,Benzhao Hou,Zongsheng Cao,Daichao Wang,Lang Chen,Shitong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ArchOR，一个以"原型（archetype）"为中心的技能型智能体，在 OptSkills 基础上加入 CARL 与 S³R 两个检索模块，在相同骨干与技能库下于五个基准上取得 65.00% Micro-Avg. Pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的优化任务求解智能体在叙述性改写（narrative rewrites）下表现脆弱，且可复用经验多以"实例级"存储，泛化与检索效率受限。

Method: 在 OptSkills 上增加两个检索导向模块：CARL（Centroid-Aware Representation Learning，通过低秩原型残差细化缓存技能表示的顺序）；S³R（Signature-Guided Retrieval，为技能附加开放维度结构签名，按加权 Jaccard 重叠优先排序候选，并将语义重排限制在 top-K）；另设保留基线的仲裁规则，决定结构路由何时取代原始选择器。

Result: 在相同 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 骨干与已发布技能库下，五个基准平均达 65.00% Micro-Avg. Pass@1，较 OptSkills 提升 1.64 pp（Micro-Avg.）与 2.68 pp（Macro-Avg.）。

Conclusion: 以原型为中心、结构签名引导的检索可提升 LLM 优化智能体技能复用与鲁棒性；基线保留仲裁机制确保增益不劣于原选择器。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can turn natural-language optimization tasks into solver-checked answers, but current agents remain brittle under narrative rewrites and often store reusable experience at the instance level. We introduce \textsc{ArchOR}, an archetype-centric skill agent that builds on \textsc{OptSkills} by adding two retrieval-focused modules. \emph{Centroid-Aware Representation Learning} (CARL) refines the ordering of cached skill representations through a low-rank prototype residual. \emph{Signature-Guided Retrieval} (S\textsuperscript{3}R) attaches open-dimensional structural signatures to skills, prioritizes candidates by weighted Jaccard overlap, and bounds semantic re-ranking to a top-$K$ set. A baseline-preserving arbitration rule determines when the structural route should replace the original selector. With the same DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash backbone and released skill library, \textsc{ArchOR} reaches 65.00\% Micro-Avg.\ Pass@1 across five benchmarks, improving over \textsc{OptSkills} by 1.64~pp Micro-Avg.\ and 2.68~pp Macro-Avg.

</details>


### [214] [Sensorium: An Open-Source Multimodal Data Collection and Curation Library for Embodied Surgical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41946)
*Lorenzo Mazza,Ariel Rodriguez,Stefanie Speidel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出开源 Python 库 Sensorium，基于 ROS/ROS2 实现可配置机器人-传感器流的多模态手术数据的录制、处理、标注与策管，并在 1516 条体模、离体及活体猪实验记录上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身手术 AI 依赖可靠的多模态数据，但手术数据采集成本高、难以复现，且强依赖特定硬件配置，亟需可配置、可重复的数据工具链。

Method: 基于 ROS 与 ROS2 中间件构建开源 Python 库，统一整合四类功能：面向操作者的数据采集、可配置处理流水线、语义标注、以及分段式（episodic）数据集生成，支持任意机器人/传感器流配置。

Result: 在跨多种手术任务与传感配置的 1516 条记录（体模、离体、活体猪实验）上完成评估，并报告了初步的可用性反馈。

Conclusion: Sensorium 提供了从采集到策管的端到端开源方案，可降低手术多模态数据采集对硬件的依赖并提升可复现性；现有结果为初步验证，尚需更系统的用户研究与下游任务评估。

Abstract: Embodied surgical AI requires reliable multimodal data, yet surgical data collection remains costly, difficult to repeat, and strongly dependent on the hardware setup. We present Sensorium, an open-source Python library for recording, processing, annotating, and curating multimodal surgical data from configurable robot and sensor streams using the Robot Operating System (ROS) and ROS2 middleware. The library combines operator-facing data acquisition, configurable processing, semantic annotation, and episodic dataset generation. We evaluate Sensorium across 1516 recordings of phantom, ex vivo, and in vivo porcine experiments involving multiple surgical tasks and sensing configurations, and report preliminary usability feedback.

</details>


### [215] [Optimizing Stochastic Policy Geometry for Few-Step Flow Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41949)
*Zhuguanyu Wu,Congjia Chen,Ruihao Gong,Yang Yong,Shiqiao Gu,Yi Xu,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Geo-FSRL：在少步流模型的GRPO中，以"给定速度更新下最小化条件KL"为选择准则，在保系数各向同性高斯转移族内推导唯一闭式最优策略，并结合梯度尺度匹配归一化策略梯度与KL权重，在4步设置下图像/视频生成均优于Flow-GRPO与Flow-CPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步流模型通过少量大幅去噪转移生成样本，对其施加GRPO需在转移中引入随机性；当单步跨越较大噪声区间时，所得随机策略的选择成为优化关键。现有构造仅约束策略保持目标流系数，该约束对应一族策略却缺乏选择准则。

Method: 提出选择原则：在固定条件状态与时步对上，选取使任意非零速度输出更新所诱导的条件KL散度最小的可行策略。在保系数的各向同性高斯转移族中，该准则化为最小化单一标量几何因子（该因子同时决定速度空间Fisher信息与条件转移对数似然比方差）；推导该族内唯一闭式最优解，与更新幅度和方向无关，转移系数由源与目标噪声水平共同确定。进一步采用梯度尺度匹配，推导闭式策略梯度权重与KL权重，以归一化显式score尺度与参考KL曲率。训练沿用原始少步调度，推理保留确定性求解器。

Result: 在图像生成与视频生成模型的4步设置下，最终奖励分数均高于Flow-GRPO与Flow-CPS。

Conclusion: 以条件KL最小化为准则可唯一确定少步流模型RL中的随机策略几何，配合梯度尺度匹配实现稳定优化，且不改变推理阶段的确定性少步采样流程。

Abstract: Few-step flow models generate samples through a handful of large denoising transitions. Applying GRPO to such models requires introducing stochasticity into the denoising transitions, and when a single transition spans a wide range of noise levels, the choice of the resulting stochastic policy becomes a major factor in optimization. Recent constructions constrain the policy to preserve the target flow coefficients, but this constraint admits a family of policies and offers no criterion for choosing among them. We introduce Geo-FSRL (Geometry-Optimized Few-Step Reinforcement Learning), built on a selection principle: at a fixed conditioning state and timestep pair, choose the admissible policy that minimizes the conditional KL divergence induced by any given nonzero velocity-output update. Within a coefficient-preserving family of isotropic Gaussian transitions, this criterion reduces to minimizing a single scalar geometry factor that determines the conditional KL for every velocity-output update. The same factor governs velocity-space Fisher information and conditional transition log-likelihood-ratio variance. We derive the unique closed-form optimizer within this family, shared across all nonzero update magnitudes and directions, with transition coefficients determined by both the source and target noise levels. Because the minimized geometry factor still varies across timesteps, we further adopt gradient-scale matching from prior work and derive closed-form policy-gradient and KL weights that normalize explicit score scales and reference-KL curvature. Geo-FSRL trains on the original few-step schedule and retains the deterministic solver at inference. Our method achieves higher final reward scores than Flow-GRPO and Flow-CPS on both image generation and video generation models under the 4-step setting. Code is available at https://github.com/ModelTC/LightX2V/tree/main/lightx2v_rl

</details>


### [216] [Prune Gradually, Adapt Sparsely: Efficient Pruning of Large Language Models at Extreme Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41968)
*Jean Lucien Randrianantenaina,Hung Nguyen,Nasib Ullah,Jinbin Zhang,Nilesh Gupta,Inderjit S. Dhillon,Rohit Babbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ETAPS 提出一种渐进式剪枝 + 稀疏增量适配的权重剪枝方法，在高稀疏度下以远低于 ELSA 的成本取得更低困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型权重剪枝可降低存储与推理成本，但在高稀疏度下保持质量困难。现有方法存在质量-成本权衡：一次性剪枝（one-shot）便宜但随稀疏度上升质量骤降，即使配合剪枝后恢复也难挽回；直接以端到端目标优化掩码的方法（如 SOTA 的 ELSA）质量高，但需要与模型等大的优化器状态和辅助变量，开销巨大。

Method: ETAPS：分阶段逐步收紧全局稀疏度约束，同时用小的固定预算训练一个稀疏 delta（稀疏适配参数）；核心观察是渐进式移除权重使每步剪枝幅度足够小，稀疏适配可补偿，从而使适配后的模型能提供端到端梯度来指导下一步剪枝决策，且无需模型规模级的优化器状态。

Result: 在 125M 到 13B 的六个模型上，50%–90% 各稀疏度下困惑度均低于 ELSA，最多降低 42%；90% 稀疏度下剪枝 LLaMA2-7B 仅需单张 H200（ELSA 需四张），持久状态小 5.0×，GPU 小时少 49%。

Conclusion: 渐进剪枝结合稀疏适配可在极端稀疏等苛刻场景下以显著更低成本实现高质量，为低成本高效剪枝提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Weight pruning can reduce the storage and inference cost of large language models (LLMs), but preserving quality at high sparsity levels remains challenging. Existing methods face a quality-cost trade-off in this regime. One-shot pruning is cheap but degrades sharply as sparsity increases, even when followed by post-pruning recovery. A growing line of work instead optimizes the mask directly from the dense model under the end-to-end objective; among these methods, [Lee et al.,2026], the recent state of the art at extreme sparsity, reaches high quality but at the cost of model-sized optimizer state and auxiliary variables. To address this gap, we propose ETAPS, which prunes gradually and adapts sparsely: it tightens a global sparsity constraint in progressive stages while training a sparse delta with a small, fixed budget. Our key observation is that gradual weight removal keeps each pruning step small enough for sparse adaptation to compensate, allowing the adapted model to provide end-to-end gradients that guide the next pruning decision without model-sized optimizer state. Across six models from 125M to 13B parameters, ETAPS achieves lower perplexity than ELSA at every evaluated sparsity from 50% to 90%, with perplexity reductions of up to 42%. ETAPS is also far cheaper: at 90% sparsity, it prunes LLaMA2-7B on a single H200, where ELSA uses four, with $5.0\times$ smaller total persistent state and 49% fewer GPU-hours. Together, our findings point to a promising direction for achieving high quality in demanding regimes such as extreme sparsity at substantially lower cost.

</details>


### [217] [When Does Test-Time Search Help World Action Models? An Empirical Audit of Candidate Selection and Simulator Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41970)
*Jun Liu,Dajie Sun,Zhenglun Kong,Zhenjiang Fan,Peiyan Dong,Fan Yang,Wenbin Zhangg,Geng Yuan,Xue Lin,Yanzhi Wang,Dong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对世界-动作模型的测试时扩展（test-time scaling）进行"前提优先、预注册"审计：在 Cosmos Policy 与 RoboCasa 上依次检验选择信号、实际加速比与评测完整性三项前提，发现学习选择器劣于随机、候选头空间极小、token 剪枝端到端加速仅约1.15×，并发现并修复了一处静默渲染损坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展（候选选择、自适应算力分配）带来的收益只有满足三个前提才可信：候选含可利用信号、名义算力节省能转化为真实加速、评测本身可靠。现有工作多以成功率单一指标汇报收益，缺乏对上述前提链的逐项审计，作者因此提出应把测试时扩展当作"前提链"来验证而非只看成功率。

Method: 采用 premise-first、预注册（pre-registered）的审计方法，在 Cosmos Policy 与 RoboCasa 上分别检验：(1) 决策信号——比较学习价值选择器与随机选择的性能，测量事后（hindsight）oracle 增益及回合级置信区间，统计状态层面候选结果的一致性比例以估计选择头空间；(2) 实现效率——测量 token 剪枝带来的每前向 FLOPs 降幅与部署路径端到端加速比（含剔除解码阶段的路径），并与 1.5× 目标对比，另以功效门控（power gate）评估自适应分配实验；(3) 评测完整性——定位并修复一条在算力密集规划路径上发生率显著上升的静默渲染损坏，并确认其不影响官方直接策略（direct-policy）结果。

Result: 干净数据上，学习价值选择器比随机选择差 1.66 个百分点；hindsight oracle 仅带来 1.40 个百分点增益，且回合级区间跨零；82% 的状态下候选结果完全相同，选择头空间极小。token 剪枝使单次前向 FLOPs 最多降低 47%，但部署路径端到端加速最多约 1.15×（剔除解码阶段路径为 1.25×），未达 1.5× 目标；自适应分配实验在功效门控下结论不确定。此外发现并修复一处静默渲染损坏，其发生率随算力密集规划路径显著上升，但不影响官方直接策略结果。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展的收益应按"前提链"（决策信号、实现效率、评测完整性）逐项审计，而非仅以成功率衡量；本文在该配置下未观察到可信的测试时扩展增益，且揭示了名义算力节省与真实端到端加速之间的显著落差。结论仅限定于所测配置，并公开检测器、修复方案与脚本以供复现与后续审计。

Abstract: World-action models increasingly apply test-time scaling, spending extra inference computation on candidate selection and adaptive allocation to improve control. Such gains are credible only when candidates carry exploitable signal, nominal compute savings become real speedup, and evaluation stays reliable. We audit all three on Cosmos Policy and RoboCasa with a premise-first, pre-registered methodology. On clean data, a learned value selector is 1.66 pp worse than random; a hindsight oracle gains only 1.40 pp, with an episode-level interval spanning zero; and 82% of states show identical candidate outcomes, leaving little selection headroom. Token pruning cuts per-forward FLOPs by up to 47% but yields at most a measured 1.15$\times$ end-to-end speedup on the deployed path (1.25$\times$ on a decode-excluded path), short of a 1.5$\times$ target, and our adaptive-allocation experiment is inconclusive under a power gate. We further identify and fix a silent rendering corruption whose rate rises sharply along compute-heavy planning paths while leaving the official direct-policy result unaffected, and release a detector, fix, and scripts. These findings show why test-time scaling should be audited as a chain of premises (decision signal, realized efficiency, evaluationintegrity) rather than by success rate alone. Conclusions are scoped to the tested configuration.

</details>


### [218] [ID2Pattern: Pretraining Generalizable Identity Behavior Patterns for Transaction Risk Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41988)
*Ze Zhao,Haoyu Jiang,Weiyu Han,Ruobing Zuo,Xiao Tan,Shuai Chen,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ID2Pattern预训练框架，通过Local Anonymous ID（LAID）分词器与多尺度模式码本，将高基数身份字段的行为模式引入交易基础模型，在1亿+工业数据与6个公开数据集上分别提升4.13%与8.83%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线支付系统中card、device、account等身份ID字段蕴含丰富的可泛化行为信号，但词表持续增长、语义含义有限，使得基于词表与基于语义的编码方式难以适用；其可泛化性不在具体取值，而在交易序列中呈现的身份行为模式。

Method: ID2Pattern包含两部分：（1）LAID分词器，将身份字段编码为其在序列中的出现与复现方式，与具体取值解耦；（2）在无标注序列上预训练多尺度模式码本，将LAID序列抽象为离散行为模式，供下游预测使用。

Result: 在超过1亿条序列的工业数据与6个公开数据集上一致优于强基线，工业场景平均提升4.13%，公开数据集平均提升8.83%；且当训练阶段所有身份取值均未见过时效果依然保持，显示其对新增用户与市场的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: 身份字段的有效利用应转向行为模式而非取值语义；LAID分词加多尺度码本的范式可作为交易基础模型中高基数ID编码的通用方案。

Abstract: Large-scale behavioral pretraining offers a promising route to generalizable representations in online payment systems. However, high-cardinality identity fields such as card, device, and account IDs, though rich in behavioral signals, remain underexploited. Their continually growing vocabularies and limited semantic meaning make existing vocabulary-based and semantics-based encodings ill-suited. Their generalizable signal lies not in specific values, but in the identity behavior patterns they exhibit across a transaction sequence. Building on this insight, we propose ID2Pattern, a pretraining framework that brings these patterns into transaction foundation models. ID2Pattern first encodes identity fields with a Local Anonymous ID (LAID) tokenizer, which captures how each identity appears and recurs within the sequence, independent of its specific value. It then pretrains a multi-scale pattern codebook on unlabeled sequences, which abstracts LAID sequences into discrete behavior patterns for downstream prediction. Experiments on an industrial dataset with over 100M sequences and six public datasets show that ID2Pattern consistently outperforms strong baselines, improving industrial by 4.13% and public datasets by 8.83% on average. Notably, ID2Pattern remains equally effective even when all identity values are unseen during training, highlighting its potential to generalize to new users and markets.

</details>


### [219] [TradeWar: A Benchmark for Execution-Grounded Trade Negotiation with Adaptive LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42004)
*Xiangyu Kong,Ruirui Yang,Xia Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出多国贸易谈判模拟基准 TradeWar，将谈判对话落地为具体关税与贸易份额政策并结算评估，同时提出跨回合自适应智能体 TACTIC，在延迟执行反馈条件下优于 CoT 与记忆类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮谈判基准主要只测"达成协议率"，忽略承诺执行后是否真正创造价值，导致 LLM 智能体在延迟执行反馈下的能力被高估。

Method: 构建 TradeWar 基准：把自然语言谈判转化为定量关税/贸易份额政策，在结算阶段施加产能与网络约束，并用 Economic Health Index (EHI) 度量结果；提出 TACTIC 智能体，在不更新基座模型权重的前提下，利用历史经验、对手行为与既往结算结果跨 episode 迭代优化谈判策略。

Result: 零样本 CoT 的 PromptLLM 达成率 75% 但最终 EHI 仅 57.1（×100），TACTIC 达 68.8；20 个连续 episode、3 个随机种子下，TACTIC 最终 EHI 由 58.2 提升至 73.2，无跨回合更新的消融为 65.2；在技术管制与能源危机冲击下，TACTIC 的性能衰减小于 PromptLLM 与 MemoryLLM。

Conclusion: 仅靠 CoT 推理与交互记忆无法充分应对延迟执行反馈，基于结算结果的自适应学习才能改善长程谈判表现；高达成率不等于高执行价值，评估需以执行结果为导向。

Abstract: Multi-turn negotiation remains a challenging problem for large language model (LLM) agents. While LLMs can conduct natural-language dialogue, existing benchmarks predominantly assess only agreement rates, overlooking whether negotiated commitments actually create value once executed. To address this gap, we introduce TradeWar, a multi-country simulation benchmark that translates negotiation dialogues into concrete tariff and trade-share policies, enforces capacity and network constraints during settlement, and evaluates outcomes via an Economic Health Index (EHI). To solve this execution-grounded task, we propose TACTIC (Trade-aware Adaptive Consolidation and Theory-of-mind for International Commerce), an agent that iteratively refines its negotiation strategy across episodes-without updating base LLM weights-by leveraging prior experience, opponent behavior, and past settlement outcomes. Despite using zero-shot chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting, PromptLLM attains a 75% agreement rate but only 57.1 final EHI (*100), whereas TACTIC reaches 68.8. Across 20 sequential episodes and three seeds, TACTIC's final EHI improves from 58.2 to 73.2, compared with 65.2 for an ablation without cross-episode updates. Under technology-controls and energy-crisis shocks, TACTIC also exhibits smaller performance degradation than PromptLLM and MemoryLLM. These results indicate that CoT deliberation and interaction memory alone do not fully address delayed execution feedback, whereas settlement-grounded adaptation improves long-horizon negotiation in TradeWar.

</details>


### [220] [Same Question, Different Answers: Auditing Persona-Dependent Variation in Six LLMs on Smoking-Cessation Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42039)
*Artin Yousefi,Aizierjiang Aiersilan,Lorien C Abroms,David A Broniatowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过12种用户人设×4个戒烟问题×6个LLM共2880条响应审计，发现LLM健康建议存在显著人设依赖差异（青少年获得FDA批准戒烟处方药提及率低32–67个百分点），并提出"事实底线"系统提示作为干预手段，但其效果跨模型不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数亿用户向LLM聊天机器人咨询健康建议，但"是否人人得到相同建议"这一公平性问题缺乏实证检验。作者选择戒烟这一有明确临床指南且具公共卫生意义的领域进行系统审计。

Method: 对来自4家提供商的6个广泛部署LLM，用12种在人生阶段、自述专业水平和吸烟史上不同的人设，提出相同的4个戒烟问题，共获得2880条响应；随后评估一种可部署干预——规定"事实底线"（每个用户应获得的核心医学事实集合）的简短系统提示，同时保留语气与措辞的自由度。

Result: 所有模型对青少年人设提及FDA批准处方戒烟药物的比例均低于成人，差距为32–67个百分点；6个模型中有5个对"自称具备医疗专业"的人设提供的事实少于"无专业背景"人设。系统提示在各模型中均缩小了人设差异，但对整体事实覆盖的影响在幅度和方向上因模型而异。

Conclusion: LLM健康建议存在系统性人设差异；单一系统上验证的提示不可假定可迁移至其他系统。需注意两点：相同提及率并非总是正确的临床目标（处方戒烟药物不推荐用于未成年人，事实底线应具年龄意识而非统一）；因人设多属性同时变化，所报告的是场景层面差异而非年龄或专业性的孤立效应。全部2880条响应与分析代码已公开。

Abstract: Hundreds of millions of people now ask large language model (LLM) chatbots for health advice, which raises a question that is simple to pose and hard to answer: does everyone get the same advice? We audit six widely deployed LLMs from four providers on smoking cessation, a domain with codified clini- cal guidance and substantial public-health stakes, by putting the same four cessation questions to twelve user personas that differ in life stage, self-described expertise, and smoking history, for a total of 2,880 responses. What the models say depends on who appears to be asking. Every model named FDA-approved prescription cessation medications less often to teen personas than to adult personas, by 32 to 67 percentage points, and in five of six models a persona presenting health- care expertise received fewer facts than a persona presenting none. We then evaluate one deployable intervention: a short system prompt specifying a factual floor, the set of core med- ical facts every user should receive when they are relevant, while leaving tone, vocabulary, and framing free to adapt. The prompt narrows persona differences in every model, but its effect on overall fact coverage varies in both magnitude and direction across models, so a prompt validated on one system cannot be assumed to transfer to another. Two cautions frame these results. Equal mention rates are not always the correct clinical target, because prescription cessation pharmacother- apy is not recommended for minors, so a defensible factual floor must be age-aware rather than uniform. And because our personas vary on several attributes at once, we report scenario- level differences rather than isolated effects of age or expertise. We release all 2,880 responses and the analysis code.

</details>


### [221] [CausalWAM: Learning the Causes of Actions in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42096)
*Juntao Cheng,Jinzhou Tang,Fan Feng,Henrik I. Christensen,Yujia Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出世界动作模型(WAM)难以保证仅从动作相关潜变量生成动作，提出稀疏约束下的可辨识性理论，并据此设计CWAM，在动作头上增加稀疏路由路径实现因果解耦，在合成数据与真实机器人上验证理论与闭环效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM需预测整个场景演化，但只有部分场景引发动作。仅靠示范拟合的模型可能将动作相关潜变量与冗余(nuisance)变量纠缠，无法保证动作由动作相关的潜在原因单独生成，从而损害泛化与决策可靠性。因此需要把"导致动作的因素"与"预测所依赖的因素"显式分离。

Method: 将动作生成所依赖的潜变量与预测所依赖的潜变量分离；在稀疏性约束下证明用于生成动作的潜变量是动作相关潜变量的可逆函数，且真实结构可辨识至潜变量置换；该结论无需充分的时间依赖性、不依赖未观测 regime 演化假设，且在动作相关潜变量与其余变量统计相关时仍成立。工程上提出CWAM：在预训练WAM的动作头上增加稀疏路由路径引入该约束，世界建模目标保持不变。

Result: 合成数据实验验证了理论结论（可辨识性/解耦性质）；真实机器人实验展示了闭环任务成功率与效率的提升。

Conclusion: 通过稀疏路由约束，可在不改变世界建模目标的前提下使WAM的动作通路仅依赖动作相关潜变量，获得可辨识的因果表示，并带来实际闭环性能提升；CWAM为将因果可辨识性理论嵌入大规模世界动作模型提供了可行的轻量方案。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) learn latent variables from observations and use these to both predict the evolution of the scene and to generate actions. The prediction has to be of the whole scene, whereas only a portion of the scene causes actions. WAMs fitted to demonstrations do not, in general, guarantee that they will generate actions solely from the latent causes of the demonstrated actions (the action-relevant latent variables), as the model could just as well learn to imitate in this way whilst entangling the action-relevant latent variables with the nuisance variables. We therefore separate out what causes action from what prediction depends on. We prove that under a sparsity constraint the latent variables used to generate actions are an invertible function of the action-relevant latent variables alone and the true structure is identified up to a permutation of the latent variables. This requires neither sufficient temporal dependence nor any assumptions on how the unobserved regimes evolve and holds even when the action-relevant latent variables are statistically dependent on the rest. We therefore propose CWAM (Causal WAM), which incorporates the constraint into a pretrained WAM by adding a sparse routing path to its action head, with the world modeling objective remaining unchanged. Experiments on both synthetic data and real-world robots validate the theory as well as showcase the improved efficiency in closed-loop success.

</details>


### [222] [ACCT: Asymmetric Cross-LLM Context Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42116)
*Yuzhe Liu,Haolin Tian,Tonghan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ACCT（非对称跨LLM上下文迁移），将同一模型族中源模型的KV cache转换为目标模型可直接使用的稀疏KV状态，实现跨模型上下文计算复用，在KV缩减75%时性能持平或更优，19K上下文下转换速度为重新prefill的6.5-21.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中LLM系统由单模型调用转向异构多模型动态调用，同一上下文在多个模型间复用时需被反复处理，造成大量重复计算开销。

Method: ACCT针对同一模型族内不同规模模型间的KV cache迁移，包含三个轻量组件：Cartographer为每个目标层与KV头识别与查询相关的上下文token；Allocator在共享的总KV预算下决定各目标层与KV头保留的token数量；Compiler将选中的源KV转换为稀疏的目标KV，使目标模型无需重处理原始上下文即可推理。

Result: 在5个通用能力基准与6个强上下文依赖任务的互补评测中，ACCT在KV cache缩减75%时仍达到或超过目标模型原始推理性能；约19K token上下文下，KV转换相对目标模型context prefill提速6.5-21.2倍。

Conclusion: 结果证明在有限KV预算下跨模型迁移上下文计算是可行的，可实现上下文计算与下游推理的解耦。

Abstract: In real-world deployments, large language model (LLM) systems are increasingly evolving from single-model invocation toward heterogeneous deployments in which different models are dynamically invoked for inference. When the same context is reused across models, however, each newly selected target model typically has to process it again, repeatedly incurring substantial computational costs. Therefore, we propose ACCT (Asymmetric Cross-LLM Context Transfer), which targets KV cache transfer between models of different scales within the same model family. ACCT transforms the KV cache produced by a source model into sparse KV states that can be directly consumed by a target model, thereby enabling cross-model reuse of context computation and decoupling context computation from downstream inference. ACCT consists of three lightweight components: the Cartographer identifies query-relevant context tokens for each target layer and KV head; the Allocator determines how many context tokens each target layer and KV head retains under a shared total KV budget; and the Compiler transforms selected source KV into sparse target KV. The target model can therefore perform inference using the transferred KV without reprocessing the original context. We evaluate ACCT in two complementary settings comprising five general-capability benchmarks and six highly context-dependent tasks. Experiments show that ACCT matches or exceeds the target model's original inference performance even when reducing the KV cache size by 75%. At a context length of approximately 19K tokens, ACCT's KV conversion achieves a 6.5-21.2 times speedup over target context prefill. These results demonstrate the feasibility of transferring context computation across models under a limited KV budget.

</details>


### [223] [Latent Sparse Attention: Towards Subquadratic Ultra-Long Context Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42147)
*Ales Kubicek,Andrea Caciolai,Liangzu Peng,Sukrit Sumant,Benjamin Hoover,Fraser Greenlee,Ruairidh M. Battleday,Radim Urban,Torsten Hoefler,James C. R. Whittington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Latent Sparse Attention (LSA)，通过在学习到的潜在空间中对键进行层次聚类、让查询对簇层次打分而非对所有键打分，在超长上下文（>1M tokens）下兼顾精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体工作流趋向长程任务，需超长上下文，但全长注意力在>1M tokens时成本过高；现有稀疏注意力索引器要么仍是关于上下文规模的二次复杂度，要么在超长上下文下精度下降。

Method: LSA：将键在可学习的潜在空间中层次化聚类，查询只需对簇的层次结构打分而非逐键打分，从而加速长上下文下的prefill、decode与训练。提出两个变体：D-LSA通过蒸馏应用于预训练模型；N-LSA仅用下一token损失原生训练。

Result: 在10M tokens的GLM-5.3-FLASH上，D-LSA相较原始稀疏注意力，单层prefill加速22.7x、decode加速4.6x；在QWEN-3.8-27B上相较全长注意力分别为72.4x与42.5x，并在多项任务上保持相近精度。N-LSA在128k tokens下单层训练加速1.6x且精度与全长注意力相当。

Conclusion: LSA为模型利用超长上下文提供了实用且高效的注意力方案。

Abstract: Agentic workflows are increasingly shifting towards long-horizon tasks, such as open-ended scientific work, where models benefit from long context windows. However, at ultra-long context (above 1M tokens), full attention is prohibitively expensive. A promising approach is sparse attention, in which a dedicated indexer selects a subset of keys for the attention. Yet current methods of indexing either still have quadratic cost in context size or lose accuracy at ultra-long context scale. We address this with Latent Sparse Attention (LSA), an efficient attention method that maintains accuracy at ultra-long context. In LSA, keys are hierarchically clustered in a learned latent space, and queries score the hierarchy of clusters instead of scoring every key, making prefill, decode and training faster at long context. We propose two variants, Distilled-LSA (D-LSA), which can be applied to pre-trained models via distillation, and Native-LSA (N-LSA), which is trained natively on the next-token loss alone. On GLM-5.3-FLASH (QWEN-3.8-27B) at 10M tokens, D-LSA speeds up one attention layer by 22.7x (72.4x) in prefill and 4.6x (42.5x) in decode over its original sparse attention (full attention), while keeping similar accuracy across a variety of tasks. N-LSA matches the accuracy of full attention with a training speedup of 1.6x per layer at 128k tokens. LSA offers practical and efficient attention for models to benefit from ultra-long context.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [224] [Automatic Construction of Strong Simple Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40700)
*Caleb N. Ellington,Jiaqi Wang,Sohan Addagudi,Elijah Cole*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作提出用大语言模型自动化构建基线，在36个取自Nature/Science/Cell系期刊的监督学习任务上，自动生成的简单基线可恢复平凡预测器与深度学习模型之间差距的中位数95.5%，并在12项研究中达到或超越原文深度模型性能，表明自动化基线构建可作为可扩展的深度学习贡献检验手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在物理与生命科学中日益普及，但大量所谓"进展"实为省略或缺失基线导致的假象；而人工同行评审难以应对快速增长的论文数量，亟需可扩展的基线核查机制。

Method: 从Nature、Science及Cell系期刊的同行评审研究中汇编36个监督分类与回归任务（均针对科学应用中的新型深度学习方法）；使用语言模型为每个任务自动构建简单基线，并与平凡预测器和原深度模型的得分进行比较；进一步分析跨学科领域与发表平台的差异，以及测试时计算量和可获得的研究上下文层级所带来的影响。

Result: 自动构建的基线表现强劲，恢复平凡预测器与原深度模型之间差距的中位数95.5%（均值85.7%）；在12项研究中，中位简单基线达到或超过深度模型性能；并刻画了不同科学领域、发表venue以及测试时计算与上下文信息水平的影响。

Conclusion: 自动化基线构建是一种实用且可扩展的检查手段，可用于评估物理与生命科学中深度学习贡献的真实性，弥补人工同行评审在规模上的不足。

Abstract: To rigorously establish the value of a novel deep learning method, it must be compared against appropriate baselines. As deep learning has become increasingly common in the physical and life sciences, there have been numerous cases in which apparent progress has turned out to be an artifact of omitted or missing baselines. In this work, we investigate whether this problem can be mitigated by using large language models to automate the construction of baselines. We assemble 36 supervised classification and regression tasks from peer-reviewed studies in Nature, Science, and Cell-family journals that investigate novel deep learning methods for scientific applications. We then use a language model to construct simple baselines for each task. These automatically constructed baselines are strong, recovering a median of 95.5% (mean 85.7%) of the gap between a trivial predictor and the score of the deep learning model. The median simple baseline matches or exceeds the performance of the deep learning model in 12 studies. We further examine the variation across scientific domains and publication venues and we characterize the effects of test-time compute and access to different levels of study context. As human peer review has struggled to keep up with the rapidly increasing volume of research articles in recent years, our results suggest that automated baseline construction is a practical and scalable check on deep learning contributions in the physical and life sciences.

</details>


### [225] [Bayesian Distribution System State Estimation with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40728)
*Nan Lin,Peter Palensky,Pedro Vergara Barrios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PoSE（后验采样状态估计）保留DSSE的贝叶斯结构，仅用flow matching学习变电站注入分布作为先验，潮流方程、拓扑与量测仅在采样时通过噪声感知的Kalman型校正引入，从而获得物理一致的后验样本，并可在任意量测集、噪声水平和拓扑下复用同一先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中压馈线实时量测稀少，DSSE依赖二次变电站伪量测（贝叶斯先验）。加权最小二乘虽保持潮流精确但将先验压缩为均值与方差；监督式深度学习估计器既学习潮流又绑定单一量测集，泛化性与不确定性刻画不足。

Method: 提出PoSE：以神经网络建模变电站注入分布，用flow matching在历史数据上训练；潮流方程、电网拓扑与量测仅在采样阶段通过噪声感知的Kalman型校正进入，网络不近似潮流，每个后验样本均为物理一致运行点；并给出快速变体。

Result: IEEE 33节点馈线12种设置下CRPS较全协方差高斯先验降低6.7–7.5%；90%区间覆盖率接近精确后验，而监督估计器仅37–78%。240节点不平衡三相Iowa馈线上注入、电压与支路电流误差均为最低。快速变体每快照0.2–0.6s保持精度，且在7种重构拓扑上无需重训练即优于高斯先验与重训练监督估计器。

Conclusion: PoSE将先验学习与物理约束解耦，兼顾物理一致性、可靠不确定性量化与跨量测集/噪声/拓扑的泛化能力，优于高斯先验与监督式学习方法。

Abstract: Medium-voltage feeders have few real-time measurements, so distribution system state estimation (DSSE) rests on pseudo-measurements of the secondary substations, a prior in Bayesian terms. Weighted least squares keeps the power flow exact but reduces this prior to a mean and variance, while supervised deep learning estimators also learn the power flow and are tied to one measurement set. We propose PoSE (posterior sampling for state estimation), which keeps the Bayesian structure of DSSE and learns only what the distribution system operator does not know, namely the distribution of substation injections, modelled by a neural network trained with flow matching on historical data. The power flow equations, grid topology and measurements enter only at sampling, through a noise-aware Kalman-type correction. The neural network therefore never approximates the power flow, but every posterior sample is a physically consistent operating point, and one trained prior serves any measurement set, noise level and topology. On the IEEE 33-bus feeder, PoSE lowers the probabilistic injection error (CRPS) by 6.7-7.5% against a full-covariance Gaussian prior in all 12 tested settings. It provides reliable uncertainty: the 90% intervals contain the true injection almost exactly as often as exact posterior samples would, whereas those of supervised estimators contain it in only 37-78% of cases. On the 240-bus unbalanced three-phase Iowa feeder, it gives the lowest injection, voltage and branch-current errors of all estimators. A fast variant matches this accuracy at 0.2-0.6s per snapshot and, without retraining, beats Gaussian priors and retrained supervised estimators on seven reconfigured topologies.

</details>


### [226] [Don't Just Impute, Detect: Task-Aware Diffusion Pipelines for Edge Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40732)
*Jeongsu Park,Taehyeon Yun,Geonwoo Kim,Yuji Lim,Moohong Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出任务感知扩散精修器TADR，以冻结检测器的损失景观训练条件扩散精修器，消除"先插补后检测"中的插补偏差，在IIoT缺失块数据上显著提升异常检测F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IIoT边缘流水线常因网络丢包与硬件故障丢失多传感器连续数据块。标准做法是"先插补后检测"的分离式流水线，插补器仅以MSE为训练目标，倾向于用最可能的正常模式填补缺失块。这导致两问题：部分观测的异常被平滑回正常，以及正常数据上的边界伪影引发误报，即"插补偏差"。

Method: TADR是条件扩散精修器，直接针对冻结下游检测器的损失景观训练。由于通过采样轨迹反向传播不可行，任务感知项作用于噪声预测已给出的一步干净窗口估计上，因此训练开销不高于普通扩散训练。评估采用严格逐点（Point-to-Point）协议、五个随机种子并做配对显著性检验，在十步采样下运行。

Result: SMD上F1较未精修检测器提升0.338（p=0.0015）；消融显示仅异常增强目标即贡献0.463。增益与可用连续且相互冗余的通道数量、停机所移除的异常证据相关；在以one-hot命令通道为主的遥测数据集及PSM上无增益。十步采样时单CPU核每窗口41.4 ms，4.03 MB模型可支撑约24路并发1Hz流。

Conclusion: 任务感知训练能有效纠正插补偏差，但收益具有条件性：依赖连续冗余通道与缺失块中的异常证据，对one-hot主导数据和PSM不适用。方法轻量、可实时部署于边缘端，代码与逐种子结果已公开。

Abstract: Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) edge pipelines routinely lose contiguous blocks of multi-sensor data to network dropouts and hardware faults. The standard remedy is a disjointed "impute-then-detect" pipeline whose imputer is trained only for Mean Squared Error (MSE). Such an imputer fills a missing block with the most probable normal pattern: a partially observed anomaly is smoothed back toward normality, and boundary artifacts on normal data raise false alarms. We call this imputation bias and propose the Task-Aware Diffusion Refiner (TADR), a conditional diffusion refiner trained against the loss landscape of a frozen downstream detector. Because back-propagating through the sampling trajectory is intractable, the task-aware terms act on the one-step clean-window estimate that the noise prediction already yields, so training costs no more than plain diffusion training. Under strict Point-to-Point evaluation over five seeds with paired significance tests, TADR raises F1 by $0.338$ over the unrefined detector on SMD ($p = 0.0015$), and the anomaly enhancement objective alone contributes $0.463$ in ablation. We also characterise when the method helps: the gain tracks the continuous, mutually redundant channels available and the anomaly evidence the outage removes, and it is absent on telemetry datasets dominated by one-hot command channels and on PSM. At ten sampling steps TADR runs in $41.4$ ms per window on a single CPU core, sustaining about $24$ concurrent 1 Hz streams from a $4.03$ MB model. Code and per-seed results are available at https://github.com/int-park422/TADR.

</details>


### [227] [Unsupervised Feature Selection Using Local Intrinsic Dimensionality -- Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40736)
*Oussama Chelly,Michael E. Houle,Ken-ichi Kawarabayashi,Muhammad Rajabinasab,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无监督特征选择方法LIDFS：将特征视为点，用局部内在维度(LID)刻画特征局部可区分性并据此排序，无需标签与类别数，时间复杂度O(d²n)，在23个高维数据集上取得领先效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维性同时损害学习算法的效率与效果，而许多应用中缺乏类别标签无法指导降维，因此需要有效的无监督特征选择方法。

Method: 基于LID与距离度量不可区分性之间的形式等价性：转置数据矩阵，把每个特征当作一个点，用其到k个最近邻特征的距离通过极大似然估计该特征的LID；LID越低表示局部可区分性越强、重要性得分越高；随后引入精化步骤，在每个特征邻域中保留一个代表并降低局部冗余特征；最终得到完整排序，可任意选取特征数而无需重跑流程。

Result: 在23个高维数据集（经典基准集合）上，与经典方法、先进方法及随机基线比较，采用5个下游分类器的准确率与AUC以及聚类准确率和NMI，覆盖完整与激进选择范围：监督评测中排名第一，聚类评测中排名第一或并列第二；与需要类别数的方法同属最优统计组，且比最接近的竞争者更快；若干近期方法未能超过随机基线；敏感性分析显示较小邻域规模效果最佳。

Conclusion: LIDFS是一种高效、完全无监督的特征选择方法，不使用标签或类别数信息，能给出便于任意截断的特征排序，并在分类与聚类指标上优于或媲美现有方法且计算更省时。

Abstract: High dimensionality degrades both the efficiency and the effectiveness of learning algorithms, and in many applications no class labels are available to guide dimensionality reduction. This paper proposes LIDFS, an unsupervised feature selection method based on Local Intrinsic Dimensionality (LID). The method builds on the formal equivalence between LID and the indiscriminability of distance measures. The data matrix is transposed so that each feature is treated as a point, and the LID of each feature is estimated by maximum likelihood from the distances to its $k$ nearest neighboring features. Features with lower LID exhibit greater local discriminability and receive higher importance scores. A refinement step then retains one representative per feature neighborhood and demotes locally redundant features. The result is a complete ranking from which any number of features can be selected without rerunning the procedure. LIDFS does not use any information about labels or the number of classes, and runs in $\mathcal{O}(d^2 n)$ time for $n$ instances and $d$ features. LIDFS is compared with classic and state-of-the-art methods and a random baseline on 23 high-dimensional datasets, the classic benchmark collection. The evaluation uses classification accuracy and AUC with five downstream classifiers, as well as clustering accuracy and normalized mutual information, over both full and aggressive selection ranges. LIDFS ranks first in the supervised evaluations and first or tied second in the clustering-based evaluations. It belongs to the top statistical group together with methods that require the number of classes, and it runs faster than its closest competitor. Several recent methods, by contrast, fail to outperform the random baseline. A sensitivity analysis shows that small neighborhood sizes yield the best results.

</details>


### [228] [Certified Behavioral Retention for Continual Adaptation with Held-Out Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40740)
*Ahanaf Hasan Ariq,Abrar Shahid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Certified Anchor-Based Adaptation (CABA)：一种与候选生成器无关的更新后门控机制，用冻结表征上的 worst-anchor KL 散度预算内最大插值系数筛选候选策略，并给出理论认证框架与 UCI HAR 实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续适应中，关键问题不仅是"如何更新"，更是"是否提交更新"——即在不超出对受保护行为所声明变更预算的前提下安全提交。现有方法缺少可审计的、无需动作标签与完整轨迹的更新门控与相应的行为散度认证。

Method: CABA 为候选无关的后更新门控：将历史观测从候选优化中保留（anchor），在冻结表征上比较旧策略与候选策略，选取满足"最差 anchor 前向 KL 散度在预算内"的最大插值系数；仅需有限次检查，不需动作标签或完整轨迹。理论层面区分"实测 anchor 保持"与"条件性 off-anchor 保证"，在显式表征覆盖与 Lipschitz 假设下给出受保护支撑集上的一致行为散度界，并推导仿射 softmax 头的正则性界、概率覆盖与压缩保证、插值单调性条件、网格分辨率界，以及轨迹级与序贯复合保证。

Result: 在受控 UCI Human Activity Recognition 非平稳传感器策略代理实验中，预算 ε=0.05 时平均遗忘由无约束适应的 1.01% 降至 0.00%，但最终平均精度由 93.04% 降至 88.48%，呈现保持—可塑性权衡；作者声明该实验不构成基础模型或机器人性能证据。

Conclusion: CABA 为满足"暴露可比策略分布并声明更新路径"的候选生成器提供了可审计的保持性检查，但部署仍需单独证明当前任务效用。

Abstract: Continual adaptation requires deciding not only how to update a model, but whether to commit an update without exceeding a declared budget for changes to protected behavior. We introduce Certified Anchor-Based Adaptation (CABA), a candidate-agnostic post-update gate that reserves historical observations from candidate optimization, compares old and candidate policies on frozen representations, and selects the largest tested interpolation coefficient whose worst-anchor forward KL divergence remains within budget. The finite check requires neither action labels nor complete trajectories. We develop a theory-led certification framework that separates measured anchor retention from conditional off-anchor guarantees. Under explicit representation-coverage and Lipschitz assumptions, an accepted update satisfies a uniform behavioral-divergence bound on protected support. The analysis gives regularity bounds for affine softmax heads, probabilistic coverage and compression guarantees, interpolation monotonicity conditions, grid-resolution bounds, and trajectory-level and sequential-composition guarantees. In a controlled UCI Human Activity Recognition study used as a non-stationary sensor-policy proxy, CABA reduces mean forgetting from 1.01 percent for unconstrained adaptation to 0.00 percent at budget epsilon = 0.05, while reducing final average accuracy from 93.04 percent to 88.48 percent. The experiment illustrates a retention-plasticity trade-off and does not establish foundation-model or robotic performance. CABA provides an auditable retention check for candidate generators exposing comparable policy distributions and a declared update path, while deployment also requires separate evidence of current-task utility.

</details>


### [229] [AutoHebb: Self-Modifying Neural Networks that Zero-Shot Infer and Continually Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40757)
*Daniel Levine,David van Dijk,Kenneth Kay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AutoHebb网络：结合元学习固定的非可塑权重、三因子Hebbian局部可塑性规则与自主控制的"神经调质"全局学习信号，实现强归纳偏置与自主学习控制，在0样本推断与持续学习上优于现代模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本推断（ZI）与持续学习（CL）建模存在两大局限：一是缺乏大脑中关键成分——ZI所需的强归纳偏置与CL所需的自主学习控制；二是模型在简洁性（可分析性）与表达性（非过度定制）之间难以兼顾。

Method: 构建一类在线学习前馈神经网络AutoHebb：外部反馈与内部生成的活动结合，产生全局学习信号（神经调质）回馈网络；该信号门控一个生物合理的局部学习规则（三因子Hebbian规则），更新网络的可塑权重；通过元学习搜索固定的非可塑权重。

Result: AutoHebb（1）成功完成ZI任务；（2）展现出未经训练的、涌现的ZI/CL能力；（3）可逆向工程为显式算法；（4）在ZI/CL任务上优于现代学习模型；与同类模型比较表明元学习、Hebbian可塑性与自主学习控制三者均为关键成分。

Conclusion: AutoHebb兼具简洁性与表达性，可作为生成假设、分析与发现生物合理的学习与泛化机制的工具。

Abstract: Humans and animals have a unique capacity to generalize and learn in novel circumstances, including "0-shot" inference (ZI) and continual learning (CL) -- two powerful cognitive abilities. Despite this importance, efforts to model ZI and CL are seriously limited in several ways. First, existing models typically lack components critical to ZI and CL in the brain, namely, strong inductive biases (for ZI) and autonomous control of learning (for CL). Second, models can be less tractable to analysis if model components are overly complex (lacking simplicity), or too task-restricted if model components are overly bespoke (lacking expressivity). Here we introduce autonomous Hebbian networks ("AutoHebb"), a class of online learning feedforward neural networks that realize strong inductive biases and autonomously controlled learning, and are both simple and expressive. Unlike standard models that learn via externally controlled optimization (e.g. gradient descent), AutoHebb networks combine external feedback with internally generated activity to produce a global learning signal ('neuromodulator') fed back to the network. The neuromodulator in turn gates a biologically grounded local learning rule (3-factor Hebbian rule) that updates networks' plastic weights. Using meta-learning to search for fixed (non-plastic) weights, we find that AutoHebb networks (1) succeed on ZI tasks, (2) show emergent (untrained) ZI/CL abilities, (3) admit reverse-engineering into explicit algorithms, and (4) outperform modern learning models on ZI/CL tasks. Lastly, performance comparisons to related models implied an essential role for three model components: meta-learning, Hebbian plasticity, and autonomous control of learning. These findings indicate that AutoHebb may be useful in hypothesis generation, analysis, and discovery of biologically grounded forms of learning and generalization.

</details>


### [230] [Frozen in Time: How a Physics Foundation Model Forgets Under Sim-to-Real Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40857)
*Haaris Mian,Miles Cranmer,Alessandro Favero,Payel Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究科学基础模型Walrus-1.3B在两阶段sim-to-real适配（先仿真圆柱绕流、后实验数据）中的灾难性遗忘问题，发现仅20个epoch微调即可使原始预训练数据集上的单步误差上升最多35倍，后续真实数据适配进一步侵蚀仿真性能；通过在真实数据微调中回放仅1%仿真数据即可恢复大部分丢失的仿真性能并保持有效适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学基础模型（SciFM）可高效适配未见数据分布，但微调同样面临灾难性遗忘风险，即适配新任务时退化已有知识。而sim-to-real适配对连续动力学模型是典型且实际的需求，其遗忘程度与驱动因素尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 以连续动力学SciFM Walrus-1.3B为对象，设计两阶段sim-to-real适配流程：先在圆柱绕流仿真数据上微调，再适配同系统的实验数据；量化各阶段在原始预训练数据集上的rollout性能与单步误差变化，并从数据集依赖性与任务表征相似性角度分析遗忘的潜在驱动因素，最后评估在真实数据微调中回放少量仿真数据的策略。

Result: 仿真数据微调后，原始预训练数据集上的rollout性能迅速退化，仅20个epoch单步误差最多上升35倍；随后真实数据适配进一步削弱仿真任务性能。在真实数据微调中回放低至1%的仿真数据，即可恢复大部分丢失的仿真性能，同时保持对实验动力学的有效适配。

Conclusion: 灾难性遗忘是SciFM进行sim-to-real适配的实际障碍；轻量级回放策略能显著提升知识保持能力，为科学基础模型的持续适配提供了简单有效的缓解手段。

Abstract: Scientific foundation models (SciFMs) have achieved remarkable performance on a wide range of PDEs, including the ability to efficiently adapt to unseen data distributions with additional finetuning. As with all neural architectures, finetuning such models comes with the risk of catastrophic forgetting, whereby adapting to new tasks degrades prior knowledge of the model. We study catastrophic forgetting in Walrus-1.3B, a SciFM for continuum dynamics, over the course of a two-stage sim-to-real adaptation: first finetuning on simulated flow around a cylinder and then adapting to experimental data of the same system. We find that finetuning on the simulations rapidly degrades rollout performance on the original pretraining datasets, with one-step errors increasing up to 35x after just 20 epochs. Subsequent adaptation to real data further erodes performance on the simulated task. We characterize the potential drivers of forgetting, such as dataset dependence and task representation similarity. We then show that replaying as little as 1% simulated data during real-data finetuning recovers most of the lost simulation performance while retaining effective adaptation to the experimental dynamics. Our results identify catastrophic forgetting as a practical obstacle to sim-to-real adaptation of SciFMs and show that lightweight replay strategies significantly improve retention.

</details>


### [231] [FARO: Function-Space Adaptive Return-to-Risk Optimization via Cross-Block Equal-Risk-Contribution Budget Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40858)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FARO是一个函数空间自适应优化器，以参数分块为决策单元：按每个块的"反事实回报"为其赋予几何，再冻结路由并用组合理论中的等风险贡献(ERC)原则重新分配各块预算。在FineWeb上的GPT模型实验中，FARO-Adaptive复现了手工设计的最优配方，FARO-ERC进一步在全部6个种子上稳定降低损失0.0136 nats。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练配方按人工先验划分优化器职责（注意力/MLP矩阵用正交化动量，其余用Adam式更新），这一划分依赖人对"哪种层该配哪种几何"的先验，并且所有参数块共享一个固定的全局更新预算，缺乏自适应性与块级预算分配的灵活性。

Method: 提出FARO：(1) 将参数划分为块，以块为实现决策单位；(2) 依据每个块实际产生的反事实回报(counterfactual return)在函数空间为其选择几何/更新规则；(3) 冻结路由后，采用投资组合理论中的风险平价原则——跨块等风险贡献(ERC)重新分配冻结的块级预算。

Result: 在FineWeb训练的GPT模型上：FARO-Adaptive在完整训练计划下达到3.2780 nats，匹配最优手工配方NorMuonAndAdam(3.2794)，即无需人工设定逐层几何即可恢复人类先验；FARO-ERC在全部6个随机种子上均降低干净数据损失，平均0.0136 nats，符号检验、配对检验与置信区间一致，且超过运行间噪声，不增加时间或内存开销。机制上通过削减风险最高的块而非扩张最安全的块实现；在数据流被污染时仍能降低平均损失并在多数种子上胜出。

Conclusion: 以参数块为决策单元、结合反事实回报定几何与ERC分配预算，可自动复现并稳定超越手工先验的优化器配方，增益虽小但具有统计稳健性，且无额外开销，具备在噪声/损坏数据流下的鲁棒性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Training a large language model is compute-bound, and current recipes divide the work between optimizers by hand: orthogonalized momentum for attention and MLP matrices, Adam-style updates for the rest. This division rests on human priors about which layer type deserves which geometry, and it fixes a single global update budget that every parameter block shares. We introduce FARO, a function-space adaptive optimizer that makes the partition of parameters into blocks the unit of decision. FARO gives each block a geometry by its realized counterfactual return, then freezes the routes and reallocates the frozen per-block budget by cross-block equal risk contribution (ERC), the risk-parity principle from portfolio theory. On a GPT-style model trained on FineWeb, the framework gives two results. First, its FARO-Adaptive arm reaches 3.2780 nats at the full schedule, matching NorMuonAndAdam (3.2794), the best hand-designed recipe: it recovers the human prior without hand-setting per-layer geometry. Second, FARO-ERC improves on that arm: it lowers the clean-data loss at every one of 6 seeds, by 0.0136 nats on average, a modest but robust gain. Its sign test, paired test, and confidence interval all agree, and it exceeds the measured run-to-run noise at no added time or memory. It works by trimming the riskiest blocks rather than expanding the safest, and it holds up under a corrupted stream, still lowering the mean loss and winning the seed majority. The source code is available at https://github.com/wxj630/FARO.

</details>


### [232] [Near-Optimal First-Order Regret for Bandits with Feedback Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40861)
*Mengxiao Zhang,Yuheng Zhang,Haipeng Luo,Paul Mineiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带固定已知有向反馈图的对抗赌博机的一阶遗憾界，在强可观测图上首次得到 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\alpha L^\star}+\alpha)$ 的期望遗憾界，弥合了最坏情况保证与一阶保证之间在图依赖上的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强可观测图上已有极小极大遗憾界为 $\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{\alpha T})$，而现有最好的一阶界为 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{(\kappa+1)L^\star})$，其中 $\kappa+1\geq\alpha$，两处图依赖（$\alpha$ 与 $\kappa$）不一致，存在差距。作者希望在一阶保证中获得与极小极大率相同的 $\alpha$ 依赖。

Method: 1) 先处理每个顶点带自环的 self-aware 图：将 EXP3 的更新与受"通过优化进行探索"（exploration by optimization）启发的采样规则结合；构造一个映射，其不动点给出采样分布，使单轮遗憾余项可由学习者的损失与图独立数 $\alpha$ 控制；证明通过几何收敛的迭代可在多项式时间内算出足够精确的不动点。2) 再通过改造 [LLZ20] 的两层聚合方法，把算法推广到一般强可观测图。

Result: 在 self-aware 图与一般强可观测图上均获得 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\alpha L^\star}+\alpha)$ 的期望遗憾界，其中 $L^\star$ 为最优臂的累积损失。

Conclusion: 该工作使一阶遗憾的图依赖由 clique 划分数 $\kappa$ 改进为独立数 $\alpha$，与极小极大最优率一致，从而闭合了对抗赌博机反馈图上一阶保证与最坏情况保证之间的差距。

Abstract: We study first-order regret guarantees for adversarial bandits with a fixed, known directed feedback graph. For strongly observable graphs, [ACBDK15] establishes a minimax regret of $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{αT})$, where $α$ denotes the independence number of the graph and $T$ denotes the horizon. On the other hand, the best existing first-order bound scales as $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{(κ+1) L^\star})$ [LLZ20], where $κ$ denotes the clique partition number, satisfying $κ+1\geqα$, and $L^\star$ denotes the cumulative loss of the best arm, leaving a gap between the graph dependence in worst-case and first-order guarantees. In this paper, we bridge this gap by obtaining an expected regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{αL^\star}+α)$. Our first result is an algorithm for the special case of self-aware graphs, in which every vertex has a self-loop. The algorithm combines the update of EXP3 [ACBFS02] with a sampling rule inspired by exploration by optimization [LS20]. More specifically, we construct a map whose fixed points yield sampling distributions that control the one-round regret remainder in terms of the learner's loss and the graph's independence number, which is crucial in deriving optimal first-order guarantees. We further show that a sufficiently accurate fixed point can be computed in polynomial time by a geometrically convergent iteration. Finally, we extend the algorithm to general strongly observable graphs and achieve the same $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{αL^\star}+α)$ guarantee by adapting the two-level aggregation approach of [LLZ20].

</details>


### [233] [TensorSSM: Memorizing Spatial Position in Dynamic State Space for Tensor Core Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40871)
*Sheng Zhang,Xinyi Wang,Maolin Wang,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TensorSSM，一种基于动态状态空间模型（SSM）的隐式神经表示（INR）函数式张量分解方法，通过 K 叉坐标序列化与跨模态状态发射，提升高维张量压缩的表达力与存储效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维张量存储开销巨大，现有函数式张量分解以神经网络将坐标映射到张量元素，减少参数量；但其将高阶张量展平为 1D 序列，导致局部坐标序列高度重叠、难以刻画跨模态空间依赖，表达力受限。

Method: 1) 按模态将坐标依据 K 叉规则分解为序列，实现由粗到细的空间 token 编码；2) 将 SSM 状态空间扩展为跨模态空间依赖的显式映射，把前序隐状态中的跨模态信息注入当前状态转移，使模态间交互增强各模态更新；3) 在序列维（粗到细 token）与状态维（跨模态记忆）两个方向上扩展张量表示，将空间信息融入状态空间与张量核。

Result: 大量实验显示 TensorSSM 在多种张量数据集上取得显著更高的重建精度与存储效率，且不依赖严格的空间假设，表现出稳健的表达能力。

Conclusion: TensorSSM 以 SSM 跨模态记忆替代 1D 折叠策略，有效提升状态自适应性与压缩表达力，是一种兼顾精度与存储效率的函数式张量分解框架。

Abstract: High-dimensional tensors are ubiquitous in real-world applications but lead to massive storage overhead. Functional tensor decomposition addresses this by employing neural networks to map element coordinates to tensor cores, significantly reducing the parameters for data compression. However, existing functional methods typically flatten high-order tensors into 1D sequences. This folding operation causes highly overlapping local coordinate sequences and struggles to capture complex cross-mode spatial dependencies, severely limiting their expressiveness on diverse datasets. To tackle these challenges, we propose TensorSSM, a novel Implicit Neural Representation (INR) for functional decomposition method that memorizes spatial positions within a dynamic State Space Model (SSM). Specifically, TensorSSM first decomposes mode-wise coordinates into sequences spit by K-ary rule, encoding spatial tokens from coarse to fine, which effectively captures spatial variations. Furthermore, we extend the SSM's state space to explicitly map cross-mode spatial dependencies, allowing inter-mode interactions to enhance the updates of each mode. By integrating cross-mode information from previous hidden states into the current state transitions, TensorSSM effectively expands tensor representation along two dimensions: the sequence dimension for coarse-to-fine spatial tokens, and the state dimension for cross-mode memorization. This design effectively integrates spatial information into the state space and the tensor core, substantially advancing the state adaptability and enhancing the expressiveness of the data compression. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TensorSSM achieves significantly high reconstruction accuracy and storage efficiency, exhibiting robust expressiveness across diverse tensor datasets without relying on strict spatial assumptions.

</details>


### [234] [TSFLab: A Self-Improving Agent-Agnostic Workbench for Time Series Forecasting Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40901)
*ChengAo Shen,Yanjun Zhao,Haoxin Liu,Bonnie Chen,Miles Ma,Dongjin Song,Hanghang Tong,Qingsong Wen,Jingrui He,Jingchao Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TSFLab：一个自改进、与智能体无关的"工作台"，为兼容的编码智能体提供共享资源和工具，以低成本开展时间序列预测（TSF）研究并持续维护研究资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSF论文与产品激增，研究者分析已有工作的负担加重：仅约20%的arXiv TSF论文提供代码链接，且已发布代码需人工维护，导致重复实现实验设置不一致、难以维护。直接使用Claude Code、Codex等现有编码智能体则成本高昂、难以扩展至大型研究项目（如基准测试），且缺乏自我维护能力。

Method: 设计TSFLab工作台，采用agent-agnostic架构，向兼容编码智能体提供共享资源与工具，支持低成本TSF研究与研究资源的持续维护；实验覆盖复现、基准测试与新方法开发三类任务。

Result: 复现与基准测试中，相较基线，任务完成率提升20–163%，每完成任务的fresh token消耗降低11–66%；智能体驱动方法开发的初步研究表明，经TSFLab可提升预测精度，且比其它设置减少32.8–73.6%的fresh token、缩短9.2–68.8%的开发时间。

Conclusion: 将AI智能体用于TSF基准测试乃至自动研究（autoresearch）是可行且高效的；TSFLab通过共享资源与自维护机制，在降低成本和提升可扩展性方面显著优于直接使用现有编码智能体，为TSF研究提供了可复用的基础设施。

Abstract: As publications and products on time series forecasting (TSF) proliferate, researchers face a growing burden of analyzing existing work. First, not all publications release code. For instance, only about 20% of arXiv TSF papers have a code link in the surveyed period. Second, released code also requires manual maintenance. Thus, TSF researchers must constantly adapt or implement existing works. However, independent implementations can differ in experimental settings and are difficult to maintain. We therefore propose to use AI agents for benchmarking TSF research and even supporting autoresearch, e.g., developing new TSF models. Yet directly applying existing coding agents, such as Claude Code and Codex, is suboptimal: prohibitive costs, limited scalability to large research projects (e.g., benchmarking), and lack of self-maintenance. To address these challenges, we propose TSFLab, a self-improving agent-agnostic "workbench" that equips compatible coding agents with shared resources and tools for low-cost TSF research and continuous maintenance of research resources. Our experiments cover reproduction, benchmarking, and new method development. In reproduction and benchmarking, TSFLab improves task completion by 20-163% and cuts fresh tokens per completed task by 11-66% across baselines. A pilot study of agent-driven method development also shows improved forecasting accuracy via TSFLab, with 32.8-73.6% fewer fresh tokens and 9.2-68.8% less development time than the other settings.

</details>


### [235] [No Channels Left Behind: Understanding Diffusion Acceleration through Residual Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40914)
*Junha Park,Jaemyung Yu,Jaehui Hwang,Jong-Seok Lee,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视扩散 Transformer 训练加速机制，发现 REPA、弥散正则等方法实质性地重组了 AdaLN-Zero 残差门控 α，而非仅依赖辅助特征结构；据此提出 nPR 度量门控通道组织，并给出无需教师的径向目标提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加速方法（REPA、dispersive regularization）通常被解释为额外监督、空间结构先验或特征几何改善，但作者怀疑加速部分来源于内部残差门控通路的重塑，需要验证这一被忽视的内部变化。

Method: 1) 分析 AdaLN-Zero 中控制条件更新进入残差流的门 α 的变化；2) 通过 α 扰动敏感性实验与门几何与类别语义一致性的分析佐证假设；3) 提出归一化参与率 nPR 刻画门控的通道级组织；4) 设计无需教师的径向目标，通过特征范数正则扩大门参与度，避免成对特征比较。

Result: 加速模型对 α 扰动显著更敏感，门几何反映类别语义关系；在多种 REPA 教师、投影器架构与径向目标下，更宽的门参与度对应更好生成质量；在 ImageNet 256×256、SiT-B/2 上，本方法相较 dispersive loss 改善 FID，并提升跨时间步与类别的平均门参与度。

Conclusion: 残差门控重组是扩散训练加速可测量的一个维度，门参与度与加速效果相关联，并据此提供了简单、无需教师的进一步加速方向。

Abstract: Training acceleration methods for diffusion transformers, such as REPA and dispersive regularization, are commonly explained by additional supervision, spatial-structure priors, or improved feature geometry. We revisit this view and uncover a striking internal change: the AdaLN-Zero residual gate $α$, which directly controls how conditioned updates enter the residual stream, is substantially reorganized by these methods. This observation motivates our hypothesis that acceleration arises, at least in part, from reshaping the residual gating pathway rather than solely from the feature structures explicitly imposed by auxiliary objectives. Supporting this view, accelerated models are markedly more sensitive to perturbations of $α$, and their gate geometry reflects semantic relationships among classes. We further introduce the normalized participation ratio (nPR) to characterize the channel-wise organization of the gates and find that, across REPA teachers, projector architectures, and radial-objective targets, broader gate participation accompanies better generation quality. Guided by this insight, we propose a simple teacher-free radial objective that promotes broader gate participation through feature-norm regularization, without pairwise feature comparisons. On ImageNet $256\times256$ with SiT-B/2, our method improves FID over dispersive loss while increasing mean gate participation over timesteps and classes, further supporting the link between gate organization and training acceleration. Our results identify residual-gate reorganization as a measurable axis of diffusion training acceleration and suggest a simple direction for further acceleration.

</details>


### [236] [A Theoretical Framework for Understanding and Building "Any-task inference" Capable Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40934)
*Matthew McDermott,Florent Pollet,Payal Chandak,Gregory Kondas,Liat Friedman Antwarg,Noa Dagan,Isaac Kohane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"任意任务推理"(ATI)基础模型的形式化框架,给出数学定义、任务约简分解与误差分解,并归纳生成式/判别式两类实现范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型研究多集中于"预训练+微调"范式,而面向零样本式"任意任务推理"的模型缺乏统一、可分析的形式化定义,导致其能力边界、最优性与误差来源难以理论刻画。

Method: 将"任务空间"(space of tasks of interest)纳入问题域并形式化;基于接口方式定义ATI模型为将数据样本x与任务t直接映射到标签空间的预测器;证明该类模型可通过"通用任务约简"分解为编码器、模型与解码器三部分;据此把基础模型误差分解为"设计项"(反映约简结构的理论性质、判定最优性)与"已实现模型超额项"(刻画学习组件与程序化组件间的交互);并给出生成式与判别式两种原型实现配方。

Result: 建立了ATI基础模型的形式化定义与通用任务约简分解;得到可分层解释的误差分解框架,含决定最优性可达性的设计项与刻画组件交互的已实现超额项;提炼出生成式与判别式两类原型范式;并用5个差异化真实案例验证定义的可适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架以简洁的任务空间+接口定义为起点,统一刻画"任意任务推理"型基础模型的结构、误差来源与实现路径,为理论分析与跨领域设计提供可迁移的参照。

Abstract: In this work, we provide a formal framework and mathematical definition for the subset of foundation models focused on enabling the property of "any-task inference" (ATI) (similar to zero-shot inference), rather than those designed for pre-training fine-tuning. To achieve this mathematical definition, we formalize the notion of a "space of tasks of interest," which we include in the problem domain, then provide an interface-based formal definition for ATI foundation models as those that map samples of data $x$ and tasks $t$ to predictions over the space of labels directly. While simple, this definition allows us to show that all ATI foundation models can be decomposed through a "universal task reduction" lens into encoder, model, and decoder aspects. We can leverage this decomposition to also decompose foundation model error into terms corresponding to different aspects of this reduction, stratified into a collection of "design terms," which are theoretical properties that can inform one of whether a particular proposed ATI foundation model can achieve optimality or not due to the structure of the proposed task reduction, and "realized model excess" terms which capture interactions between learned and programmatic components of foundation model systems. Lastly, we can use this perspective to offer two "prototypical" recipes for realizing ATI foundation models in diverse domains, which we term generative and discriminative variants of ATI foundation models. This exercise is grounded throughout by 5 real-world examples of foundation models in different settings, varying greatly in how these capabilities are realized, showing the applicability of this definition to real-world settings.

</details>


### [237] [Fair Recommender Systems for Intersecting Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40936)
*Christine Ling,Juba Ziani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究动态推荐系统中用户属于可交叉多重群体时的公平性问题，证明群体盲策略线性遗憾，并提出基于UCB的群体感知算法（Intersection-Fitting及Inner-Outer UCB、Group-Item UCB两种启发式），在Goodreads与BoardGameGeek数据集上验证了不同算法适用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向"平均用户"优化的推荐系统难以捕捉多元人口群体的偏好差异；且现有工作多忽略群体的交叉性（用户可同时属于多个群体），需要对交叉群体下的动态推荐遗憾进行建模与算法设计。

Method: 1) 理论分析：证明在温和条件下，任何群体盲策略（不使用当前用户群体信息）推荐K≥1个物品时会产生线性总遗憾。2) 提出三种基于UCB的群体感知算法：i) Intersection-Fitting，对每个交叉群体独立运行UCB，具有亚线性群体级与交叉级遗憾保证，但对群体数量呈指数依赖；ii) 两种群体级启发式——Inner-Outer UCB与Group-Item UCB，为不同群体分配不同臂，并用sleeping expert元学习器决定某交叉群体在任一时步应继承哪个活跃群体的推荐。

Result: 在Goodreads和BoardGameGeek两个数据集上的实验表明：当交叉层级偏好与群体层级偏好高度相关时，启发式算法优于Intersection-Fitting；而当同一群体内不同交叉群体偏好不同物品时，Intersection-Fitting表现更好。

Conclusion: 群体盲策略在交叉群体动态推荐中不可行（线性遗憾）；群体感知算法可获亚线性遗憾，但精度与效率存在权衡——算法选择应依据交叉偏好与群体偏好的相关程度决定。

Abstract: Recommendation systems that optimize for the average user can fail to capture the preferences of diverse demographic groups. We consider a dynamic recommendation system, where arriving users can belong to different demographic groups. Importantly, these groups can intersect, and a user may belong to several groups. We first show that over time, any group-blind policy (which excludes all current-user group information) recommending $K \geq 1$ items incurs linear total regret under mild conditions. In turn, we introduce three group-aware algorithms based on the classical Upper Confidence Bound algorithm (UCB): i) Intersection-Fitting, which runs UCB on each intersection, but has a sublinear group- and intersection-level regret guarantee with an exponential dependence in the number of groups; and ii) two group-level heuristics, Inner-Outer UCB and Group-Item UCB which allocate different arms to different groups, and use a sleeping expert meta-learner to choose which active group's recommendation an intersection should inherit at any given time step. We provide experimental results on two datasets, Goodreads and BoardGameGeek, and highlight that our heuristics can be preferable to the intersection-fitting algorithm when intersection-level preferences are closely related to group-level preferences, but that intersection-fitting performs better when different intersections within a same group prefer different items.

</details>


### [238] [Kernel-based Interpolatory Methods for Context Compression of Attention Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40943)
*Michel Fabrice Serret,Laura Grigori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文从核函数对应的Hilbert空间视角重构注意力，将注意力表示为两个线性映射之比，并据此提出GAIM与Q-DAIM两种KV-cache压缩方法，配合Taylor级数、Performer与RFF代理模型实现线性时间压缩，并扩展到流式注意力场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制关于上下文长度的二次复杂度是语言模型的根本瓶颈，KV-cache压缩因此受到关注；现有方法缺乏从函数插值/核空间角度刻画"信息集中于何处"的理论框架。

Method: 先在核关联的Hilbert空间中把注意力表述为两个线性映射之比，且该比值等于其插值之比，从而可直接对线性映射做插值；基于GEIM与Q-DEIM分别构造GAIM和Q-DAIM两种压缩方法；由于原方法代价随上下文长度呈二次增长，引入基于Taylor级数、Performer和Random Fourier Features的代理模型实现线性时间压缩；进一步推广到流式注意力，加入依据已插值上下文惩罚插值节点选择的机制，并支持基于新信息更新已有插值。

Result: 给出了残差收敛性与未来查询输出误差的数值结果；在LongBench-E基准上的初步结果显示性能与最先进方法相当。

Conclusion: 实验表明Q-DAIM所揭示的"范围"有助于定位KV cache中信息集中的位置，从而支撑高效压缩；该方法为KV-cache压缩提供了基于核空间插值的统一框架与线性时间实现路径。

Abstract: The bottleneck of attention mechanisms, which are the fundamental building block of modern language models, is their quadratic complexity in terms of context size. There has thus been an increasing interest in the compression of the context, also called KV-cache compression. In this article, we consider the problem of compressing the context of attention mechanisms by first formulating it in the Hilbert space associated with its kernel. In this formalism, attention can be represented as the ratio of two linear maps whose interpolant is also the ratio of interpolants, this allows us to consider the interpolation of the linear maps directly. We thus introduce two novel KV-cache compression methods which we call GAIM and Q-DAIM, based on the Generalized Empirical Interpolation Method (GEIM) and the Q-Discrete Empirical Interpolation Method (Q-DEIM), respectively. As the cost of these methods is quadratic in terms of context length, we propose surrogate models based on the Taylor series, the Performer and the Random Fourier Features method which allow the compression to be done in linear time. Finally, we extend this result to the streaming attention setting, providing new methods which allow one to penalize the selection of interpolation nodes based on the previously interpolated context and to update the previous interpolation based on new information. We then provide numerical results on the convergence of the residuals and the output error in future queries and preliminary results on the LongBench-E benchmark showing performance comparable to the state-of-the-art methods. These experiments suggest that the range revealed by our Q-DAIM method could help identify where information is concentrated in the KV cache, thus enabling an efficient compression.

</details>


### [239] [On the Origin and Evolution of Training-Inference Mismatch in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40945)
*Yu Li,Yufa Zhou,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分析了大模型on-policy RL中训练-推理不一致（training-inference mismatch）的两种来源——推理引擎数值差异与异步采样造成的陈旧权重，指出二者需要不同的纠正方式：数值差异近似恒定、可通过在每个前缀处对score中心化消除其主偏差；策略滞后则因训练器更新形成反馈回路，需重要性加权裁剪并在有效样本量（ESS）跌破0.5时重同步采样器。据此提出DLC（discrepancy-lag correction）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy RL理论上要求训练响应来自当前策略，但实际由独立推理引擎采样、训练器计算梯度，导致数据实际为off-policy。这种不匹配有数值与陈旧权重两个来源，而一种能稳定其中一源的纠正可能在另一源下失效，因此需要回答：决定纠正方式的是不匹配来源的哪一种性质。

Method: 在采样器权重处评估训练器，将每个采样token误差精确分解为引擎的numerical discrepancy与训练器的policy lag，并在训练全程跟踪二者。据此设计DLC：对numerical discrepancy做中心化（在每个前缀处对score中心化以去除主偏差）、用采样器权重对lag ratio进行裁剪、并在ratio的有效样本量低于0.5时重同步采样器。

Result: 数值差异近似恒定，因而以近乎固定量偏置更新；policy lag由训练器自身更新生成，形成“偏差—更大滞后”的反馈回路，重要性加权仅当lag ratio保持集中时才有效，在Qwen3-1.7B上ESS降至0.5以下时失效。在陈旧采样器下，DLC使Qwen3-0.6B与Qwen3-1.7B上相对on-policy训练的KL保持在两次独立on-policy训练之间KL的两倍以内；其重同步可防止逐token纠正遗留的崩溃，而这种崩溃无法被基于采样器前缀的KL检测到。
