As a secondary contribution, we therefore present a compact persistent-world scenario as a playable companion to the desiderata and outline a proposed benchmark that examines a broad range of associated behavioral capabilities together within one such lifetime.

</details>


### [311] [DIFFNAS: Predictor-Free Neural Architecture Search via Diffusion Model and Zero-Cost Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41818)
*Yize Sun,Hanzhang Ma,Zixin Wu,Xuechun Li,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DIFFNAS是一种无预测器的NAS框架，用零成本代理指标引导离散图扩散模型生成架构，在NAS-Bench-201上以更低成本找到最优结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NAS依赖模型训练或性能预测器，计算开销大；已有的扩散式NAS还需训练性能预测器来引导生成过程。

Method: 提出DIFFNAS：基于离散图扩散模型，以训练无关的零成本代理（MeCo、SWAP）作为条件引导生成过程，完全去除预训练性能预测器。

Result: 在NAS-Bench-201上无需预训练预测器即找到CIFAR-10与CIFAR-100最优架构（94.37%/73.51%），搜索成本较DiffusionNAG降低最高17.5倍；在MobileNetV3搜索空间上精度低于DiffusionNAG。

Conclusion: 扩散模型结合零成本代理可实现可扩展、高效的架构搜索，但在较大搜索空间上精度仍有待提升。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has advanced the automated design of deep networks, but remains computationally expensive due to reliance on model training or performance predictors. We introduce DIFFNAS, a predictor-free NAS framework that leverages a discrete graph diffusion model guided by zero-cost performance proxies. Unlike prior diffusion-based NAS methods that require learned performance predictors, DIFFNAS conditions the generative process on training-free metrics such as MeCo and SWAP, enabling efficient architecture search with minimal computational overhead. Experiments on NAS-Bench-201 show that DIFFNAS finds the optimal architectures for CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 (94.37% and 73.51%) without a pretrained predictor, while reducing search cost by up to 17.5x compared to DiffusionNAG. On the MobileNetV3 search space, its accuracy is lower than that of DiffusionNAG. These results highlight the promise of diffusion models combined with zero-cost proxies for scalable and effective architecture search.

</details>


### [312] [ElTab: Elastically Looped Tabular Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41819)
*Mieszko Komisarczyk,Saurabh Mathur,Maurice Kraus,Sriraam Natarajan,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Elastically Looped Tabular Transformer（ElTab），通过在随机采样的早期退出处施加监督，训练单个模型即可适配不同计算预算，在30个OpenML分类数据集上以更低的训练与推理成本达到与深度特定模型及12层TabPFN式基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TabPFN等表格Transformer预测性能强但推理成本高，难以在资源受限场景部署；loop transformer虽通过权重共享降低参数量，但过早停止会显著损失预测能力，缺乏在部署预算与性能间灵活权衡的手段。

Method: 提出ElTab模型：对循环结构在不同深度（随机采样的early exit）处同时施加监督，训练一个可在多种计算预算下运行的单一模型，实现弹性深度（elastic-depth）推理。

Result: 在30个OpenML分类数据集上，单个ElTab模型性能与6个深度特定的loop模型相当，训练成本降低一半以上，且以少25%的推理FLOPs匹配12层TabPFN式基线。

Conclusion: 弹性深度训练能产生一个可自适应部署预算的单一模型，在预测性能、存储与计算成本之间给出有利的权衡。

Abstract: Tabular transformer models such as TabPFN achieve strong predictive performance but incur substantial inference costs, making them difficult to deploy in resource-constrained settings. While looped transformers reduce parameter count through weight sharing, they can lose significant predictive power when stopped early. To address this challenge, we introduce the Elastically Looped Tabular Transformer (ElTab), a single model trained to operate across different computational budgets through supervision at randomly sampled early exits. Across 30 OpenML classification datasets, a single ElTab model matches six depth-specific looped models while cutting training cost by more than half, and matches a 12-layer TabPFN-style baseline with 25% fewer inference FLOPs. These results show that elastic-depth training yields a single model that adapts to the deployment budget at hand, offering a favorable trade-off among predictive performance, storage, and computational cost.

</details>


### [313] [Trainable Vector Quantization for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41822)
*Anastasiia Prutianova,Alina Bogdanova,Mikhail Shutov,Dmitrii Poimanov,Anatoly Gorkov,Ilia Zharikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TVQ，利用直通估计（STE）将 VQ 量化权重重参数化为可训练隐权重，实现基于梯度下降的任意目标优化，结合分块知识蒸馏范式，在低比特 PTQ 下取得 SOTA 质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQ 是压缩 LLM 权重、降低显存与加速推理的有效手段，但极端压缩会损害模型性能；由于 VQ 参数的离散性，性能难以恢复。现有方法仅为免训练方案，局限于逐层重建损失最小化，优化目标受限、恢复能力不足。

Method: 1) 引入直通估计（STE），使向量量化 LLM 可通过梯度下降优化；2) 以可训练隐权重（trainable latent weights）重表述 VQ LLM 优化问题，从而支持任意目标函数的最小化；3) 将 TVQ 嵌入分块（block-wise）知识蒸馏框架，兼顾质量与训练效率；4) 提出若干加速训练的实用策略；实现基于朴素 PyTorch。

Result: 在现代 LLM 上验证 TVQ 与主流 VQ 方案兼容；在低比特训练后量化（PTQ）设定下以适度训练预算取得 SOTA 质量。例如对 LLaMA2-70B 实现近乎无损的 2-bit 量化，在主流问答基准上精度仅下降约 1%。

Conclusion: TVQ 通过可训练隐权重与 STE 突破了免训练 VQ 方法仅优化逐层重建损失的局限，配合分块蒸馏在极低比特下实现近无损压缩，为 VQ 量化 LLM 提供了通用、高效且易实现的优化范式。

Abstract: Vector Quantization (VQ) is a rapidly evolving technique for compressing Large Language Model (LLM) weights, reducing memory usage, and speeding up inference. However, extreme compression deteriorates model performance, which is challenging to recover due to the discrete nature of VQ parameters. Existing methods employ training-free approaches that are limited to layer-wise reconstruction loss minimization. To address this limitation, we propose TVQ, a novel method that leverages straight-through estimation to enable gradient descent-based optimization of vector-quantized LLMs. By reformulating VQ LLM optimization with trainable latent weights, TVQ enables arbitrary objective minimization. We constitute our approach by applying TVQ in a block-wise knowledge distillation paradigm, which balances quality and training efficiency, and propose several practical solutions to accelerate training. In experiments on modern LLMs, we demonstrate that TVQ is compatible with modern VQ schemes. As a result, TVQ achieves state-of-the-art quality in low-bit post-training quantization settings with a modest training budget and straightforward PyTorch implementation. For instance, TVQ achieves near-lossless 2-bit quantization for LLaMA2-70B with only 1% accuracy drop on popular Question-Answering benchmarks.

</details>


### [314] [On k-nearest-neighbor regression in metric spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41896)
*László Györfi,Vladimir G. Pestov,Harro Walk,Roi Weiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究度量空间取值特征的k近邻回归的均方一致性，通过"选择密度"将误差分解为逼近误差与条件响应方差两部分，给出弱于Stone支配条件的充分条件，并指出σ-有限Nagata维下k-NN分类的普适一致性不能推广到无界响应的均方一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典k-NN均方一致性依赖Stone支配条件，但该条件在高维或一般度量空间中常不成立且过于保守。作者希望刻画邻居选择如何通过概率再分配影响误差，从而在更强的响应矩条件下放宽支配条件，并厘清Nagata维与均方一致性之间的确切关系。

Method: 对固定特征分布mu（距离分布无原子），定义独立查询点所选最近邻的"选择密度"，用其刻画邻居选择导致的概率再分配；据此将均方误差分解为逼近误差项和条件响应方差项，分别给出独立条件。将条件改写为固定总体质量邻域的形式，并给出反例构造：在σ-有限Nagata维的完全可分空间中构造一个所有矩有限的无噪声响应，使均方误差发散。

Result: 当k_n→∞、k_n/n→0且响应四阶矩有限时，选择密度的L2(mu)一致有界即可保证均方一致性；在固定质量邻域形式下可恢复欧氏密度界；有限全局Nagata维蕴含Stone支配条件。反例表明σ-有限Nagata维保证k-NN分类普适一致性，但对无界响应的均方一致性不成立。

Conclusion: 邻居选择密度为k-NN回归的一致性分析提供了精细且与维数无关的工具，弱化了Stone条件；Nagata维对均方一致性不足，分类与回归的一致性在无界响应下存在本质差异。

Abstract: We study mean squared consistency of k-nearest-neighbor regression for metric space valued features. For a fixed feature distribution mu with atomless distance distributions, we describe how neighbor selection redistributes probability through the density of a uniformly chosen neighbor of an independent query. This gives separate conditions for the approximation error and the conditional response variance, and weakens Stone's domination condition when stronger response moments are available. In particular, when k_n tends to infinity and k_n/n tends to zero, a uniform L2(mu) bound on the selection densities suffices under a finite fourth response moment. We give a corresponding condition in terms of neighborhoods of fixed population mass and recover the Euclidean density bound. Finite global Nagata dimension gives Stone's domination. While sigma-finite Nagata dimensionality is known to ensure universal consistency of k-NN classification in complete separable spaces, we show that this is not the case for mean squared consistency with unbounded responses: we construct a space in this class on which one fixed noiseless response with all finite moments has diverging mean squared error.

</details>


### [315] [Binary Sparse Codes: Where are we on Energy Efficient Representations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41933)
*Jose Miguel Ramos,Luis SaCouto,Andreas Wichert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将"信息保留"形式化为"相似性保留"的度量框架，在匹配条件下比较四类二值稀疏编码方法，发现稀疏性与信息保留之间存在系统性权衡；仅竞争性稀疏编码（CSC）同时达到近最大稀疏性与有意义的信息保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物神经系统在严格代谢约束下依靠稀疏皮层表征实现高效计算，启发了人工稀疏编码研究。现有二值稀疏编码方法分三大范式（高效编码理论方法、反向传播深度学习方法、基于局部Hebbian规则的生物可行方法），其编码质量既取决于低激活密度，也取决于是否保留输入的信息结构；但后者缺乏公认的定量定义，导致领域内无法有原则地比较各方法。

Method: 提出一组将"信息保留"形式化为"相似性保留"的指标，衡量二值稀疏码在多大程度上忠实再现输入空间的相对几何关系（关系结构）；并以此框架在匹配条件下比较四种代表性方法（覆盖上述三大范式）。

Result: 实验揭示一致性的权衡：信息保留高的模型以牺牲稀疏性为代价，而接近最优随机稀疏码均匀性的模型往往牺牲信息保留。竞争性稀疏编码（Competitive Sparse Codes, CSC）作为生物可行方法，是唯一占据"近最大稀疏性且保留有意义信息"区域的架构。

Conclusion: 同时满足高稀疏性与高信息保留仍是该领域的开放挑战；本文提供的可复用评估框架可用于衡量面向该目标的进展。

Abstract: Biological neural systems achieve impressive computational capabilities under tight metabolic constraints, relying on sparse cortical representations that minimise total neural activity. This combination of efficiency and representational power has motivated growing interest in sparse coding as a computational strategy for artificial systems. Artificial models designed to produce binary sparse codes span three broad paradigms, theoretical methods grounded in efficient coding, deep learning methods trained via backpropagation, and biologically plausible methods that learn through local Hebbian rules, each yielding codes whose quality depends not only on achieving low activation density but also on preserving the informational structure of the input. The latter property, however, lacks an agreed-upon quantitative definition, leaving the field without a principled basis for comparing methods. We propose a set of metrics that formalise information preservation as similarity preservation, measuring how faithfully a binary sparse code reproduces the relational geometry of the input space, and apply this framework to compare four representative methods under matched conditions. The results reveal a consistent trade-off in which models that achieve high information preservation do so at the cost of sparsity, while models approaching the uniformity of an optimal random sparse code tend to sacrifice it. Competitive Sparse Codes, a biologically plausible approach, is the only architecture to occupy a region of near-maximal sparsity while retaining meaningful information preservation. These findings frame the simultaneous satisfaction of both factors as an open challenge for the field and provide a reusable evaluation framework for measuring progress toward it.

</details>


### [316] [Observable-Specific Validity Tests for Scientific AI: Controlled Approximations and Intervention Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41939)
*Xiaoshn Nee,Haobo Zhong,Xiaomin Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"可观测特定有效性记录"（observable-specific validity records）框架，将科学AI的近似结果与其参考模型、误差度量、容差、参数域及变换/干预绑定，并为奇异摄动振子、Hubbard二聚体、神经拟合、因果模型与机制判别等任务给出可独立检验的有效性凭证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似模型可能复现能量却缺失响应，或满足误差容差却未获特定界认证；现有科学AI评估缺乏将近似与参考模型、误差度量、容差、参数域和变换联系起来的显式有效性凭证，导致能量与响应准则接受不同耦合域时难以判断。

Method: 给出可观测特定有效性记录的定义，并在多类体系上构造显式凭证：（1）奇异摄动振子的位移与速度凭证，恢复不同收敛阶；（2）Hubbard二聚体的精确关系解析能量与响应准则的耦合域差异；（3）16次重复神经拟合中施加量纲齐次性以检验能量尺度迁移；（4）配对变换验证受保护的标度关系，耦合交叉点识别响应迁移所需额外信息；（5）因果模型在强负观测关联下恢复正干预响应；（6）高斯噪声下响应首次在 $x^k$ 阶不同的两机制，推导精确固定控制查询需求 $x^{-2k}$。

Result: 施加量纲齐次性将能量尺度迁移误差从52.3%降至0.0768%，并将同时满足能隙与响应容差的中位比例由0升至100%；配对变换验证了受保护标度关系；耦合交叉点揭示响应迁移所需额外信息；因果模型在强负观测关联下恢复正向干预响应；推导出精确控制查询需求按 $x^{-2k}$ 缩放并数值验证其完整成功曲线；提供256任务评估接口。

Conclusion: 该框架为科学AI中的受控近似、物理迁移与机制判别提供可独立检验的测试，强调响应凭证与量纲/变换约束对保证近似有效性的必要性。

Abstract: An approximation can reproduce an energy while missing its response, or satisfy an error tolerance without being certified by a given bound. We formulate observable-specific validity records for scientific artificial intelligence (AI) that link a reduction to its reference model, error measure, tolerance, parameter domain, and transformations or interventions. Explicit displacement and velocity certificates for a singularly perturbed oscillator recover distinct convergence orders and provide sufficient validity guarantees. Exact Hubbard dimer relations resolve the different coupling domains accepted by energy and response criteria. Across 16 repeated neural fits, imposing dimensional homogeneity reduces energy scale transfer error from 52.3% to 0.0768% and raises the median fraction jointly satisfying gap and response tolerances from zero to 100%. Paired transformations verify the protected scaling relation, while a coupling crossover identifies the additional information needed for response transfer. A causal model recovers a positive intervention response beneath a strong negative observational association. For two mechanisms observed with Gaussian noise whose responses first differ at order $x^k$, we derive an exact fixed control query requirement scaling as $x^{-2k}$ and verify its full success curve numerically. Together with a 256-task evaluation interface, these results provide independently checkable tests of controlled approximation, physical transfer, and mechanism discrimination in scientific AI.

</details>


### [317] [Chronos-2: A foundation model for multivariate time series forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41956)
*Abdul Fatir Ansari,Oleksandr Shchur,Jaris Küken,Andreas Auer,Boran Han,Pedro Mercado,Syama Sundar Rangapuram,Huibin Shen,Lorenzo Stella,Xiyuan Zhang,Mononito Goswami,Shubham Kapoor,Danielle C. Maddix,Pablo Guerron,Florian Saupe,Markus Reichstein,Tony Hu,Junming Yin,Nick Erickson,Prateek Mutalik Desai,Hao Wang,Huzefa Rangwala,George Karypis,Yuyang Wang,Michael Bohlke-Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出时间序列基础模型Chronos-2，可在零样本条件下对多变量时间序列与异构协变量之间的交互建模并输出概率预测，在多个基准与科学领域任务上达到SOTA且推理高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准预测并量化不确定性对流行病学、经济学、能源与地球科学等至关重要，但现有预测模型通常需针对每个领域数据集单独开发与训练。时间序列基础模型（TSFM）旨在通过大规模预训练实现跨域通用，然而多数现有TSFM将各时间序列独立处理，无法建模序列间交互及外部驱动因素，限制了其在科学场景中的适用性。

Method: 提出Chronos-2基础模型：直接从历史上下文推断多变量序列之间及与异构协变量的关系，无需任务特定处理，即可生成零样本概率预测；模型通过大规模预训练获得跨域泛化能力。

Result: 在多种基准上平均达到SOTA，技能评分较最强基线提升5.2个百分点，胜率提高10个百分点；计算高效，单块中端GPU上不到1秒即可为100条序列生成预测，端到端分别比最佳任务特定点预测器与概率预测器快约50倍和1000倍；在传染病预测、宏观经济建模、碳通量预测与径流模拟中，性能与各领域专用方法持平或更优。

Conclusion: 单一预训练模型即可捕捉跨时间系统共享的结构，支持跨领域零样本概率预测，表明基础模型范式可有效替代逐域定制的预测方法。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting with uncertainty quantification is critical in a variety of scientific applications ranging from epidemiology and economics to energy systems and earth sciences. Yet most forecasting models still need to be developed and trained separately for each domain-specific dataset. Time series foundation models (TSFMs) aim to remove this limitation and enable the application of the same model across domains through large-scale pretraining. However, most existing TSFMs treat each time series independently and therefore cannot model interactions among time series and external drivers. Here, we introduce Chronos-2, a foundation model for zero-shot forecasting that models the interactions between multivariate time series and heterogeneous covariates, which commonly arise in scientific applications. The model infers relationships among time series directly from historical context and generates probabilistic forecasts without task-specific treatment. Across various benchmarks, Chronos-2 achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on average by improving skill scores by 5.2 percentage points relative to the strongest baselines and increasing win rates by 10 percentage points. Chronos-2 is also computationally efficient, generating forecasts for 100 time series in under one second on a single mid-range GPU, 50x and 1000x faster end-to-end than the best task-specific point and probabilistic forecasters, respectively. In addition, we demonstrate its effectiveness in diverse scientific settings -- including infectious-disease forecasting, macroeconomic modeling, carbon-flux prediction, and runoff simulation -- where the model matches or surpasses specialized methods developed for each domain. These results show that a single pretrained model can capture structure shared across temporal systems.

</details>


### [318] [Robust Learning of Multi-Index Models under Logconcave Distributions via Weak Partial Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41961)
*Ilias Diakonikolas,Daniel M. Kane,Mingchen Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于“弱部分学习器”的 boosting 框架，结合递归矩分区与矩差异检测，实现对 logconcave 分布下多指标模型等概念类更快、鲁棒的 agnostic 学习器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 boosting 依赖难以实现的 distribution-free 弱学习器，或只能保证弱近似；而在 logconcave 分布下已有的多指标模型与半空间函数学习器效率不足。

Method: 以部分分类（标签可部分未定义）的弱学习器支撑强 agnostic 学习；为解决条件分布不再保证 logconcave 的问题，设计递归的基于矩的分区：将空间划分为多项式多个凸区域，使大多数区域上 X 与 X' 的低阶矩近似一致；好区域上多指标模型与低阶多项式相关，产生弱假设。核心组件是矩差异检测：结合矩张量谱分析与 logconcave 测度的等周不等式，找到捕捉 X 与 X' 非平凡差异的低维子空间。

Result: 在 logconcave 边缘分布下，为半空间函数、多类线性分类器等基础概念类获得改进的学习器；相较已有方法显著更快且鲁棒，并可扩展到部分分类与可检验学习（testable learning）。

Conclusion: 弱部分学习器足以实现强 agnostic 学习，矩分区分区与矩差异检测是可行且通用的技术工具，具有独立价值。

Abstract: We develop a new algorithmic framework for learning Boolean concept classes under logconcave distributions. Our framework applies to broad families of multi-index models and yields significantly faster robust learners than were previously known in these settings. It extends to partial classification and testable learning. Consequently, we obtain improved learners for several fundamental concept classes, including functions of halfspaces and multiclass linear classifiers, under logconcave marginals.
  Our starting point is a new boosting framework based on weak partial learners. Classical boosting approaches require often-intractable distribution-free weak learners or yield weak approximation guarantees. We show instead that weak learners for partial classification, where labels may be undefined on part of the domain, suffice for strong agnostic learning. The key challenge is that boosting requires a weak learner that succeeds under conditioned distributions that are not guaranteed to be logconcave. To address this, we develop a recursive moment-based partitioning procedure: given a logconcave distribution $X$ and a conditional version $X'$, we partition the space into polynomially many convex regions so that, on most regions, the low-degree moments of $X$ and $X'$ approximately agree. On each good region, a well-behaved multi-index model correlates with a low-degree polynomial, giving a weak hypothesis. Combining these hypotheses yields the required weak learner.
  A key ingredient of our approach is an efficient method for detecting moment discrepancy. Specifically, we show how to find a low-dimensional subspace that captures any non-trivial discrepancy between $X$ and $X'$, if one exists. Our approach combines spectral analysis of moment tensors with recent progress on isoperimetry for logconcave measures. This discrepancy-finding procedure may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [319] [New Lower Bounds for Private PAC Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41980)
*Prathamesh Dharangutte,Amartya Sanyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究近似差分隐私下的可实现PAC学习，提出ICP（独立类打包）参数框架，证明VC维与Littlestone维不能决定私有样本复杂度，并给出多个概念类（轴对齐矩形、区间并、线性子空间、半空间、凸多边形等）的紧/改进下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作表明VC维与Littlestone维决定非私有PAC学习的样本复杂度，但在差分隐私约束下，二者是否仍能刻画样本复杂度尚不明确；作者希望给出通用下界框架并厘清维数与私有样本复杂度之间的关系。

Method: 构造具有相同VC维和Littlestone维但在常见隐私/精度参数下私有样本复杂度不同的概念类，证明这些维数不能决定样本复杂度（即便允许非真学习器）；进而引入独立类打包参数ICP(ℋ,𝒢)，定义为可独立打包进ℋ的概念类𝒢的最大样本复杂度总和，并证明任意(ε,δ)-DP、(α,β)-PAC学习器（可为非真）需要Ω(ICP(ℋ,𝒢)/(αε))个样本。

Result: 利用ICP框架得到轴对齐矩形、区间并、线性子空间的紧下界，以及半空间和凸多边形的改进下界；同时证明对乘积类（更一般地，当相同类既打包进ℋ又覆盖其域、可直接使用目标标签时），ICP在对数因子内刻画私有PAC样本复杂度。

Conclusion: VC维和Littlestone维不足以确定差分隐私下的可实现PAC样本复杂度；ICP参数提供了统一且往往紧的下界工具，并在乘积类等结构下给出对数因子内的刻画。

Abstract: We study realizable PAC learning under approximate differential privacy, and give a framework for proving lower bounds on its sample complexity. We construct classes with identical VC and Littlestone dimensions but different private sample complexities at common privacy and accuracy parameters, showing that these dimensions do not determine the sample complexity, even for improper learners. To address this we introduce the Independent Class Packing (ICP) parameter, the largest total sample complexity of classes in $\mathcal{G}$ that can be packed independently into a class $\mathcal{H}$, and prove that every $(\varepsilon, δ)$-DP, $(α, β)$-PAC learner for $\mathcal{H}$, possibly improper, uses $Ω\left(\mathrm{ICP}(\mathcal{H}, \mathcal{G})/(α\varepsilon)\right)$ examples. Using this framework we prove tight lower bounds for axis-aligned rectangles, union of intervals and linear subspaces and improved lower bounds for halfspaces and convex polygons. Finally we also show that for product classes, and more generally when the same classes pack into $\mathcal{H}$ and cover its domain so that target labels can be used directly, ICP characterizes private PAC sample complexity up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [320] [When Does Jacobian Fine-Tuning Stabilize Neural PDE Emulators?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42013)
*Shreeyam Bangera,Manya Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一阶误差分解，用"放大率"量化神经PDE仿真器自身动力学对一步误差的放大程度，并说明Jacobian微调的收益约等于一步精度损失除以放大率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经仿真器以单步预测训练、却自回归部署，误差会累积。基于Jacobian的正则化试图抑制误差增长，但在混沌系统中部分增长是物理的、无法被训练消除，因此需要区分误差增长的可控与不可控来源。

Method: 采用一阶分解，将仿真器误差与一个"反事实"对比：把相同的一步误差通过求解器Jacobian传播，从而定义放大率（模型自身动力学放大其误差的倍数）。在四个PDE、五种架构、两种模型规模（8–10k与33k参数）共21种配置上，于一个Lyapunov时间处测量误差。并比较沿模型自身误差方向匹配求解器Jacobian（EDJM）与随机方向的微调效果，以及输入噪声注入。

Result: 放大率≤1.25的34个种子中仅1个在Jacobian微调后误差下降超过10%；放大率≥4的20个种子则全部受益，误差几何平均下降8倍（小模型）和55倍（33k模型）。增益约等于微调的一步精度代价除以放大率。EDJM在35个种子中的28个上以同等代价优于随机方向；输入噪声注入效果与EDJM相当。

Conclusion: 放大率可预测Jacobian类微调的收益上限，并给出收益与一步精度代价之间的定量权衡；当模型动力学误差放大不强时，此类微调几乎无效。

Abstract: Neural emulators of chaotic PDEs are trained on one-step prediction but deployed autoregressively, where their errors compound. Jacobian-based regularisers aim to curb this growth, but in a chaotic system part of the growth is physical and cannot be trained away. We separate the two sources of this error with a first-order decomposition. We compare the emulator's error with a counterfactual that propagates the same one-step mistakes through the solver's Jacobian, giving an amplification ratio: the factor by which the model's own dynamics amplify its errors. We study 21 configurations across four PDEs, five architectures and two model sizes (8-10k and 33k parameters), with errors measured at one Lyapunov time. Jacobian fine-tuning reduced the error by more than 10% for 1 of 34 seeds whose amplification ratio was at most 1.25, and for all 20 seeds whose ratio was at least four; for the latter, the error fell by a factor of 8 (small models) and 55 (33k models) in geometric mean over seeds. The gain approximately equals the one-step accuracy cost of fine-tuning divided by the amplification ratio. Matching the solver's Jacobian along the model's own error direction (EDJM) outperformed random directions at the same cost on 28 of 35 seeds; input-noise injection performed comparably to EDJM.

</details>


### [321] [Know When the Equation Is Wrong: Structural Uncertainty for LLM-Guided Symbolic Regression under Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42024)
*Mostafa Cham,Bayu Adhi Tama,Jianwu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LLM-SR-UQ 框架，通过保留多个竞争方程结构并用其分歧刻画函数形式不确定性，扩展固定宽度保形预测为"结构区间"，在 LLM-SRBench 上显著改善外推覆盖率并保持分布内覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 引导的符号回归方法只输出单一最优方程，缺乏对备选函数形式的不确定性量化；不同方程可对观测数据拟合相当却在推断（外推）时差异巨大，而标准保形区间虽在分布内达到名义覆盖率，在外推场景下频繁失效。

Method: 保留相互竞争的多个方程结构，利用它们之间的预测分歧来表征关于潜在函数形式的不确定性；将标准固定宽度保形（conformal）方法扩展为"结构区间"（structural intervals），即用候选方程间的一致性/分歧来度量不确定性，并据此识别预测区间应在何处加宽。

Result: 在 LLM-SRBench 上的大量实验表明：标准保形区间分布内覆盖达标但外推下经常失效；本框架通过引入结构分歧显著提升覆盖率达成度，同时保持分布内覆盖率；应用于其他符号回归方法时亦表现出良好泛化性。

Conclusion: 以方程结构分歧为核心的不确定性量化是弥补保形预测在外推下失效的有效途径，且该思路可推广至其他符号回归方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can help discover mathematical equations from data, but most LLM-guided symbolic regression methods focus on a single best equation without quantifying uncertainty over alternative functional forms. This is challenging because distinct equations can fit the observed data similarly while producing substantially different extrapolations. Our experiments on the LLM-SRBench dataset show that standard conformal intervals achieve nominal in-distribution coverage but frequently fail under extrapolation. To tackle this challenge, we introduce LLM-SR-UQ, a framework that retains competing equation structures and uses their disagreement to characterize uncertainty about the underlying functional form. We extend the standard fixed-width conformal approach to quantify structural intervals, which measure disagreement among candidate equations and identify where prediction intervals should widen. Extensive experiments show our framework substantially improves coverage attainment by incorporating structural disagreement while preserving in-distribution coverage. Our approach also shows good generalizability when it is applied to other symbolic-regression methods.

</details>


### [322] [Hear No Evil: Detecting Gradient Leakage by Malicious Servers in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20651)
*Fei Wang,Baochun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从防御者视角系统分析联邦学习中的恶意梯度泄露攻击及其模型操纵技术，揭示"攻击有效性"与"隐蔽性"之间的核心权衡；并提出一种轻量级客户端侧检测机制，在本地训练前标记可疑的模型更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明联邦学习中的梯度更新可能泄露客户端本地数据的敏感信息，且恶意服务器通过操纵全局模型可诱发信息量更大的更新，使风险加剧。然而，现有工作多从攻击者视角出发，缺乏对这类恶意梯度泄露攻击及其使能手段的系统性防御方分析。

Method: 以防御者为视角，对恶意梯度泄露攻击及模型操纵技术进行综合分析，重点考察在包含常见归一化技术与联邦平均（FedAvg）的现实FL设置下攻击的表现；据此提出一种简单、轻量、通用性强的客户端侧检测机制，在本地训练开始前对可疑模型更新进行标记。

Result: 研究发现这些攻击无法同时做到高效重建私有数据与足够隐蔽以规避检测；在引入归一化技术与联邦平均的现实FL场景中，攻击的实际威胁显著受限，且通常可通过基础监控被检测出来。所提出的客户端侧检测机制能够以极小开销标记可疑更新。

Conclusion: 恶意梯度泄露攻击虽在理论上值得关注，但在实践中本质受限且往往可检测；所提轻量级检测机制进一步证明了以最低开销防御此类攻击的可行性，为注重隐私的联邦学习系统提供了可部署的安全保障。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that gradient updates in federated learning (FL) can unintentionally reveal sensitive information about a client's local data. This risk becomes significantly greater when a malicious server manipulates the global model to provoke information-rich updates from clients. In this paper, we adopt a defender's perspective to provide the first comprehensive analysis of malicious gradient leakage attacks and the model manipulation techniques that enable them. Our investigation reveals a core trade-off: these attacks cannot be both highly effective in reconstructing private data and sufficiently stealthy to evade detection -- especially in realistic FL settings that incorporate common normalization techniques and federated averaging.
  Building on this insight, we argue that malicious gradient leakage attacks, while theoretically concerning, are inherently limited in practice and often detectable through basic monitoring. As a complementary contribution, we propose a simple, lightweight, and broadly applicable client-side detection mechanism that flags suspicious model updates before local training begins, despite the fact that such detection may not be strictly necessary in realistic FL settings. This mechanism further underscores the feasibility of defending against these attacks with minimal overhead, offering a deployable safeguard for privacy-conscious federated learning systems.

</details>


### [323] [GhostPrefix: Synchronization-Free Universal Prefix Attacks for ASR Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42026)
*Shengding Liu,Ashwin Innuganti,Qiben Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GhostPrefix 是首个面向 ASR 的免同步通用对抗前缀攻击：学习一段短小可复用的音频前缀，前置到任意实时语音即可在无需预知内容与时间对齐的情况下实时操控识别输出，并在13个开源模型与真实空中传播场景下取得高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有音频对抗攻击多为逐句定制、需与受害者波形同步、或仅针对狭窄目标，难以在实时未知语音上生效；论文旨在解决跨未知、动态语音上下文保持稳定对抗效果的核心难题。

Method: 提出 GhostPrefix：结合 token 级损失与新颖的表示级目标以获得输入无关的稳定效果；支持三种模式（提示式语义引导、有目标转录劫持、无目标转写退化）；通过向良性环境声分布正则化提升感知隐蔽性，并增强对空中传播失真的鲁棒性。

Result: 在四个家族13个开源 ASR 模型上评估：数字域提示式引导成功率97.6%、有目标劫持93.4%，无目标攻击使 WER 升至1.18；空中传播下两模式成功率分别为91.5%和89.5%；攻击可传导至语音助手与 AI 笔记等下游管线，引发系统性语义误解。

Conclusion: GhostPrefix 揭示了免同步通用音频前缀是对真实世界 ASR 及其下游应用的一种实用且被低估的威胁。

Abstract: We present GhostPrefix, the first synchronization-free universal adversarial prefix attack against automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems. Unlike prior audio attacks, which are typically crafted per utterance, require synchronization with the victim waveform, or target narrow objectives, GhostPrefix learns a short, reusable audio segment prepended to arbitrary live speech to exert real-time control over ASR outputs without knowledge of the future utterance or temporal alignment. A key challenge is maintaining stable adversarial effects across unknown, evolving speech contexts. GhostPrefix combines token-level losses with a novel representation-level objective for stable, input-agnostic effects. GhostPrefix supports three attack modes: (i) prompted prefix attacks for semantic steering, (ii) targeted prefix attacks for transcript hijacking, and (iii) untargeted prefix attacks for transcription degradation. To remain perceptually inconspicuous, we regularize prefixes toward benign environmental-sound distributions and improve robustness against over-the-air distortions. We evaluate GhostPrefix across 13 open-source ASR models from four families. In digital settings, it achieves average attack success rates of 97.6% for prompted steering and 93.4% for targeted hijacking, while driving word error rate to 1.18 under untargeted attacks. Over the air, it achieves success rates of 91.5% and 89.5% for prompted and targeted modes, respectively. These manipulations propagate into downstream AI-assisted pipelines, including voice assistants and AI note-taking tools, causing systematic semantic misinterpretations. GhostPrefix exposes synchronization-free universal prefixes as a practical, under-explored threat to real-world ASR and downstream applications. Artifacts and demos are available at https://ghostprefix.github.io/.

</details>


### [324] [Leaner Training, Lower Leakage: Revisiting Memorization in LLM Fine-Tuning with LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20856)
*Fei Wang,Baochun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRA微调相比全参数微调显著降低大模型记忆（memorization）风险，同时保持任务性能；且模型规模、数据重复等在全参数微调中影响记忆的因素在LoRA下趋势不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练中的记忆问题已被充分研究，但微调阶段（尤其是广泛采用的LoRA参数高效微调）的记忆风险研究较少，且已有结论是否适用于LoRA尚不明确。

Method: 重新审视微调中的记忆现象，系统对比不同微调策略（LoRA与全参数微调），考察模型规模、数据重复等因素的影响，并采用更宽松的基于相似度的记忆度量指标进行评估。

Result: 发现LoRA微调的记忆趋势与预训练/全参数微调的先验发现存在显著差异：模型规模与数据重复等因素的效应不再遵循相同规律；在相似度度量下LoRA的记忆风险明显低于全参数微调，且任务性能仍然保持强劲。

Conclusion: LoRA不仅是高效的参数微调方法，还具备内在的记忆抑制优势，可作为降低数据抽取攻击风险的微调选择。

Abstract: Memorization in large language models (LLMs) makes them vulnerable to data extraction attacks. While pre-training memorization has been extensively studied, fewer works have explored its impact in fine-tuning, particularly for LoRA fine-tuning, a widely adopted parameter-efficient method.
  In this work, we re-examine memorization in fine-tuning and uncover a surprising divergence from prior findings across different fine-tuning strategies. Factors such as model scale and data duplication, which strongly influence memorization in pre-training and full fine-tuning, do not follow the same trend in LoRA fine-tuning. Using a more relaxed similarity-based memorization metric, we demonstrate that LoRA significantly reduces memorization risks compared to full fine-tuning, while still maintaining strong task performance.

</details>


### [325] [CausalXRL: Explainable Reinforcement Learning through Causal Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42028)
*Yanming Zhang,Eric Papenhausen,Klaus Mueller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出CausalXRL框架，利用因果图推理为强化学习智能体提供透明、结构化、多层次的决策解释，并通过量化实验与专家定性研究验证其解释保真度、因果结构学习效率及实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习虽在复杂环境中表现优异，但可解释性不足导致信任下降，并增加调试与改进智能体行为的难度，因此需要可解释强化学习（XRL）方案。

Method: 提出CausalXRL框架，核心为因果图推理，对智能体决策生成透明、结构化、多层次的解释；通过案例研究及两部分评估进行验证：一是基准RL环境中解释保真度与因果结构学习效率的定量分析，二是MicroRTS实时策略基准上的专家定性研究。

Result: 定量结果显示CausalXRL在提供保真解释的同时能高效学习因果结构；定性专家研究表明参与者认为该框架有助于审视高层RTS策略。

Conclusion: CausalXRL通过因果图推理有效兼顾解释保真性与学习效率，并在RTS场景中获得专家认可，为可解释强化学习提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a powerful paradigm for training autonomous agents and has achieved impressive performance in complex environments. However, this success often comes at the cost of interpretability, diminishing trust and complicating efforts to debug and improve agent behavior. To address these challenges, we introduce CausalXRL, a novel framework for explainable reinforcement learning (XRL). A key feature of CausalXRL is its use of causal graph reasoning, which provides transparent, structured, multi-level explanations of agent decision-making. We validate CausalXRL through comprehensive case studies and a two-part evaluation: (1) a quantitative analysis of explanation fidelity and causal-structure learning efficiency in benchmark RL environments, and (2) a qualitative expert study assessing explainability in the real-time strategy (RTS) benchmark MicroRTS. The quantitative results show that CausalXRL can provide faithful explanations while efficiently learning causal structures, and the qualitative expert study suggests that participants found CausalXRL useful for inspecting high-level RTS strategies.

</details>


### [326] [There Is No Free Loop: When Does Looping a Trained Model Pay for Its Inference Compute?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42030)
*Rhea Sanjay Sukthanker,Pashmina Cameron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文检验“预训练后加循环（looping）”是否值得其额外推理开销：在Qwen3/Qwen3.5 0.8B–9B上，只有0.8B模型的循环版本同时优于等参数无循环基线与等FLOPs无循环基线；4B与9B均未超过等参数基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环复用层块可在不增加参数量、磁盘体积、内存占用与训练成本的前提下加深有效计算，因此对已训练模型加循环很有吸引力；但循环使每个token的计算成本升至约(N+m)/N倍。大规模预训练中学到的循环可为其代价辩护，而预训练后追加的循环是否值得该成本尚不清楚。

Method: 设置两个简单基线进行对照：第一是与循环模型完全相同但不加循环的训练（参数量相等）；第二是在相同推理FLOPs下最优的无循环用法（FLOPs相等）。实验覆盖Qwen3与Qwen3.5，参数规模0.8B至9B。

Result: 仅循环版0.8B模型同时超越两个基线；在4B与9B上，循环模型未能超越第一个基线（同模型不加循环）。该结果并非因基准过易：同一基准上无循环9B模型仍比4B模型高出至多13分，说明提升空间充足。

Conclusion: 在已预训练模型中加入循环，只对参数预算固定的中小模型（0.8B量级）具有收益；对于4B及更大模型，预训练后加循环的收益不足以抵消额外推理开销。

Abstract: Looping re-applies a block of layers so that each token passes through the block more than once. Adding a loop to a trained model is attractive because it leaves the parameter count, and hence the disk size, the memory footprint and the training cost unchanged. Unfortunately, every generated token pays for the loop. Re-applying m of N layers makes each token about (N+m)/N times as expensive. Loops that a model learns during large-scale pretraining justify this price, but whether loops added after pretraining justify this cost remains unclear. We compare a looped model with two simple baselines. We first compare it with the same model trained identically without the loop (equal parameters). Secondly, we compare it with the best unlooped use of the same inference floating-point operations (equal FLOPs). Across Qwen3 and Qwen3.5 models from 0.8B to 9B parameters, only the looped 0.8B model beats both baselines. At 4B and 9B, the looped model does not beat the first baseline, i.e. the same model trained without the loop. This is not because the benchmarks are too easy, since on the same benchmarks the unlooped 9B model still scores up to 13 points higher than the 4B model, showing that there is ample room for improvement. Introducing loops in an already pretrained model therefore only pays for small models with a fixed parameter budget.

</details>


### [327] [A richness-sensitive Acoustic Biodiversity Index from species-level detections: classifier benchmarking, false-positive control and validation against standard soundscape indices in an African savanna and an urban zoo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42042)
*Muhammad Saeed,Dmitry Mikhaylov,Mohamed H. Alhosani,Yasser F. Al Wahedi,Rory Pilossof,Ahmed Arabi,Walid Shaaba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出并检验基于物种级检测的声学生物多样性指数ABI（主形式ABI_E为检测数Shannon多样性按区域物种池归一化），在热带强标注集与野外录音上验证其优于传统能量型声学指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统声学指数仅概括声能分布而非发声物种，与物种丰富度相关性弱，难以作为可靠生物多样性代理。

Method: 构建ABI_E = H/ln S_ref（区域期望物种池归一化的检测Shannon多样性），时间稳定性T单列；在1,216条叫声、24类的热带强标注数据上以录音分组交叉验证基准测试四类分类器；再用BirdNET v2.4加地点与季节过滤及事后地理过滤处理津巴布韦赞比西河谷稀树草原与阿布扎比动物园三生境录音，做昼夜匹配与稀释至等努力量(6 h)后比较。

Result: BirdNET嵌入+逻辑回归最优(macro-F1 0.750±0.029)，随机帧级划分将准确率由0.41虚增至0.70；事后过滤剔除517条9个不合理物种高置信检测(均来自动物园)。等努力量下稀树草原21种(1D=9.83，ABI_E=0.38)，动物园1.3–13种(ABI_E=0.03–0.21)。149小时录音中ABI_E与丰富度强相关(Spearman rho=0.93)，六个标准指数均不显著(最佳ACI rho=0.48)。稳定性T在稀树草原(0.39)低于动物园(0.65–0.67)。

Conclusion: 含显式假阳性控制、声明区域参考池与努力量标准化的物种级指数，比能量型声学指数更具可解释性与生态指示价值。

Abstract: Acoustic indices are widely used as rapid proxies for biodiversity, but they summarise the distribution of sound energy rather than the species producing it and often track richness poorly. We revise and test an Acoustic Biodiversity Index (ABI) built from species-level detections. Its primary form, ABI_E, is Shannon diversity of detections normalised by the regional species pool expected at the site and season (H / ln S_ref); temporal stability T is reported as a separate component. We first benchmarked four classifiers on a strongly labelled tropical dataset (1,216 calls, 24 classes) with recording-grouped cross-validation. BirdNET embeddings + logistic regression performed best (macro-F1 0.750 +/- 0.029), and random frame-level splits inflated accuracy from 0.41 to 0.70. We then applied BirdNET v2.4 with a location and season filter to recordings from a riverine savanna in the Zambezi Valley, Zimbabwe, and from three habitats at Emirates Park Zoo and Resort, Abu Dhabi, United Arab Emirates. A stricter post-hoc location filter removed 517 high-confidence detections of 9 implausible species, all at the zoo. After diel-matched rarefaction to equal effort (6 h), the savanna site held 21 species (Hill number 1D = 9.83; ABI_E = 0.38) against 1.3-13 species (ABI_E = 0.03-0.21) at the zoo sites. Across 149 recorded hours, ABI_E correlated strongly with richness (Spearman rho = 0.93), whereas six standard indices did not (best: ACI, rho = 0.48). The stability term was lower at the savanna site (0.39) than at the zoo (0.65-0.67), showing that it penalises the natural diel cycle of bird activity. Species-level indices with explicit false-positive control, a declared regional reference pool and effort standardisation provide a more interpretable biodiversity indicator than energy-based acoustic indices.

</details>


### [328] [Differentially Private Synthetic Data from Sliced Chebyshev Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42081)
*Lucas Rosenblatt,R. Teal Witter,Christopher Musco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出差分隐私合成数据算法 SPLICE，通过噪声切片 Chebyshev 多项式矩匹配，在高维下实现与维度 d 无关的全局分布精度，并在实用基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实用 DP 合成数据方法主要近似保留低维边缘分布，只能"碰运气"地保留高维全局结构；而追求全局精度保证的方法则面临随维度 d 指数增长的代价（维度灾难），缺乏兼顾高维全局保证与实际性能的算法。

Method: 测量目标分布的噪声切片 Chebyshev 多项式矩，并拟合一个分布使其匹配这些矩；这是对近期一维合成数据多项式方法的推广。理论层面证明矩匹配引理：单位球上分布沿 Õ(d/α²) 个随机方向匹配前 O(1/α) 个 Chebyshev 矩即可保证 α 的切片 Wasserstein 误差。实现上借助张量分解工具。

Result: 在 n 个样本、常数隐私参数下，SPLICE 返回的私有分布与目标分布的 Sliced Wasserstein-1 距离为 Õ(1/√n)，且该界与数据维度 d 无关；在大规模基准上相比既有 DP 合成数据方法取得 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: SPLICE 缓解了 DP 合成数据中的维度灾难，同时具备与维度无关的全局分布保证和实用最优性能，并给出具有独立价值的矩匹配理论结果。

Abstract: Research on practical differentially private (DP) synthetic data has largely focused on methods that approximately preserve low-dimensional marginals of a target distribution, and can thus only hope to preserve higher-dimensional, global structure as a lucky by-product. At the same time, existing methods that target global accuracy guarantees pay a price exponential in the data dimension, $d$.
  In this work, we address this ``curse of dimensionality'' by presenting a new DP synthetic data algorithm, SPLICE, that comes with a compelling global distributional guarantee, even in high-dimensions, and moreover, is practically state-of-the-art. Our algorithm is based on measuring noisy sliced Chebyshev polynomial moments of the target distribution and fitting a distribution to match, a twist on recent polynomial methods for 1-D synthetic data.
  We prove that, given a dataset of $n$ examples, SPLICE returns a private distribution with Sliced Wasserstein-1 distance $\tilde{O}(1/\sqrt{n})$ from the target, for constant privacy parameters. Notably, this bound is independent of the data dimension, $d$. Along the way, we prove a moment matching result of independent interest: for a distribution on the unit ball, matching the first $O(1/α)$ Chebyshev moments along $\tilde{O}(d/α^2)$ random directions guarantees $α$ Sliced Wasserstein error.
  Finally, we provide a practical implementation of the algorithm that borrows tools from the tensor decomposition literature and achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to prior DP synthetic data methods on an extensive benchmark.

</details>


### [329] [Cost-Aware Stopping for Freeze-Thaw Bayesian Optimization: When Does One-Step Lookahead Suffice?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42085)
*Long Vu,Jongseong Brad Choi,Binoy Ravindran,Zhoulai Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"EI停止"规则：在冻结-解冻贝叶斯优化中，逐步推进最大期望改进(EI)的候选，当无EI超过单次增量成本时停止，最大化"最优观测分数减增量成本"的无预算效用；并给出可证明过早停止的证明证书与修正步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有freeze-thaw BO以预算或收敛判定为停止条件，缺少围绕"分数-成本"权衡的无预算效用目标与原则性的停止准则。

Method: 定义无预算效用（最优观测分数减每次增量成本），采用一步前瞻：在固定代理模型下推进EI最大者，EI≤成本即停。构造下界证书证明该停止为时过早，并迭代应用该修正。

Result: 理论：EI停止不劣于立即停止，但可能过早停；重复修正不降低代理模型下的期望效用。实验：代理模型下低成本的EI停止平均过早、高成本损失小；三个超参优化基准的预注册检验显示多数高成本情形EI停止非劣，两个基准低成本下修正更优；代理模型信息共享更多可提升EI停止效用；两个已发表方法的代理模型上，EI停止以更低分数换取更少训练增量。

Conclusion: EI停止是简单、成本感知的参考停止准则，其效用高度依赖代理模型质量与信息共享程度。

Abstract: Freeze-thaw Bayesian optimization (BO) evaluates candidates in resumable increments but usually stops at a budget or convergence test. We maximize a budget-free utility, the best observed score minus a cost per increment, with a one-step lookahead: advance the candidate with the largest expected improvement (EI) under a fixed surrogate, and stop once no EI exceeds the cost. This rule, EI stopping, never falls below stopping immediately in expected utility, yet it can stop too early. We construct a certificate, a lower bound on the value of two-step lookahead, that proves such a stop premature, and repeating this correction never lowers expected utility under the surrogate. Under the surrogate, EI stops are premature on average at low costs and lose little at high costs. On three hyperparameter optimization benchmarks, a pre-registered test finds EI stopping noninferior to the correction in most high-cost settings, while the correction gains at low costs on two of the three; more information sharing in the surrogate raises EI stopping's utility. On the surrogates of two published methods, EI stopping trades lower scores for fewer training increments. EI stopping is a simple cost-aware reference whose utility depends on the surrogate.

</details>


### [330] [Towards Understanding the Impact of Plasticity Loss on Reinforcement Learning in Stochastic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42100)
*Philipp Bordne,André Biedenkapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究随机环境下深度强化学习中的可塑性丧失，在带观测/动作噪声的DMC任务上训练SAC，发现保可塑性方法提升鲁棒性、噪声与可塑性呈非单调关系、可塑性丧失集中在训练早期，极端噪声下TD不稳定性成为主要失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度RL脆弱，已有研究将脆弱性归因于神经网络可塑性丧失，但随机环境中的可塑性丧失问题研究不足，需系统考察随机性与可塑性的交互作用。

Method: 在三个DeepMind Control任务上引入受控水平的观测噪声与动作噪声，训练SAC智能体，系统比较保可塑性方法（如重置、层归一化）在不同噪声水平下的表现。

Result: 在较难的两个任务上保可塑性方法提升学习鲁棒性，且优势随观测噪声增大而扩大；重置+层归一化在所有测试噪声水平下均劣于单独重置；随机性在难任务中加速可塑性丧失，在简单任务中噪声反而起正则化作用、维持可塑性；可塑性丧失在训练早期最明显；最难随机设置下TD不稳定性取代渐进性可塑性丧失成为主导失效模式。

Conclusion: 随机性与可塑性丧失存在复杂交互且具有任务依赖性；保可塑性方法并非普遍有效（重置+层归一化的组合即失效）；诊断失效原因需区分渐进可塑性丧失与TD不稳定性。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (RL) is a powerful framework for sequential decision-making. Despite many high-profile successes in recent years, deep RL is known to be brittle. An emerging line of research attributes this brittleness to a loss of plasticity in neural networks. However, plasticity loss has so far been understudied in stochastic environments. We study this interplay systematically, training SAC agents on three DeepMind Control tasks augmented with controlled levels of observation and action noise. We find that plasticity-preserving methods increase learning robustness on the two harder tasks, with their advantage growing as observation noise increases, while combining resets with layer normalization underperforms resets alone at every observation noise level tested. Further, stochasticity can accelerate the loss of plasticity in harder environments, whereas in easier settings noise can act as a regularizer that sustains plasticity. Lastly, plasticity loss is most pronounced in the early stages of training. In the most challenging stochastic settings, TD instability rather than gradual plasticity loss becomes the dominant failure mode.

</details>


### [331] [Hyperbolic Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42104)
*Iulia Rusu,Anoop Praturu,Sergei Gepshtein,Tatyana Sharpee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出双曲逆强化学习（Hyperbolic IRL），将奖励参数化为特征向量与学习参数向量之间双曲距离（及欧氏距离变体）的函数，源于神经回路中的几何组织启发，在已有基准、低数据和大规模稠密环境中取得可比或更优的表现，并在博物馆导航任务中揭示了非可分离的合取式奖励结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IRL的奖励函数虽可用线性权重、神经网络或其他函数近似器实现，但均未对底层特征空间的几何结构作任何假设；而神经回路中观察到几何组织，提示引入几何先验（双曲结构）可能提升奖励建模的预测精度与可解释性。

Method: 将奖励定义为特征向量与学习到的参数向量之间的双曲距离函数（并给出欧氏距离对照变体），在确定性策略与随机策略下，与多种线性及非线性IRL基线进行对比；随后应用于多展品博物馆房间中的人类导航数据，检验奖励结构。

Result: 在既有基准上与SOTA模型性能相当，在低数据条件下以及大规模稠密环境中表现更优；在博物馆导航任务中，双曲IRL揭示的奖励结构不仅依赖单个展品距离特征，还包含特征间非可分离的合取结构。

Conclusion: 双曲奖励参数化可同时提升IRL的预测精度与可解释性，为研究非可分离、合取式结构如何解释行为提供了框架。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) seeks to infer the reward function underlying observed behavior, but its predictive accuracy and interpretability depend on how that reward is computed from the underlying features. While prior IRL reward functions have been implemented through a variety of techniques including linear weights, neural networks, or other function approximators, none make assumptions about the geometry of the underlying feature space. Inspired by geometric organization observed in neural circuits, we introduce Hyperbolic inverse reinforcement learning (Hyperbolic IRL), which parameterizes reward as a function of Hyperbolic distance between the feature vector and a learned parameter vector, as well as its Euclidean distance variant. We tested our methods against a range of linear and nonlinear IRL baselines under both deterministic and stochastic policies. Our Hyperbolic approach achieved comparable performance to state-of-the-art models on established benchmarks and excelled in the low data limit and across dense environments. Applied to human navigation among multiple exhibits in a museum room, Hyperbolic IRL reveals reward structure that depends not only on individual exhibit-distance features, but also on non-separable conjunctions between them. Together, these results demonstrate that a Hyperbolic reward parameterization can improve both the predictive accuracy and interpretability of IRL, providing a framework for studying how non-separable, conjunctive structure can begin to explain behavior.

</details>


### [332] [Fast Privacy Loss Accounting for Random Allocation via Multiscale FFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42117)
*Vitaly Feldman,Moshe Shenfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对随机分配采样（random allocation）方案，提出多尺度FFT算法，将PLD会计器的运行时间对精度参数α的依赖从O(α^{-2})改进为O(α^{-1}log(1/α))，并用均值保持的"连线投影"降低舍入误差至O(α^2/log t)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机分配采样在隐私优化与通信高效聚合中优于Poisson采样，但其现有的隐私损失分布（PLD）会计器运行时间为O(α^{-2})，高精度下代价过高，需要更高效的精确记账方法。

Method: 提出multiscale FFT算法：在每对二进区间内采用均匀网格表示似然比，并在各区间内用FFT精确计算每一对两两和的舍入；同时证明舍入可通过Doroshenko等人的均值保持"连线投影"（mean-preserving connect-the-dots projection）完成。

Result: 算法保持原会计器的保证，运行时间从O(α^{-2})降至O(α^{-1}log(1/α))；投影式舍入把隐私损失均值误差与所计算对的χ²散度误差从O(α)量级降到O(α^2/log t)。实验中在高精度下获得数个数量级的加速，并为使用PREAMBLE的DP-SGD给出更紧的隐私界。

Conclusion: 多尺度FFT结合均值保持投影，为随机分配采样提供了高精度下显著更快的PLD会计，实用上可提升DP-SGD等训练流程的隐私-效用权衡。

Abstract: In the random allocation sampling scheme each user's data is used in $k$ steps chosen randomly and uniformly from a sequence of $t$ steps. This scheme is known to improve on Poisson sampling in private optimization and in communication-efficient aggregation. The existing privacy loss distribution (PLD) accountant for this scheme runs in time quadratic in the inverse of the accuracy parameter $α$ (Feldman and Shenfeld, 2026). We give an algorithm, \emph{multiscale FFT}, that represents likelihood ratios on a grid that is uniform within each dyadic interval and computes the exact rounding of every pairwise sum using FFTs within each interval. It keeps the guarantees of the accountant and improves the dependence of its running time on $α$ from $O(α^{-2})$ to $O(α^{-1}\log(1/α))$. In addition, we demonstrate that rounding can be performed via the mean-preserving connect-the-dots projection of (Doroshenko et al., 2026). This reduces the errors that rounding introduces in the mean of the privacy loss and in the $χ^2$ divergence of the computed pair from the order of $α$ to $O(α^2/\log(t))$. In our experiments, our algorithm achieves speedups of several orders of magnitude at high accuracy and gives improved privacy bounds for DP-SGD with PREAMBLE.

</details>


### [333] [Gradient Descent on Two ReLU Neurons: Global Landscape and Bifurcation Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42118)
*Binghua Li,Mengzhe Li,Denny Wu,Tianhao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在良好设定的两层ReLU教师-学生模型中，从小的初始化出发的运行总体梯度流呈现由“易”平分线方向与“难”分裂方向组织的多阶段动力学：学生神经元先向平分线鞍点塌缩，逃逸后完成专业化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解梯度下降如何学习特征仍是神经网络理论的核心问题；作者希望在一个能复现丰富多阶段梯度动力学的最小多指标模型（两层ReLU教师-学生、各向同性高斯输入、小初始化下的总体梯度流）中，给出损失景观与动力学机制的统一刻画。

Method: (1) 构造最小多指标设定：良好设定的两层ReLU教师-学生模型，各向同性高斯输入，采用总体梯度流、小初始化；(2) 引入两个刻画方向——“易”平分线方向（承载主导信号）与“难”分裂方向（控制专业化）；(3) 分析总体平方损失景观，证明所有学生权重均非零的临界点必为全局最小值或鞍点；(4) 追踪梯度流轨迹，分析塌缩与逃逸过程。

Result: (1) 景观层面：任一两者权重皆非零的临界点要么是全局最优、要么是鞍点，不存在次优局部极小；(2) 动力学层面：学生神经元首先沿平分线方向塌缩至鞍点，随后逃逸并沿分裂方向对称破缺、专业化对齐教师神经元；(3) 该多阶段行为由标准算法与架构选择（总体梯度流、小初始化、ReLU）自然产生。

Conclusion: 本文从损失景观与梯度动力学两个角度，解释了简单多指标模型中出现的多阶段对称破缺学习行为，为特征学习的理论理解提供了可分析的最小范例。

Abstract: Understanding how gradient descent learns features remains a central challenge in neural network theory. We study this question in a minimal multi-index setting that already exhibits rich multi-phase gradient dynamics: a well-specified two-neuron ReLU teacher-student model under isotropic Gaussian inputs, trained by population gradient flow from small initialization. We show that the dynamics are organized by two directions: the "easy" bisector direction, which carries the leading signal, and the "hard" splitting direction, which governs specialization. To characterize the loss structure and learning behavior, we analyze the population squared-loss landscape and show that every critical point with both student weights nonzero is either a global minimum or a saddle. We then track the gradient-flow trajectory, showing that student neurons first collapse toward a bisector saddle before escaping and specializing to teacher neurons. Our results provide both a landscape and dynamics account of the multi-phase symmetry-breaking behavior that arises in a simple multi-index model and under standard algorithmic and architectural choices.

</details>


### [334] [Physiological Trajectories and Observation Patterns in ICU Mortality Prediction: Transportability Under Changing Measurement Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42126)
*Benjamin T. Pike*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在MIMIC-III/IV与eICU跨库外部验证中，生理轨迹特征（R2）的迁移预测价值稳定，而观察模式（测量频次与时机，R3）特征的增益依赖本地测量条件，泛化不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU死亡预测中，以往难以区分"生理轨迹本身的可迁移预测信息"与"测量模式（频次、时机）"所携带的额外信息；且不清楚测量时点是否在独立于观测数量的情况下影响性能。

Method: 基于MIMIC-III（7,762例MetaVision患者建模）、MIMIC-IV（21,682例）与eICU（43,878例、204家医院）预测48小时院内死亡。构建嵌套表征：R1最新值快照→R2纵向生理汇总→R3测量频次与时序特征。通过源数据bootstrap重拟合、固定保留观测数量下的随机删失对照（时间散布 vs 随机保留、末12小时剔除）以及降低测量可及性的情景，分离时机效应。

Result: 外部迁移中R2较R1改善eICU log-loss（差值-0.00922，95% CI -0.01148~-0.00704）；R3较R2在本地eICU模型有改善（-0.00257，95% CI -0.00370~-0.00146），但跨库迁移后不确定（-0.00012，95% CI -0.00206~+0.00208），30次源bootstrap中仅10次支持R3。固定观测数量时，窗口内时间散布保留较随机保留降低log-loss：R3为0.03280，R2为0.00703；剔除末12小时相对等量随机剔除增加log-loss：R3为0.01947，R2为0.00822。测量可及性降低时，本地R3增益由正转负。

Conclusion: 生理轨迹特征跨人群迁移更稳健；观察模式特征对测量时机与可及性高度敏感。模型评估不仅应跨新人群，还应在不同测量条件下进行。

Abstract: Objective: To distinguish the transferable predictive value of physiological trajectories from that of observation patterns in ICU mortality prediction, and to assess whether measurement timing affects performance independently of observation quantity.
  Materials and Methods: In-hospital mortality was predicted at 48 hours using MIMIC-III, MIMIC-IV, and eICU. Nested representations progressively integrated latest-value snapshots (R1), longitudinal physiological summaries (R2), and measurement frequency and timing features (R3). Models fitted on 7,762 MetaVision patients were evaluated on 21,682 MIMIC-IV patients and 43,878 eICU patients across 204 hospitals. The effect of measurement timing was assessed while holding the number of retained observations constant.
  Results: Physiological trajectories improved external prediction in eICU (R2-R1 log-loss difference -0.00922, 95% CI -0.01148 to -0.00704). Observation-pattern features improved locally developed eICU models (R3-R2 -0.00257, 95% CI -0.00370 to -0.00146), but not clearly after transfer from MetaVision (-0.00012, 95% CI -0.00206 to +0.00208). R3 was favoured in 10 of 30 source bootstrap refits. At matched observation counts, retaining measurements spread throughout the window reduced log loss relative to random retention by 0.03280 for R3 versus 0.00703 for R2. Removing the final 12 hours increased log loss relative to count-matched random removal by 0.01947 for R3 versus 0.00822 for R2. Under reduced availability, the local R3 increment changed from beneficial to harmful.
  Discussion: Physiological trajectories transferred more consistently, while observation-pattern features were particularly sensitive to the timing and availability of measurements.
  Conclusion: Clinical prediction models should be evaluated not only in new patient populations but also under the measurement conditions that supply their inputs.

</details>


### [335] [Instance-dependent sequential limits as an exact information ledger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42133)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将赌臂后悔、序贯检验、最佳臂识别与MDP的instance-dependent下界证明统一表述为一条"精确台账"式证明链：divergence分解→数据处理→统计量→检验步骤→对替代与分配的优化，并逐项量化各环节的"损耗缺陷"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典instance-dependent极限通常分散在多个独立证明中，各步松弛的损耗隐式且不可比较，难以诊断实际算法与理论极限之间的差距来源。作者希望把证明链写成可审计的精确账目，使每一步的损失显式化、可计算。

Method: 以divergence decomposition为唯一恒等步骤；用chain rule证明数据处理恰好损失一个条件散度，并在自然指数族下于各臂计数与奖励和处消失；构造单参数martingale恒等式族，将分解推广到任意两实例之间的tilt；随后通过带命名缺陷的松弛导出经典极限。除渐近步外，其余缺陷均可由算法自身运行估计。

Result: 在七个已发表来源（从电影评分到发动机更换）上，台账定位了标准算法与经典极限之间的差异来源；对已训练模型间的识别问题与Wald检验，最大项通常来自uniform quantification缺陷（仅当类保证为数值时可量化）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了统一、可核算的instance-dependent下界证明框架，将理论极限的松弛损耗分解为可命名、可计算的缺陷，为算法与下界之间的gap提供诊断工具。

Abstract: Instance-dependent limits for bandit regret, sequential tests, best-arm identification and Markov decision processes are proved by one chain. It runs from a divergence decomposition through data processing to a statistic, a testing step that turns a class guarantee into a requirement on the history, and optimization over alternatives and allocations. Only the first step is an identity. This work writes the chain as an exact ledger. By the chain rule, data processing loses exactly a conditional divergence, which vanishes for arms in natural exponential families at the per-arm counts and reward sums. A one-parameter family of martingale identities extends the decomposition to every tilt between two instances. The classical limits follow by relaxations with named defects. All defects but the asymptotic step's can be computed from an algorithm's own runs, the uniform quantification's only when the class guarantee is a number. On seven published sources, from movie ratings to engine replacement, the ledger locates the difference between standard algorithms and the classical limits. For identification among trained models and for Wald's test the largest term is usually the uniform quantification's.

</details>


### [336] [Counting on Dummies: Privacy Amplification with Observable Counts and Truncated Batches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42139)
*Vitaly Feldman,Audra McMillan,Kunal Talwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 DP-SGD 中变长 batch 泄露批大小与截断导致隐私损失增大的问题，提出用随机数量的 dummy points 对批大小进行私有化的简单机制，并在 Poisson 采样与 random allocation 下给出通用且紧致的隐私会计分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DP-SGD 常用随机采用以借助隐私放大获得更好界限，但 Poisson 采样、random allocation 等会产生变长 batch，其隐私保证要求批大小不被泄露；在分布式场景下服务器或通信观测者可获知批大小。此外内存与计算约束要求每批不超过上限 s，超限截断已知会显著增大隐私损失。

Method: 引入随机数量的 dummy points 来隐藏真实批大小，从而在截断情形下仍维持隐私保证；机制在分布式设置中易于实现。对 Poisson 采样与 random allocation 给出通用分析，分析支持高效且相对紧致的隐私会计，并借助子采样放大 count 发布的隐私，使期望 dummy 点数渐近小于直接用非负噪声私有化（敏感度为 1 的）批大小所需数量。

Result: 在截断批次的 Poisson 采样下，经实验表明该机制相比 Ganesh (2025) 近期给出的截断直接会计，取得更好的隐私—效率权衡。

Conclusion: 利用随机数量 dummy points 可同时解决变长批大小的隐私泄露与截断引起的隐私损失增大问题，且实现简单、会计高效紧致、dummy 开销渐近更省，在截断 Poisson 采样下优于现有直接会计方法。

Abstract: In many practical deployments of Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD), the server processes data in batches. Randomly sampling these batches allows for better privacy bounds via privacy amplification results. Most sampling schemes with provable privacy amplification, including Poisson sampling and random allocation, result in variable batch sizes. The privacy guarantee of these schemes requires that the batch sizes are not revealed. In distributed settings, the server performing the aggregation or any party observing the communication traffic may learn these batch sizes. Furthermore, in both centralized and distributed settings, memory and computation constraints may require each batch to be of some maximum size $s$, necessitating truncation of the batch when it exceeds this limit. It is known that this truncation can result in a significant increase in privacy loss. In this work, we address both of these challenges via a simple mechanism that uses a random number of \emph{dummy points} to privatize the batch size and maintain the privacy guarantee even under truncation. We demonstrate that our mechanism is easy to implement in a distributed setting, and provide a general analysis of this mechanism for both Poisson sampling and random allocation. Our analysis is designed to support efficient and relatively tight privacy accounting. It also effectively amplifies the privacy of the count releases by subsampling, making the expected number of dummy points asymptotically smaller than what is needed to directly privatize the (sensitivity-one) batch sizes with non-negative noise. For Poisson sampling with truncated batches, we demonstrate empirically that our mechanism achieves better privacy--efficiency tradeoffs than the direct accounting for truncation recently given by (Ganesh, 2025).

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [337] [Joint Estimation and Network Configuration in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40826)
*Mohammadreza Barzegaran,Hamid Jafarkhani*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出采样式“共识+新息”框架，在真实无线网络约束下同时刻画智能体间分歧与估计不准确度，推导可计算的最差稳态估计误差上界，并以该上界为目标设计半分布式拍卖算法联合优化通信拓扑与链路速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式共识感知依赖邻居信息融合，但通信拓扑、传输时延与有限速率会加剧智能体间分歧；而分歧小并不代表估计准确，尤其当传感器测量不确定度各异时。因此需要同时兼顾分歧与不准确度的统一误差刻画及相应的网络设计方法。

Method: 构建采样共识+新息框架；推导可计算的最差稳态误差上界，涵盖由图结构引起的共识分歧、量化误差、传输时延，以及相对真值的测量不确定性导致的估计不准确度；将该上界作为网络设计目标，提出半分布式拍卖算法，联合调整通信图与链路速率分配。

Result: 算法保持连通性，有限时间收敛到局部拍卖稳定的网络配置，且每次被接受的改动都降低误差上界；多组测试表明该方法在更少链路、适中链路速率下于短时间内降低估计误差。

Conclusion: 以统一误差上界驱动的拓扑-速率联合设计可在受限无线条件下有效平衡共识分歧与估计精度，实现快速、连通保持的网络配置优化。

Abstract: Distributed consensus-based sensing enables a multi-agent system to collaboratively estimate an unknown state by combining local measurements with information received from neighboring agents. In practice, however, the communication network, transmission delays, and finite-rate communication directly affect the disagreement among agents. On the other hand, agreement among agents does not ensure accuracy, particularly when sensors have different measurement uncertainties. We develop a sampled consensus-plus-innovations framework under realistic wireless network constraints that jointly accounts for inter-agent disagreement and estimation inaccuracy. We derive a computable upper bound on the worst-case steady-state estimation error that captures consensus disagreement due to the communication graph, quantization error, and transmission delay, together with estimation inaccuracy, which accounts for measurement uncertainty against the true state. We then use this bound as a network design objective and propose a semi-distributed auction-based algorithm that jointly modifies the communication graph and link-rate allocation. The proposed method preserves connectivity, converges in finite time to a locally auction-stable network configuration, and reduces the bound on the estimation error at every accepted change. The proposed method has been evaluated on various test cases, and the results show that it reduces the estimation error while maintaining fewer links and allocating moderate link rates in a short time.

</details>


### [338] [Maxwell-Consistent 3-D Magnetic Field Camera using Scalar-Potential-Based Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40856)
*Paul Jürß,Marija Boberg,Philip Suskin,Tobias Knopp,Fynn Foerger*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该文提出基于磁标量势及其球谐展开的三维磁场重建方法，替代逐分量展开，天然满足无源麦克斯韦方程，获得无旋无散场模型，重建精度更高、对噪声与传感器失效更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多种应用需要高时空分辨率的三维磁场精确表征；此前基于86个三轴霍尔传感器球形阵列的磁场相机采用各矢量分量独立球谐展开的重建方式，缺乏与麦克斯韦方程的物理一致性，易产生非物理的散度与旋度。

Method: 在球形磁场相机硬件基础上，构建磁标量势的球谐展开模型，由标量势梯度导出磁场矢量，避免逐分量独立拟合；使用两个永磁体产生的模拟梯度磁场进行验证，与分量式基线方法对比精度、系数数量、对传感器噪声与失效的鲁棒性，以及散度/旋度误差。

Result: 相比分量式基线，所提方法重建精度更高，对传感器噪声和传感器失效更鲁棒，非物理散度与旋度显著降低；且在更少系数下即达到更优性能，说明测量数据利用效率更高，可支持更高阶多项式表示。

Conclusion: 以磁标量势为核心的球谐重建框架在设计上保证无源场的无旋无散性，是一种更物理一致、更数据高效的球形磁场相机重建方案。

Abstract: Various applications require accurate 3-D characterization of magnetic fields with high spatial and temporal resolution. Building on our previously introduced spherical magnetic field camera employing 86 three-axis Hall sensors arranged on a spherical surface, we present a novel magnetic-field reconstruction approach based on the magnetic scalar potential and its spherical harmonic expansion. Unlike the previous reconstruction method that expands each vector component separately, our scalar-potential-based formulation is inherently consistent with Maxwell's equations for source-free fields by design, yielding curl-free and divergence-free field models. Using a simulated magnetic gradient field generated by two permanent magnets, we demonstrate that the proposed method achieves higher reconstruction accuracy, improved robustness to sensor noise and failure, and substantially reduced non-physical divergence and curl compared to the component-wise baseline. Furthermore, the scalar-potential approach attains superior performance with fewer coefficients, indicating a more efficient use of measurement data allowing higher-degree polynomial representations.

</details>


### [339] [Automated Optimization of Patient-Specific Electro-Anatomical Models Fitted to Cochlear Implant SCINSEV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41054)
*Minh Q. Vu,Erin L. Bratu,Jack H. Noble*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出自动化贝叶斯优化流程，在分钟级时间内完成患者特异性耳蜗电阻抗模型拟合，较全分辨率穷举搜索快约210倍且精度无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者特异性耳蜗电解剖模型对CI编程与听神经健康评估至关重要，但现有SCINSEV拟合依赖专家手动调整或全分辨率穷举搜索（每耳约9.1小时），无法规模化用于常规临床。

Method: 基于术后CT构建四分区（外淋巴、软组织、蜗轴、骨）有限差分正演模型，用高斯过程贝叶斯优化拟合实测SCINSEV；三项加速策略：粗化网格配合亚体素接触位置的三线性注入与读出；利用互易性只求解半数刺激电极、补齐3/4矩阵；基于雅可比的不可辨识性分析将4个电阻率降至2个。各简化分别以互易残差与拟合可重复性验证。

Result: 43只植入耳中每耳中位拟合时间2.6分钟；降分辨率目标下SCINSEV RMSE中位0.069 mV，优于同等评估预算网格搜索的0.084 mV；与全分辨率穷举搜索（中位9.1小时/耳）相比，拟合误差统计上不可区分，速度约快210倍。

Conclusion: 患者特异性耳蜗EAM拟合可在临床实用时间尺度上无需专家监督自动完成，消除了个性化CI编程与耳蜗仿真的主要计算障碍。

Abstract: Purpose: Patient-specific electro-anatomical models (EAMs) of the implanted cochlea are crucial for auditory nerve fiber simulation in cochlear implant (CI) programming and neural health estimation. Fitting an EAM to the stimulation current induced non-stimulating electrode voltage (SCINSEV) currently requires manual expert adjustment or exhaustive search over precomputed forward solutions, neither of which scales to routine clinical use. We present an automated optimization pipeline that fits patient specific EAMs in minutes. Methods: Four compartment tissue label maps (perilymph, soft tissue, modiolus, and bone) derived from postoperative CT were used to construct a finite difference forward model, fitted to the measured SCINSEV by Gaussian process Bayesian optimization. Three reductions cut fitting cost: a coarsened grid with trilinear injection and readout at sub-voxel contact positions; solving half the stimulating contacts and completing three quarters of the matrix by reciprocity; and a Jacobian-based identifiability analysis reducing four resistivities to two. Each reduction was validated separately against the measurement's reciprocity residual and the reproducibility of the fit. Results: Across 43 implanted ears, fitting took a median of 2.6 minutes per ear. Median SCINSEV RMSE on the reduced-resolution objective was 0.069 mV, against 0.084 mV for grid search at a matched evaluation budget. Against an exhaustive full-resolution grid search requiring a median of 9.1 hours per ear, the pipeline reached statistically indistinguishable fitting error roughly 210x faster. Conclusion: Patient-specific cochlear EAM fitting can be performed at clinically practical timescales without expert supervision, removing a major computational barrier to patient-specific cochlear implant programming and cochlear simulation.

</details>


### [340] [Prediction-Induced Sparsity for Tracked FMCW Radar: A Track-Conditioned Event-Gap FPGA Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41145)
*Huy Trinh*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出"预测诱导稀疏性"(PIS)跟踪模式FMCW雷达架构，在事件形成与硬件调度之前利用跟踪先验，按协方差约束残差支撑K进行稀疏事件-间隙谐振处理，实现事件量、存储与功耗的显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跟踪式FMCW雷达本已携带下一目标状态的预测，但下游数字硬件通常按固定频谱搜索规模来配置资源，导致先验信息未被用于硬件容量设计，造成冗余事件、存储与功耗开销。

Method: 将预测拍频解旋到以新息为中心的坐标；把跟踪器协方差传播为有界残差支撑K；生成稀疏电平交叉(level-crossing)数据包，并由精确事件-间隙谐振引擎执行，采用共享复数处理单元与K相关的状态/系数存储；在公开FMCW数据集上以因果、仅先验的方式条件化，并在Artix-7上完成定点RTL实现与测量。

Result: 公开数据集上事件记录平均减少33.1%，代表性定点流在RTL中位精确(bit-exact)；Artix-7上将物理支撑从129降至23状态，LUT与BRAM分别降低65.0%与88.1%，匹配吞吐下实测VCCINT功耗降低19.9%。

Conclusion: 跟踪不确定性可转化为硬件容量的直接设计约束，为未来自适应雷达加速器提供了"预测驱动稀疏化"的实现路径。

Abstract: Tracked FMCW radar already carries a prediction of the next target state, yet the downstream digital hardware is commonly provisioned for a fixed spectral search. We present prediction-induced sparsity (PIS), a tracked-mode architecture that uses the prior before event formation and hardware scheduling. The predicted beat is derotated to an innovation-centred coordinate, while tracker covariance is propagated to a bounded residual support K. Sparse level-crossing packets are then executed by an exact event-gap resonator engine with a shared complex processing element and K-dependent state/coefficient memory. On a public FMCW dataset, causal prior-only con- ditioning reduces event records by 33.1% on average, and the representative fixed-point stream is bit-exact in RTL. And on an Artix-7 implementation, reducing physical support from 129 to 23 states lowers LUT and BRAM use by 65.0% and 88.1%, respectively, and reduces measured VCCINT power by 19.9% at matched throughput. The results show how tracking uncertainty can become a hardware-capacity implication for future adaptive radar accelerators.

</details>


### [341] [Downlink MU-MIMO for LEO Direct-to-Device: Feasibility, Architecture, and Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41248)
*Bikshapathi Gouda,Neng Wang,Masoud Sajadieh,Meghna Agrawal*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 单颗LEO卫星利用米级阵列与MU-MIMO预编码，在同一时频资源上服务数百用户；空间自由度由孔径波长数、轨道高度与最小仰角决定，采用MMSE预编码可使吞吐随SNR增长，并可用几何信息更新预编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LEO直连设备的吞吐受卫星天线尺寸限制，且载荷仅依靠波束与频率复用区分用户，用户隔离能力有限，亟需在单星上提升同一时频资源的复用能力。

Method: 将多用户MIMO预编码引入单颗LEO卫星米级阵列，推导空间自由度与孔径波长数、轨道高度、最小仰角的关系；对比MMSE预编码与最大比传输；利用用户位置、卫星星历与姿态计算预编码器；将DoF作为混合载荷数字链路数，并权衡星地功能划分；按10 ms无线帧基于几何更新预编码器、数秒级保持用户配对；固定波束承载小区专用信号、跨小区联合预编码用户数据。

Result: MMSE预编码下用户吞吐随SNR增长，而MRT受干扰限制；在3GPP非地面信道模型下，基于位置与星历的预编码相较完美信道知识在20 dB SNR时吞吐损失小于0.5%；按帧更新预编码器、数秒级保持用户配对的吞吐损失低于1%；50×50阵列、600 km轨道、60°最小仰角时单星服务410用户，平均6.1 bps/Hz，为四色频率复用固定波束的4倍以上。

Conclusion: 单星米级阵列结合MU-MIMO预编码可显著提升LEO直连吞吐，空间自由度是系统设计的核心参数，且基于几何的低开销预编码更新与分层功能划分具备工程可行性。

Abstract: Direct-to-device connectivity from low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites is commercially available, but its throughput is limited by the satellite antenna size and by payloads that separate users only by beams and frequency reuse. This article shows that a single LEO satellite with a meter-scale array can serve hundreds of users on the same time-frequency resource through multi-user multiple-input multiple-output precoding. The number of users it can separate with a single stream each, i.e., its spatial degrees of freedom (DoF), follows from the aperture in wavelengths, the orbit altitude, and the minimum elevation angle. With minimum mean-square error (MMSE) precoding, the throughput of these users grows with the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), whereas maximum-ratio transmission becomes interference-limited. The precoder can be computed from the user positions and the satellite ephemeris and attitude, with a throughput loss of less than 0.5\% at 20~dB SNR compared with perfect channel knowledge under the 3GPP non-terrestrial channel models. The DoF also set the number of digital chains of a hybrid payload, and the functional split among satellites and ground trades inter-satellite-link and feeder-link load against on-board processing. As the satellite moves, the precoder can be updated from the known geometry every radio frame, i.e., 10~ms, while the computationally intensive user pairing can be kept for a few seconds with a throughput loss below 1\%. Finally, we keep the cell-specific signals on fixed beams while precoding the user data jointly across all cells of the satellite. For a $50\times50$ array at 600~km and a minimum elevation angle of $60^\circ$, one satellite serves 410 users at 6.1~bps/Hz on average, more than four times the throughput of fixed beams with four-color frequency reuse.

</details>


### [342] [Pinching Antennas for ISAC: Waveguide Architectures, Solutions, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41380)
*Qian Gao,Ruikang Zhong*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该文系统综述了波导馈电"捏合天线系统"(PASS)赋能的通感一体化(ISAC)，按单波导、多波导、分段波导三类架构比较其馈电结构、空间自由度、协议与上下行支持，并给出物理一致建模、联合空间-信号优化、动态信道获取、实时稳定控制四条技术路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC是6G核心设计原则，但固定天线几何限制了性能；PASS通过介质波导引导电磁波并由位置自适应天线辐射，可获得空间重构增益，因此需系统梳理PASS-ISAC的架构与关键问题。

Method: 综述性分析：以单波导、多波导、分段波导三类架构为框架进行对比；围绕物理一致建模、联合空间-信号优化、动态信道获取、稳定实时控制组织解法；并以分段波导捏合天线阵列(SWAN)为案例，采用分段滞回强化学习联合优化段选择、天线定位与发射波束成形。

Result: SWAN案例表明，波导损耗与分段孔径增益共同导致通信-感知折中；分段滞回强化学习可有效完成段选择、天线定位与波束成形的联合决策。

Conclusion: PASS为ISAC提供空间重构新自由度，但存在损耗与增益导致的权衡；未来研究方向包括联合收发操作、测量校准的数字孪生以及可信控制。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) is a core design principle for sixth-generation (6G) wireless systems, yet fixed antenna geometry limits its performance. Pinching antenna systems (PASS) guide electromagnetic waves along dielectric waveguides and radiate them through position-adaptive antennas, allowing communication links and sensing observations to benefit from spatial reconfiguration. This article reviews PASS-enabled ISAC through single-waveguide, multi-waveguide, and segmented-waveguide architectures, comparing their feed structures, spatial degrees of freedom, operating protocols, and uplink/downlink support. We organize solution pathways around physics-consistent modeling, joint spatial-signal optimization, dynamic channel acquisition, and stable real-time control. A segmented waveguide-enabled pinching-antenna array (SWAN) case study demonstrates joint segment selection, antenna po?sitioning, and transmit beamforming using segment-hysteresis reinforcement learning. The results illustrate how waveguide loss and per-segment aperture gain create communication-sensing tradeoffs. We conclude with research directions in joint transmit?receive operation, measurement-calibrated digital twins, and trustworthy control.

</details>


### [343] [Fluid Antenna Systems for ISAC Design: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41382)
*Yueling Zhao,Ping Yang,Hong Niu,Shuaixin Yang,Yue Xiao,Gang Wu,Merouane Debbah,Ertugrul Basar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文综述流体天线系统（FAS）赋能的通感一体化（ISAC），从理论性能极限到实用收发架构，并提出流体天线多模调制（FA-MM）框架作为多维物理层设计案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FAS引入动态位置自由度，将空间域从静态离散资源扩展为准连续、可重构的设计空间，可缓解ISAC系统几何刚性与功能冲突的固有限制。

Method: 综述性研究：梳理FAS-ISAC从理论性能界到收发机架构的演进脉络；进而提出FA-MM框架作为多维物理层设计的代表性案例进行分析。

Result: 给出FAS-ISAC的系统性技术全景（性能极限—架构实现—物理层设计），并以FA-MM框架示范多维联合设计的可行路径。

Conclusion: 指出未来FAS-ISAC的开放挑战，重点包括预测性感知、自适应协调与空间状态安全管理。

Abstract: Fluid antenna systems (FAS) introduce dynamic positional degrees of freedom (DoFs), extending the spatial domain from a static and discrete resource to a quasi-continuous and reconfigurable design space. This spatial flexibility provides an effective means of alleviating the geometric rigidity and functional conflicts that constrain integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems. Against this backdrop, we provide a comprehensive overview of FAS-enabled ISAC, tracing the evolution from theoretical performance limits to practical transceiver architectures. To illustrate the surveyed design evolution, we further propose the fluid antenna multi-mode modulation (FA-MM) framework as a representative case study of multidimensional physical-layer design for FAS-ISAC. Finally, several open challenges are discussed, with emphasis on predictive sensing, adaptive coordination, and secure spatial-state management in future FAS-ISAC systems.

</details>


### [344] [PolarFlow: Spectral Geometric Flow Matching for Directed Graph Generation via Polar Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41471)
*Yanan Zhao,Feng Ji,Alba Carballo-Castro,Jingyang Dai,Antonio Ortega,Pascal Frossard,Wee Peng Tay*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: PolarFlow：基于极分解与截断谱坐标的连续几何流匹配框架，用于高效生成有向图，在保持生成质量的同时显著降低训练与采样时间，并新增Circuit与Twitter两个基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有向图可建模生物、交通、社交网络及EDA中的非对称关系。现有离散扩散与流匹配方法虽能有效生成有向图，但采用逐边类别建模，需对所有有序节点对做稠密预测并在生成中反复进行类别更新，计算开销大；且现有基准覆盖的网络结构有限，难以全面评估。由此引出问题：能否利用有向邻接矩阵结构，以更高效的计算形式达到可比生成质量。

Method: 提出PolarFlow，基于极分解与截断谱坐标的连续几何流匹配框架。图表示由四个耦合分量构成：编码正交因子的一个Stiefel框架与旋转角，以及刻画半正定因子的第二个Stiefel框架与非负特征值。在该乘积空间上沿几何感知的条件路径学习联合速度场，重建连续邻接分数矩阵，再离散化为二值有向图；以联合连续传输替代逐边离散生成。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上，PolarFlow取得与竞争性离散方法相当的生成质量，同时大幅缩短训练与采样时间；并引入覆盖工程功能网络与自然演化社交网络的Circuit和Twitter补充基准，扩展评估范围。

Conclusion: 用极分解谱参数化与连续流匹配联合建模邻接矩阵，可在保留全局邻接结构的同时提升有向图生成的计算效率，为有向图生成提供了兼具质量与效率的替代方案。

Abstract: Directed graphs model asymmetric relationships in biology, transportation, social networks, and electronic design automation. Recent discrete diffusion and flow-matching methods generate directed graphs effectively, but their edge-wise categorical formulations require dense predictions over all ordered node pairs and repeated categorical updates during generation. This motivates a fundamental question: can comparable generative quality be achieved through a more computationally efficient formulation that better exploits the structure of directed adjacency matrices? Existing benchmarks also cover limited network structures, restricting comprehensive evaluation. We introduce PolarFlow, a continuous geometric flow-matching framework based on polar decomposition and truncated spectral coordinates. Its representation comprises four coupled components: a Stiefel frame and rotation angles encoding the orthogonal factor, and a second Stiefel frame and nonnegative eigenvalues characterizing the positive-semidefinite factor. PolarFlow learns a joint velocity field along geometry-aware conditional paths on this product space, reconstructs a continuous adjacency-score matrix, and discretizes it into a binary directed graph. By replacing discrete edge-wise generation with joint continuous transport, PolarFlow improves computational efficiency while retaining global adjacency structure. To broaden evaluation, we introduce complementary Circuit and Twitter benchmarks spanning engineered functional and naturally evolved social networks. Across synthetic and real-world datasets, PolarFlow achieves generative quality comparable to competitive discrete methods while substantially reducing training and sampling time.

</details>


### [345] [GNSS Receiver Operating Point Comparison Between Vector and Scalar Tracking Under High-Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41498)
*J. Fermín Llorente,Ernesto M. López,Javier A. Smidt,Pedro A. Roncagliolo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 面向运载火箭gnss接收机，提出基于C/N0与动力学指示双层门控的测量噪声方差R自适应机制，在保持20g动态响应的同时提升低载噪比下的锁失保持率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对20 g加速度阶跃，跟踪环需宽带宽，但宽带宽在低C/N0下易因噪声导致失锁，形成动态响应与噪声鲁棒性的权衡。

Method: 以信号C/N0为键构造sigmoid包络，并由环路带宽控制算法(LBCA)鉴相器输出统计导出动力学指示量进行调制，双层门控放大测量噪声协方差R；噪声主导时增大R、降低卡尔曼增益，使滤波器依托导航辅助预测滑行，检测到动力学时门控在一个历元内开启以恢复高带宽；导航辅助建模为随机控制输入，其不确定度由导航EKF协方差传播。

Result: 20 g加速度阶跃仿真中，在C/N0=31 dB-Hz下，自适应矢量架构的锁失保持率相对独立标量滤波器提升一倍；接收机在开源MATLAB GNSS软件接收机MSGR中实现。

Conclusion: 该门控型R自适应机制有效解耦了高动态带宽需求与低C/N0噪声敏感性，结合导航辅助的矢量跟踪可显著提升运载火箭场景下的跟踪稳健性。

Abstract: This paper presents a measurement noise adaptation mechanism for Kalman filter-based carrier tracking in a navigation-aided GNSS receiver designed for launch-vehicle applications. The tracking loop is tuned for 20\,g acceleration steps, which requires a wide bandwidth that makes the loop vulnerable to noise-driven pull-out at low carrier-to-noise density ratios. To resolve this tradeoff, the measurement noise covariance, $R$, is inflated via a two-layer gate: a sigmoid envelope keyed to the signal $C/N_0$, modulated by a dynamics indicator derived from the discriminator output statistics of the Loop Bandwidth Control Algorithm. During noise-dominated periods the gate inflates~$R$, reducing the Kalman gain so the filter coasts on the navigation-aided prediction; when dynamics are detected the gate opens within one epoch, restoring the high-bandwidth tuning. The navigation aiding is formulated as a stochastic control input whose uncertainty is propagated from the navigation EKF covariance. Simulation results on a 20\,g acceleration step scenario show that the adapted vector architecture doubles the lock-retention rate relative to the standalone scalar filter at $C/N_0 = 31$\,dB-Hz. The receiver is implemented in MSGR, an open-source MATLAB GNSS software-defined receiver.

</details>


### [346] [Group Physical-Layer Authentication and Confidential Beamforming in Non-Terrestrial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41579)
*Parsa Rajabi,Mohammad Reza Abedi,Paeiz Azmi,Nader Mokari*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对功率受限的协同高空平台(HAPS)网络，本文研究以叠加新鲜秘密标签为手段的联合群组物理层认证(PLA)与保密波束成形，并用SDR+秩约简+SCA进行功率最小化优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同HAPS网络需在共享功率约束下同时完成群组响应的认证与私有数据保护，而认证可靠性、保密性与发射功率之间存在耦合与折衷，缺乏统一的设计准则。

Method: 对与当前标签独立的接收攻击推导有限块长下的虚警接受与合法拒绝精确表达式，并将其映射为标签SINR需求；分析非零标签能量下限、残余频偏与峰值功率限制；合法接收机用正交投影去除标签（损失一个时间维度）；针对合谋窃听者给出AN使其SINR有界所需的空间覆盖条件；联合优化私有波束、标签波束与AN协方差，在认证、保密与单HAPS功率约束下最小化发射功率，算法结合半定松弛、私有波束精确秩约简与可行SCA，并用修正对偶下界评估最优性间隙。
