Result: 标签长度由16增至64符号可降低所需标签SINR 7.16 dB；相较优化后的固定标签方向基线，联合波束设计使平均发射功率降低27.4%；认证保障依赖新鲜秘密标签，并排除对当前响应的中继转发。

Conclusion: 联合标签与波束设计可在满足认证与保密要求的同时显著节省发射功率，标签长度是最有效的调节杠杆；所提SDR-秩约简-SCA框架配合对偶下界可有效刻画并量化优化间隙。

Abstract: Coordinated high-altitude platform station (HAPS) networks need to authenticate group responses and protect private data under shared power constraints. This paper studies joint group physical-layer authentication (PLA) and confidential beamforming using a fresh secret tag superimposed on private data. For received attacks independent of the current tag, we derive exact finite-block expressions for false acceptance and legitimate rejection. These expressions translate authentication error targets into a required tag signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR). We establish a nonzero asymptotic tag-energy floor and analyze residual frequency offset and peak-power restrictions. The legitimate receiver removes the tag by orthogonal projection, which discards one temporal dimension. For colluding eavesdroppers, we identify the spatial coverage condition under which artificial noise (AN) keeps their SINR bounded as data and AN powers increase together. We jointly optimize the private beams, tag beam, and AN covariance to minimize transmit power under authentication, secrecy, and per-HAPS power constraints. The method combines semidefinite relaxation (SDR), exact private-beam rank reduction, and feasible successive convex approximation. A corrected dual lower bound measures the remaining optimality gap. Numerical results show that increasing the tag length from 16 to 64 symbols reduces the required tag SINR by 7.16 dB. Joint beam design reduces mean transmit power by 27.4% relative to an optimized fixed-tag-direction baseline. The authentication guarantee requires fresh secret tags and excludes relays of the current response.

</details>


### [347] [Learning Joint Synchronization, Channel Estimation, and Decoding: Comparison with a Finite-Blocklength Achievability Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41580)
*Bingcheng Chen,Giuseppe Durisi,Christian Häger*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 将TurboAE扩展至含未知时延与无记忆块衰落的SISO波形信道，引入无导频、模型驱动的神经同步器实现联合同步、信道估计与译码，并与有限块长可达界及5G基线对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短包通信中联合同步与译码的学习型方案效果可观，但其性能与信息论界的差距尚不明确，需在具备有限块长可达界的波形信道上进行量化评估。

Method: 在Dörner等(2023)的TurboAE基础上，扩展至单输入单输出波形信道（未知时延、无记忆块衰落）；设计无导频、模型引导的神经同步器，实现同步、信道估计与译码的联合处理；块长64复符号、频谱效率1 bit/信道使用，与导频辅助的5G LDPC基线及极化码+SCL(list 8)基线比较，并参照导频辅助可达界。

Result: BER=10⁻³时，较LDPC基线增益1.9 dB、较极化码SCL基线增益1.7 dB，BER低于所有评估的导频辅助5G基线；BLER=10⁻³时优于LDPC基线但落后极化码基线约0.8 dB；所有方案距导频辅助可达界至少2.1 dB。

Conclusion: 学习型联合同步译码在短块BER上具优势，但BLER未全面领先，且与有限块长可达界仍有≥2.1 dB差距，提示设计存在显著改进空间。

Abstract: Learned schemes that jointly perform tasks such as synchronization and decoding have achieved promising results for short-packet communication. However, it remains unclear how close their performance is to information-theoretic bounds. To investigate this question, we extend the turbo autoencoder (TurboAE) proposed in Dörner et al. (2023) to a single-input single-output waveform channel with an unknown delay and memoryless block fading, for which an achievability bound from finite-blocklength information theory is available. Our extension incorporates a pilot-free, model-guided neural synchronizer and enables joint synchronization, channel estimation, and decoding. At a blocklength of $64$ complex symbols and a rate of one bit per channel use, the proposed TurboAE achieves a lower bit error rate (BER) than all evaluated pilot-assisted 5G baselines, with gains of $1.9$ dB at a BER of $10^{-3}$ over a baseline involving a low-density parity-check (LDPC) code and $1.7$ dB over a baseline involving a polar code and successive cancellation list decoding (list size $8$). In terms of block error rate (BLER), the TurboAE outperforms the LDPC-coded baseline, but lags behind the polar-coded baseline by about $0.8$ dB at a BLER of $10^{-3}$. Moreover, all evaluated schemes remain at least $2.1$ dB away from a pilot-assisted achievability bound, indicating substantial room for improved designs.

</details>


### [348] [Dynamic-Aware LC-DMA Beam Tracking for LEO Satellite Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41612)
*Mohamadreza Delbari,Mohammad Zarei,Denis Giniatoulline,Vahid Jamali*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该文针对液晶动态超表面天线(LC-DMA)在LEO卫星通信中的波束跟踪问题，提出动态感知框架，联合建模用户/卫星移动引起的波束时变与LC移相器有限响应时间两种耦合动态，通过非凸优化与交替优化实现累积SNR最大化，并证明在合理考虑LC响应时可支撑实际移动场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LC-DMA具备功耗低、成本低、可扩展的优势，适合LEO卫星通信中需要大口径补偿大路径损耗的场景；但波束跟踪受两种耦合动态制约：用户与卫星移动导致的波束方向时变，以及LC移相器的有限响应时间。由此引出核心问题：LC-DMA的重构速度能否支撑高移动性LEO链路。

Method: 建立显式刻画两种动态的波束跟踪框架；构造在预测跟踪时域内最大化累积接收SNR的优化问题，约束包括LC单元物理动态与发射功率约束；针对非凸性，采用交替优化：数字预编码用闭式MRT、接收端用MRC，相位偏移采用并行闭式动态感知更新。

Result: 仿真表明：当恰当考虑LC-DMA时间响应时，其可支撑实际移动场景；所提动态感知算法保持SNR稳定，并优于传统设计，尤其在高车速与低轨道方位角情形下优势明显。

Conclusion: LC-DMA的有限响应时间是LEO波束跟踪的关键约束，必须在设计中显式建模；所提低复杂度交替优化方案在兼顾物理动态与功率约束下实现稳健跟踪，为LC-DMA在LEO卫星通信中的可行性提供依据。

Abstract: Liquid crystal (LC)-based dynamic metasurface antennas (DMAs) offer a power-efficient, low-cost, and scalable solution for low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite communications where large apertures are needed to compensate for large path loss in the Earth-satellite link. However, beam tracking with LC-DMAs is governed by two coupled dynamics: the required time-varying beam direction caused by user and satellite mobility, and the finite response time of the LC phase shifters. This raises the question of whether LC-DMAs can reconfigure sufficiently fast to support high-mobility LEO satellite links. To address this problem, we develop a dynamic-aware beam-tracking framework that explicitly accounts for both dynamics. In particular, we formulate an optimization problem to maximize the cumulative received signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) over a predictive tracking horizon subject to the physical dynamics of the LC unit cells as well as transmit power constraints. To solve this non-convex problem efficiently, we develop an alternating optimization framework combining a closed-form maximum ratio transmission (MRT) digital precoder and maximum ratio combining (MRC) with a parallel, closed-form dynamic-aware phase-shift update. Simulation results show that when the time response of LC-DMAs is properly accounted for, it can support practical mobility scenarios. Moreover, the proposed dynamic-aware algorithm maintains stable SNR and outperforms conventional designs, particularly at high vehicle velocities and low orbital azimuth angles.

</details>


### [349] [Limitation of Bessel Beams in Near Field Communications: A Depth of Field Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41656)
*Aditya Jolly,Vitaly Petrov,Gábor Fodor,Emil Björnson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文从景深（depth-of-field）角度解析地证明了有限孔径（圆形/方形）贝塞尔波束的无衍射传播范围存在上限，定义绝对与峰值归一化3-dB景深准则，揭示划分配置空间的临界锥角阈值，并给出最大可用无衍射距离约为Fraunhofer距离的22%（圆孔径）与19%（方孔径）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝塞尔波束因无衍射与自愈特性被视为辐射近场通信中聚焦波束的候选补充，但近期研究表明有限孔径贝塞尔波束的实用范围仅占辐射近场区的一部分，即存在有限传播极限；该文旨在从景深视角为这一限制建立解析理论依据。

Method: 推导有限圆形与方形孔径产生贝塞尔波束的归一化阵列增益解析表达式，据此建立绝对与峰值归一化的3-dB景深判据，并分析其对应的临界锥角阈值，将参数空间划分为无定义、有限与无限景深三种区域。

Result: 在所提景深判据下，最大可用无衍射范围对圆孔径与方孔径分别约为Fraunhofer距离的22%与19%；数值结果验证了所导出的极限对离散平面阵列的准确性。

Conclusion: 有限孔径贝塞尔波束的无衍射传播距离存在本质上限，该解析极限为未来近场通信中有限孔径贝塞尔波束的设计提供工程指导准则。

Abstract: Bessel beams have recently been explored as a candidate complement to focused beams in radiative near-field communications due to their non-diffracting and self-healing properties. However, recent work has shown that the practically useful range of finite-aperture Bessel beams can occupy only a fraction of the radiative near-field region, establishing the existence of a finite propagation limit. In this paper, we establish such a limitation analytically from a depth-of-field perspective for Bessel beams generated by finite circular and square apertures. To quantify the Bessel beam depth, we first derive analytical expressions for the normalized array gain and use them to establish absolute and peak-normalized $3$-dB depth-of-field criteria. These criteria reveal critical cone-angle thresholds that partition the Bessel-beam configuration space into (i) undefined, (ii) finite, and (iii) infinite depth-of-field regimes. Our analysis further reveals that the maximum usable non-diffracting range under the proposed depth-of-field criterion is limited to $\approx22\%$ and $\approx19\%$ of the Fraunhofer distance for circular and square apertures, respectively. Numerical results confirm the accuracy of the derived limits for discrete planar arrays, providing practical engineering guidelines for the design of finite-aperture Bessel beams in future near-field communications.

</details>


### [350] [RFLocMatch: Reference-Conditioned Residual Learning from Angle-Delay Spectra for Accurate Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41757)
*Leihao Zhao,Xiucheng Wang,Junxi Huang,Conghao Zhou,Yuxiang Zhang,Huixin Xu,Nan Cheng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出RFLocMatch：联合匹配+链路特定匹配、参考条件残差学习与候选上下文建模，并以尾部聚焦训练和验证校准的替换阈值抑制空间混淆与测量扰动导致的尾部误差，显著提升射频指纹定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角度-时延功率指纹虽能刻画功率在角度与时延上的分布，但定位仍受空间模糊与测量扰动影响：反射与遮挡可使远距离位置出现相似指纹；查询指纹噪声会改变查询-参考排序，即便参考库干净，也可能使远处参考排到首位，产生尾部误差。仅靠相似度分数可产生候选，但可靠选择需要候选特定的残差证据及候选间比较。

Method: RFLocMatch将相似度生成候选与证据选择分离：(1)采用互补的联合匹配与链路特定匹配，保留由不同链路支撑的候选位置；(2)参考条件残差学习，以查询与参考指纹、二者绝对差及逐元素乘积对每个候选打分；(3)候选上下文建模，对比不同候选；(4)尾部聚焦训练强调高代价错误；(5)推理阶段设置经验证集校准的阈值，仅当候选分数优势超过阈值时才替换初始匹配。

Result: 在射线追踪生成数据上，于链路与接收机扰动条件下，双发射机99百分位误差相对最佳基线降低75.2%；在真实单发射机信道冲激响应（CIR）测量并结合运动辅助角度谱估计时，达到8.25 m的90百分位误差与3.83 m的平均绝对误差。

Conclusion: 通过将候选生成与候选特定证据比较解耦，并结合尾部聚焦训练与校准的替换准则，RFLocMatch有效缓解空间模糊与扰动引起的排序失效，在仿真与实测中均取得显著优于基线的尾部与整体精度。

Abstract: Radio fingerprint localization supports mobile robots and location-aware services when satellite positioning is unreliable. Angle-delay power fingerprints capture power distributions over angles and delays, but localization remains vulnerable to spatial ambiguity and measurement perturbations. Reflections and blockage can produce similar fingerprints at distant locations, while noise in query fingerprints can alter query-reference rankings despite a clean reference library. These effects can place a distant reference first in the ranking, creating tail errors. Similarity scores can generate plausible candidates, but reliable selection requires candidate-specific residual evidence and comparison across alternatives. This separation motivates RFLocMatch, which uses complementary joint and link-specific matching to preserve candidate locations supported by different links. Reference-conditioned residual learning then scores each candidate using the query and reference fingerprints, their absolute difference, and their element-wise product, while candidate-context modeling compares alternatives. Tail-focused training emphasizes costly errors, and inference replaces the initial match only when a candidate's score advantage exceeds a validation-calibrated threshold. On ray-tracing-generated data, RFLocMatch reduces dual-transmitter 99th-percentile error by 75.2% under link and receiver perturbations relative to the best-performing baseline. On real-world single-transmitter channel impulse response (CIR) measurements with motion-assisted angular-spectrum estimation, it attains 8.25-m 90th-percentile error and 3.83-m mean absolute error.

</details>


### [351] [RSS-Based Verification of Drone Remote ID Position Claims with Unknown Transmit Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41783)
*Cole Dickerson,Ismail Guvenc,Wahab Khawaja,Ali Cafer Gurbuz*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对广播式远程识别（Remote ID）位置可被低成本伪造的问题，在发射功率与路径损耗指数均未知的条件下，提出基于RSS的Rao分数检验来验证申报位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Remote ID是无人机融入空域的关键使能技术，但其上报位置为自申报，可用低成本硬件伪造；现有接收端缺乏在链路参数未知时可靠的验证手段。

Method: 将发射功率与路径损耗指数视为未知冗余参数，推导Rao分数检验；该检验在任意数据包数下零分布严格服从卡方分布，阈值与检测概率均有闭式表达；进而给出对未知链路参数不变的任意检测器检测概率上界，并据此推导最坏方位角、闭合该漏洞所需观测长度及接收机布设规则。

Result: 给出了检验的闭式阈值与检测概率；证明沿固定方位角方向伪造位置后退时检测概率上界饱和，故足够大的伪造可能不可检测；获得最坏方位角的闭式解、所需观测长度与接收机布设规则；仿真显示在低虚警率下所提检验显著优于“先估计再门限”检测器。

Conclusion: RSS可用于在未知链路参数下验证Remote ID位置，但存在原理性可检测性极限；通过最坏方位分析与接收机布设与观测长度设计可缓解该极限。

Abstract: Broadcast Remote Identification (Remote ID) is a key enabler for integrating unmanned aircraft into the airspace. However, the position it reports is self-declared and can be falsified with low-cost hardware. In this paper, we study how Remote ID receivers can verify the declared position from the received signal strength (RSS) when neither the transmit power nor the path-loss exponent is known. In particular, we derive a Rao score test whose null distribution is exactly chi-squared for any number of packets. Its threshold and detection probability therefore follow in closed form. We then bound the detection probability of any detector that is invariant to the unknown link parameters. This bound saturates as the falsified position recedes along a fixed bearing, so a sufficiently large falsification can remain undetectable. From this limit, we obtain the worst-case bearing in closed form, the observation length needed to close it, and a receiver placement rule. Simulation results show that the proposed test considerably outperforms an estimate-then-threshold detector at low false-alarm rates.

</details>


### [352] [RadioTraj3D: Height-Aware Adaptive Sampling for 3D Radio-Map Reconstruction from Ultra-Sparse Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41786)
*Zixuan Guo,Xiucheng Wang,Wenshuo Chen,Conghao Zhou,Chuan Huang,Ruijin Sun,Nan Cheng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出RadioTraj3D，利用建筑高度信息协调测量选择与分层重建，从AAV轨迹测量中构建三维无线电地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空网络需在未访问位置与高度上获取无线电传播信息以进行路径规划、链路选择与资源分配。三维无线电地图可支撑飞行前决策，但仅有轨迹上的测量而目标覆盖三维服务体积；现有方法采用固定轨迹，忽略建筑分布与高度对电磁传播的影响，遗漏对重建最有信息量的高度。

Method: 基于建筑高度信息协调测量选择与分层重建：建筑高度变化揭示传播敏感层，引导高度自适应采样；提出G-METS在信道采集前选取几何敏感高度，并在边缘、走廊与开阔区域间分配精确预算；双视角重建器通过逐层单一有界系数融合局部三维边界与全高度上下文。

Result: 仿真室外与室内地图在多种环境和垂直网格上取得最优重建性能；在LACN数据集真实AAV测量上，相比Completion AE，PSNR提升0.84 dB，NMSE降低17.5%。

Conclusion: 利用建筑高度先验可有效指导高度自适应测量与分层重建，显著提升三维无线电地图的重建精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Aerial networks require radio propagation information at unvisited positions and heights to plan routes, select links, and allocate wireless resources. A three-dimensional radio map (3D RM) represents propagation conditions over the service area, enabling these decisions before flight. However, constructing such a map from autonomous aerial vehicle (AAV) measurements is challenging, since radio measurements are available only along the trajectory, whereas the target covers the 3D service volume. Existing methods use fixed AAV trajectories that ignore how building distributions and heights affect electromagnetic propagation, missing the heights most informative for RM reconstruction. To address these issues, we propose RadioTraj3D, which uses building-height information to coordinate measurement selection and layer-wise reconstruction. Building-height variations reveal propagation-sensitive layers that guide height-adaptive sampling. We therefore propose G-METS to select geometry-sensitive heights and allocate an exact budget across edge, corridor, and open regions before channel acquisition. A dual-view reconstructor then integrates local three-dimensional boundaries with full-height context through one bounded coefficient per layer. Simulated outdoor and indoor maps demonstrate state-of-the-art reconstruction across environments and vertical grids. On real-world AAV measurements from the LACN dataset, RadioTraj3D improves peak signal-to-noise ratio by 0.84 dB and reduces normalized mean squared error by 17.5% over Completion AE.

</details>


### [353] [Improving Spectral Resolution via Simultaneous Extracochlear and Apical Ground Contacts: A Neural Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41791)
*Jared A. Rybarczyk,Erin L. Bratu,Robert F. Labadie,Jack H. Noble*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该回顾性研究通过患者个体化容积传导模型与听神经纤维(ANF)房室模型，比较耳蜗顶回返回电极与耳蜗外接地电极单独及联合使用时的激活阈值与纤维激活比例离散度，发现双接地配置可降低通道交互、提升频谱分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工耳蜗虽能提供良好言语感知，但通道交互导致噪声下言语识别持续受限。近期在耳蜗蜗孔处放置顶回返回电极具备临床可行性，为新的刺激方案提供了可能，因此需明确顶部与耳蜗外接地电极之间的相互作用及其对听觉神经动力学与频谱分辨率的影响。

Method: 回顾性纳入6例人工耳蜗植入者。构建包含患者特异性电解剖特征的容积传导模型，并结合定义神经动力学的ANF房室模型，对每个建模耳蜗内75个均匀分布纤维束、4种接地配置（含联合接地）计算激活阈值；以阈值偏移和纤维激活比例标准差比较患者内模型，评估接地配置对通道交互的影响。

Result: 6例患者中，联合接地模型的ANF激活阈值均出现偏移，提示每位触点可产生更具特异性的纤维激活；同时，与标准耳蜗外接地或顶回接地配置相比，联合接地模型在刺激阵列的多个触点上表现出更小的纤维激活比例标准差。

Conclusion: 阈值偏移与标准差降低表明，联合使用耳蜗外接地与顶回接地的刺激方案可在阵列的特定触点上减少通道交互并增强频谱分辨率，为该方案的实验与临床转化提供了建模依据。

Abstract: Objectives: While cochlear implants (CI) can provide excellent speech perception outcomes, speech recognition in noise remains a consistent problem for many patients due to channel interaction. Recent work shows the clinical feasibility of an apical return contact placed in the cochlear helicotrema, which presents new possibilities in terms of experimental stimulation schemes. This study explores the interactions between apical and extracochlear ground contacts, aiming to characterize the effects of dual ground usage on auditory nerve fiber (ANF) dynamics and determine their utility in improving spectral resolution. Design: 6 patients with CIs were used in this retrospective study. Volume conduction models encompassing patient specific electro-anatomical characteristics and ANF compartment models defining neural dynamics were used to generate activation thresholds for 75 evenly spaced fiber bundles throughout each modeled cochlea and for 4 different grounding configurations, including the use of combined grounds. We compare our intra-patient models in terms of threshold shifts and standard deviation of fiber proportions to determine the effects of ground configuration used on channel interaction. Results: For all 6 patients, we observe shifts in ANF activation thresholds for the combined ground models indicative of more specific fiber stimulation per contact. Additionally, our combined ground models exhibit reduced fiber proportion standard deviation for several contacts of the stimulating array when compared to the standard extracochlear ground configuration or apical ground configuration. Conclusions: The shifts in activation thresholds and reduction in standard deviations suggest that stimulation schemes utilizing extracochlear and apical grounds in conjunction can provide reduced channel interaction and enhanced spectral resolution for certain contacts in an array.

</details>


### [354] [B-ExposNet: Bayesian Deep Learning for EMF Exposure Mapping with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41913)
*Yukun Liu,Shanshan Wang,Paul Lagouanelle,Yarui Zhang,Joe Wiart*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出B-ExposNet——一种基于贝叶斯神经网络的城市电磁场空间暴露估计方法，可量化不确定性，在三个法国城市的实测数据上同时优于通用克里金等传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市环境中电磁场暴露高度可变，传统暴露地图仅给出确定性估计，忽略了估计的不确定性，无法反映预测可信度。

Method: 在ExposNet基础上扩展：将回归头权重视为随机变量，采用变分推断近似后验，通过后验预测分布输出概率化估计（含不确定性量化）。在法国三城 drive-test 实测数据上，与通用克里金等传统方法进行精度与不确定性质量对比。

Result: 测试区域上平均绝对百分比误差（MAPE）为28.19%，连续排名概率得分（CRPS）为0.198 V/m，在预测精度与不确定性量化两方面均优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 将贝叶斯不确定性量化引入神经网络暴露估计是有效的，能在保持精度的同时提供可靠的置信信息，弥补确定性暴露地图的不足。

Abstract: Electromagnetic field exposure in urban environments is highly variable, and conventional exposure maps provide a deterministic estimate, without considering its uncertainty. To address the gap, this paper proposes B-ExposNet, a Bayesian neural network for spatial exposure estimation with uncertainty quantification. B-ExposNet extends ExposNet by treating the weights of regression head as random variables, inferred by variational inference, so that the model provides probabilistic estimates from posterior predictive distribution. The method is evaluated on drive-test measurement campaigns conducted in three French cities, and compared with conventional method such as universal Kriging, in both predictive accuracy and the quality of the estimated uncertainty. On the test region, B-ExposNet achieves a mean absolute percentage error of 28.19\% and a continuous ranked probability score of 0.198~V/m, improving on canonical methods in both accuracy and uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [355] [Grid-Refinement SBL for AFDM Channel Estimation Under Fractional Delay and Fractional Doppler Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41997)
*Qu Luo,Limin Liu,Zhe Li,Qihao Peng,Haowei Wu,Pei Xiao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对分数时延与分数多普勒（FDFD）信道下的AFDM系统信道估计，建立精确FDFD信道模型，并分别提出FLA-GR-SBL与2D-GA-SBL两种稀疏贝叶斯学习算法，通过网格细化缓解时延—多普勒耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AFDM系统在存在分数时延与分数多普勒偏移的实际信道中，会产生时延—多普勒耦合效应，现有on-grid与off-grid SBL方法建模不精确，导致估计性能受限。

Method: 首先建立精确的FDFD信道模型并推导输入—输出关系；其次提出一阶线性近似网格细化SBL（FLA-GR-SBL）以处理时延—多普勒耦合；进而针对一阶近似精度不足引起的收敛与稳定性问题，提出二维网格近似SBL（2D-GA-SBL），对时延—多普勒网格进行迭代细化。

Result: 仿真表明，所提算法在归一化均方误差（NMSE）与误码率（BER）两方面均显著优于现有on-grid与off-grid SBL基准方法。

Conclusion: 通过精确FDFD建模与迭代式二维网格细化SBL，可有效缓解时延—多普勒耦合，提升AFDM系统信道估计的精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper investigates channel estimation for affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) systems over channels with fractional delay and fractional Doppler (FDFD) shifts. An exact FDFD channel model is first established, and the corresponding input--output relationship is also derived. To address the FDFD-induced delay--Doppler coupling, a first-order linear approximation grid-refinement sparse Bayesian learning (FLA-GR-SBL) method is developed. To further remedy the convergence and stability issues caused by the limited accuracy of the first-order approximation, we propose a two-dimensional grid-approximation SBL (2D-GA-SBL) algorithm, which iteratively refines the delay--Doppler grid and thereby characterizes the channel more accurately. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed algorithms significantly outperform existing on-grid and off-grid SBL benchmarks in terms of both normalized mean square error (NMSE) and bit error rate (BER).

</details>


### [356] [Embodied Movable Antennas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42078)
*Yewen Cao,Dengke Wei,Yulin Shao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出"具身可动天线"新架构，利用物理智能体自身已有的机械自由度（而非专用天线驱动器）重构天线位置与朝向，并以冗余机械臂为例证明任务保持型自运动可带来显著通信增益，将机械冗余确立为可量化的无线信道重构资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可动天线依赖专用天线驱动器来改变位置/朝向，带来额外硬件与执行开销；本文追问天线移动性的来源，试图挖取智能体本身已有的机械自由度，把机械冗余转化为无线信道重构的资源。

Method: 区分任务耦合型与任务保持型两类具身MA，聚焦后者：以冗余机械臂为典型实现，刻画任务保持型自运动引起的天线平移—朝向内在耦合，推导对应多径信道的精确傅里叶表示；分析可行构型集合与耦合运动同极化、多径传播的交互；据此设计固定安装即可跨任务适配的天线安装方案。

Result: 数值结果表明任务保持型运动可带来显著的通信增益；并揭示具身移动性的无线价值不仅取决于天线可移动的距离，还取决于物理任务下仍可行的构型集合及耦合运动与极化、多径的相互作用。

Conclusion: 机械冗余是一种新的、可量化的无线信道重构资源，具身MA可在不改变既定物理任务的前提下实现无线适配。

Abstract: This paper introduces embodied movable antennas (MAs), a new MA architecture that rethinks the source of antenna mobility. Unlike conventional MAs that rely on dedicated antenna actuators, embodied MAs harness the mechanical degrees of freedom already present in physical agents to reconfigure antenna position and orientation. We distinguish two modes of embodied MAs: task-coupled and task-preserving. Focusing on the latter, we show how wireless channels can be reconfigured by exploiting task-equivalent host configurations without altering the prescribed physical task. Using a redundant robotic manipulator as a canonical realization, we characterize the intrinsically coupled antenna translation and orientation induced by task-preserving self-motion and derive an exact Fourier representation of the resulting multipath channel. Our analysis reveals that the wireless value of embodied mobility depends not merely on how far an antenna can move, but on which configurations remain feasible under the physical task and how their coupled motion interacts with polarization and multipath propagation. Building on this insight, we develop a cross-task antenna mounting design that enables wireless adaptation with a fixed physical installation. Numerical results demonstrate substantial communication gains through task-preserving motion. These findings establish mechanical redundancy as a new, quantifiable resource for wireless channel reconfiguration.

</details>

### [357] [Metadevices that compute: mathematical foundations and experimental implementations for neuromorphic nanophotonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40903)
*Xian Hadia,Odysseas Tsilipakos,Dimitrios C. Tzarouchis,Raphaël Pestourie*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Wave-based analog computing has re-emerged as a compelling alternative to digital electronics for specific linear-algebraic and machine-learning workloads, promising massively parallel, high-bandwidth, and energy-efficient information processing. Metamaterials, metasurfaces, and integrated metastructures (collectively, metadevices) now perform mathematical operations, solve integral and differential equations, multiply matrices, and implement trainable photonic neural networks directly in the wave domain. This focused review consolidates the mathematical foundations that underpin such devices, i.e., the mapping between linear operators and wave scattering, the feedback architectures that realize equation inversion, and the inverse-design and scientific machine-learning toolchains that make their synthesis and training tractable, and connects them to the rapidly growing body of experimental demonstrations, from free-space diffractive processors to reconfigurable guided-wave metastructures. We conclude with open problems and opportunities for this emerging neuromorphic nanophotonics ecosystem.

</details>


### [358] [Comparison of melanin subtypes using pump-probe microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40969)
*Emma Bao,David Grass,Martin C. Fischer,Warren S. Warren*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该论文利用泵浦-探测显微镜（PPM）表征黑色素的激发态动力学，在6类病变、21个生物样本、109个感兴趣区域上定量分析黑色素的化学与结构异质性，发现异质性可区分皮肤与非皮肤病变但与恶性程度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑色素在光保护、皮肤与眼部色素沉着及黑色素瘤发病机制中作用关键，但其结构与化学复杂性使其难以分析；同时PPM可探测内源性生色团的激发态动力学，提供传统显微术无法获得的对比度，因此作者希望用PPM刻画黑色素的光物理特性。

Method: 采用泵浦-探测显微镜对来自6类病变（皮肤痣、雀斑样痣、皮肤恶性黑色素瘤、结膜黑素细胞病变、外阴黑素细胞病变及小鼠黑色素瘤模型）的黑色素成像，覆盖21个独立生物样本与109个感兴趣区域；结合模型拟合、改进的相量分析（phasor analysis）与支持向量机分类，将激发态动力学作为化学与结构异质性的替代指标进行表征与量化。

Result: 黑色素异质性可将皮肤病变与非皮肤病变区分开，但与恶性程度无相关性；良性雀斑样痣是数据集中异质性最高的群体，超过皮肤恶性黑色素瘤，而结膜病变尽管直接受日光照射却最为均一；基于完整瞬态吸收曲线的分类在多数亚型上显著优于六分类随机水平。

Conclusion: PPM能够揭示黑色素在不同组织环境中光物理性质的细微差异，对光学组织分类及黑色素瘤发病机制的理解具有重要意义。

Abstract: Pump-probe microscopy (PPM) is used to probe excited state dynamics of endogenous chromophores to generate contrast that is unavailable in traditional forms of microscopy. Here we apply PPM to characterize melanin, a pigment central to photoprotection, skin and eye pigmentation, and melanoma pathogenesis. Melanin is notoriously difficult to analyze due to its structural and chemical complexity. We imaged melanin from six different lesion categories: cutaneous nevi, lentigo, cutaneous malignant melanoma, conjunctival melanocytic lesions, vulvar melanocytic lesions, and a murine melanoma model, across 21 independent biological samples and 109 regions of interest. Using model fitting, adapted phasor analysis, and support vector machine classification, we characterize and quantify excited-state dynamics as a surrogate for chemical and structural heterogeneity. Heterogeneity of melanin separates cutaneous from non-cutaneous lesions, but is not correlated with malignancy. Benign lentigo is the most heterogeneous population in the dataset, exceeding cutaneous malignant melanoma, while conjunctival lesions are the most homogeneous despite receiving direct solar exposure. Classification on full transient absorption curves separates most subtypes well above the six-class chance level. These findings demonstrate the ability of PPM to reveal subtle differences in melanin photophysics across diverse tissue environments, with implications for optical tissue classification and the understanding of melanoma pathogenesis.

</details>


### [359] [Microscopic Theory of Spin-Orbit Interactions of Light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40979)
*Saeed Rahmanian Koshkaki,Ding Xu,Michael Fowler,Milan Delor,Arkajit Mandal*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文建立从非相对论量子电动力学出发的微观量子理论，揭示腔中光学自旋-轨道相互作用源于受限光子与材料激发之间的虚相互作用，并给出调控其各分量的材料设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前光学自旋-轨道相互作用（SOI）实验上已在光学腔中观测到，但仅用唯象模型描述，无法揭示其微观量子起源，材料电子结构与光学SOI之间的严格联系尚不清楚。

Method: 从光-物质相互作用的非相对论量子电动力学（QED）描述出发，构建微观量子理论，将光学SOI归因于受限光子与材料激发之间的虚相互作用。

Result: 理论框架给出了调控光学SOI各分量的微观材料设计原则，包括光学双折射、光子自能、TE-TM劈裂与光学塞曼劈裂，从而可精确工程化有效磁场、狄亚波点（diabolical points）与赝自旋织构。

Conclusion: 该微观理论具备预测能力，可用于系统筛选与设计面向光子自旋电子学及光学技术的材料体系。

Abstract: Optical spin-orbit interactions (SOI), demonstrated experimentally in optical cavities, enable the polarization degrees of freedom of light to be described and controlled as an effective pseudospin, providing a foundation for spintronic-like control of light and the development of next-generation optical technologies. However, prior works describe this emergent effect using phenomenological models that fail to uncover its microscopic quantum origins, and as a result, a rigorous connection between material electronic structure and the optical SOI remains elusive. In this work, we address this fundamental challenge by developing a microscopic quantum theory starting from the non-relativistic quantum electrodynamical (QED) description of light-matter interactions. We show that this effect arises from virtual interactions between confined photons and material excitations. Crucially, our theoretical framework provides microscopic material-design principles for tuning the distinct components of optical SOI, which include the optical birefringence, photonic self-energies, TE-TM splitting, and optical Zeeman splitting, enabling precise engineering of effective magnetic fields, diabolical points, and pseudospin textures. We anticipate that the predictive capability of our microscopic theory will enable systematic screening and design of material systems for photonic spintronics and optical technologies.

</details>


### [360] [From proof of concept to practice: the next decade of artificial intelligence in metasurface photonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41000)
*Sensong An,Haoyue Jiang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文通过系统阅读100篇高被引光学研究，辨析AI在超表面（metasurface）电磁响应预测与逆向设计任务中"哪些不可替代、哪些确有优势、哪些无明显优势"，并主张未来十年应转向共享数据、可复用模型与物理嵌入学习，使成果可验证、可迁移、可被实验校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年，AI已能快速预测电磁响应并生成高性能超表面设计，但一个根本问题仍未解决：相较于不含学习模型的传统设计流程，AI是否使"完整设计流程"更快、所得器件是否更好。作者认为答案取决于具体任务，因而需要审视AI的实际增益边界，而非泛化地宣称成功。

Method: 采用文献综述与深度阅读（qualitative synthesis）方法：选取并精读100篇高被引光学文献，按设计任务类型对AI的作用进行分层归类——不可替代、明显有利、无明显优势；并在此基础上提出领域发展路径的建议性论证。

Result: 核心结论为任务依赖性：AI的价值并非在所有设计任务中普遍成立，需区分其在响应预测、逆向设计等环节的相对贡献；仅以孤立任务的成功作为评价标准不足以证明整体工作流优势。

Conclusion: 作者主张下一阶段重点应是将单点成功转化为领域基础设施——共享数据集、训练好的模型与嵌入物理先验的学习方法；AI在本领域的持久价值取决于其收益能否被验证、方法能否迁移、设计能否经受实验校正。

Abstract: Over the past decade, artificial intelligence has learned to predict electromagnetic responses rapidly and generate high-performance metasurface designs. However, one question remains open: compared with conventional design workflows that contain no learned model, has it made the complete design workflow faster, and are the resulting devices better? We argue that the answer depends on the task. Reading 100 highly cited optical studies in depth, we distinguish the design tasks in which artificial intelligence is irreplaceable, clearly advantageous, or without clear advantage. In our view, the key to the next decade is not more successes in isolated tasks but making each success a foundation for the field, through shifts such as shared data, trained models, and physics written into learning. Whether artificial intelligence leaves lasting value in this field will depend on whether its benefits can be verified, its methods transferred, and its designs corrected by experiment.

</details>


### [361] [Intensity-Domain Demultiplexing of Orbital Angular Momentum Channels for Optical Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41259)
*Junsu Kim,Jiwon Jung,Hyunchae Chun,Seung Ryong Park*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出以OAM"花瓣图样"的方位角谐波作为解复用基，使接收端等价于射频OAM链路中的圆形天线阵，仅需对花瓣强度方位角分布做一次傅里叶变换即可分离全部通道，无需光栅或模式分类器；两通道515 nm实验达5.2 Gb/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OAM复用在实际应用中的主要瓶颈在接收端：分离各信道的器件结构复杂、光学损耗大（如光栅、模式分类器等）。作者希望用更简洁的光学方案实现低损耗、可扩展的解复用。

Method: 以OAM花瓣图样的方位角谐波为解复用基：拓扑电荷为ℓ的花瓣图样只含一个2ℓ阶方位角谐波，因此对花瓣的方位角强度分布做一次傅里叶变换即可读出所有信道。花瓣由单个q板产生的矢量涡旋光束经线偏振投影形成。实验搭建515 nm两通道系统，接收端采用16位圆形光电探测器阵列，单通道由1.4 GHz光电接收机驱动。

Result: 两通道系统在低于前向纠错（FEC）阈值的条件下实现5.2 Gb/s总速率，单通道2.6 Gb/s（源于1.4 GHz光电接收机）。

Conclusion: 花瓣谐波解复用提供了无需光栅/模式分类器的简化接收架构，与射频圆形天线阵形成光学类比，具备低复杂度、低损耗、易扩展的潜力，可用于高速OAM复用通信。

Abstract: Orbital angular momentum (OAM) multiplexing has been limited in practice by the complexity and optical loss of the elements that separate the channels at the receiver side. Here we adopt the azimuthal harmonics of OAM petal patterns as the demultiplexing basis, which makes the receiver the optical counterpart of the circular antenna arrays used in radio OAM links: the petal pattern of topological charge $\ell$ carries a single azimuthal harmonic of order $2\ell$, so one Fourier transform of the azimuthal intensity profile returns every channel, without gratings or mode sorters. The petals are formed by projecting a vector vortex beam from a single q-plate onto a linear polarization. A two-channel system at 515 nm with a 16-position circular photoreceiver array reaches 5.2 Gb/s below the forward error correction threshold, 2.6 Gb/s per channel from a 1.4 GHz photoreceiver.

</details>


### [362] [Non-contact temperature sensing and optical scatter in cryogenically-cooled crystalline silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41261)
*Disha Pankaj Kapasi,Travis Counihan,Kevin Kimes,Alina Sendowsky,Alexandra Gruson,Daniel Martinez,Michael Rezac,Kevin Silverio,Sean Sorto,Joshua R. Smith*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文设计并验证了利用前后表面反射干涉条纹（光学fringe）非接触测量平板-平板无镀膜低温晶体硅样品温度的方法，并测量了其在123–294 K真空低温下的光学散射（平均BRDF ≈ (4.43±0.2)×10⁻³ sr⁻¹ @ 7.5°），未见冰层生长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温晶体硅因低机械损耗（热噪声驱动因素）、高热导率和123 K下零热膨胀系数，是下一代引力波探测器（如爱因斯坦望远镜、LIGO Voyager）的理想光学材料；需解决低温测试质量的温度监测与光学散射表征问题。

Method: 基于前后表面反射形成的干涉（光学条纹）实现非接触温度传感，用于测量平板-平板无镀膜晶体硅样品的温度；并在真空中、低温条件下用高灵敏度红外相机表征样品的光学散射，提出将该技术应用于未来引力波探测器低温测试质量的实施方案。

Result: 散射随冷板温度在123–294 K范围内基本保持恒定，散射角θs=7.5°处平均BRDF为(4.43±0.2)×10⁻³ sr⁻¹；在当前装置中未观察到低温分子层（冰）在样品上生长。

Conclusion: 干涉式非接触测温方法可用于低温晶体硅测试质量，并具备推广至爱因斯坦望远镜、LIGO Voyager等未来探测器低温测试质量的潜力；低温下样品散射稳定且无明显冰污染。

Abstract: Cryogenic crystalline silicon is a promising optical material for next generation gravitational-wave detectors due to its low mechanical loss (a driver of thermal noise), high thermal conductivity and zero coefficient of thermal expansion at 123 K. In this paper, we report on the design and results of an experiment to measure the temperature of a flat-flat uncoated crystalline silicon sample using a non-contact sensing method relying on interference (optical fringes) made from reflections between the front and back surfaces. We also propose a scheme for implementation of this technique for cryogenic test masses in future gravitational-wave detectors such as Einstein Telescope and LIGO Voyager. Additionally, we characterize the optical scattering of the sample in vacuum at cryogenic temperatures by using a high-sensitivity infrared camera. We measured relatively constant scatter with an average bidirectional reflectance distribution function (BRDF) of $(4.43\pm0.2)\times 10^{-3}\,\mathrm{sr^{-1}}$ at a scattering angle of $θ_{s} = 7.5^\circ$ over the (cold-plate) temperature range of 123-294 K and saw no evidence for growth of cryogenic molecular layers (ice) on the sample in our current setup.

</details>


### [363] [Dual-rate optical frequency comb based on a cylindrical microresonator with a nanoscale radius variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41315)
*Alena Kolesnikova,Andrey Gelash,Ilya Vatnik*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 圆柱形微谐振器中通过半径纳米级变化可形成二维轴向-方位角耗散克尔孤子(2D DKS)，从而产生双重复频率、可由器件几何独立调控的相干微梳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻求能在同一器件中同时跨不同频率尺度工作、且重复频率可独立设计的紧凑光频梳源。

Method: 基于真实器件参数对非线性耦合的Lugiato-Lefever方程进行数值模拟，研究半径近抛物线变化的圆柱微谐振器（兼容SNAP平台）中轴向-方位角二维孤子的形成与同步机制。

Result: 发现尽管不同轴向数下方位角模的色散特性存在差异，自发同步机制仍导致统一的孤子重复频率；示例中产生方位角速率约329 GHz、轴向速率约6 GHz的2D DKS。

Conclusion: 该双重复频率微梳可通过选择合适谐振器几何按需调节，具有作为跨频段紧凑相干频率梳源的潜力。

Abstract: We theoretically find that a cylindrical microresonator with nanoscale radius variation can be a source of coherent microcombs with two repetition rates, independently controlled by the device geometry. This phenomenon arises from the formation of two-dimensional axial-azimuthal dissipative Kerr solitons (2D DKS), which we discovered through numerical simulations of the nonlinearly coupled Lugiato-Lefever equations with realistic microresonator device parameters. We demonstrate that, despite the variation in the dispersion characteristics of azimuthal modes at different axial numbers, the spontaneous synchronization mechanism leads to a unified soliton repetition rate. We consider near-parabolic variations in the radius of a cylindrical resonator, compatible with the surface nanoscale axial photonics (SNAP) platform. In our example, we generate 2D DKS with an azimuthal rate of approximately 329 GHz and an axial rate of 6 GHz, which can be adjusted on demand by selecting the appropriate resonator geometry, thereby promising compact sources of frequency combs operating simultaneously across different frequency scales.

</details>


### [364] [Non-Hermitian Spectral Topology Shaped by Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41370)
*Hao Pan,Bo Xue,Wanyue Xiao,Qiuyang Li,Yuqiong Cheng,Yumeng Wang,Kun Ding,Alex M. H. Wong,Shubo Wang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 将耦合非对称性从空间推广到时间（引入记忆效应），揭示非马尔可夫耦合如何塑造非厄米谱拓扑并产生反常趋肤效应及其耗散调控反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米谱拓扑与开系统体—边对应关系的既有研究基本局限于瞬时耦合的马氏近似，记忆（时间非局域性）对非厄米谱拓扑的作用尚未被系统探讨。

Method: 把耦合非对称性由空间扩展到时间，构建时空非互易与记忆诱导谱多值性的理论框架；在多叶黎曼面上分析谱拓扑；通过全波仿真与微波实验验证。

Result: 时空非互易与记忆诱导的谱多值性在多叶黎曼面上产生非平庸谱拓扑，受空间连通性与相互作用历史共同支配；长程记忆通道的相干干涉可导致与主导耦合方向相反的反常谱绕数与趋肤局域化；仅靠非对称延迟即可在无空间耦合非对称时产生非厄米谱拓扑与趋肤积累；通道耗散驱动拓扑相变并反转趋肤效应，临界点对应通道损耗与晶格损耗速率相等。

Conclusion: 时间非局域性是超越马尔可夫范式的非厄米拓扑基本自由度，可带来马尔可夫框架下无法获得的谱拓扑与趋肤行为。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian spectral topology has transformed our understanding of bulk--boundary correspondence in open systems, yet its exploration has largely remained within the Markovian regime of instantaneous coupling. Here, we reveal how memory shapes non-Hermitian spectral topology by extending coupling asymmetry from space to time. Spatiotemporal nonreciprocity and memory-induced spectral multivaluedness give rise to nontrivial spectral topology on multi-sheeted Riemann surfaces, governed by both spatial connectivity and interaction history. Coherent interference among long-range memory channels can induce anomalous spectral winding and skin localization opposite to the dominant-coupling direction. Moreover, asymmetric retardation alone can generate non-Hermitian spectral topology and skin accumulation even in the absence of spatial coupling asymmetry. Channel dissipation further drives topological transitions that reverse the skin effect, with a critical point at matched channel and lattice decay rates. We validate the anomalous skin effect and its dissipation-controlled reversal through full-wave simulations and microwave experiments. Our results establish time nonlocality as a fundamental degree of freedom in non-Hermitian topology, revealing phenomena inaccessible within the Markovian paradigm.

</details>


### [365] [Reconfigurable Generation of Terahertz Spiral Vector Beams Based on All-Dielectric Cascaded Metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41392)
*Hongjun Chen,Yuxin Tang,Jianming Li,Jitao Li,Li Luo,Tingting Tang,Jie Li,Jianquan Yao*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种全介质级联超表面方案，通过各向同性相位补偿层与各向异性圆偏振敏感层的相对旋转，实现太赫兹螺旋矢量光束的可重构生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂结构太赫兹光场（尤其矢量光束）的可重构生成仍具挑战，现有方案难以同时实现偏振手性可逆与螺旋数可切换。

Method: 全介质级联超表面：将各向同性相位补偿层与各向异性圆偏振敏感层组合，并设计适配螺旋光束的新相位分布；利用两层间相对旋转角独立调控LCP与RCP分量。

Result: 在线偏振太赫兹波照射下，约0.9 THz处实现螺旋数1–3可切换、圆偏振手性可逆的太赫兹矢量光束；三种光束沿传播方向分别呈现279.9°、125.7°和75.5°的方位角旋转。

Conclusion: 该方案兼具可重构性与纵向演化特性，可应用于下一代无线通信与太赫兹成像等领域，并为高维矢量光束的可重构生成提供参考。

Abstract: The reconfigurable generation of complex structured terahertz (THz) fields, and vector beams in particular, remains a significant challenge. Here, we propose a novel all-dielectric cascaded-metasurface scheme for the reconfigurable generation of THz spiral vector beams. By combining an isotropic phase-compensation layer with an anisotropic circular-polarization-sensitive layer, together with a new phase design tailored to spiral beams, we achieve independent control of the left- and right-handed circularly polarized (LCP and RCP) components through the relative rotation angle between the two layers. Under linearly polarized THz wave illumination, THz vector beams with switchable spiral numbers of 1-3 and reversible circular-polarization handedness are demonstrated at frequencies around 0.9 THz. Moreover, owing to the longitudinal evolution characteristic of spiral beams, the three generated THz vector beams exhibit azimuthal rotations of 279.9°, 125.7° and 75.5°, respectively, along the propagation direction. Such complex THz vector beams, which combine reconfigurability with longitudinal evolution, are expected to find applications in emerging fields such as next-generation wireless communications and THz imaging. The proposed generation scheme also offers a useful reference for the reconfigurable generation of high-dimensional vector beams.

</details>


### [366] [Ultraviolet Raman spectroscopy using multimode aluminum oxide waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41503)
*Roman Zakoldaev,Nikhil Jayakumar,Elie Vennat-Bresson,Jean-Claude Tinguely,Firehun Tsige Dullo,Balpreet Singh Ahluwalia*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文研究氧化铝（Al2O3）波导作为可见至紫外波段低背景拉曼光谱平台，在532 nm与325 nm激发下将其背景与Si3N4对比，并以浸油为模型分析物验证信号可辨识性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数波导拉曼平台工作在近红外，而向可见/紫外扩展可利用更短波长获得更强拉曼散射及共振增强响应；但短波长对材料的光学损耗、吸收与波导自身光谱背景提出更高要求，因此需寻找低背景的短波长波导材料。

Method: 采用背向收集激发与检测几何，在532 nm与325 nm激发下测试Al2O3波导的波导自身光谱背景（532 nm处与Si3N4对比，并按入射激发功率-时间乘积归一化；325 nm下考察不同波导宽度），并以覆盖于波导表面的浸油为模型分析物，用信背比与光谱对比度定量评估信号可见性。

Result: 532 nm激发下Al2O3测得背景较Si3N4低两个数量级以上；325 nm激发下各宽度波导背景弱且可重复；两种波长下均能在波导背景之上恢复出分析物特征拉曼指纹，信背比与光谱对比度一致确认分析物谱带被清晰检测。

Conclusion: Al2O3兼具低实测光谱背景与可见到紫外的宽波段工作能力，是通向更短激发波长集成拉曼光谱的可行平台。

Abstract: Waveguide-based Raman spectroscopy offers a route toward compact and integrated photonic sensors. While most waveguide Raman platforms operate in the near-infrared, extending excitation toward the visible and ultraviolet is attractive because shorter excitation wavelengths can provide substantially stronger Raman scattering and access to resonance-enhanced molecular responses. This transition, however, places greater demands on the photonic material because of optical loss, absorption and waveguide-generated spectral background. Here, we investigate aluminum oxide ($Al_2O_3$) waveguides as a low-background platform for Raman spectroscopy at 532 nm and 325 nm using a back-collected excitation and detection geometry. At 532 nm, after normalization to the incident excitation power-time product, the measured $Al_2O_3$ background was more than two orders of magnitude lower than that of $Si_3N_4$. Under 325 nm excitation, the waveguides maintained a weak and reproducible spectral background across the investigated widths. Using immersion oil as a model analyte deposited over the waveguide surface, we demonstrate that analyte-specific Raman fingerprints can be recovered above the waveguide background at both wavelengths. Signal-to-background ratio and spectral contrast were used to quantify analyte visibility relative to the waveguide background, and both metrics consistently confirmed clear detection of the analyte bands. These results demonstrate that $Al_2O_3$ combines low measured spectral background with operation from the visible into the ultraviolet, providing a promising route toward integrated Raman spectroscopy at shorter excitation wavelengths.

</details>


### [367] [The Generalized Twin Image: Understanding Regularized Phase Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41531)
*Zhiyuan Hu,Michael Unser,Jonathan Dong*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 论文提出"广义孪生像"（generalized twin image），将经典傅里叶相位恢复中的孪生像歧义推广到任意可逆感知算子，并以此给出评估图像先验正则化有效性的简洁判据，用全变差比值（total-variation ratio）量化先验对真值相对其广义孪生像的偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像先验能显著改善相位恢复重建，但其有效性在不同成像模态间差异很大，缺乏解释该差异的理论工具。论文希望从歧义结构角度理解先验为何在某些感知模型下有效、在另一些下失效。

Method: 1) 将经典孪生像歧义从傅里叶相位恢复推广至任意可逆感知算子，构造广义孪生像；2) 分析其在欠采样与过采样（含编码傅里叶测量）下的精确性；3) 提出判据：图像先验应在真值与广义孪生像之间偏好真值，正则化才有效；4) 以全变差比值作为该偏好的简单度量，并为结构化随机相位恢复模型推导闭式估计；5) 用预训练去噪器开展实验，将比值与正则化重建性能对照。

Result: 广义孪生像歧义在欠采样测量下保持精确，对过采样编码傅里叶测量同样精确；实验表明全变差比值能够预测不同感知模型下正则化重建的实际性能。

Conclusion: 先验正则化的有效性可由感知算子的广义孪生像结构解释，全变差比值提供了一个简单、可计算的预测性指标，有助于针对不同成像模态选择或评估先验。

Abstract: Image priors can dramatically improve phase retrieval reconstruction, but their effectiveness strongly varies between imaging modalities. We introduce the generalized twin image, which extends the classical twin-image ambiguity from Fourier phase retrieval to any invertible sensing operator, to understand this effectiveness. The ambiguity extends to undersampled and oversampled measurements, remaining exact under undersampling and for oversampled coded Fourier measurements. We show that it provides a simple criterion to assess regularization effectiveness: the image prior should favor the ground truth over its generalized twin image. We propose the total-variation ratio as a simple measure of this preference, and derive closed-form estimates for structured random phase retrieval models. Experiments with a pretrained denoiser demonstrate that the ratio predicts regularized reconstruction performance across different sensing models.

</details>


### [368] [Photonic Crystal Integrated Graphene-Silicon Schottky Diodes for Resonant Infrared Photodetection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41701)
*Lorenzo Pettorosso,Michael Horodynski,Dmitry K. Polyushkin,Thomas Mueller*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种将光子晶体板集成于石墨烯-硅肖特基结的短波红外窄带光电探测器，光电流提升逾两个数量级且与数值仿真一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代智能低功耗光电探测需要窄带红外探测能力，而石墨烯吸收率低且宽带响应，难以实现波长选择性探测。

Method: 构建石墨烯-硅肖特基结器件，集成光子晶体（PhC）板以对石墨烯吸收进行波长相关调控；通过光电流测量表征器件，并用数值仿真预测增强峰。

Result: PhC集成使光电流增强超过两个数量级，出现尖锐的窄带响应峰；增强位置与幅度可由数值仿真准确预测。

Conclusion: 该方案具备高可扩展性、波长可调且CMOS兼容，为下一代低功耗窄带红外传感器提供可行路径。

Abstract: Narrowband infrared (IR) photodetection has become a critical technology for the next generation of smart low-power photodetectors. In this work, we present a device based on a graphene-silicon Schottky junction with narrowband response in the short-wave IR (SWIR) regime. Integrating a photonic crystal (PhC) slab in the device enables a wavelength dependant tailoring of graphene absorption. The devices were characterized by photocurrent measurement, which was enhanced more than two orders of magnitude by the PhC integration. The sharp enhancements obtained can also be predicted by numerical simulation. This approach offers a highly scalable, tunable, CMOS-compatible architecture for narrowband IR photodetection, paving the way for next-generation, low-power IR sensors.

</details>


### [369] [Focusing through coupled multimode fibers for miniaturized single-atom tweezer arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41917)
*Benjamin P. Maddox,Daniel K. Ruttley*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 利用2×1多模光纤熔融耦合器在共用出射端面上形成两路独立、近衍射极限的光镊，实现对准不敏感的可重构双光镊系统，为低SWaP-C环境下的单原子阵列提供方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子技术实地部署需要低SWaP-C（尺寸、重量、功耗与成本）系统。基于超冷原子的平台通常通过庞大物镜聚焦光镊来囚禁与操控原子，成本高且对失准敏感；多模光纤（MMF）虽可紧凑聚焦，但此前仅用于单根光纤形成光镊，缺乏多路独立控制能力。

Method: 采用2×1多模光纤熔融耦合器，获得共享同一远端端面的两条独立控制通道；分别通过每个通道形成近衍射极限光镊；在通道间施加小幅频率失谐以抑制干涉；并测试光镊间距对输入耦合失准的敏感性。

Result: 每个通道均可形成近衍射极限光镊；频率失谐有效抑制了通道间干涉；实验证明光镊间距对输入耦合失准不敏感，具备对准鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作为低SWaP-C环境下实现可重构单原子阵列提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Real-world deployment of quantum technologies requires systems in low SWaP-C packages. Quantum platforms based on ultracold atoms typically trap and control atoms with optical tweezers using light focused through bulky objective lenses, which are costly and sensitive to misalignment. Multimode fibers (MMFs) offer a compact route to focusing light, but their use in forming optical tweezers for cold atoms has been limited to a single fiber. Here we use a 2x1 MMF fused coupler to obtain two independent control paths sharing a common distal facet. We form near-diffraction-limited tweezers through each channel, suppress interference by applying a small frequency offset between the channels, and demonstrate that the tweezer separation is insensitive to misalignment of the input coupling. This work provides a route to reconfigurable single-atom arrays in low SWaP-C environments.

</details>


### [370] [Gearbox cavities: Ultrahigh wave confinement via broken rotational symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42003)
*Alessio Amaolo,Sean Molesky,Alejandro W. Rodriguez,George Shaker,Steven G. Johnson,Pengning Chao*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文结合逆设计与基本极限理论，证明在无损二维腔中必须打破圆柱对称性才能最大化局域态密度；破缺对称性设计相对所有圆柱对称结构的极限提升可达8个数量级，其机制是作为"变速箱"将点源辐射转换为被困于离心势垒后的高角动量波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腔增强点源辐射依赖保持圆柱对称的结构（如圆Bragg光栅），但此类对称性是否构成性能最优的必要条件尚不清楚；作者希望借助逆设计与基本极限，厘清对称性对局域态密度上限的约束。

Method: 将逆设计方法与二维无损腔的局域态密度基本极限理论相结合，在圆柱对称结构类上推导极限，并与破缺对称性的优化设计进行对比。

Result: 破缺对称性的设计相对所有圆柱对称结构的极限提升最高达8个数量级；其物理图像为"变速箱"机制，把源辐射转换为高角动量波，并被离心势垒囚禁。

Conclusion: 圆柱对称性并非最优腔增强的必要条件，反而是限制因素；打破对称性可显著超越对称结构的上限，离心势垒囚禁高角动量波为超强局域态密度提供新途径。

Abstract: Cavity enhancement of emission from a point source typically relies on structures that preserve its cylindrical symmetry, such as circular Bragg gratings. Combining inverse design with fundamental limits, we show that breaking this symmetry is necessary to maximize the local density of states in lossless two-dimensional cavities. Symmetry-broken designs exceed limits over all cylindrically symmetric structures by up to eight orders of magnitude, acting as ``gearboxes'' that convert the source's emission into high-angular-momentum waves trapped behind a centrifugal barrier.

</details>


### [371] [Field-resolved observation and quantification of the instantaneous Kerr response in semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42098)
*Haifeng Lin,Hannes Kempf,Hinduja Bhuvanendran,Aitor De Andrés,Markus Ludwig,Nicolò Maccaferri*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 采用场分辨简并泵浦-探测光谱在5 μm处直接测量锗的泵致相移及其时间演化，获得瞬时Kerr主导证据与n₂=(0.87±0.16)×10⁻¹⁷ m²/W。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于强度的非线性折射率测量方法只能从光束传播或光谱变化间接推断非线性相移，且z-scan等无法分辨非线性响应的时间演化，难以区分瞬时Kerr效应与自由载流子等延迟贡献。

Method: 在5 μm波段对锗开展场分辨简并泵浦-探测光谱：测量透射探测光电场的泵浦-探测延迟依赖，直接提取泵致相移及其时间演化，同时保留对吸收变化的敏感性。

Result: 观测到跟随泵浦强度包络、在泵浦与探测不再重叠时消失的交叉相位调制，符合瞬时Kerr响应；相移随泵浦强度线性变化，拟合得n₂=(0.87±0.16)×10⁻¹⁷ m²/W。

Conclusion: 在所给实验条件下瞬时Kerr效应是测得非线性相移的主导贡献；场分辨泵浦-探测光谱可作为直接、时间分辨的非线性折射率测量手段，并能区分瞬时与延迟光学响应。

Abstract: The optical Kerr effect, whereby the refractive index of a material changes with light intensity, underpins nonlinear photonics and emerging mid-infrared technologies. Accurate determination of the nonlinear refractive index is therefore essential, yet conventional intensity-based techniques infer the nonlinear phase shift indirectly from changes in beam propagation or optical spectra. Moreover, methods such as the $z$-scan cannot resolve the temporal evolution of the nonlinear response, making it difficult to distinguish instantaneous Kerr nonlinearities from delayed contributions, including free-carrier effects. Here, we investigate the nonlinear optical response of germanium at $5 μ\mathrm{m}$ using field-resolved degenerate pump-probe spectroscopy. By measuring the transmitted probe electric field as a function of pump-probe delay, we directly retrieve the pump-induced phase shift and its temporal evolution while retaining sensitivity to changes in absorption. We observe cross-phase modulation that follows the pump intensity envelope and vanishes when the pump and probe no longer overlap, consistent with an instantaneous Kerr response. The phase shift scales linearly with pump intensity, yielding a nonlinear refractive index of $n_2=(0.87\pm0.16)\times10^{-17} \mathrm{m}^2/\mathrm{W}$. Our results establish the instantaneous Kerr effect as the dominant contribution to the measured nonlinear phase shift under our experimental conditions and demonstrate field-resolved pump-probe spectroscopy as a direct, time-resolved method for determining the nonlinear refractive index and distinguishing instantaneous from delayed optical responses.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [372] [HippocamFLEX: FPGA Prototyping of Hippocampus-Inspired Architecture for Context-Aware Sequence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40932)
*Bhuvanesh S. Kayarabettu,Hamed Poursiami,Keiland Cooper,Babak Shahbaba,Norbert Fortin,Maryam Parsa,Akhilesh Jaiswal*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出HippocamFLEX——海马三突触通路在FPGA上的全可综合数字实现，实现上下文门控的序列预测与跨时间重放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统硬件将记忆视为静态模式存储，缺乏海马式的有序序列组织与时间压缩重放能力，因而无法进行上下文适宜的序列预测；深度加速器与现有神经形态硬件均不具备该能力。

Method: 用SystemVerilog RTL在AMD Zynq UltraScale+ XCZU9EG上实现三级流水线：DG/EC级做超向量近邻匹配；CA3级做上下文门控的循环序列预测并带多相位时间标记；CA1级融合过去、现在与预测未来事件生成θ序列式输出。设计支持64个事件、16个上下文。

Result: 在物理硅片上通过上下文隔离、时间排序与序列中段滑窗测试验证，且可扩展到更大事件与上下文规模。

Conclusion: 选择FPGA原型而非模拟混合信号方案是刻意的架构取舍——免流片成本、便于设计空间探索、位精确确定性简化验证；证明海马计算原理可完全数字化综合实现。

Abstract: Unlike conventional architectures that treat memory as static pattern storage, the hippocampus organizes experience as ordered sequences and retrieves them through temporally compressed replay spanning prior, current, and anticipated states. This capacity of context-appropriate sequence prediction remains absent from conventional deep learning accelerators and existing neuromorphic fabrics. We present HippocamFLEX, a fully synthesizable digital implementation of these hippocampal computational principles, realized with SystemVerilog RTL on the AMD Zynq UltraScale+,XCZU9EG. The design maps the hippocampal trisynaptic pathway onto three pipeline stages: a Dentate Gyrus / Entorhinal Cortex (DG/EC) stage for hypervector proximity matching, a CA3 stage for context-gated recurrent sequence prediction with multi-phase temporal tagging, and a CA1 stage that integrates past, present, and predicted future events into a theta-sequence inspired output. While much brain-inspired hardware relies on analog mixed-signal design for energy efficiency, this work targets FPGA prototyping as a deliberate architectural choice: FPGAs offer rapid design-space exploration without tape-out cost, bit-exact determinism that simplifies verification. The design supports 64 events across 16 contexts and is verified on physical silicon across context-isolation, temporal-ordering, and mid-sequence sliding-window test cases and is inherently scalable to larger event and context sets.

</details>
